miyā eṉṟa muṉṉili aciccolliluḷḷa ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaram ākum. iḵtu orucollil varum kuṟṟiya likaram. kēṇmiyā eṉṟa collil yā eṉṉum eḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl, makaratti ūrntu varum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikara māka oru māttiriyiṟ kuṟuki ari māttiriyāy olikkum.	(naṉ. 93)
acipaṟṟik kuṟṟukara vaki kōṭal -
‘neṭilē kuṟiliṇi kuṟilneṭil eṉṟivi
 oṟṟoṭu varutaloṭu kuṟṟoṟ ṟiṟutieṉ(ṟu)
 ēḻkuṟ ṟukarak(ku) iṭaṉeṉa moḻipa’
eṉṉum ivvēḻiṭattum (acipaṟṟi) varum kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ kaḷ neṭiṟṟoṭar mutalākiya āṟu toṭarkaḷuḷḷum aṭaṅkum. ivvāṟu ēḻiṭam koḷvārkkuc cuṇṇāmpu - āmaṇakku - piṇṇākku - mutalāyiṉavum, āytam toṭarn taṉavum aṭaṅkā eṉṟaṟika.	(naṉ. 93 mayili.)
acipaṟṟik kuṟṟiyalukaram varumāṟu -
e-ṭu :	kācu - neṭilaciyi aṭuttatu; varaku - kuṟiliṇi aciyi aṭuttatu; malāṭu - kuṟilneṭil aciyi aṭuttatu; kāṟṟu - neṭiloṟṟu aciyi aṭuttatu; eḻuttu - kuṟiliṇiyoṟṟu aciyi aṭuttatu; vilāṅku - kuṟilneṭiloṟṟu aciyi aṭuttatu. kappu	- kuṟṟoṟṟu aciyi aṭuttatu.
iviyēḻum muṟiyē neṭiṟṟoṭar - uyirttoṭar - uyirttoṭar - vaṉtoṭar - vaṉṟoṭar - meṉṟoṭar - vaṉṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷāka aṭaṅkumāṟu kāṇka.
añcevi añciṟi  aṅki  : toṭarvaki -
nakarppuṟam eṉpataṉip puṟanakar eṉṟāṟpōlac ceviyakam - kiyakam - eṉṉum ivviṭavuṟupputtaṟkiḻamip peyaroṭu muṭinta āṟām vēṟṟumittokic colli muṉ piṉṉāka vaḻaṅkaliṉ, ivviraṇṭum ilakkaṇap pōli yāyt taḻāttoṭarāy alvaḻiyuḷ oṉṟām eṉka. akañciṟi eṉpatu ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉ ciṟi pōlap puṟattiṉ kaṇṇa aṉṟi akattiṉkaṇṇa ātaliṉ ēḻām vēṟṟumit toki.	(naṉ. 222. caṅkara.)
aṇpal mutal -
palliṉatu aṇiya iṭam	(to. e. 96. iḷa. uri)
aṇpalliṉ aṭi	(nac. uri)
aṇpal : viṉittoki. nāviḷimpu aṇukutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya pal eṉṟu ataṟkoru peyarāyiṟṟu. (aṇpal nāviḷimpu aṇukutaṟkākavē amintaṉa alla. iṅṅaṉam uḷporuḷ allataṉi uḷporuḷ pōlak kuṟippiṭu tal tantu puṇarnturittal eṉṟa uttivakiyām.)	(to. e. 86 nac. uri)
mēlvāyp paṟkaḷukkup piṉṉaruḷḷa aṇṇam	(e. ku. pak.40)
mēṟpalliṉ mutaliṭam	(e. ā. pak. 78)
mēlvāyppalliṉ aṭi eṉpatē teḷivāṉa poruḷām.
aṇi eṉṟa iṭiccol puṇarcci -
aṇi eṉpatu aṇiya iṭatti uṇarttum ikarīṟṟu iṭic col. aḵtu aṇikkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumvaḻi iṭiyē valloṟṟu mikum.		(to. e. 236 nac.)
aṇi + aṇi = aṇṇaṇi eṉap puṇarntu, atu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumvaḻi iṭiyē valloṟṟup peṟum.
varumāṟu :	aṇṇaṇik koṇṭāṉ. (to. e. 246 nac.)
aṇuttiraḷ oli
oli aṇuttiraḷik kāraṇamākak koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ kāriyamāka varum eḻuttu. moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇam eḻut tāṉāṟ pōla, eḻuttukku mutaṟkāraṇam oliaṇuttiraḷ eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.	(naṉ. 58 caṅ)

attiṉ akaram akaramuṉik keṭutal -
nilimoḻi akarīṟṟuc collāka niṟka, atu varumoḻi yoṭu cērumiṭattu iṭiyē attuccāriyi variṉ, attuc cāriyiyiṉ akaram keṭa, ēṉiya eḻuttukkaḷē nili moḻiyoṭu puṇarum eṉpatu viti.
e-ṭu : maka+attu+ki ḻū maka+ttu+ki = makattukki 		(to. e. 125.nac).
	oḻika + iṉi ḻū oḻik+iṉi = oḻikiṉi; uṟṟa + uḻi ḻū uṟṟ + uḻi = uṟṟuḻi; ceyka + eṉṟāṉ ḻū ceyk+eṉṟāṉ = ceykeṉṟāṉ; nāṭāka + oṉṟō ḻū nāṭāk+oṉṟō = nāṭākoṉṟō
ivvāṟu varumoḻimutaṟkaṇ i u e o eṉpaṉa variṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram keṭutalē perumpāṉmi. maka + attu = makattu eṉa, iṅkum nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaramē keṭṭatu eṉal vēṇṭum. nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram keṭa varumoḻi akaram cēra ‘makattu’ eṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉpataṉi viṭa, varumoḻimutal akaram keṭṭup puṇarntatu eṉṟu kūṟutal eḷitākaliṉ aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭṭatu.
maka + attu ḻū mak + attu = makattu eṉpataṉiviṭa, maka + attu ḻū maka + ttu = makattu eṉṟal eḷitākaliṉ, muṭinta muṭivil vēṟṟumiyiṉṟu ātaliṉ ivvāṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.		(e.ā.pak. 101)
attiṉ akaram akaramuṉṉarēyaṉṟip piṟavuyir muṉṉarum orōvaḻik keṭutaluṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	aṇṇā + attu + ēri = aṇṇāttēri;	tiṭṭā + attu + kuḷam = tiṭṭāttukkuḷam	(to. e. 133 nac.uri)
nilimoḻi meyyīṟṟatāka, attuccāriyi variṉ, nili moḻi oṟṟuk keṭātu muṭitalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	viṇ + attu + koṭkum = viṇṇattuk koṭkum
	veyil + attu + ceṉṟāṉ = veyilattuc ceṉ ṟāṉ; iruḷ + attu + ceṉṟāṉ = iruḷattuc ceṉṟāṉ	(nac. 133 uri)
attuccāriyi puṇarumāṟu -
akarīṟṟuccolmuṉ attuccāriyi varumiṭattu ac cāriyiyiṉ akaram keṭap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	maka + attu + ki ḻū maka + ttu + ki = makattukki
	meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ attuccāriyi varumiṭattu nilimoḻiyiṉ īṟṟumey perumpālum keṭum.
e-ṭu : 	maram + attu + kōṭu ḻū mara + attu + kōṭu ḻū mara+ ttu + kōṭu = marattuk kōṭu; varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ, vanta valloṟṟu iṭiyē mikum.
ciṟupāṉmi nilimoḻi yīṟṟumey keṭāmal, veyi lattuc ceṉṟāṉ - iruḷattuc ceṉṟāṉ - viṇṇattuk koṭkum - eṉṟu muṭitalumuṇṭu.	(to. e. 133 nac. uri)
attuccāriyiyiṉ akaram keṭutal -
attuccāriyi varumiṭattu, nilimoḻiyīṟṟil iyalpu vakiyālō vitivakiyālō akaram niṟpa, attuc cāriyi yiṉ akaram keṭṭu muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	maka + attu + ki = makattukki - iyalpu akarīṟu; maram + attu + kuṟi ḻū mara + attu + kuṟi = marattuk kuṟi - viti akarīṟu	(naṉ. 252)

attuccāriyi varum iṭaṅkaḷ -
akara ākāra īṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻaṉurupoṭu puṇaru miṭattum, makarīṟṟup peyarkaḷ urupoṭu puṇarumiṭattum, aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṟa coṟkaḷ urupoṭu puṇarumiṭattum iṭiyē attuc cāriyi varum.
e-ṭu :	viḷa + attu + kaṇ = viḷavattukkaṇ; palā + attu + kaṇ = palāvattukkaṇ (to. e. 181 nac.); maram + attu + ai = maratti (to.e. 185 nac.); aḻaṉ + attu + ai = aḻatti; puḻaṉ + attu + ai = puḻatti (to.e. 193 nac.); ekiṉ + attu + ai = ekiṉatti	(to.e. nac. uri)
iṉi, poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, kalam eṉṟa colmuṉ kuṟi eṉṟa col ummittokippaṭap puṇarumiṭattu attuccāriyi iṭiyē varak kalattukkuṟi eṉa muṭiyum.	(to.e. 168 nac.)
maka eṉṟa peyar poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ makattukki eṉa attuc cāriyi iṭiyē peṟum.	(to.e. 219 nac.)
nilā eṉṟa col nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa attuc cāriyi peṟum.	(to.e. 228 nac.)
paṉi, vaḷi, maḻi, veyil, iruḷ, viṇ, makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar, āyiram eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar eṉṉumiviyum iṭiyē attuccāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉa.
e-ṭu  paṉiyattuc ceṉṟāṉ- to.e. 241 nac.
vaḷiyattuc ceṉṟāṉ - to.e. 242 nac.
		maḻiyattuc ceṉṟāṉ-	to.e. 287 nac.
		veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ	-	to.e. 377 nac.
		iruḷattuc ceṉṟāṉ	-	to.e. 402 nac.
		viṇṇattuk koṭkum	-	to.e. 305 nac.
		makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ	-	to.e. 331 nac.
		āyirat toṉṟu	-	to.e. 317 nac.
‘ataṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ mutaṉili iyaṟṟē’ -
uriyāciriyaṉmār ellām ‘makaṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōtiṉar. makaṉatu viṉiyik kūṟumiṭattu val leḻuttu mikum eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, ‘makaṉṟāyk kalām’ eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭi, makaṉ tāyoṭu kalāytta kalām- eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉar.
makaṉ viṉiyē aṉṟi, ‘makaḷ tāyk kalām’ eṉa makaḷ viṉi kiḷappiṉum, vāḷā ‘tāyk kalām’ eṉavē kiḷappiṉum, valleḻuttu mikutal ām;tāy eṉpatu viravuppeyar ātaliṉ, makavu - piḷḷi - eṉa aḵṟiṇic coṟkaḷik kūṭṭik kūṟiṉum, ivviti poruntum. ātalāṉum ‘makaṉ’ eṉa varintu kūṟutal kuṉṟak kūṟalām. aṉṟiyum, val leḻuttu mikutaṟku makaṉ eṉṉum col evvāṟṟāṉum ētuvākāmiyum kāṇka. ‘ataṉviṉi’ eṉpatu ‘mutaṉili’ eṉṉum cīroṭu poḻippetuki porunti yāppici ciṟantu niṟṟalum kāṇka.	(to.e. 359 ca.pāla.)
atāaṉṟu -
‘atu +aṉṟu’ eṉṉum puṇarmoḻi ‘atāaṉṟu’ eṉpatu. ippuṇarmoḻit toṭar ‘aḵtaṉṟu’ eṉa muṟṟāyum, ‘atuvē aṉṟiyum’ eṉa eccamāyum caṅka vilakkiyattuḷ payiṉṟu varutal kāṇalām. ākavē, ic coṟkaḷ viṉā viṭi muṟiyil ‘atuvā? aṉṟu’ eṉa niṉṟu (atu- ā - aṉṟu) atā - aṉṟu, atāaṉṟu - eṉṟu puṇarntuḷḷami aṟiyalām.	(to.e. 258. ca.pāla.)
atāaṉṟu eṉṟa collamippu -
atu eṉṟa nilimoḻi aṉṟu eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭumiṭattu nilimoḻit tukaram ‘tā’ eṉattiriya, atāaṉṟu eṉa muṭiyum.	(to. e. 258 nac.)
atāaṉṟu eṉpatu atuvaṉṟi eṉṉum poruḷatu. aṉṟi eṉṟa viṉiyeccam aṉṟu eṉattirintatō eṉak karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, iṉṟi eṉpatu iṉṟu eṉat tirivatu pōla, orōvaḻi aṉṟi eṉpatum aṉṟu eṉat tiritalum peṟappaṭum.
atu itu utu eṉpaṉa mūlat tirāviṭa moḻiyil at it ut eṉa meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷāka iruntaṉa. atu itu utu eṉpaṉa vaṭa maliyāḷattil pōlavē, tamiḻilum ata ita uta eṉac cilaviṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyiruttal kūṭum. akarīṟṟiṉ muṉ akaram variṉ, orucol nīrmiyil ivviraṇṭu akaramum ōr ākāramāki vaḻaṅkum. ātaliṉ ata + aṉṟu = atāṉṟu eṉṟu vantatu. mara + aṭi = marāṭi eṉṟāvatu pōlvatu itu. varumoḻi ‘aṉṟu’ eṉpatit terivikka, atāaṉṟu eṉṟu akaram aṟikuṟi eṉṟa aḷavil eḻutap paṭṭa tākum.	(e. ā. pak. 143)
atikāram -
patiṉāṉku vakiyāṉa uriyuḷ, atikāramāvatu eṭuttuk koṇṭa atikāram ituvātaliṉ iccūttirattu atikaritta poruḷ ituveṉa avvatikārattoṭu porunta urikka vēṇṭuḻi urittal.	(naṉ. 21 caṅkara.)
atikāram eṉṟa coṟporuḷ -
atikāram - muṟimi	(to. e. 1. nac. uri)
atikāram - atikarittal. vaṭanūlārum ōriṭattu niṉṟa col palacūttiraṅkaḷoṭu ceṉṟiyitaliyum, oṉṟaṉ ilakkaṇam paṟṟi varum palacūttirat tokutiyiyum atikāram eṉpa.	(to. co. 1 cēṉā. uri)
atikāram atikarittal. aḵtu iruvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, vēntaṉ iruntuḻi iruntu taṉ nilam muḻuvatum taṉṉāṇiyiṉ naṭappac ceyvatu pōla, orucol niṉṟuḻi niṉṟu palacūttiraṅkaḷum pala ōttukkaḷum taṉ poruḷē nutalivarac ceyvatu. maṟṟoṉṟu, ceṉṟu naṭāttum taṇṭattalivar pōla, ōriṭattu niṉṟa col palacūttiraṅkaḷoṭu ceṉṟiyintu taṉporuḷip payappippatu. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē vaṭanūlār yatōttēca pakkam eṉavum, kāriyakāla pakkam eṉavum kūṟupa.	(cū. vi. pak. 17) (naṉ. 56 civañā).
e-ṭu	: eḻuttup paṟṟivarum palacūttiraṅkaḷiṉ tokuti eḻuttatikāram eṉappaṭṭatu.
	tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāram 34ām cūttiratti luḷḷa ‘niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa toṭar ‘kuṟṟiya lukaram vallāṟu ūrntu  niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu ceṉṟiyintu taṉ poruḷip payap pippatu atikārattāṉ vantatākum.
atikāram : coṟporuḷ viḷakkam  -
atikāram - atikarittal. ‘atikāram eṉṟa coṟporuḷ’ kāṇka.
atumuṉ varum aṉṟu -
‘atu’ nilimoḻiyāka, ataṉmuṉ varum ‘aṉṟu’ ceyyuṭ kaṇ atāṉṟu eṉa muṭiyum. atuvaṉṟi eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ.	(naṉ. 180)
‘atimaṟṟamma’ eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar amippu -
atu + maṟṟu + amma ḻū atu + ai + maṟṟu + amma = ati maṟṟamma.
atu eṉṟa coṟkum maṟṟu eṉṟa coṟkumiṭiyē aikārac cāriyi vantavaḻi, nilimoḻi ukaram keṭa atu ‘at’ eṉṟāka, ataṉōṭu aikāram cērntu ‘ati’ eṉṟāki varumoḻiyōṭu atimaṟṟamma eṉap puṇarntatu.	(to. e. 258 nac. uri)
antarppāvitaṇic -
ṇic eṉpatu piṟaviṉi vikutiyākum. antarppāvitam eṉpatu maṟital eṉṉum poruḷatu. eṉavē, antarppā- vitaṇic eṉpatu piṟaviṉivikuti maṟintiruppatām.
terivittu eṉṟa collil piṟaviṉiyik kāṭṭum viv vikuti veḷippaṭiyāka uḷḷatu. terintu eṉṟa col terivittu eṉṟu poruḷ tarumiṭattup piṟaviṉi vikuti maṟintuḷḷatu.
tapu eṉpatu ‘nī cā’ eṉṟa poruḷil taṉviṉiyām; ‘nī oṉṟaṉic cāvappaṇṇu’ eṉṟa poruḷil piṟaviṉiyām; piṟaviṉip poruḷil ‘tapuvi’ eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa piṟaviṉi vikuti maṟintu vantatu. aḵtu antarppāvita ṇic eṉpar pirayōkavivēka nūlār. avarip piṉpaṟṟic civañāṉa muṉivarum, “terivittu eṉaṟpālatu ‘terintu’ eṉa vantami antarppāvita ṇic ākiya piṟaviṉi vikuti tokku niṟṟal” eṉṟār.	(pā. vi. civa. pak. 10).
antarppāvita ṇic eṉpatu tamiḻmarapukku ēlātu eṉpati aracañ caṇmukaṉār viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār. 	(pā. vi. pak. 218 - 220)
anuvātam -
muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa oruceytiyiyē piṉṉarum piṟitoru kāraṇam paṟṟi eṭuttukkūṟutal kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṟa kuṟṟa mākātu, anuvātam eṉṉum vaḻimoḻitalāy aṭaṅkum.
e-ṭu ‘aṉ āṉ aḷ āḷ ar ār pammār
 aā kuṭu tuṟu eṉ ēṉ al aṉ’ (naṉ. 140).
eṉa, aṉ eṉpataṉip paṭarkki āṇpāl vikutiyātal kāraṇattāl muṉṉark kūṟit taṉmiyorumi vikuti yātal kāraṇattāl piṉṉarum kūṟutal kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟamākātu, vaḻimoḻital eṉṉum anuvāta mām.
‘kūṟiṉa piṉṉum kūṟiṉa cila; avi
 anuvātam eṉṟē aṟintē aṭakkuka’
eṉṉum ilakkaṇak kottu 7 uriyiṉi nōkkuka.
appum uppum kalantami pōlvatu -
taṉimeyyiṉ māttiri ari, uyirkkuṟiliṉ māttiri oṉṟu; uyirneṭiliṉ māttiri iraṇṭu. āyiṉ uyir meykkuṟiliṉ māttiri oṉṟu; uyirmey neṭiliṉ māttiri iraṇṭu. nīr taṉittu aḷantapōtum nāḻiyāy arināḻi uppik kalantapōtum oṉṟari nāḻiyāy mikātavāṟu pōlvatu, oṉṟari māttiriyum iraṇṭari māttiriyum peṟa vēṇṭiya uyirmeyk kuṟilum uyir mey neṭilum muṟiyē oru māttiriyum iru māttiri yum peṟum nili. nīr uppiṉ cuviyatu āyiṉavāṟu pōla, uyirmeyyiluḷḷa uyirum meykkuriya vaṉmi - meṉmi - iṭimi - eṉṟa olikaḷip peṟum eṉpatu.		(to. e. 10, 47 nac. uri)
ammuc cāriyi tiriyumāṟu -
ammuc cāriyiyiṉ makaram varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ kakara cakara takaraṅkaḷ varumiṭattu muṟiyē ṅakara ñakara nakaraṅkaḷākat tiriyum; varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇa mum varumiṭattē keṭum. uyirkkaṇam varumiṭattē makaram keṭutalum ammuc cāriyi muḻutum keṭutalum ām. (pakarattiṟku iṉamelleḻuttu makaram ātaliṉ, pakaram varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varumiṭattu ammuc cāriyiyiṉ makaram iyalpākavē niṟkum.)
e-ṭu	: puḷi + am + kōṭu = puḷiyaṅ kōṭu; puḷi + am + cetiḷ = puḷiyañ cetiḷ; puḷi + am + tōl = puḷiyan tōl; puḷi + am + ñeri = puḷiya ñeri; puḷi + am + yāḻ = puḷiya yāḻ; puḷi + am + ili = puḷiya vili (vakaram uṭampaṭumey); puḷiyili (yakaram uṭampaṭumey); (puḷi + am + paḻam = puḷiyampaḻam - iyalpu)(to. e. 129, 130 nac.)
ammuc cāriyi varumiṭaṅkaḷ -
poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ kīḻkkaṇṭa coṟkaḷ nilimoḻi- yām.
puḷi eṉṉum marappeyar - puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañeri		(to. e. 244 nac.)
eru, ceru eṉṉum coṟkaḷ - eruvaṅkuḻi, ceruvakkaḷam (260)
paṉi, ari, āviri eṉṉum marappeyarkaḷ - paṉaṅkāy, ariyaṅkōṭu, āviraṅkōṭu	(283)
āṇ eṉṟa marappeyar - āṇaṅkōṭu	(304)
ekiṉ eṉṟa marappeyar - ekiṉaṅkōṭu	(336)
ṉakara īṟṟu iyaṟpeyarmuṉṉar makkaḷ muṟippeyar - koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ	(350)
pīr eṉṉum col - pīraṅkoṭi	(365)
pūl, vēl, āl eṉṉum marappeyarkaḷ - pūlaṅkōṭu, vēlaṅkōṭu, ālaṅkōṭu	(375)
kumiḻ eṉṟa marappeyar - kumiḻaṅkōṭu	(386)
kuṟṟukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ ākiya ēṟu, cūtu, vaṭṭu, puṟṟu mutaliyaṉa - ēṟaṅkōḷ, cūtampōr, vaṭṭam pōr, puṟṟam paḻañcōṟu mutalākap puṇarum.	(417)
ivaṟṟuḷ, ceru eṉpataṉmuṉ ammiṉ iṟuti keṭum; paṉi āviri eṉpavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟu aikāram keṭum; ṉakara īṟṟu iyaṟ peyariṉ īṟṟu aṉ keṭṭu ammup puṇarum eṉpaṉa koḷka.
ammuṉ ikarayakaram ai ottu icittal, ammuṉ ukaravakaram oḷa ottu icittal -
uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkam iṉmiyiṉ, ai - au - eṉa iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya kuṟṟuyirkaḷi valintu mayakkik kūṟiṉ aikāra oḷakāram pōl icittalāṉum, kuṟṟoṟṟukkaḷ oṉṟari māttiriya ātaliṉ iraṇṭumāttiriyavākiya aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷōṭu ovvāmiyiṉ olivakiyāṉ mayakkik kūṟiṉ avi otticittalāṉum, avaṟṟuḷḷum vakaram piṟappu vēṟupāṭṭāṉ oliyum orupuṭi ottalāṉum ‘eytiṉ’ eṉṟār.
varumāṟu : ai = ai, ay = ai; ki = ki, kay = ki.		au = oḷa, av = oḷa; ku = ku, kav = ku	(naṉ.125 caṅkara.)
arimā nōkku -
ciṅkam nōkkumiṭattu muṉṉiyāriyum piṉṉiyāri yum nōkkumatu pōla, iṟanta cūttirattiṉōṭum etirnta cūttirattiṉōṭum oru cūttiram iyipupaṭak kiṭakkum nili. e-ṭu : to.e. 88 nac.	(naṉ. 18 mayili.)
aril tapat terital -
aril - mayakkam; tapa - keṭa.
ituvō atuvō eṉṟu aiyuṟum aiyamum, oṉṟaṉi maṟṟoṉṟākap piṟaḻakkoḷḷum tiripum ākiya mayak kaṅkaḷ nīṅkumāṟu terivittal. terital eṉṟa taṉviṉi terivittal eṉṟa piṟaviṉip poruḷatu eṉpa. 	(to. ci. pāyi.)
kaṭā aṟat terintu kūṟi - iḷa.
kuṟṟamaṟa ārāyntu kūṟi - nac.
kuṟṟamaṟat terivittu - civa. pā. vi., e.ku.
‘ariltapat terintu’ eṉpataṉit taṉviṉiyākak koṇṭu, nūṟkuc collappaṭṭa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟa viḷaṅki (oṉṟaṉuḷ piṟitoṉṟu kalavāta marapiṉiyuṭiya taṉatu nūlmuṟiyik kāṭṭi) eṉṟu uri kūṟuvar, aracañ caṇmukaṉār.	(pā. vi. pak. 161)
“terintu eṉpataṟku viḷaṅki eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi marapiṉ viṉi ākkātu, kaṭāaṟat terintu kūṟi eṉavum, kuṟṟamaṟa ārāyntu kūṟi eṉavum, terivittu eṉavum āciriyaṉ viṉiyākki ammūvarum urittār. kaṭāaṟat teritalum kuṟṟamaṟa ārāytalum nūl ceyyuṅkāli vēṇṭappaṭutal aṉṟi nūl araṅkēṟṟuṅkāli vēṇṭappaṭāmiyāṉum, ōr ētuvumiṉṟik ‘kūṟi’ eṉṉum col varuvittal kūṭāmi yāṉum, ‘terivittu eṉpatu terintu eṉa niṉṟatu’ eṉal ilakkaṇam ākāmiyāṉum avaruri poruntā eṉpatu.”	(pā. vi. pak. 215).
caṇmukaṉār karuttāl, ‘nūṟkuc collappaṭṭa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟa viḷaṅki, oṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu kalavāta marapiṉiyuṭiyatu tolkāppiyam’ eṉa, ‘aril tapat terital’ eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūlukku aṭimoḻi ākum.
aril tapa nāṭal -
ituvō atuvō eṉṟu aiyuṟum aiyamum, oṉṟaṉi maṟṟoṉ ṟākavē uṟutiyākak koḷḷum tiripum ākiya mayakkaṅkaḷ nīṅka ārāytal.	(to. e. 102 nac.)
ari eṉṟa marappeyar cāriyi peṟutal -
ari (araca maram) eṉṟa marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟṟu, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ ariyaṅ kōṭu - ariya nuṉi - ariyavaṭṭu eṉṟāṟpōla, ac cāriyik kuriya tiripukaḷ peṟṟup puṇarum.	(to. e. 283. nac.)
alvaḻip puṇarcci -
vēṟṟumip puṇarcci allātatu alvaḻip puṇarcciyām. atu viṉittoki, paṇputtoki, uvamittoki, ummit toki, innāṉkaṉ puṟattum piṟanta aṉ moḻit toki, eḻuvāyttoṭar, viḷittoṭar, iruvaki viṉimuṟṟut toṭar, peyareccat toṭar, viṉiyeccat toṭar, iṭiccoṟṟoṭar, uriccoṟ ṟoṭar, aṭukkuttoṭar eṉap patiṉāṉkām. (ittoki tokā nilit toṭarkaḷil nilimoḻiyum varumoḻi yum puṇarumāṟu alvaḻippuṇarcciyām).
e-ṭu :	kolyāṉi, karuṅkutiri - palāmaram, matimukam, kapila paraṇar, karuṅkuḻal (vantāḷ) - iviyāṟum tokinilit toṭar. poṉṉaṉ vantāṉ, poṉṉā vā, vanta maṉṉaṉ - periya maṉṉaṉ, vantupōṉāṉ - mellap pōṉāṉ, vantāṉ poṉṉaṉ - kuḻiyaṉ poṉṉaṉ, maṟṟoṉṟu, naṉipēti, pāmpu pāmpu - eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka. (teriniliyum kuṟippumāka iruvakip peya reccat toṭar viṉiyeccat toṭarkaḷ viṉi muṟṟut toṭarkaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa).	(naṉ. 152)
alvaḻip puṇarccikku uriyaṉa -
eḻuvāyum, viḷiyum, teriniliviṉiyum kuṟippuviṉiyu mākiya muṟṟuccoṟkaḷum, peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum ākiya eccac coṟkaḷum, iṭiccollum, uriccollum ākiya ivi (nilimoḻiyāka niṟpa,) tamakkēṟṟa peyaro ṭum viṉiyoṭum puṇarum puṇarcciyum, virivu ātiyiṉ varum aṭukkum, uvamit tokiyum, ummit tokiyum (eṉṉumivaṟṟuḷ nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākiya) tamakkēṟṟa peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum puṇarum puṇarcciyum alvaḻip puṇarcciyām.	(i. vi. eḻut. 54)
alvaḻip poruṭpeyar, vēṟṟumip poruṭpeyar -
viṉiccollic cārnta mutaṟpeyarāki virikkumiṭattu vēṟṟumi yurupu peṟātu niṟkum peyarē alvaḻip poruṭ peyar. (e-ṭu : maram peritu).
kalleṭuttāṉ - kalvīṭu - kalliyalpu - eṉpavaṟṟuḷ urupu tōṉṟātāyiṉum poruḷi virittāl kalliyeṭuttāṉ - kallāl ākiya vīṭu - kalliṉatu iyalpu - eṉṟu avvurupu kūṭṭa vēṇṭiṉamiyāl, itilē kal eṉṉum col vēṟṟumip poruṭpeyar eṉappaṭum.
avvurupu tōṉṟāmalum kūṭṭāmalum viritturikkap paṭum peyar alvaḻip poruṭpeyar eṉappaṭum.	(to. vi. 22 uri)
alvaḻiyāvaṉa -
vēṟṟumip puṇarcci allātaṉa alvaḻip puṇarcciyām. ‘vēṟṟumi alvaḻi’ eṉpatē ataṉ muḻuppeyar; curukkam karuti iḵtu ‘alvaḻi’ eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
eḻuvāy vēṟṟumiyāṉatu poruṇmi cuṭṭal - viyaṅkoḷa varutal - viṉinili urittal - viṉāviṟku ēṟṟal - paṇpu koḷa varutal - peyarkoḷa varutal - eṉṟa āṟupayaṉili kaḷoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, muṟṟāṉatu peyaroṭum viṉi yoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, peya reccamum viṉiyeccamum muṟiyē peyaroṭum viṉi yoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, uvamattokiyum ummit tokiyum irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyum tammuḷ nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, iṭiccollum uriccollum peyaroṭum viṉiyo ṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, aṉmoḻittoki peyar viṉikaḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viṉittokiyum paṇputtokiyum virintu niṉṟavaḻip puṇarnta puṇarcci yum alvaḻip puṇarcciyām.	(to. e. 112 nac.)
puṇarcci eṉpatu nilimoḻi varumoḻi iraṇṭa ṉiṭiyē nikaḻvatātaliṉ, paṇputtokiyum viṉit tokiyum pirittup puṇarkkappaṭā (to. e. 483) eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟiyataṉāṉ, avaṟṟi irucollāka avaṟṟiṉ iyalpāṉa niliyil koḷḷutal iyalātu eṉṟu karuti, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ‘viṉittokiyum paṇputtokiyum virintu niṉṟuvaḻip puṇarum puṇarcci’ eṉṟu viḷakkik kūṟiṉār.
‘avvaḷapu uṭiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal’ -
iraṇṭu māttiriyavākiya neṭilkaḷ nīṇṭolittali vēṇṭumpōtu nīṭṭattukkut tēviyāṉa aḷavu anneṭilkaḷukku iṉa motta kuṟilkaḷip piḷavupaṭāmal kūṭṭi ammāttirikaḷi eḻuppuka.	(to. e. 6. iḷa. uri)
vaḻakkiṭattum ceyyuḷiṭattum ōciyum poruḷum peṟutal kāraṇamāka, iraṇṭu māttiri peṟṟa eḻuttu ammāttiriyiṉ mikkolittali virumpuvarāyiṉ, tām karutiya māttiriyik tarutaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷik kūṭṭi ammāttiriyi eḻuppuka.	(nac. uri)
neṭiloṭu kuṟilum kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṟanta piṉṉar ap piḷavupaṭā ōciyi aḷapeṭi eṉṟu āciriyar vēṇṭi ṉār. ivi kūṭṭic colliya kālattallatu pulappaṭā, eḷḷāṭṭiyavaḻi allatu eṇṇey pulappaṭā vāṟu pōla eṉa uṇarka.	(nac. uri)
(eḻu : taṉviṉi; eḻūu : piṟaviṉi - aḷapeṭi poruḷ vēṟupāṭu tantatu.)
tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil aḷapeṭiyi aciniliyākac cila iṭaṅkaḷil koṇṭamiyāṉum, neṭṭeḻutti iraṇ ṭeḻuttākak koṇṭu iraṇṭacikaḷākak koḷḷāmi yāṉum ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār.	(e.ku. pak. 15)
‘avvāṟeḻuttum mūvakip piṟappiṉa’ ātal -
piṟappiṭam : kaṅa mutalnā mutalaṇṇam, caña - iṭinā iṭiyaṇṇam, ṭaṇa - nuṉinā nuṉiyaṇṇam - eṉa ivi uṟap piṟakkum.
kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappiṭamum muyaṟciyum mūvakiyiṉavē; āyiṉ melleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṟku mūkkoli tuṇi ceyyum eṉṟa oṉṟē vēṟupāṭu. ātaliṉ ka mutalnāvilum ṅa mutal aṇṇattilum piṟakkum eṉṟāṟ pōlak koḷḷaṟka eṉpatu.	(to. e. 92. nac.uri)
‘mutalnā aṇṇam’ eṉpatu mutalnāvoṭu poruntiya aṇṇam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. ‘iṭinā aṇṇam’ mutaliyavaṟṟukkum iḵtu okkum.	(e. ku. pak. 93).
avappirañcaṉam -
avappirañcaṉamāvatu iḻicaṉar pēcumoḻi.	(mu.vī. moḻi. 28).
avalōkitaṉ -
palarum āyntu pōṟṟum paṇpuṭiyaṉ avalōkitaṉ eṉṉum puttap periyōṉ eṉavum, avaṉiṭattil akattiyaṉ māṇākkaṉāka iruntu pāṭam kēṭṭut teḷintu ulakōr payaṉpeṟat tamiḻilakkaṇatti iyaṟṟit tantāṉ eṉavum vīracōḻiyam avalōkitaṉip paṟṟik kuṟikkiṟatu.	(vī. cō. pāyi. 2)
avā ‘vēṭki’yoṭu puṇartal -
vēṭki eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka, avā eṉpatu varu moḻiyākap puṇarum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, vēṭki + avā = vēṇavā eṉṟu puṇarum. vēṭki + avā ḻū vēṭ + avā ḻū vēṇ + avā = vēṇavā. vēṭki. yāṉ tōṉṟiya avā eṉpatu poruḷ. vēṭkiyāvatu poruḷkaḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam. avāvāvatu apporuḷkaḷip peṟavēṇṭum eṉa mēṉmēl nikaḻum āci. vēṭkiyum avāvum eṉa alvaḻip puṇarcci koḷḷiṉum ‘vēṇavā’ eṉṟa muṭipu okkum.		(to. e. 288 nac. uri)
vēḷ + avā ḻū vēṇ + avā = vēṇavā eṉa muṭintatu. āḷ - āṇ eṉavum, eḷ - eṇ eṉavum tirintāṟpōla, vēḷ - vēṇ eṉat tirintu puṇarntatu eṉalām.	(e. ā. pak. 148)
‘avitām iyaṟkiya ākum ceyaṟkiya’  poruḷ  -
iyaṟki - cāriyi peṟātu muṭital; ceyaṟki - ceyki. neṭṭeḻutti aṭuttu varum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuccoṟkaḷ urupukaḷi ēṟkumiṭattup potuvitippaṭi iṉcāriyi peṟātu, (iṉoṟṟu aṭuttu) iyalpāka muṭiyum ceykiyi yuṭiyaṉa.
e-ṭu : kāṭu + ai ḻū kāṭṭu + ai = kāṭṭi
 āṟu + ai ḻū āṟṟu + ai = āṟṟi
kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu muṭintavāṟu.	
kavaṭu + ai ḻū kavaṭṭu + ai = kavaṭṭi
muyiṟu + ai ḻū muyiṟṟu + ai = muyiṟṟi
eṉa, uyirttoṭarkaḷiyum uriyāciriyarkaḷ koṇṭa ṉar.	(to. e. 197 nac).
‘aḻaṉ’ urupēṟkiyil peṟum cāriyi -
aḻaṉ eṉṟa col urupēṟkumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum taṉittaṉip peṟṟu muṭiyum; orōvaḻi irucāriyikaḷiyum peṟutaluṇṭu.
varumāṟu :	aḻaṉ + attu + ai = aḻatti; aḻaṉ + iṉ + ai = aḻaṉiṉi; aḻaṉ + attu + iṉ + ai = aḻattiṉi
poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi ṉakaram keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku, aḻaṉ + kuṭam ḻū aḻa + kuṭam ḻū aḻak + kuṭam = aḻakkuṭam; aḻaṉ + koṭi ḻū aḻa + koṭi ḻū aḻa + k + koṭi = aḻakkoṭi - eṉa muṭiyum. aḻaṉ - piṇam, pēy.	(to. e. 193, 354 nac. uri)
‘aḷapu iṟantu uyirttal’ -
uyirkkuṟilum uyirmeykkuṟilum oru māttiri aḷaviṉa. uyirneṭilum uyirmey neṭilum iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṉa. ivi tamakku ōtappaṭṭa ōciyaḷaviṉik kaṭantolittal aḷapiṟantu uyirttalām.	(to. e. 33 nac.)
(icinūliṭattu, uyir 12 māttiri yaḷavum mey 11 māttiri yaḷavum nīṇṭolittalumuṇṭu eṉpa.)	(naṉ. 100 mayili.)
‘aḷapu iṟantu icittalum’: iccūttirattiṉ nōkkum payaṉum -
iyaṟṟamiḻc ceyyuḷuḷ, uyireḻuttukkaḷ nāṉku māttiriyiṉum kaṭantu icittalum, oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aḷapeṭiyik kaṭantu icittalum uḷa. avi ēḻici yoṭum  poruntiya narampiṉiyuṭiya yāḻ nūliṭattaṉa vām eṉpa - eṉṉum iccūttirattāl, pākkaḷi ōci nayampaṭak kūṟutaṟkaṇṇum, koccakak kali- paripāṭal - paṇṇatti - ākiyavaṟṟip paṇṇōṭu oppak kūṟutaṟ kaṇṇum, uyirum oṟṟum māttiriyaḷavu nīṇṭicikku miṭattu, avaṟṟiṟku iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇattuḷ viti yiṉmiyiṉ, avaṟṟi icittamiḻ ilakkaṇa muṟiyāṉ amittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉpatu iccūttirattiṉ nōkkum payaṉum ām.	(to.e.33 ca.pāla.)
aḷapeṭi ilakkaṇam -
aḷapeṭi eṉpatu neṭil nīṇṭolikkumiṭattum, kuṟil neṭilāki nīṇṭolikkumiṭattum annīṭcikkēṟpa olikkappeṟum oliyaḷavi uṇara uṭaṉolikkappeṟum iṉakkuṟilkaḷām. iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṟṟāya neṭil oli mūṉṟu nāṉku māttiriyāka nīṇṭolikka ataṉ iṉakkuṟil muṟiyē oṉṟu iraṇṭu aṭuttiṇittu olikkappaṭum. tolkāppiyaṉārkku iṉamāṉa kuṟilē aḷapeṭi yeḻuttām.
neṭiloṉṟē taṉittu mūṉṟu māttiriyum nāṉku māttiriyumāka olikka, ataṉ iṉakkuṟil oṉṟō iraṇṭō aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭum eṉpatu naṉṉūlār koḷki.
iṉi, mey aḷapeṭukkumiṭattu anta meyyē mīṇṭum olikkappaṭum. varivaṭivil ammeyyē iraṭṭittu eḻutappaṭum.
uyiraḷapeṭiyāṉatu iyaṟkiyaḷapeṭi, ceyaṟki yaḷapeṭi, eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi, icinūlaḷapeṭi, kuṟṟeḻuttaḷapeṭi, neṭṭeḻuttaḷapeṭi, iṉṉici yaḷapeṭi, collici yaḷapeṭi eṉap palavakippaṭum.
e-ṭu : 	iyaṟkiyaḷapeṭi - kurīi; ceyaṟkiyaḷa peṭi - ‘tuppāya tūu maḻi’ (kuṟaḷ 12); eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi	- uvāappatiṉāṉku; kuṟṟeḻuttaḷapeṭi - taḻūu (taḻu); neṭṭeḻut taḷapeṭi - āṭūu; iṉṉiciyaḷapeṭi - ‘keṭuppatūum keṭṭārkkuc......’ eṭuppatūum (kuṟaḷ 15); colliciyaḷapeṭi - eḻūu (eḻuppi)
ivvaḷapeṭi poruḷvēṟupāṭu aṟivippatūum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu : eḻu : taṉviṉi, eḻūu : piṟaviṉi;
veḷa - kippaṟṟu, veḷu - kippaṟṟiyē viṭu.		(to. co. 283 nac. uri)
aḷapeṭi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ toki -
aḷapeṭikaḷit taṉiyeḻuttākat tolkāppiyaṉār eṇṇavilli. neṭil ēḻum aḷapeṭuttaliṉ, iḷam pūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum aḷapeṭiyeḻuttu ēḻ eṉṟaṉar. i.vi. nūlārum aḷapeṭi ēḻ eṉṟē kūṟiṉār. taṉi - mutal - iṭi - kaṭi - eṉa aḷapeṭiyi nāl vakip paṭuttik kūṟuvārumuḷar.
naṉṉūlār taṉiniliyi mutalniliyil aṭakki, ēḻ neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷum moḻi mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉṟa mūviṭattum vara, aḷapeṭi 21 eṉṟār. neṭil ēḻaṉuḷ oḷakāram moḻimutalilēyē aḷapeṭiyāka varutal kūṭum; ēṉiya āṟum moḻimūviṭattum varum. ātaliṉ aḷapeṭi eṇṇikki 19 āka, avaṟṟuṭaṉ iṉṉici yaḷapeṭi colliciyaḷapeṭi eṉṟa ivaṟṟik kūṭṭi aḷapeṭi 21 eṉpar caṅkaranamaccivāyar. iṉṉici col liciyaḷapeṭikaḷ muṟkūṟiya aḷapeṭiyuḷ aṭaṅkum ātaliṉ, 21 eṉṟu ivaṟṟik kūṭṭik koḷḷutal cālātu. aḷapeṭiyiṉ kaṇakkil kurīi mutaliya iyaṟki yaḷapeṭiyiyum koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
aḷapeṭi eṉpaṉa neṭiliyaṭuttu ōci niṟikka varum iṉak kuṟṟuyirākiya aintē ātaliṉ, avaṟṟic cārpeḻut tākak koṇṭu taṉiyē kaṇakkiṭutal tolkāppiya ṉārkkuk karuttaṉṟu.
aḷapeṭi piḷavupaṭā ōciyāyiṉ, ‘kaṭāak kaḷiṟṟiṉ mēl’, ‘paṭāa mulimēl’ (kuṟaḷ 1087) eṉpaṉavaṟṟil ka, ṭāa; pa, ṭāa - eṉṟu alaku pirittal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu pirittāl taḷi citiyum. ātaliṉ kaṭā, a; paṭā, a - eṉṟē pirittal vēṇṭum. ōciyi neṭilākavum kuṟilākavum pakuttu acikoḷḷavēṇṭu meṉil, oru neṭiliyē irukuṟilākap pakuttuk kaṭā eṉpataṉik kaṭa, a eṉṟum aci koḷḷalām; avvāṟu yārum koḷvatilli. ātaliṉ aḷapeṭi eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttut taṉakkotta neṭili aṭutta taṉikkuṟilāy acinili peṟutal, cīrum taḷiyum citiyiṉ acinili peṟāmi - ākiya irunili yum peṟutal uṇarappaṭum. 	(e. ā. pak. 44, 45)
(‘kāṭṭil vaḷantaḻittal kāṇūuk kaḷittuttaṉ’ eṉṉum veṇpā aṭikkaṇ, aḷapeṭiyākiya ukarak kuṟṟuyir alaku peṟātu niṟpa, kāṇū eṉṟa col pōla varuñcīrōṭu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷiyāṉ icintavāṟu.)
aḷapeṭiyil iṉakkuṟil kuṟi ātal -
olivēṟṟumiyāl eḻuttup palavāyiṉa. aṅṅaṉamāka neṭilatu vikāramāy ōroliyāyp piṟakkum aḷa peṭiyi iraṇṭeḻuttuk kūṭi mūṉṟu māttiri āyiṟṟu eṉak koḷḷiṉ, iraṇṭu eḻuttoli aṅṅaṉam oṉṟilli. ataṟku aḷapeṭi eṉṉum peyar ēlā toḻiyum. ātaliṉ avvāṟu iraṇṭeḻuttoliyākak koḷḷātu aṟikuṟiyē eṉṟu kōṭaṟkuk ‘kuṟiyē’ eṉṟār. tolkāppiyaṉār, ‘kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum neṭṭeḻuttu’ eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ piṉ aṟikuṟiyāka varum otta kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟum kūṟiṉār (eṉavē, neṭilatu vikāramāy ōroliyāyp piṟakkum mūṉṟu māttiri aḷaviṟṟām eḻuttē uyiraḷapeṭiyām eṉpatu.)	(naṉ. 91 caṅkara.)
aḷapeṭiyil kuṟṟuyir īṟāy niṉṟu uṭampaṭumey peṟāmi -
neṭil aḷapeṭukkumiṭattu iṉamotta kuṟṟuyir varivaṭivil aṟikuṟiyāka varum. akkuṟṟuyir neṭiloṭu toṭarntu uṭampaṭumeyyuṭaṉ īṟām taṉmiyataṉṟit taṉittu niṟṟaliṉ, ‘kuṟṟuyir aḷapiṉ īṟām’ eṉṟār. (āa eṉṟa aḷapeṭi iṭiyē vakaruṭampaṭumey peṟṟu ā+v+a = āva eṉṟum, īi eṉṟa aḷapeṭi iṭiyē yakaruṭampaṭumey peṟṟu ī + y + i = īyi eṉṟum olikkap paṭuvatō varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭuvatō illi.) aṟikuṟi māttiramāy niṟkum ivveḻuttik ‘kuṟṟuyir’ eṉṟārē yaṉṟi, ataṉit taṉiyē ōreḻuttu eṉṟārallar. 	(naṉ. 108 caṅkara.)
aḷapeṭiyiṉ ōci -
aḷapeṭiyāvatu neṭiloṭu kūṭi varum kuṟṟeḻuttākum. neṭilōciyoṭu kuṟilōci piḷavupaṭātu iṇintu varuvatām. kōṭṭu nūṟum mañcaḷum kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cevvaṇṇam pōla, neṭilum kuṟilum kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṉṉar ap piḷavupaṭā ōciyi aḷapeṭi eṉṟu āciriyar vēṇṭiṉār. ivi kūṭṭic colliya kālat tallatu pulappaṭā, eḷḷāṭṭiya vaḻiyallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭātavāṟu pōla eṉpatu.	(to. e. 6 nac. uri)
uyiraḷapeṭiyil oru neṭilum oru kuṟilum taṉittaṉiyē niṟkum ātaliṉ, ‘kaṭāa’ eṉpataṉik kaṭā, a eṉṟu pirittu aci amikkiṟōm. ātaliṉ aḷapeṭiyil neṭilum kuṟilum, nīrum nīrum cērntāṟ pōlavum kōṭṭu nūṟum mañcaḷum kūṭiṉāṟ pōlavum iṇiyātu, viralum viralum cērntāṟ pōla iṇintuḷḷavi eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum.	(e. ā. pak. 46).

aḷapeṭiyiṉ vaki -
iyaṟkiyaḷapeṭi, ceyaṟkiyaḷapeṭi, iṉṉici yaḷapeṭi, colliciyaḷapeṭi, neṭilaḷapeṭi, kuṟilaḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi, eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi eṉa aḷapeṭi eṭṭu vakip paṭum.
e-ṭu	: aḻittal, vilikūṟal, pulampal - ivaṟṟil eḻuttuc ceyaṟkiyiṉṟip piṟantatu iyaṟki yaḷapeṭi. (cēey, nūṟōoo nūṟu, ammā- vōo)
ceyyuḷil cīrtaḷi keṭṭaviṭattup pulavaṉ koḷḷutal ceyaṟki yaḷapeṭi (‘naṟṟāḷ toḻāa reṉiṉ’ kuṟaḷ 2)
‘keṭuppatūum keṭṭārkkuc .... (kuṟaḷ. 15) : kuṟṟukaram aḷapeṭutta iṉṉiciyaḷapeṭi.
‘taḷii’ aikārakkuṟukkam aḷapeṭutta collici yaḷapeṭi; āa : neṭilaḷapeṭi; maṇīi : kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttāki aḷapeṭutta kuṟilaḷapeṭi; ciṉanntu : oṟṟaḷapeṭi; cantiraṉi arāap paṟṟiṟṟu : vikārat teḻunta eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi. 	(mu. vī.eḻut. 32)
aḷapeṭiyiṉ vakikaḷ eṭṭu -
uyiraḷapeṭi eṭṭu iyaṟcīrkaḷiṉ pāṟpaṭṭu eṇvaki yāka amiyum. eṭṭu iyaṟcīrkaḷāvaṉa nērnēr - nirinēr - nērniri - niriniri - nērnērpu - nērniripu - nirinērpu - niriniripu eṉpaṉa.
e-ṭu :	ā, a - nēr nēr; ā, aṅku - nērnērpu; kaṭā, a - niri nēr; ā, avatu - nēr niripu;	ā, aḻi - nēr niri; pukā, arttu - nirinērpu; paṭā, aki - niri niri; virā, ayatu - niriniripu
ivvāṟu eṇvakiyāka uyiraḷapeṭikaḷic cīrniliyi yoṭṭik koḷvar.		(to. e. 41 nac. uri; to. po. 329 pērā. uri)
aḷapeṭiyiṉ vakikaḷ nāṉku -
uyiraḷapeṭi, taṉinili - mutaṉili - iṭinili - iṟuti nili - eṉa nālvakittu.
e-ṭu	: ‘āa aḷiya alavaṉ’ - taṉinili; āaḻi -	mutalnili; paṭāaki - iṭinili; ‘nalla paṭāa paṟi’ - iṟutinili (kuṟaḷ 1115)
collamippiṉi oṭṭi uyiraḷapeṭi nāṉku vakika ḷākap pakukkappaṭṭavāṟu. (taṉiniliyi mutaṉiliyuḷ aṭakki uyiraḷapeṭi mūvakittu eṉṟalumuṇṭu). 	(to. po. 329 pērā. uri)
aḷapeṭiyik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ -
aḷapu, pulutam, aḷapeṭi eṉpaṉa oruporuṭkiḷavi kaḷām.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 33)
aḷapeṭi vēṟeḻuttu ākāmi -
tolkāppiyaṉār aḷapeṭiyi mutaleḻuttuḷ aṭakkik koṇṭār. naṉṉūlār mutalāyiṉār aḷa peṭiyic cārpeḻuttākak koṇṭaṉar. taṉakkeṉap piṟappiṭa miṉṟit tāṉ cārnta eḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭamē taṉakkup piṟappiṭamākak koṇṭolikkum eḻuttē cārpeḻut tām. annili kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟaṟkē uriyatu ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiyē tolkāppiyaṉār cārpeḻuttākak koṇṭār; aḷa peṭi uyireḻuttuḷ aṭaṅkaliṉ ataṉit taṉiyeḻut tākak koṇṭilar.
aḷapeṭi, neṭṭeḻuttōṭu iṉakkuṟṟeḻuttu niṉṟu nīṇṭicippa toṉṟu eṉiṉum, moḻikkāraṇamāy vēṟuporuḷ tārātu iciniṟittal māttirip payattatāy niṟṟaliṉ, vēṟeḻuttu eṉavittu eṇṇap paṭātatāyiṟṟu. ippeṟṟi aṟiyātār neṭilum kuṟilum viralum viralum cēra niṉṟāṟpōla iṇintu aḷapeṭuk kum eṉavum, aḷapeṭiyeḻuttu uyireḻuttuḷ aṭaṅ kātu eṉavum, cārpeḻuttu eṉa vēṟu vittu eṇṇap paṭum eṉavum, tamakku vēṇṭiyavāṟu kūṟupa. neṭilum kuṟilum viralum viralum cēraniṉṟāṟpōla aḷapeḻum eṉṟal poruntāmikku eḻutteṭi eṉṉātu aḷa peṭi eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭē cāṉṟākum. aḷapeṭi yāppi ṉuḷ ōci paṟṟiyē iraṇṭu māttiriyum oru māttiriyu mākap pirikkappaṭum.	(cū. vi. pak. 24)
aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimutalākum oṉpateḻuttu -
ka ca ta pa na ma va a u eṉpaṉa, aḷavuppeyar niṟippeyar eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ moḻi mutalākiya oṉpatu eḻuttukkaḷām. avi kalam - cāṭi - tūti - pāṉi - nāḻi - maṇṭi - vaṭṭi - akal - uḻakku - eṉṟa aḷavuppeyarkkaṇṇum, kaḻañcu - cīrakam - toṭi - palam - niṟi - mā - vari - anti - mutaliya niṟip peyarkkaṇṇum kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. ippeyarkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa (tolkāppiyaṉār kalam (e. 168), paṉi (169), uḻakku (457), nāḻi, uri (240) patakku, tūṇi (239) eṉṟa aḷavup peyarkaḷiyum kā eṉṟa niṟippeyariyum (169) nūṟpākkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār).		(to. e. 170 nac. uri)
immi - ōraṭi - iṭā - eṉṟa peyarkaḷum, oru ñār - oru tuvali - eṉṟa peyarkaḷum uriyāciriyarkaḷāl koḷḷap paṭṭaṉa.	(to. e. 170 nac.)
immi - ōraṭi - ōrāṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟi iḷampūraṇar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.	(to. e. 171)
aḷavuppeyarum, niṟippeyarum, poruṭpeyarum, oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟāka uḷḷa eṇkaḷoṭu puṇartal:
	orukalam orukaḻañcu orukaṭal	-	oṉṟu ‘oru’ eṉṟākum.
	irukalam irukaḻañcu irucuṭar	-	iraṇṭu ‘iru’ eṉṟā kum.    (to. e. 446 nac.)
	mukkalam mukkaḻañcu muccuṭar	-	mūṉṟu ‘mu’ eṉa niṉṟu vanta valleḻuttu mikum.	(447)
	nāṟkalam nāṟkaḻañcu nāṟporuḷ - nāṉku īṟukeṭṭu ṉakaram ṟakaram ākum. 	(442)
	aiṅkalam aiṅkaḻañcu aintī	-	aintaṉ nakaram varum val leḻuttiṉatu iṉa mel leḻuttu ākum.	(448)
	aṟukalam aṟukaḻañcu aṟucuvi -	āṟu ‘aṟu’ eṉak kuṟukip puṇarum.	(440)
	eḻukalam eḻukaḻañcu eḻuporuḷ - ēḻ ‘eḻu’ eṉat tirintu puṇarum	(389)
	eṇkalam eṇkaḻañcu eṇporuḷ -	eṭṭu īṟukeṭṭu ṭaka ram ṇakaramākat tirintu puṇarum.	(444)
varumoḻi mutalil na ma va eṉpaṉavum uyirum variṉ, eṭṭu ‘eṇ’ eṉṟāka, varumoḻi nakaram ṇakaramāka eṇṇāḻi eṉavum, eṇ maṇṭi - eṇ vaṭṭi - eṉa vēṟu tiripu iṉṟiyum, eṇ + akal = eṇṇakal eṉṟāṟ pōla nili moḻiyil taṉikkuṟili yaṭutta ṇakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittum puṇarum. 	(450)
oṉpatu, oṉpatiṉ akal - oṉpatiṉ cāṭi - eṉavum, oṉpatiṟṟuk kalam - oṉpatiṟṟakal - eṉavum, ‘iṉ’ peṟṟum ‘iṉ’ iṟṟākat tirintum puṇarum. 	(459)
aintum mūṉṟum, varumoḻiyil na ma variṉ, vanta oṟṟu mikum.
varumāṟu :	ainnāḻi, aimmaṇṭi; munnāḻi, mummaṇṭi	(451)
mūṉṟu, varumoḻiyil vakaram varuvaḻi īṟukeṭṭu (neṭu mutal kuṟuki) ṉakaram vakaramākip puṇarum. varumāṟu: muvvaṭṭi  	(452)
nāṉku, vakaramutaṉmoḻi varuvaḻi, īṟukeṭṭu ṉakaram lakaramākip puṇarum. varumāṟu : nāl vaṭṭi  	(453)
aintu, vakaramutaṉmoḻi varuvaḻi īṟukeṭṭu iṭi nakaram keṭṭum vakaroṟṟu mikkum puṇarum. varumāṟu : aivaṭṭi, aivvaṭṭi  	(454)
oṉṟu iraṇṭu eṉpaṉa nilimoḻikaḷāka, varumoḻi mutalil uyirvariṉ, ‘oru’ ōrākavum, ‘iru’ īrākavum tirintu, 
ōrakal - īrakal - ōruḻakku - īruḻakku eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.  	(455)
mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintum nilimoḻikaḷāka, varumoḻi mutalil vakaram variṉ, muvvakal - muvvuḻakku, nālakal - nāluḻakku, aiyakal - aiyuḻakku - eṉa varum. 	(456)
āṟu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka, varumoḻi mutalil uyir variṉ, āṟakal - āṟuḻakku - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarum.	(458)
mūṉṟu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka, varumoḻi mutalil uyir variṉ, mūṉṟu + uḻakku = mūvuḻakku, mūṉṟu + akal = mūvakal eṉṟāṟ pōla, nilimoḻiyil mutaleḻuttu nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya keṭṭu uṭampaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarum. 	(457)
aḷaviyiṉ eḻuvakikaḷ -
1.	 niṟuttu aḷattal - kaḻañcu mutaliya niṟip paṭikkaṟkaḷit tarāciṉ oru taṭṭiliṭṭu, niṟuttaṟiya vēṇṭiya poruḷi maṟutaṭṭiliṭṭu niṟuttaḷattal; 2. peytu aḷattal - eṇṇey mutaliyavaṟṟi aḷavukalaṉkaḷil ūṟṟi aḷattal;  3. teṟittu aḷattal - oṉṟaṉi olittu oli yuṇṭākki av voliyic cevikaruviyāka aḷantu kōṭal; 4. tēṅkamukantu aḷattal - nel mutaliyavaṟṟi aḷavukalaṉkaḷiṉ mēl kuviyumāṟu kuviyumaḷavum peytaḷat tal; 5. nīṭṭi aḷattal - cāṇ, muḻam mutaliya nīṭṭal aḷavikaḷāl tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟi aḷattal; 6. eṇṇi aḷattal - oṉṟu iraṇṭu mutalāka eṇṇiyaḷattal; 7. cārtti aḷattal - oṉṟaṉ aḷavoṭu maṟṟoṉṟaṉ aḷavi oppiṭṭu aḷattal; kaṇṇimippoḻutu, kinnoṭippoḻutu ākiya aḷavikaḷōṭu eḻuttiṉ aḷavi oppiṭṭaḷattal.	(to. e. 7 nac. uri)
aṟāyiram : puṇarcci muṭipu -
āṟu + āyiram ḻū aṟu + āyiram ḻū aṟ + āyiram = aṟāyiram.
āṟu eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟu eṉṟāka, īṟṟu muṟṟukaram keṭavē, atu varumoḻi āyirattoṭu puṇarntu aṟāyiram eṉṟāyiṟṟu.	(e. ā. pak. 175).
aṉ cāriyi varumiṭaṅkaḷ -
a) urupu puṇarcci: atu itu utu eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ aṉcāriyi iṭiyē vara urupoṭu puṇarntu, nili moḻi ukaram keṭa, atu + aṉ + ai ḻū at + aṉ + ai = ataṉi eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum. (to. e. 176 nac.) ēḻ eṉpatu urupōṭu aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum. ēḻaṉi eṉa varum. (194) kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupoṭu puṇarum. oṉṟaṉi, iraṇṭaṉi eṉa varum. (198) yātu eṉṟa viṉāppeyarum, aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉṟa cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum. aḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ āytam keṭum. yātaṉi, ataṉi, itaṉi, utaṉi eṉa varum.	(200)
ā) poruṭpuṇarcci: atu itu utu eṉpaṉa poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ ataṉkōṭu - itaṉkōṭu - utaṉkōṭu - eṉṟāṟ pōla aṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum. (263); ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar, ēḻaṉcukku - ēḻaṉkāyam - eṉṟāṟ pōla aṉcāriyi peṟum. (388); kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇup peyar, oṉṟaṉ kāyam - oṉṟaṉ cukku - eṉṟāṟ pōla aṉ cāriyi peṟum. (419); peṇṭu eṉṉum collum peṇ ṭaṉki eṉṟāṟ pōl aṉcāriyi peṟum. (421); yātu aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉpaṉa urupu puṇarcci pōlap poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum yātaṉkōṭu - ataṉkōṭu - itaṉkōṭu - utaṉkōṭu -  eṉṟāṟ pōla aṉcāriyi peṟum.	(422)
aṉṟi, iṉṟi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu -
iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉiyeccam iṉṟu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukara īṟākat tirintavaḻi, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyal pākap puṇarum.
‘vaḻuviṉṟu niliiya iyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum’	(po. 111. nac.)
‘poṟiyiṉṟu perukiya paruvaraṟ kaṇṇum’ 	(po. 151. nac.)
‘kāyviṉṟu avaṉvayiṉ poruttaṟ kaṇṇum	(po. 151. nac.)
‘tāiṉṟu uriya tattam kūṟṟē’	(po. 241. nac.)
eṉat tolkāppiyattil ‘iṉṟi’ eṉpatu ceyyuḷiṉpam karuti ‘iṉṟu’ eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
‘atuvaṉṟi’ eṉpatu atāaṉṟu eṉat tirintu vantuḷḷatu.
iṉṟi eṉṟa colliluḷḷa iraṇṭu ikaramum kātukku iṉimiyāka irāmiyāṉ, oṉṟu ukaramāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. aṉṟi eṉpatiluḷḷa ikaram ukaramākat tiriya vēṇṭiyatiṉṟu. atu piṟkālat tiripu pōlum.
iṉṟi eṉṟa viṉiyeccam ‘viruntiṉṟik kaḻinta pakal’, eṉṟāṟ pōla, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. aḵtu ‘iṉṟu’ eṉa viṉimuṟṟup pōla īṟutirinta vaṭivam koṇṭavaḻi, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam mikātu iṉṉōci payattaliṉ, ceyyuḷil payila vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. annili ‘aṉṟi’ eṉpataṟkum piṉṉar ēṟpaṭṭatu.
	varumāṟu :	nāḷaṉṟip pōki - ‘nāḷaṉṟu pōki’ 	(puṟanā. 124)
		uppiṉṟip puṟki uṇka - ‘uppiṉṟu puṟki uṇka’ 	(to. e. 237 nac.)
aṉṟi, iṉṟi eṉṟa viṉiyeccam -
aṉṟi, iṉṟi eṉṟa kuṟippu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉ potuvitiyāl valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. ikara īṟu ukaramākat tirintu aṉṟu - iṉṟu - eṉṟākumiṭattu iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	‘nāḷaṉṟu pōki’	(puṟanā. 124),
	‘uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇka’	(naṉ. 173)
aṉuvatittal -
muṉṉark kūṟiyataṉip piṉṉumoru payaṉkaruti vaḻi moḻital. ‘ṇaṉa muṉṉum vaḵkāṉ miciyum mak kuṟukum’ (naṉ. 96) eṉṟu muṉṉark kūṟiyataṉi,
‘laḷamey tirinta ṉaṇamuṉ makāram
nintīr oṟṟām ceyyu ḷuḷḷē’
eṉa aṉuvatittār.
ivvanuvātattiṉ payaṉ : makaram kuṟukutaṟkut toṭarum ṉakaramum ṇakaramum muṟiyē lakaramum ḷakaramum tirintaṉa eṉpatum, im makarakkuṟukkam ceyyuṭkaṇṇatu eṉpatum uṇarttutal.	(naṉ. 120. caṅkara.)
‘anuvātam’ kāṇka.


ā
ā -
aḵṟiṇiyil peṇpāli uṇarttum peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itu peṟṟam, mari, erumi ivaṟṟiṉ peṇpāli uṇarttum.
ā eṉṟa ivvōreḻuttorumoḻi, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷoṭum puṇarumvaḻi ṉakaravoṟṟic cāriyiyākap peṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	āṉkaṇam, āṉney(āṉey), āṉvarici, āṉiṉam; ciṟupāṉmi, meṉkaṇam varuvaḻi ṉakarac cāriyiyōṭu akarac cāriyiyum peṟṟup puṇarum. e-ṭu : āṉaney, āṉamaṇi		(to. e. 231, 232 nac.)
ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcci -
eḻuvāyt toṭaril ākāra īṟṟup peyari aṭuttu vaṉkaṇam variṉ, avvanta valloṟṟu iṭiyē mikku muṭiyum. āyiṉ, ā - mā - yā - eṉṟa ōreḻuttu moḻikaḷ mikā.
e-ṭu :	mūṅkāk kaṭitu, tārāk kaṭitu (ā kuṟitu, mā kuṟitu, yā kuṟiya)
ceyyā eṉṉum uṭaṉpāṭṭu viṉiyeccamum, ceyyā eṉṉum etirmaṟip peyareccamum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ, vanta valloṟṟu mikku muṭiyum. āyiṉ etirmaṟi viṉimuṟ ṟum, viṉippeyarum, taṉmiviṉimuṟṟuviṉāvum mikā.
e-ṭu :	uṇṇāk koṇṭāṉ, uṇṇāc cōṟu (uṇṇā kutirikaḷ, uṇkā koṟṟā; uṇkā - uṇpēṉō)
taṉikkuṟili aṭutta ākāra īṟṟuppeyar nilimoḻiyāka niṟka, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam mutalākiya peyar vantu ummit tokiyākap puṇariṉ, akaram eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyāka vara, vanta valleḻuttu mikum.
e-ṭu :	uvāap patiṉāṉku, irāap pakal
ākārīṟṟup peyar nilimoḻiyāka varum irupeya roṭṭup paṇputtoki, eḻuvāy muṭipu, peyarecca maṟi eṉpaṉavum eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyoṭu val leḻuttu mikap peṟum.
e-ṭu :	arāappāmpu, irāak koṭitu, irāak kākki (illāta kākki eṉṉum poruṭṭu) eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.
iyalpukaṇattum akarappēṟu iṟāa vaḻutuṇaṅkāy - eṉṟāṟ pōla varum.
ākāra īṟṟu viḷittoṭarum, iṭiccoṟṟoṭarum mikā.
e-ṭu :	ūrā kēḷ; kēṇmiyā koṟṟā. 	(to. e. 221-224 nac.)
ākāra īṟṟuḷ iyalpākap puṇarvaṉa -
1. ā eṉṉum peyar - ā kuṟitu, ā kuṟiya; 2. mā eṉṉum peyar - mā kuṟitu, mā kuṟiya; 3. īṟṟayal nīṇṭu ākāramāki īṟu keṭṭa viḷippeyar - ūrā kēḷ; 4. yā eṉṉum viṉāppeyar - yā kuṟiya; 5. palaviṉpāl etirmaṟi viṉi muṟṟum viṉippeyarum - uṇṇā kutirikaḷ; 6. miyā  eṉṉum ākāra īṟṟu iṭiccol - kēṇmiyā koṟṟā; 7. taṉtoḻilic collum ākāra īṟṟu viṉimuṟṟuc col - uṇkā koṟṟā (uṇkā - uṇpēṉō)
ivvēḻum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum ākāra īṟṟuc coṟkaḷām.	(to. e. 224. nac.)
palavaṟṟiṟutiyil viṉimuṟṟoṭu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariyum koḷḷal vēṇṭum (5 kāṇka).	(e.ku. pak. 213.)


ākāra īṟṟuḷ ‘kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉikeṭa ukaram’ peṟṟu varuvaṉa -
kuṟṟeḻutti aṭutta ākārīṟṟup peyarākiya īreḻuttu moḻikaḷ iṟuti ākāram akaramākak kuṟukip piṉ ukaram peṟutal perumpāṉmiyum ceyyuṭkē urittu; ciṟu pāṉmi ulakanaṭiyilum varum.
e-ṭu :	iṟā - ‘iṟavup puṟattaṉṉa’	(naṟ. 19)
	cuṟā - ‘cuṟavuk kōṭṭaṉṉa’	(naṟ. 19)
	puṟā - ‘puṟavup puṟattaṉṉa’	(kuṟun. 274)
‘puṟavu niliyaṉṉa’ eṉa iyalpukaṇam variṉum it tiripu nili uṇṭām. ciṟupāṉmi ākāram kuṟuki ukaram peṟāmal muṭitalum koḷka. aravuyar koṭi, muḻavuṟaḻ tōḷ, cuṟavuyar koṭi eṉa varum. (īṇṭu vakaram uṭampaṭu meyyāka vantatu).	(to. e. 234 nac.)
ākāra īṟu peṟum eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi -
kuṟṟeḻutti aṭuttum taṉittum varum ākāra īṟṟup peyarkaḷ alvaḻippuṇarcciyil ummittokikkaṇ ṇum, ciṟupāṉmi paṇputtoki - eḻuvāyt toṭar - peyarecca maṟi - ivaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, nilimoḻi varu moḻikaḷukkiṭiyē ākārīṟṟi aṭuttu akaramākiya eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi varum.
e-ṭu :	uvāappatiṉāṉku ummittoki; kāakkuṟi (kāvum kuṟiyum - kā : oru niṟippeyar); arāap pāmpu - paṇputtoki; irāak koṭitu - eḻuvāyt toṭar; irāak kākki - peyarecca maṟit toṭar (irāta kākki); iṟāa vaḻu tuṇaṅkāy - ummit toki (iyalpukaṇam)
innili vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇṇum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu	: palāakkōṭu, kāakkuṟi - āṟām vēṟṟu mittoki; palāavili, (palā+ili) palāanār - āṟām vēṟṟumit toki. (iyalpukaṇam ivi)
irāppoḻuti uṇarttum irā eṉṟa col vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ iv akaram peṟātu. irākkākki, irāk kūttu eṉavarum. irāviṭattuk kākki, irāviṭattuk kūttu eṉṟu ivi poruḷpaṭum.
ākāra īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ cāriyi peṟumiṭattum iṭiyē ivveḻuttup pēṟaḷapeṭi peṟutalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	aṇṇāa + attu + ēri = aṇṇāattēri; tiṭṭāa + attu + kuḷam = tiṭṭāattukkuḷam; uvāa + attu + ñāṉṟu + koṇṭāṉ = uvā- attuñāṉṟukoṇṭāṉ; iṭāa + iṉ + uḷ + koṇṭāṉ = iṭāaviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ
nilā eṉṟu col akara eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟātu attuccāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu. nilā + attu + koṇ ṭāṉ = nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ. (nilāvattu eṉa vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟutalumām)
cāriyi peṟātu vēṟṟumippuṇarccikkaṇ nilāakkatir, nilāamuṟṟam - eṉa eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟṟu muṭitalu muṇṭu.	(to. e. 223, 226 - 228 nac. uri)
ākupeyarp patam -
ākupeyarppatamum kāraṇattiṉāṉ āmē eṉiṉum, vikutiyiṉṟip pakutiyāṉa pakāppatam tāṉē piṟitumoru poruḷi viḷakkum. avi teṅku, kaṭu, puḷi, kuḻippāṭi, cīṉam, ēṟu, kuttu, nāḻi - eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa.	(naṉ. 131 mayili.)
ācāṉ uvakkum tiṟattukku uvami -
–‘ettiṟattu ācāṉ uvakkum attiṟam’ purintati, maṟiyōṉ vēṇṭa māṇṭārip paṇṭu aḻittut tanta kaṇṇaṉiṭattum, turōṇācāriyaṉ vēṇṭa valakkip peruviral aṟuttut tanta vaṉacaraṉiṭattum kāṇka.	(naṉ. 46 irāmā.)
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār orumoḻiilakkaṇam kūṟāmi -
tolkāppiyaṉār virivañci orumoḻiyilakkaṇam kūṟā rāyiṉār. pāṇiṉiyār eṭṭu attiyāyattuḷ vikutimātti rikkē mūṉṟu attiyāyam kūṟi, vikutippuṇarccikkaṇ paṭum ceyki mutaliyaṉavum vēṟu kūṟiṉār. avaṟṟuḷḷum aṭaṅkātu eñci niṉṟa coṟkaḷip piṉṉuḷḷōr ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇamākavē puṇarttuc ceykiceytu muṭittaṉar.
“intiraṉukkup pirakaṟpati ‘ivi vaḻu, ivi vaḻuillaṉa’ eṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷit taṉittaṉi eṭuttu ōtaluṟṟārkkut teyva yāṇṭil āyiram ceṉṟatu; ceṉṟum coṟkaḷ muṭintila” eṉpatu māpāṭiyattuḷ kaṇṭatu. tamiḻmoḻi yum avvāṟu perukik kiṭattaliṉ eṭuttōtap pukiṉ muṭivu peṟātu ātaliṉ, cilavaṟṟi eṭuttōttāṉum cilavaṟṟi ilēcāṉum, cilavaṟṟip puṟaṉaṭiyāṉum cilavaṟṟi uttivakiyāṉum uṇarntu koḷḷumāṟu tokuttut tolkāppiyaṉār nūl ceytār.
naṉṉūlār pataviyalil orumoḻiyilakkaṇam kūṟiṉār.
āyiṉ, tolkāppiyaṉarāl puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷip piṉṉuḷḷōr pirittu muṭittal mutalnūloṭu māṟu koḷak kūṟalām eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.	(to. e. 482 uri)
‘taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ karutik kūṟal’ eṉṉum citivukku eṭuttukkāṭṭup patamuṭippu eṉpatōr ilakkaṇam paṭittuk kōṭal eṉpar pērāciriyar.	(to. po. 663 uri)

āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār pattoṉpatu eṉpataṟkup puṇarcciviti kūṟāmi -
patiṉoṉṟu mutal patiṉeṭṭu īṟākiya eṇṇuppeyar kaḷukkup puṇarcci viti kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār pattoṉ patu eṉpataṟku viti kūṟavilli. pattu eṉpataṉ īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉpatu eṉpataṉ mutaleḻuttākiya uyir cāra iṭam koṭukkum iyalpu puṇarcciyātaliṉ ataṉik kūṟiṟṟilar. itaṉāl kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭātu eṉpatum, meyyīṟu pōl uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum eṉpatum peṟṟām.	(e. ā.pak. 171.)
āciriyavacaṉam : piṟa peyarkaḷ  -
āciriyavacaṉam eṉiṉum, mēṟkōḷ eṉiṉum, paḻañcūttirat tiṉ kōḷ eṉiṉum okkum.	(naṉ. 9 caṅkara.)
āṭciyum kāraṇamum paṟṟiya kuṟi -
kuṟi - aṭiyāḷam, iṭappaṭum peyar. āṭci ac collip piṉṉar eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttutal; kāraṇam - apporuḷukku appeyar ēṟpaṭṭataṉ kāraṇamām. ākavē, piṉṉarc curukkamāṉa peyarāl eṭuttuk kuṟippataṟkum, peyariṭuvataṟkuriya kāraṇatti aṟivippataṟkum muṉṉarp peyariṭuvatu āṭciyum kāraṇamum paṟṟiya kuṟiyām.
e-ṭu :	‘oḷakāra iṟuvāyp - paṉṉīreḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa’ 	(to. e. 8 nac.)
akaram mutal oḷakāram iṟutiyāka uḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷukkum uyir eṉṟa potuppeyar, piṉṉarp puṇarcci paṟṟi ivaṟṟi uyir eṉṟa peyarāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟuvataṟku eḷimiyāka amital āṭci paṟṟiya kuṟiyām. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷiyum, akaram mutal oḷakāram īṟāka uḷḷa paṉṉīreḻuttukkaḷum, uṭampiṉi uyir iyakkutal pōla iyakkutaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟku uyir eṉṟa potuppeyar vaḻaṅkutal kāraṇam paṟṟiya kuṟiyām.	(to. e. 8 nac. uri)
āṭū makaṭū eṉṟa coṟkaḷ -
āṭū makaṭū eṉpaṉa muṟiyē uyartiṇi āṇpāl - peṇpāl - orumippeyarkaḷām. ivi ūkāra īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ. ivi varumoḻiyōṭu iṇiyuṅkāl iṭiyē eḻuttup pēṟākiya ukaram peṟutalumuṇṭu eṉpar uri yāciriyaṉmār.
e-ṭu :	āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ, makaṭūuk kuṟiyaḷ -alvaḻip puṇarcci; āṭūukki, makaṭūukki - vēṟṟumip puṇarcci	(to. e. 267 nac).
ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟutalu muṇṭu eṉpar tolkāppiyaṉār. āṭūuviṉki, makaṭūuviṉ ki eṉa varum.	(to.e. 271 nac.)
potuvākat tolkāppiyaṉār uyartiṇip peyar kaḷukku iṉcāriyi vitittārallar. āṭū, makaṭū eṉṟa peyarkaḷukku iṉcāriyi amiyātāyiṉum, avi collāl aḵṟiṇi pōṟaliṉ iṉcāriyi varutal kuṟṟamilli eṉappaṭṭatu.	(e. ā. pak. 144).
āṇ eṉṟa potupeyarp puṇarcci -
āṇ eṉṟa potuppeyar, avvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyilum, vaṉkaṇam varumoḻi mutalil variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu : āṇ kaṭitu; āṇki (to. e. 303 nac.)
āṇpāṟpeyarppakupata muṭivu -
aracuttoḻiliyuṭiyāṉ aracaṉ, vāṇikam uṭiyāṉ vāṇikaṉ, uḻaviyuṭiyāṉ uḻavaṉ, vēḷāṇmi ceyvāṉ vēḷāḷaṉ, kaṇakkāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ kaṇakkaṉ, kuntattoḻilāl uṇpāṉ kuntavaṉ, valiyāl muyaṉ ṟuṇpāṉ valiyaṉ, uvaccattoḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ uvaccaṉ, taccut toḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ taccaṉ, vaṇṇārat toḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ vaṇṇāṉ, kaṇavāḷat toḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ kaṇavāḷaṉ - eṉṟu ivvāṟē āṇpāṟ pakupataṅkaḷellām muṭikkap paṭum.	(naṉ. 144 mayili.)
āṇmarappeyar peṟum cāriyi -
āṇ eṉṟa marappeyar vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ ammuc cāriyi peṟṟu āṇaṅkōṭu - āṇanār - eṉṟāṟpōla muṭiyum.	(to. e. 304 nac.)
āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki -
āṇ peṇ eṉpaṉa viravuppeyarāyiṉum, taṉiyē kūṟiya vaḻip peṇṇum āṇum piḷḷiyum uyartiṇikkē uriyaṉa. vāḷātē, peṇ vantatu eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḻi aḵṟiṇipporuḷ eṉpatu uṇaralākātu. peṇkuraṅku vantatu eṉṟu vitantē kūṟavēṇṭum. peṇ piṟantatu, āṇ piṟantatu, piḷḷi piṟantatu eṉṟu aṭiyaṭātu colliya vaḻi uyartiṇikkēyām. āṇ peṇ eṉpaṉa uyartiṇip peyarkaḷāyiṉum, aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷ pōlap puṇarcci viti peṟum eṉpatu.	(to. po. 624 pērā.)
“āṇ peṇ eṉpaṉa, āṇ vantatu, peṇ vantatu  eṉa iru tiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇi muṭipē peṟutaliṉ, ‘aḵṟiṇi iyalpiṉa’ eṉṟār” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.	(to. e. 303)
āti virutticanti -
oru patattiṉuḷḷē muṉṉiṉṟa ikara īkāra ēkāraṅkaḷ aikāramākavum, ukara ūkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ oḷakāramākavum, akaram ākāramākavum, ēḻāmuyir ār ākavum tirintu varutal āti virutticantiyām.
 varumāṟu :	i - civaṉip paṇivōṉ : civaṉ;  ī - vīrattiṉ taṉmi : viram; ē - kēvalat taṉmi : kival yam; u - puttaṉip paṇivōṉ : puttaṉ; ū - cūraṉ taṉmi : curiyam; ō - kōcalaṉ puttiri: kucalyi; a - tacarataṉ puttiraṉ : tācarati; ru - kruttiki puttiraṉ: kārttikēyaṉ 	(to. vi. 38 uri)
ā, mā, kō  ‘iṉ’ aṭital -
ā,  mā,  kō immūṉṟu peyarkaḷum  iṉcāriyi peṟu talumām.
 varumāṟu :	āvi - āviṉi, māvi - māviṉi, kōvi - kōviṉi.	(mu. vī. puṇa. 16)
ā, mā, kō  puṇarumāṟu -
ā mā kō eṉṟa mūṉṟu peyarkaḷuḷ, ā pacuviṉi yuṇarttum peyar; mā vilaṅkiṉ potuppeyar; kō iṟivaṉi yuṇarttum peyar. ivi urupukaḷ puṇarumiṭattu ṉakaraccāriyi poruntavum peṟum.
varumāṟu :	ā + ai = āvi, āṉi; mā + ai = māvi, māṉi; kō + ai = kōvi, kōṉi.
ivi ṉakaraccāriyiyōṭu ukaraccāriyiyum peṟum.
e-ṭu :	nāṉkaṉurupoṭu puṇarumiṭattu āṉukku - māṉukku - kōṉukku - eṉa varum. āvukku - māvukku - kōvukku - ukaraccāriyi oṉṟē peṟutal. āviṉukku - māviṉukku - kōviṉukku - iṉcāri- yiyum ukaraccāriyiyum peṟutal. āviṉi, māviṉi, kōviṉi eṉa ippeyarkaḷ iṉṉurupu oḻinta ēṉi yurupukaḷoṭu puṇarkiyil iṉ cāriyi peṟutalum koḷka.
kāṭṭuppacuvik kuṟikkum āmā eṉṟa peyarum āmāṉi, āmāviṉi, āmāṉukku, āmāviṉukku eṉa ṉakaraccāriyi, iṉcāriyi, ukarac cāriyi eṉa mūṉṟum peṟumāṟum kāṇka.	(naṉ. 248)


ā, mā, miyā  alvaḻip puṇarcci -
alvaḻikkaṇ ā mā eṉṟa peyarkaḷum, miyā eṉṟa muṉṉili aciccollum vaṉkaṇam varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ variṉum iyalpākavē muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	ā kuṟitu, mā kuṟitu, kēṇmiyā koṟṟā	(naṉ. 171)
āyta iṟuti -
‘cuṭṭumutalākiya āyta iṟuti’ - cuṭṭiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉṟa coṟkaḷ. āytam īṟāka eccollilum vārātu. aḵtu īṟṟayal eḻuttākavē vantuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyaṉār īṟṟayal eḻuttiyum īṟu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum vaḻakkamuṭiyavar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
eṉavē, ‘āyta iṟuti’ eṉpatu āyta īṟṟayal col eṉṉum poruḷatu.	(to. e. 200, 422 nac.)
āyta eḻuttu aḷapeṭuttal -
niṟattiṉkaṇṇum ōciyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi āytam tōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittaliyuṭiya coṟkaḷ āyta vōci mikku naṭakkum. ceyyuḷici niṟikka āytam aḷapeṭuttaluṇṭu. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil āytam iciniṟik kākavē iṭiyē tōṉṟi aḷapeṭuttalumuṇṭu.
varumāṟu :	‘kaḵḵ ṟeṉṉum kallatar attam’ - niṟam paṟṟiyatu.
		‘cuḵḵ ṟeṉṉum taṇtōṭṭup peṇṇi’- ōci paṟṟiyatu.
		‘eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kiyarāy iṉṉuyir, veḵḵku vārkkilli vīṭu’ - ici niṟikka āytam aḷapeṭuttatu.
		‘vilaḵḵku vīṅkiruḷ ōṭṭumē mātar, ilaḵḵku muttiṉ iṉam’ - ici niṟikka āytam tōṉṟi aḷa peṭuttatu.
veṇpāvil tēmā eṉṟa īraciccīri uṇṭākka eḵḵ, veḵḵ eṉa āytam aḷapeṭuttatu. vilaku, ilaku eṉpaṉavē collāyiṉum, avi varuñcīroṭu nērē iṇiyiṉ taḷi piḻikkum ātaliṉ, vilaḵku eṉa variṉum atē puḷimāvāyt taḷi tavaṟutaliṉ, vilaḵḵku eṉṟu tōṉṟiya āytatti aḷapeṭiyākkap puḷimāṅkāyāy varum cīroṭu veṇṭaḷik kēṟpa oṉṟum eṉpatu.	(to. e. 40 nac.)
āytak kuṟukkam -
nilimoḻi taṉikkuṟili aṭutta lakara ḷakara īṟṟup peyarkaḷāka niṟpa, varumoḻi mutalil takaram variṉ, al vaḻippuṇarccikkaṇ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ āytamākat tiriyum. avvāytam taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttiriyāka olikkum āytakkuṟukkamām.
e-ṭu :	kal + tītu = kaḵṟītu; muḷ + tītu = muḵṭītu (naṉ. 97)
āytakkuṟukkam eṉa oṉṟillāmi -
‘kuṟiyataṉmuṉṉar āytap puḷḷi, uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittē’ (e. 38) eṉa orumoḻikkaṇ varum āytam kūṟi, ‘īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum icimi tōṉṟum’ (39) eṉa nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkumiṭattuk kaḵṟītu - muḵṭītu - eṉat taṉ arimāt tiriyē icikkum taṉmi tōṉṟum eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ataṉip puṇarmoḻikkaṇ varum āytamākak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, āytakkuṟukkam eṉa oṉṟu iṉṟu eṉpatu.	(i. vi. eḻut. 5 uri)
āytattiṉ iṭam neṭuṅkaṇakkil āmāṟu -
cārpeḻuttukkaḷuḷ āytam akarākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉ piṟattalāṉum, uyir ēṟātu ōcivikāramāy niṟpatāyiṉum eḻuttiyal taḻā ōcipōlak koḷḷalākātu eḻuttē yākum eṉṟu meyyeḻuttiṉpāṟ paṭuttup ‘puḷḷi’ eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu ātalāṉum, uyirpaṉṉiraṇṭum meypatiṉeṭṭum ākiya ivviraṇ ṭaṟkumiṭiyē vikkappaṭṭatu.	(i. vi. eḻut. 8 uri)
āytattiṉ tōṟṟam -
cārpeḻuttākiya āytam, kuṟṟeḻutti aṭuttu ukara uyi reḻutti yūrnta valliṉap puḷḷi (yākiya ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu - eṉṟa uyirmeyyiṉ) muṉṉar, colliṉiṭiyē vāy tiṟantu urappik kūṟum oliyāl, meyyeḻuttup pōṉṟa iyalpoṭu tōṉṟum. māttiri vakiyāl mey yeḻuttip pōṉṟu olippiṉum, uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭā mal taṉittu niṟkum taṉmittu āytap puḷḷi.	(cuvāmi. eḻut. 18)
āytattiṉ piṟappiṭam -
āyta eḻuttu neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟu olikkum ōci yāṉum aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉum piṟakkum. 		(i. vi. eḻut. 13).
āytattiṉ piṟappu -
āytam cārpeḻuttuḷ oṉṟu ātaliṉ, taṉakkumuṉ niṟkum valleḻuttōṭu ottu atu piṟakkumiṭattil piṟakkum. mutaliluḷḷa kuṟṟeḻuttu āytattukkuc cārpāyiṉum, muṉniṟkum valleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭamē āytattukkum piṟappiṭamāka varum. āytam taliyiṭattil piṟakkumeṉa iḷampūraṇarum naṉṉūlārum piṟappiṭam kūṟuvar. nacci ṉārkkiṉiyarum i.vi. nūlārum āytattukkup piṟappiṭam neñcu eṉpar. āytam taṉakku muṉṉuḷḷa valleḻuttup piṟakkumiṭattē piṟantu, ataṉoliyiyē taṉatu olikkum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. (muṉ - iṭamuṉ)
eḵku, kaḵcu, aḵtu, paḵtu, paḵṟi eṉpaṉa ekku, kaccu, attu, pattu, paṟṟi eṉpaṉa pōla olikkum.
muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu, kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ āytam ṭakaraṟakara oliyi oṭṭiyamivatāl, ṭakara ṟakaraṅkaḷ āytamākat tiriyum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē āytam taṉakku muṉṉuḷḷa eḻutti nōkka aṟuvaki olikaḷi yuṭiyatu eṉalām.	(e. ā. pak. 84, 85).
paṇṭik kālattil, āytam eṉpatu ōriṭattil piṟavātu āṟu iṭaṅkaḷil piṟantatu eṉpatum, akkāraṇam paṟṟi aṟuvakippaṭṭatu eṉpatum veḷippaṭi. avvāṟē kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum, avaṟṟiṉ paṟṟuk kōṭākiya valliṉamey mutaliyaṉa piṟakkumiṭaṅkaḷil piṟantu palavakippaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum veḷippaṭi. ivaṟṟuḷ muṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalikaramum ōriṉam alla eṉpatum  viḷaṅkum. eḵku, kaḵcu, muḵṭītu, iḵtu, aḵpōkam, kaḵṟītu eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil āytattiṉ oli māṟupaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. itu muṭṭītu eṉṟatu muḵṭītu eṉṟēṉum, kaṟṟītu eṉṟatum kaḵṟītu eṉṟēṉum māṟalām eṉṟu kūṟappaṭutalāl viḷaṅkum.
cārpeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam avaṟṟiṟkuc cārpāṉa eḻuttukkaḷukku arukiluḷḷa avaṟṟi olikkat takunta iṭamē yākum.	(e. ku. pak. 103 - 105).
āytattik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ -
aḵkēṉam eṉiṉum, taṉinili eṉiṉum, āytam eṉiṉum okkum.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 28)
āytattoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu, varumoḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉum, alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa īriṭattum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	eḵku kaṭitu, eḵku peritu - alvaḻi (to. e. 425 nac.); eḵku kaṭumi, eḵku perumi - vēṟṟumi (413 nac.)
āytattoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram -
moḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ ayaleḻuttu āytamā yiṉ, akkuṟṟiyalukaram āytattoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiya lukaramām.
e-ṭu : eḵku, kaḵcu
ikkuṟṟiyalukaram alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpākap puṇarum. vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāyum, iṉcāriyi aṭuttu iyalpāyum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	eḵku kaṭitu, kaḵcu tīrntatu - alvaḻi; eḵku kaṭumi, eḵkiṉ kaṭumi - vēṟṟumi (naṉ. 181, 182)
āytap puḷḷi -
āytamāṉa puḷḷi āytappuḷḷi. ‘āytappuḷḷi’ eṉṟār, itaṉiyum oṟṟiṉpāl cārttutaṟku eṉka. oṟṟēl, uyirēṟap peṟal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, cārpeḻuttātaliṉ uyir ēṟappeṟātu eṉka.	(naṉ. 89 mayili.)
āytam iṭiyeḻuntu olittal -
āytam orumoḻikkaṇṇum (eḵku), toṭarmoḻik kaṇṇum (aḵkaṭiya), vititta mutaleḻuttukkaḷ irumaruṅ kum niṉṟeḻuppa, iruciṟaku eḻuppa eḻum uṭalatu pōla, iṭiyeḻuntu olippataṉṟi oruvāṟṟāṉum īṟāy varum taṉmiyatu aṉṟu. ātaliṉ, aḵkaṭiya mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ (vakaram) tirinta āytam, aḵkāṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya āytam pōlat toṭarmoḻikkaṇ iṭiyil niṟṟalākak koḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭumaṉṟi, vitiyīṟāka vanta taṉṟu. ivvāṟu ataṉ uṇmi tuṇintu ataṉi iṟutik kaṇ vilakki oṟṟaḷapeṭi 42 eṉṟu āmāṟu kāṇka.	(naṉ. 92 caṅkara.)
āytam eṭṭu ātal -
valliṉavakiyāṉ iyalpāka vanta āytam āṟu. seḵku (aḵkāṉ), kaḵcu, irupaḵtu, cuḵṟeṉṉum; ēṉiya ṭakara pakaraṅkaḷ āytattit toṭarntu vantaṉavākak kaḵṭu, kaḵpu - eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭuvar]; puṇarcci vikārattāl vanta āytam oṉṟu (av + kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya); ceyyuḷvikārat tāl vanta āytam oṉṟu (ceyvatu eṉaṟpālatu ceyvaḵtu eṉaviriyum); āka arimāttiri yāka olikkum āytam eṭṭām.	(naṉ. 90 caṅkara.)
āytam cārpeḻuttātal -
kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci eṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭup ‘puḷḷi’ eṉṟataṉāṉum oṟṟiṉ pāṟpa ṭumēṉum, uyir ēṟātu ōcivikāramāy iṭampaṟṟi nikaḻvatoṉ ṟākaliṉ, āytam cārpeḻuttu eṉa oṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.	(i. vi. 17 uri)
āytam paṟṟiya karuttu -
āytam cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāy ēṉiyavi pōlap puḷḷi peṟum. itaṉ tamiḻppeyar aḵkēṉam eṉpatu. āytam ācritam eṉpataṉ tiripu. paḻiya ēṭukaḷil āytamāṉatu, : eṉa ivvāṟu iraṇṭu puḷḷikaḷōṭu eḻutappaṭṭatu. oliyil atu taṉmuṉ varum valleḻuttiṉ oliyip peṟukiṟatu. (muṉ - iṭamuṉ) neṭuṅkaṇakkil āytam uyirvariyiṉ iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa visarkkam pōlvatu. vaṭamoḻiyil visarkkam kakara pakaraṅkaḷ varumiṭattu avaṟṟiṉ oli peṟum. “ataṉi ikkālattār naṭuvu vāṅkiyiṭṭu eḻutupa” eṉṟu nac. kūṟuvatāl, avarkālattil āytam iraṇṭu puḷḷi vaṭiviṉatākavē eḻutappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
āytattukkut taṉiyē oliyiṉṟu. aḵtu aṭuttu varum valliṉa meyyiṉ olikku ēṟpattāṉum tirintolikkum. ikkālattār kakarameyyi aṭutta āyta oliyiyē ēṉiya valleḻuttukkaḷi aṭutta āytattukkum oliyākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. taṉ cārpiṉ piṟappoṭu civaṇi āytam olikkum eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟaliṉ, āytam val leḻuttu āṟaṉiyum aṭutta eḻuttākak koṇṭuvarum aṟuvaki iṭaṅkaḷilum avvoliyi oṭṭi aṟuvaki olittākum. aṭutta vallolikkum āyta olikkum mikak kuṟinta olivēṟupāṭē iruttaliṉ, muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu, kaṟṟītu kaḵṟītu eṉṟāyiṉami uḷaṅkoḷat takkatu.	(e. ā. pak. 14, 84, 85)
āytam neṭuṅkaṇakkil oḷakārattukkup piṉṉarum kakara meykku muṉṉarum iṭṭu, mutaleḻuttukkaḷi 31 eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭum vaḻakkam vīracōḻiya kālatti oṭṭi vantatu.	(e. ku. pak. 5)
āytatti olikkumpōtu vāy ciṟitu tiṟantum ciṟitu mūṭiyum iruttaliṉ, ataṉi iṭiyeḻuttup pōṉṟa mey eṉak kūṟal takum.	(e. ku. pak. 48)
āytamum meyyātal -
meyyeḻuttukkup puḷḷi eṉpatum peyar; āytamum puḷḷi yeḻuttām. meyyiṉ māttiri ari; āytattiṉ māttiriyum ari. mey puṇarum iyalip puḷḷi mayaṅ kiyal eṉṟē tol. peyariṭṭuḷḷār. āytatti avar ‘āytap puḷḷi’ eṉṟē kuṟippiṭu kiṟār. orumey maṟṟoru meyyākat tiriyum puṇarccikkaṇ, kal + tītu = kaḵṟītu (to. e. 369. nac.), muḷ + tītu = muḵṭītu (399) eṉpaṉa iṭiyē kūṟappaṭutalāṉum āytam meyyākavē koḷḷappaṭum.			(to. e. 38, 39 nac. uri)
āyiṭi : uṭampaṭumey peṟāmi - 
‘ivvaṇi, āyiṭi : uṭampaṭumey peṟāmi’ kāṇka.
āyiyalpu iṉṟu -
avviyalpu eṉpatu ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ akaraccuṭṭu nīṇṭu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu āyiyalpu eṉṟu varum. āyiyalpu iṉṟu - anta iyaṟkiyip peṟātu; aḵtāvatu anta īṟṟup potumuṭipip peṟātu eṉpatu.
pū eṉṟa ūkāra īṟṟup peyar, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ potuvāka avvīṟṟuc coṟkaḷukkuk kūṟappaṭum ukarap pēṟum valleḻuttu mikutalum peṟātu, varumoḻi mutal valleḻuttukku iṉamāṉa melleḻuttu mikutalē perumpāṉmi eṉpatu.
varumāṟu :  pū + koṭi = pūṅkoṭi 	(to. e. 268 nac.)
āyiram attup peṟutal -
āyiram taṉṉiviṭak kuṟinta aḷaviṟṟākiya eṇṇup peyar taṉmuṉ varumiṭattē, vaḻakkamākiya ē eṉṉum cāriyi peṟātu, attuccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum. ciṟupāṉmi makaram keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu.
	varumāṟu : āyiram + oṉṟu ḻū āyiram + attu + oṉṟu = āyirattoṉṟu; āyiram + pattu ḻū āyiram + attu + pattu = āyirattuppattu; āyiram + pattu ḻū āyira + pattu = āyirappattu		(to. e. 317 nac).
āyiram eṉṟa collamippu -
āyiram eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar tolkāppiyam, pattup pāṭṭu, patiṟṟup pattu, paripāṭal, kalittoki, puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliya paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇilakkiyaṅkaḷil payiṉṟu vantuḷḷatu.
sakasram eṉṟa āriyaccol cāciram eṉak kaṉṉaṭattil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ‘cāciram’ āyiram eṉat tirintu amin tirukkalām.	(e. ā. pak. 153).
‘ār’ ‘pīr’ marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
ār eṉṟa marappeyar varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarum vaḻi iṭiyē melleḻuttuppēṟum, ciṟupāṉmi ammuc cāriyiyum attuccāriyiyum eytum.
e-ṭu :	ār + kōṭu = ārṅkōṭu; - ār + kaṇṇi = āraṅ kaṇṇi (am); pīr + alar = pīrattalar (attu)	(to. e. 363 nac.)

‘āl’ marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
āl eṉṟa marappeyar, vaṉkaṇam - meṉkaṇam - iṭik kaṇam - varumoḻi mutalil varumiṭattu ammuccāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻikkēṟpat tirintu muṭiyum. uyirkkaṇam varumiṭattu ammuccāriyi peṟutaliṉṟu.
e-ṭu :	āl + am + kōṭu = ālaṅkōṭu; āl + am + ñeri = ālañeri; āl + am + viṟaku = āla viṟaku; āl + ili = ālili
itu vēṟṟumippuṇarcci.	(to. e. 375 nac.)
āvayiṉ eṉṟa collamippu -
ēḻāmvēṟṟumi yiṭapporuḷ uṇarttum vayiṉ eṉṟa col akaramākiya cuṭṭiṭiccolloṭu puṇarntu avvayiṉ eṉṟāki (to. e. 334. nac), ceyyuṭkaṇ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu āvayiṉ eṉṟu muṭintu avviṭattil eṉṟa poruḷil varum.	(to. e. 250 nac.)
āvayiṉāṉa - avviṭattukkaṇ	(to.e. 148)
āviri eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
āviri eṉṟa marappeyar, vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccik- kaṇ ammuccāriyi peṟṟu, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu aikāram keṭa, āvir eṉṟāki, āviraṅkōṭu, āvirañcetiḷ, āvirantōl eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(to. e. 283 nac.)
āviyum oṟṟum aḷaviṟantu icikkum iṭam -
uyirum meyyum ici - viḷi - paṇṭamāṟṟu - nāval - kuṟip pici - muṟiyīṭu - pulampal - mutalāya iṭaṅkaḷil tam māt tiriyaḷaviṉik kaṭantu olikkum. iciyiṉ aḷaviṟantu olikkumiṭattu uyir 12 māttiri īṟākavum, mey 11 māttiri īṟākavum olikkum eṉpar icinūlār. nāval - neṟpōr teḻippōr pakaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷi ōṭṭuvatoru col. paṇṭamāṟṟu - paṇṭaṅkaḷi (vili kūvi) viṟṟal.
e-ṭu :	‘nāvalōoo eṉṟicikkum nāḷōti’ - nāval
	‘uppōoo eṉavurittu mīḷvāḷ’ - paṇṭamāṟṟu
	‘kaḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉum kallata rattam’, ‘cuḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉum taṇtōṭṭup peṇṇi’ - kuṟippici	(naṉ. 101)
āviyum oṟṟum tamakkuc coṉṉa māttiriyiṉ mikku icikkavum peṟum. nāvalum muṟiyīṭum pulampum kuṟippiciyum mutalāyiṉa koḷka.
‘kaḵḵṟeṉṉum kallatar attam’ eṉpatu kuṟippici.
uyir 12 māttiriyum oṟṟu 11 māttiriyum nīḷum eṉṟār kantaruvanūluṭiyār. avi vantavaḻik kāṇka. 	(naṉ. 100 mayili.)
‘āṟaṉ urupiṟku īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyal -
neṭumutal kuṟukum coṟkaḷākiya tām - yām - nām - tāṉ - yāṉ - nī - eṉṟa coṟkaḷ, urupēṟkumiṭattum urupu tokka poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum, mutal kuṟukit tam - em - nam - taṉ - eṉ - niṉ - eṉṟāki,  akaraccāriyi peṟṟut tama, ema - mutalāka niṉṟu, āṟaṉ urupoṭu puṇarum. āṟām vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ atu, a eṉpaṉa. akarattoṭu puṇarum- vaḻi uṭampaṭumey peṟṟut tamava - emava - namava - taṉava - eṉava - niṉava eṉa muṭiyum. atu urupoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, atu urupiṉ akaram keṭa, tama + atu ḻū tama + tu = tamatu eṉṟāṟpōlap puṇarum eṉpar tolkāppiyaṉār. 	(e. 115 na.)
naṉṉūlār nilimoḻiyiṉ īṟu akaram peṟum eṉṟō, varumoḻiyākiya atuurupiṉ akaram keṭum eṉṟō kūṟātu vāḷā viṭuttār.
naṉṉūlārukku, tām + atu = tamatu eṉap puṇarum. taṉik kuṟil muṉ oṟṟu āṟām vēṟṟumikku iraṭṭātu eṉṟu avar vitittār.
kaṉṉaṭamoḻiyil, taṉatu - tamatu - taṉuyir - tamuyir mutaliyavaṟṟit tol. kūṟuvatu pōlavē, taṉ + a + atu, tam + a + atu, taṉ + a + uyir, tam + a + uyir eṉṟu pirittuk kūṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. am moḻiyil akaram āṟaṉ urupākum. teluṅkil paṉmi ēṟṟa peyarkaḷukku akaramē urupāka varukiṟatu. tamiḻilum āṟaṉpaṉmi urupu ‘a’ eṉṟu naṉṉūlārum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ‘atu’ aḵṟiṇi orumic cuṭṭuppeyarātaliṉ, uyartiṇikku atu cālātu eṉṟu, nampiyatu makaṉ eṉṉātu, nampikku makaṉ eṉa nāṉka ṉurupiṉi iṇittaṉar.
āṟām vēṟṟumikku naṉṉūlār ātu eṉṟa urupum kūṟiṉār.
taṉ + a + atu ḻū taṉa + atu = taṉātu; mara + aṭi = marāṭi iraṇṭu akaraṅkaḷ ākāramāṉa vaṭivamē atu. inta ātu urupu taṉ - eṉ - niṉ - eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkē varuvatu; irāma ṉātu - kaṇṇaṉātu eṉap piṟa peyaroṭu vārātu. itaṉi nōkka, neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻiyiṟutiyil varum akaram - urupu mutaleḻuttākiya akaram iraṇṭum cērntu ‘ā’ āka, atuurupi ataṉuṇmi nōkkātu ātu eṉṟatōr urupāka naṉṉūlār kuṟippiṭṭatu pōtarum.  (e. ā. pak. 96.)
āṟaṉurupiṉ akaram keṭum iṭamum keṭāta iṭamum -
yāṉ - yām - nām - tāṉ - tām - nī - eṉpaṉa neṭumutal kuṟuki, eṉ - em - nam - taṉ - tam - niṉ - eṉṟāki, akaraccāriyi peṟṟu, eṉa - ema - nama - taṉa - tama - niṉa eṉṟāyavaḻi (to. e. 161 nac.), varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ atu eṉṟa āṟaṉurupu variṉ, avvurupiṉ akaram keṭa, eṉa + atu ḻū eṉa + tu = eṉatu eṉṟāṟpōlap puṇarum. num eṉpatum num + atu ḻū numa + tu = numatu eṉap puṇarum. varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ akaram eṉṟa āṟaṉurupu variṉ, avvurupu keṭātu, eṉa + a ḻū eṉa + v + a = eṉava eṉṟāṟ pōla (iṭiyē vakaruṭampaṭumey peṟṟu) anneṭumutal kuṟukum coṟkaḷ puṇarum.	(to. e. 115 nac. uri.)
āṟu vikāramum mūṉṟu tiripil aṭaṅkumāṟu -
virittal vikāram tōṉṟalākavum, valittal - melittal - nīṭṭal - kuṟukkal eṉṉum vikāraṅkaḷ tiritalākavum, eñciya tokuttal vikāramum mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉa mūviṭattuk kuṟitalum keṭutalākavum aṭaṅkum. ākavē ceyyuḷ vikāramum mūṉṟeṉa amiyum.	(i. vi. eḻut. 58.)
āṉcāriyi, āṉurupu  ivaṟṟiṭi vēṟupāṭu -
āṉcāriyi tōṉṟiyaviṭattu yātāṉum ōrurupiṉi aḵtu ēṟkum āṟṟaluṭiyatu. e-ṭu : orupāṉ + ai = oru pāṉi; orupāṉ + ku = orupāṟku. āṉurupu tōṉṟiya viṭattu atu vēṟōr urupiṉi ēlātu. e-ṭu : vāḷāṉ veṭṭiṉāṉ, vāṇikattāṉ āyiṉāṉ.	(to. e. 119 nac. uri)
āṉcāriyi varum iṭaṅkaḷ -
orupaḵtu irupaḵtu.... eṇpaḵtu eṉṟa coṟkaḷ urupuka ḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, accoṟkaḷiluḷḷa ‘aḵtu’ eṉpatu keṭa, āṉcāriyi vara, orupāṉ irupāṉ... eṇpāṉ - eṉa niṉṟu urupēṟṟu, orupāṉi, orupāṉoṭu ..... eṇpāṉi - eṇpāṉoṭu - eṉap puṇarum. oṉpatu eṉpatum oṉpāṉ eṉa āṉcāriyi peṟṟut tirintu, oṉpāṉi - oṉpā ṉoṭu eṉa urupēṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	‘oṉpāṉ mutaṉili’ - (to. e. 463 nac.)
	‘oṉpāṟku oṟṟiṭi mikumē’ - (to. e. 475) (to. e. 199 nac.)
ikara aikāra īṟṟu nāṭpeyar, tam muṉṉar viṉiccol varu miṭattu iṭiyē āṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, cittiriyāṟ koṇ ṭāṉ	(to. e. 247, 286 nac.)
makarīṟṟu nāṭpeyar attuc cāriyiyōṭu āṉcāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻiyāka varum viṉiccolloṭu puṇarum.
e-ṭu : makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ 	(to. e. 331 nac.)
viṉiyeṉavē, viṉippeyarum aṭaṅkum.
paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭavaṉ, cittiriyāṟ koṇṭavaṉ, makattāṟ koṇṭavaṉ eṉak koḷka. ivi ēḻām vēṟṟumip poruḷaṉa.
āṉcāriyi vēṟṟumippuṇarccikkaṇ tiriyumāṟu -
nāṭpeyarkku muṉ varum āṉcāriyiyiṉ ṉakaram, varu moḻi viṉiyākavum viṉippeyarākavum vaṉkaṇam mutaleḻuttākavariṉ, ṟakaramākat tirintu puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, cittiriyāṟ koṇ ṭāṉ, makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ
	orupāṟku eṉa urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ, āṉ cāriyiyatu ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tiriyum.		(to. e. 123, 124 nac.)
āṉmāccirayam -
itu ‘taṉṉip paṟṟutal’ eṉṉum kuṟṟam.
ēṉiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkuk kāraṇamāka irukkum akaram taṉ tōṟṟattukkum tāṉē kāraṇam eṉṟu kūṟutal āṉmāccirayamām.
kāraṇamum kāriyamum oṉṟu eṉṟu koḷḷum niliyil ik kuṟṟam ēṟpaṭum. kāraṇam muṉṉarum kāriyam piṉṉarum varutalē muṟi. immuṟiyi viṭuttu iraṇṭum oṉṟeṉal kuṟṟamām.	(cū. vi. pak. 50).
‘āṉiṉ ṉakaramum’ - ummiyi iraṭṭuṟa moḻital -
‘āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟē, nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal toḻiṟkē’ - (e. 125) ummiyi iraṭṭuṟamoḻi- talāṉ etiratu taḻīiya tākki, iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum nāḷ allavaṟṟu muṉ varum vaṉmutal toḻiṟku ataṉōraṟṟu eṉak koṇṭu, ‘paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ’ mutaliyaṉa koḷḷap paṭum.
iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaram ātaṟku itu payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.		(to. e. 125 iḷa. uri)
“ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyatu ākki” eṉpar nac.		(to.e. 124 uri)
āṉimukattāṉi ‘mummatattaṉ’ eṉṟal -
kaḻuttiṟkumēlaṉṟi yāṉiuṟuppu illāta kaṭavuḷ vināyakaṉ. vēḻamukattiṟkēṟpa avaṉukku irumatamē uḷa eṉṟal poruntum. maṟṟu, ‘mummatattaṉ’ eṉṟatu evvāṟu poruntumeṉiṉ, pañcāṭcarattiṉ pētamākiya eṭ ṭeḻuttu - āṟeḻuttu - nāleḻuttu - mutalāyiṉavum ‘pañcāṭcaram’ eṉṟē kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, mummatattiṉ vaki yākiya orumatam irumataṅkaḷum ‘mummatam’ eṉappaṭutal poruntum eṉpatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 eṉat toki koṭut tami, neṭiṟṟoṭar mutaliya āṟaṉiyum vaṉmiyūr ukaram (ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu eṉṉum) āṟaṉōṭu uṟaḻtalāl eṉka. mayakka viti iṉmiyiṉ iṭiyiṉa meyyiṉi ṭakāra ṟakāraṅkaḷ ūrnta ukaram toṭarātu ātaliṉ, avviraṇ ṭaṉiyum vilakkiyē kaṇakkiṭal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu vilakkāmi, āṉimukattuk kaṭavuḷi ‘mummatattaṉ’ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa niyāyattiṉpāṟ paṭṭatu.	(naṉ. 94 caṅkara.)


i
i -
iḵtu uyireḻuttu variciyuḷ mūṉṟāvatu; aṅkāppōṭu aṇpal mutaliṉi nāviḷimpu uṟap piṟappatu; orumāttiri aḷaviṉatām kuṟṟeḻuttu.
iḵtu aṇmic cuṭṭup poruḷil varum iṭiccol - ivaṉ, ikkoṟṟaṉ.
iḵtu oru cāriyi - maṇ + yātu = maṇṇiyātu; vēḷ + yāvaṉ = vēḷiyāvaṉ.	(naṉ- 206 caṅ)
ya ra la mutalavākum āriyac coṟkaḷi iyakkaṉ, irāmaṉ, ilāpam eṉa vaṭacol ākka itu muṉ varuvatu.
āriyac coṟkaḷi vaṭacollākka,	vākyam - vākkiyam kramam - kiramam, cuklam - cukkilam eṉa, valloṟṟukkaḷi aṭutta ya ra la ivaṟṟukku iṭiyē itu varuvatu.	(naṉ. 148, 149 caṅ.)
itu viravuppeyar vikuti - ceviyili; āṇpāṟpeyar vikuti - villi; peṇpāṟpeyar vikuti - kūṉi; aḵṟiṇip peyar vikuti - kaṉali; viṉimutaṟpeyar vikuti - alari; ceyappaṭuporuḷ vikuti - ūruṇi; karuvipporuḷ vikuti	- maṇveṭṭi; ēval orumi vikuti - celluti; viyaṅkōḷ vikuti - kāṇṭi (kāṇka); viṉiyecca vikuti - ōṭi; toḻiṟ peyar vikuti - vekuḷi; pakutipporuḷ vikuti - uruḷi. 	(ilakkaṇac curukkam mutaliyaṉa)
i ī mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭamum muyaṟciyum -
i ī e ē ai eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭam miṭaṟu. i ī eṉpaṉa aṇpal mutali nāviḷimpu uṟutalāl piṟappaṉa. aṇpal mutalāvatu mēlvāyppaṟkaḷukkup piṉṉar uḷḷa aṇṇam.	(e. ku. pak. 90).
palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉi nāviṉatu aṭiviḷimpu ceṉṟu uṟa, i ī mutaliyaṉa piṟakkum eṉpar iḷampūraṇar (to. e. 86). i ī mutaliyaṉa aṇpallum aṭināviḷimpum uṟap piṟappaṉa eṉpar nac.
ikaram mutalāyiṉa vaṭamoḻiyil tālavyam eṉappaṭuta liṉ, iṭinākkut tāluviṉic cāra avi piṟakkum. tāluvāvatu mūrttāviṉ mēṟpakuti (aṇṇam) ātaliṉ, aṭinākkiṉ viḷimpu aṇpallip poruntutaliṉṟu.
‘aṇpal mutalnā viḷimpu uṟal’ (86) eṉṟa toṭari ‘aṇpal mutalnāviḷimpu uṟal’ eṉṟu pirittup poruḷ koḷḷātu,  ‘aṇpalmutal nāviḷimpu uṟal’ eṉṟu pirittup poruḷkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. iṭinākkut tāluviṉil aṭuttalāl ikara īkāram piṟakkum.
i ī e ē ai eṉṟa ainteḻuttukkum piṟappiṭamum muyaṟciyum oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭiṉum, ekara ēkāraṅkaḷukku muyaṟciyil ciṟitu vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. aikārattil, kaṇṭattup piṟakkum akaram mutalilum tāluvil piṟakkum ēkāram muṭivilum uḷḷaṉa eṉpatum, avaṟṟiṉ oli muṟiyē arimāttiri yum oṉṟari māttiriyum ām eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.	(e. ā. pak. 77, 78, 79)
ikkuccāriyi puṇarumāṟu -
ikkuccāriyi ikara aikāra īṟṟu nilimoḻikaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi atu taṉ mutaleḻuttākiya ikaram keṭap puṇarum; varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam mikum.
e-ṭu :	āṭi + ikku + koṇṭāṉ = āṭikkuk koṇ ṭāṉ; cittiri + ikku + koṇṭāṉ = cittirik kuk koṇṭāṉ	(to. e. 126, 127 nac.)


ikkuccāriyi varum iṭaṅkaḷ -
ikara aikāra īṟṟut tiṅkaṭpeyarkaḷ, varumoḻi val leḻuttu mutalākiya viṉiccollōṭu ēḻām vēṟṟu mip poruḷiṟ puṇarumiṭattu, iṭiyē ikkuccāriyi varum.
e-ṭu : āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ, cittirikkuk koṇ ṭāṉ
tamiḻil tiṅkaṭpeyarkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum ikara aikāra īṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum. āṭikku, cittirikku eṉpaṉa āṭikkaṇ, cittirikkaṇ eṉa ēḻām vēṟṟumip poruḷpaṭuvaṉa.	(to. e. 248, 286 nac.)
ikarīṟṟu alvaḻip puṇarcci -
uyartiṇikkaṇ ikarīṟṟup peyar, nampikkollaṉ - nampiccāṉṟāṉ - nampittuṇi - nampippiḷḷi - eṉavum, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ - ceṭṭiccāttaṉ - ceṭṭittēvaṉ - ceṭṭip pūtaṉ - eṉavum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokikkaṇ varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikum.
aḵṟiṇikkaṇ ikarīṟṟup peyar, mācittiṅkaḷ - alik koṟṟaṉ - kāvikkaṇ - kuvaḷikkaṇ eṉa irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyiṉ iṭiyē varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikum.
parutti kuṟitu - eḻuvāyttoṭar iyalpākap puṇarum. uṇṇuti cāttā eṉa viṉimuṟṟut toṭar iyalpākap puṇarum. kiḷi kuṟitu kiḷikkuṟitu eṉac ciṟupāṉmi eḻuvāyt toṭar uṟaḻntu muṭiyum. ōṭic ceṉṟāṉ eṉa viṉi yeccam mikkatu. ‘ceṉmati pāka’ eṉa ikarīṟṟu iṭic col iyalpāka muṭintatu. kaṭikā eṉa ikara īṟṟu uric col iyalpāka muṭintatu. iṉik koṇṭāṉ aṇik koṇṭāṉ ik koṟṟaṉ eṉa ikara īṟṟu iṭiccol mikkatu 	(to. e. 236 nac.) ( 158 nac. uri)
avvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, avvaḻik koṇṭāṉ eṉa ikara īṟṟu iṭiccol uṟaḻntu muṭintatu.	(159 nac. uri)
ikarīṟṟu iṭiccol puṇarcci -
atōḷi - itōḷi - utōḷi - etōḷi - eṉṟa iṭap poruḷi uṇartti niṉṟa ikarīṟṟu iṭiccoṟkaḷ, atōḷik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōl valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. avvaḻi - ivvaḻi - uvvaḻi - evvaḻi - eṉpaṉa, avvaḻi koṇṭāṉ avvaḻik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
iṉi - aṇi - eṉpaṉavum, cuṭṭiṭiccollum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ vanta valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	iṉikkoṇṭāṉ, aṇikkoṇṭāṉ, ik koṟṟaṉ. (iṉi - ippoḻutu; aṇi - aṇiya iṭattu; i - ivviṭattu)	(to. e. 159, 236. nac).
ikarīṟṟuccuṭṭup puṇarcci -
ikarīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṭiccol, vaṉkaṇam variṉ, vanta valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇam variṉ vanta melleḻuttum, iṭikkaṇamākiya yakaravakaraṅkaḷ variṉ vakaramum iṭiyē peṟṟup puṇarum; uyirmutalmoḻi varumiṭattuk kuṟili aṭutta oṟṟāka vakaram iraṭṭikkum; ceyyuṭkaṇ ikaram nīṇṭu puṇarum.
va-ṟu:	i + koṟṟaṉ = ikkoṟṟaṉ; i + ñālam = iññālam; i + nāy, māṭu = innāy, immāṭu; i + yāṉi = ivyāṉi; i + vāṭi = ivvāṭi; i + āṭu = ivvāṭu; i + vayiṉāṉa = īvayiṉāṉa	(to. e. 238 nac.)
ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaṭpeyarp puṇarcci -
tamiḻil mātaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ ikaram aikāram eṉṟa iraṇṭu īṟukaḷē peṟṟuḷa. ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaṭpeyar, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattil toṭaṅkum viṉiyum viṉip peyarum eṉṟa ivaṟṟoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, ikkuccāriyi yum iyipu valleḻuttum peṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	āṭi + koṇṭāṉ = āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ; āṭi + koṇṭavaṉ = āṭikkuk koṇṭavaṉ
āṭikku eṉpatu āṭimātattiṉkaṇ eṉṉum ēḻaṉ poruḷatu.	(to. e. 248 nac.)
ikarīṟṟu nāṭpeyarp puṇarcci -
nāḷ - nāḷmīṉ. tamiḻil nāṇmīṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ ikarīṟu - aikāra īṟu - makarīṟu - eṉṟa mūvīṟṟu moḻikaḷākavē uḷḷaṉa. ikarīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattil toṭaṅkum viṉi viṉippeyar eṉṟa ivaṟṟoṭu puṇaru miṭattu, āṉcāriyi peṟṟu āṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	paraṇi + koṇṭāṉ = paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; paraṇi + koṇṭavaṉ = paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭavaṉ.
itaṟkup paraṇikkaṇ eṉa ēḻaṉporuḷ virikka.		(to. e. 247 nac.)
ikarīṟṟup peyar  alvaḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci -
ikarīṟṟu uyartiṇippeyar vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkup puṇarum. e-ṭu : eṭṭikkumaraṉ, nampippēṟu	(to. e. 154 nac.)
ikarīṟṟup potuppeyar, irupeyaroṭṭāyiṉ mikkum, eḻuvāyt toṭarāyiṉ iyalpāyum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻip puṇarum. e-ṭu :	cāttippeṇ, cātti periyaḷ eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(to. e. 155 nac.)
ivi alvaḻi muṭipu.
ikarīṟṟu aḵṟiṇippeyar, vēṟṟumikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum, ciṟupāṉmi uṟaḻntum puṇarum. e-ṭu : kiḷikkāl, kiḷicciṟaku - mikuti; kiḷikuṟumi, kiḷikkuṟumi - uṟaḻcci  	(to. e. 235 nac.)
ikarīṟu ikaram keṭṭu ammuccāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu. 
e-ṭu : kūtāḷi + kōṭu = kūtāḷaṅkōṭu; kaṇaviri + kōṭu = kaṇaviraṅkōṭu	(to. e. 246 nac.)
ikarīṟu varumoḻiyōṭu ikaram keṭṭup puṇartalu muṇṭu. e-ṭu : kaṭṭi + iṭi = kaṭṭiṭi; kaṭṭi + akal = kaṭṭakal	(to. e. 246. nac.)
cila ikarīṟu ammup peṟātu melleḻuttup peṟutalu muṇṭu. e-ṭu : puḷiṅkāy, puḷimpaḻam	(to. e. 246 nac.)
ivi vēṟṟumi muṭipu.
‘ikara ukarattu iyaṟkiyum aṟṟē’ -
piṟarellām ‘ekara okarattu iyaṟkiyum’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōtiṉar. cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ āytap puḷḷi nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiyalukaram ākiyavaṟṟiṉ iyalpum meyyeḻuttiṟkuk kūṟiya avvāṟē ām. aḵtāvatu iviyum arimāttiriyaḷavu puḷḷi peṟṟu olikkum eṉṟavāṟu. varivaṭiviṉkaṇ aṭiyāḷam ceytukōṭal ivaṟṟiṟkum okkum.	(to. e. 16 ca. pāla.)
ikaram ekaramākat tiriyum vaṭanaṭip patam -
cipi marumāṉ cempiyaṉ eṉpuḻi, nilimoḻi mutal ikaram ekaramākat tirintatu.	(to. vi. 86 uri)
ikaramuṉi -
muṉi, muṉ, muṉṉar eṉpaṉa oruporuḷaṉa. ikaram nili moḻi īṟṟeḻuttāka varuvataṉ muṉṉar eṉṟavāṟu.		(to. e. 126 nac.)
ikara yakaram pōliyāy iruttal -
moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ yakaram varumiṭattu ataṟkup pōliyāka ikaram vantu icikkum. e-ṭu : nāy - nāi	 (to. e. 58 nac.)


ikara vikutiyiṉ iyalpu -
ikara īṟu taṉittu iyalāmiyiṉ yātāṉum oru mey yiṉi ūrntu varum. ammey kālam kāṭṭātu; atu kālam kāṭṭiṉ ikara vikuti kālam kāṭṭātu. (e-ṭu: cēṟi- ikaram etirkālam kāṭṭiṟṟu (cel+t+i); āṇṭut takaram kālam kāṭṭāmal niṉṟatu. aḵtu eḻuttup pēṟām. ceṉṟi, cellāniṉṟi- eṉpuḻi iṭinilikaḷ kālam kāṭṭiṉa; āṇṭu ikaravikuti kālam kāṭṭātu muṉ ṉili yorumip pāliyē cuṭṭuvatu.	(naṉ. 145 caṅkara.)
ikaravikuti viṉimutaṟporuṇmi uṇarttal -
ikaravikuti viṉimutaṟporuṇmiyi uṇarttutalic cērntārik kolli - nūṟṟuvarik kolli - nāḷōti - nūlōti - mutaliya coṟkaḷil kāṇalām. ivaṟṟukku muṟiyē taṉṉic cērntavari aḻippatu - orēnērattil nūṟupērkaḷi aḻippatu - naṭcattiraṅkaḷic collu pavaṉ - nūliṉi ōtupavaṉ - eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuka.	(cū. vi. pak. 33)
iciniṟi aḷapeṭi -
ceyyuṭkaṇ ōciyiniṟikka varum uyiraḷapeṭikaḷum, oṟṟaḷapeṭikaḷum (ceyyuḷ) ici niṟi aḷapeṭi kaḷām. aḷapeṭiyuḷ iviyē perumpālavāy nikaḻvaṉa.
e-ṭu :	‘uṟāark kuṟunōy urippāy kaṭalic
	ceṟāaay vāḻieṉ neñcu’
	‘kaṇṇ karuviḷi kārmulli kūr eyiṟu’
‘uṟāar’ eṉa aḷapeṭukkavilliyēl ōraciyākic cīr nili eytātu. ‘ceṟāaay’ eṉa īraḷapu eṭātoḻiyiṉ taḷi citiyum. ‘kaṇṇ’ eṉa ṇakaramey aḷapeṭāviṭil ‘kaṇ’ ōraciyēyākic cīrnili eytātu. ātaliṉ ivi iraṇṭaḷapeṭiyum ceyyuḷici niṟikka vantaṉavām.	(naṉ. 91 irāmā.)
iciniṟivu ātal -
neṭil nīṇṭicikka vēṇṭiṉ, annīṭcikkuriya ōciyi anneṭiliṉ iṉamākiya kuṟil uṭaṉvantu niṟittal.
e-ṭu :	ai oḷa nīṇṭicikka vēṇṭiṉ, avaṟṟi aṭuttu muṟiyē ikara ukaraṅkaḷ vantu anta nīḷavēṇ ṭiya iciyi niṟivuceytal. aii aiii; oḷu oḷuu eṉavarum. 	(to. e.42 nac.)
iciyiṭaṉ makaram kuṟukutal -
lakaraḷakaraṅkaḷ tirinta ṉakaraṇakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ (iṭamuṉ) varum makaram, tāṉ moḻiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttāka, taṉakku muṉṉaruḷḷa eḻuttiṉ ōciyāṉ taṉ arimāttiriyiṉ kuṟukutal. pōṉm, maruṇm eṉa varum. (muṉ - kālamuṉ).
iciyiṭaṉ -	 taṉakku muṉṉaruḷḷa eḻuttiṉ ōciyiṭattu. (to. e. 13 iḷa., nac.) (piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉum utti eṉpatu tavaṟu) iṭiyiṭaṉ - icinūliṭattu	(to. po. 665 pērā.) ituvē piṟaṉkōḷ kūṟal eṉṟum uttivaki.
iciyiṉ tirital -
eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital eṉṟa ōcivēṟupāṭṭāṉ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal.
‘cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi’ eṉṟa puṇarmoḻi, cempu eṉṟa colli eṭuttuc colliyavaḻic cempu oṉpatiṉtoṭi eṉavum, poṉ eṉṟa colli eṭuttuc colliyavaḻic cempoṉ patiṉtoṭi eṉavum pirintu poruḷpaṭṭu orēvakiyāṉa puṇarmoḻi āyiṉavāṟu.	(to. e. 141 nac.)
‘iciyoṭu civaṇiya’ -
kural mutaliya ēḻiciyoṭu poruntiya	(to. e. 33 iḷa.)
ōciyoṭu kūṭiya āciriyam veṇpā kali vañci eṉṟa nālvakip pāṭalkaḷ. civaṇiya : viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar (aḵṟiṇip paṉmi)	(to. e. nac. uri)
iṭappeyarp pakupatam -
vāṉāṉ, vāṉāḷ, vāṉār, vāṉatu, vāṉaṉa, vāṉēṉ, vāṉēm, vāṉāy, vāṉīr - eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa iv viṭattiṉiyuṭiyār eṉṉum poruṇmi iṭap peyarp pakupatam.	(naṉ. 133 mayili.)
iṭam vari kiḷavi -
mēl eṉṟa iṭatti variyaṟuttuṇarttap payaṉpaṭum, mēl eṉpataṉ tiripākiya ‘mī’ eṉṟa col ‘iṭam vari kiḷavi’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
itu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi iyalpākavum, val leḻuttu mikkum, ciṟupāṉmi melleḻuttu mikkum puṇarum. varumāṟu : mīkaṇ, mīkkōḷ, mīntōl	(to. e. 251 nac.)
iṭamuṉ -
‘muṉ’ eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉum, atu kālamuṉ eṉavum iṭamuṉ eṉavum iruvakiyākak koḷḷappaṭum. kāla muṉṉip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl, ‘muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu’ eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum. eṉavē, ‘kālamuṉ’ iṟantakālac ceytiyik kuṟippatām. iṭa muṉṉip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl, ‘muṉṉark kūṟappaṭum’ eṉṟu kūṟa vēṇṭum. eṉavē, ‘iṭamuṉ’ etirkālac ceytiyik kuṟippatām.
iṉi, oru collil vittu nōkkumiṭattu, ‘atu’ eṉṟa collil a eṉpatu muṉṉar olikkappaṭutaliṉ atu ‘kālamuṉ’ eṉappaṭum. ‘tu’ eṉpatu ‘kālappiṉ’ ākum. a eṉṟa oli kēṭkappaṭṭapiṉ iṉimēl kēṭkappaṭum oli eṉa amiyum ‘tu’ eṉpatu ‘iṭamuṉ’ ākum; appoḻutu muṉṉark kēṭkappaṭṭa a eṉṟa oli ‘iṭappiṉ’ ākum.
‘ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar’	-	to. e. 27 nac.
‘laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar’	-	to. e. 24 nac.
‘maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ’	-	to. e 28 nac.
‘yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar’	-	to. e. 29 nac.
‘muṉṉart tōṉṟum’	-	to. e. 35 nac.
‘ṅañaṇa namaṉa eṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉar’	-	to. e. 25 nac.
‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar’	-	to. e. 38 nac.
‘yaraḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ oṟṟa’	-	to. e. 48 iḷa.
‘ṉakāri muṉṉar’	-	to. e. 52
mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil muṉ - muṉṉar - eṉpaṉa ‘iṭamuṉ’ eṉṟa poruḷilēyē vantuḷḷaṉa.
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟa collamiti -
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟatu, ‘ittiripu perumpāṉmi aṉṟu; orōvaḻiyē varum’ eṉṟa poruḷatu. itaṉik kāṇalām iṭaṅkaḷ cila:
‘āṉoṟṟu akaramoṭu niliiṭaṉ uṭittē’	- to. e. 232 nac.
‘ḻakara ukaram nīṭuiṭaṉ uṭittē’	- to. e. 261 nac.
‘verineṉ iṟuti muḻutum keṭuvaḻi
varumiṭaṉ uṭittē melleḻut tiyaṟki’	- to. e. 300 nac.
‘ikara iṟupeyar tiripuiṭaṉ uṭittē’	- to. e. 154 nac.
	(e. ā. pak. 117).
iṭu eṉṟa vikuti -
iṭu eṉṟa vikuti taṉakkeṉa oruporuḷiṉṟip pakutiyic cārntu pakutipporuḷvikutiyāy, mēl iṭinili vikuti eṉpaṉa peṟṟuc colli nirappa utavuvatu.
e-ṭu :	eḻuntiṭṭāṉ, kaṭantiṭuvāṉ (ivi eḻuntāṉ, kaṭappāṉ eṉṉum poruḷaṉa).	(cū. vi. pak. 41)
iṭukuṟi kāraṇap peyar -
oru kāraṇam paṟṟātu oru poruṭkut toṉṟutoṭṭu iṭappaṭṭu varum peyar iṭukuṟi eṉappaṭum. aḵtu iṭu kuṟip potuppeyar eṉavum, iṭukuṟic ciṟappuppeyar eṉavum iruvakittu.
maram: iṭukuṟip potuppeyar; palā - iṭukuṟic ciṟappup peyar.
oru kāraṇam paṟṟi oruporuṭku amiyum peyar kāraṇap peyar eṉappaṭum. atuvum potu ciṟappu eṉa iruvakittu.
aṇi : kāraṇap potuppeyar; muṭi: kāraṇac ciṟappup peyar.
iṉi, kāraṇa iṭukuṟip peyarāvatu kāraṇam paṟṟi amiyum peyar, akkāraṇam nikaḻum ellāvaṟṟiṟkum peyarākātu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟkē iṭukuṟippeyar pōla varuvatu.
e-ṭu :	mukkaṇṇaṉ - mukkaṇṇiyuṭiya iṟivar palaruḷ civaṉukkē peyarāvatu.; antaṇar - karuṇiyuṭiya palaruḷ pārppārukkē  peyarā vatu; maṟavar - vīramikka palaruḷ oru cātiyārkkē peyarāvatu; muḷḷi - muṭceṭikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ ōriṉac ceṭikkē peyarāvatu.	(naṉ. 62 irāmā.)
iṭukuṟi, kāraṇam, potuvum ciṟappumeṉa nāṉkāvaṉa -
āvi, uyir, mey, uṭampu eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟip potuppeyar; (avaṟṟuḷ) a ā, ka ṅa eṉṟa toṭakkattiṉa iṭukuṟic ciṟappuppeyar.
kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa kāraṇap potuppeyar; kuṟṟikaram, kuṟṟukaram, makarakkuṟukkam, āytakkuṟukkam eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa kāraṇac ciṟappuppeyar.	(naṉ. 61 mayili.)
iṭukuṟippeyar, kāraṇappeyar vaki -
oru poruḷik kuṟittaṟkuk kaṭavuḷāṉum aṟivuṭiyō rāṉum iṭṭa kuṟiyākiya peyar iṭukuṟip peyar; kāraṇat tāṉ varum peyar kāraṇappeyar. ivi pala poruṭkum potuppeyarākiyum, oru poruṭkuc ciṟappup peyarāki yum varum.
e-ṭu :	maram - iṭukuṟip potuppeyar; paṉi - iṭukuṟic ciṟappup peyar; aṇi - kāraṇap potuppeyar: muṭi - kāraṇac ciṟappup peyar	(naṉ. 62 caṅkara.)
iṭi, uri, vaṭacol, pōli, marūu  ivaṟṟatu puṇarcci -
āṉkaṉṟu, māṉṟali, kōṉkuṇam, vaṇṭiṉkāl, nāyiṉ kāl, tēriṉcelavu, yāḻiṉpuṟam eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ cāriyi iṭiccoṟkaḷ (ṉ, iṉ) iyalpāyiṉa. taṭan tōḷ eṉa akarīṟṟu uriccol (taṭa) melleḻuttu mikkatu. maḻakaḷiṟu, uṟukāl (naṟ. 300) eṉa uriccol (maḻa, uṟu) iyalpāyiṉa. aḷikulam (kōviyār 123), vayirakaṭakam, tāmakaṇṇaṉ, kaṉakacāti, kamalapātam, taṉataṭam eṉa vaṭa col iyalpāyiṉa. ilmuṉ - muṉṟil, paṭimuṉ - muṉpaṭi, kaṇmī - mīkaṇ, potuvil - potiyil, vēṭki nīr - vēṇīr (kali. 23), vēṭki avā - vēṇavā (naṟ. 61), piṉ - piṉṟi eṉṉum toṭakkattup pōlimoḻikaḷum, arumaruntaṉṉāṉ - arumantāṉ, kiḻaṅkaṉṉa paḻañcōṟu- kiḻaṅkam paḻañcōṟu, kuṇakkuḷḷatu - kuṇātu, teṟkuḷḷatu - teṉātu, kuṭapālatu - kuṭātu, maliyamāṉāṭu - malāṭu, cōḻaṉāṭu - cōṇāṭu, pāṇṭiyaṉāṭu - pāṇṭi nāṭu, tañcāvūr - tañci, paṉiyūr - paṉaci, cēntamaṅkalam - cēnti, āṟṟūr - āṟi, ātaṉtanti - ānti, pūtaṉtanti - pūnti, vaṭukaṉtanti - vaṭukanti eṉṉum toṭakkattu marūumoḻikaḷum, nilivarumoḻikaḷil ēṟkum ceyki aṟintu muṭikka. ivaṟṟuḷ, arumantāṉ mutalāṉavaṟṟi valittal mutaliya vikāraṅkaḷāṉ amikka eṉpārum, ānti mutalāṉavaṟṟi ilakkaṇa moḻikaḷāka vēṟeṭuttu muṭippārum uḷar.	(naṉ. 238 mayili.)
iṭikkuṟi -
pakāppatattil iṭiyē ōreḻuttuk kuṟintum eḻuttuk kuṟiyāta colliṉ poruḷit taruvatu.
e-ṭu :	ōti: iḵtu ‘ōnti’  eṉpataṉ iṭikkuṟi; ōṇāṉ eṉṉum poruṭṭu. tokuttal vikāram pakupatattiṉ kaṇṇatu; itu pakāppatattiṉ kaṇṇatu eṉpatē vēṟupāṭu.	(naṉ. 156)
iṭittoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram -
īṟṟeḻuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaramāy īṟṟayaleḻuttu iṭi oṟṟāka amiyum colliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟṟilukaram iṭittoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaramām. iṭittoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaḷ alvaḻi kkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum;vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpākavum iṉcāriyi peṟṟum puṇarum. e-ṭu : teḷkukaṭitu - alvaḻi; teḷkukāl, teḷkiṉ kāl - vēṟṟumi(naṉ. 182)
iṭittoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
iṭittoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaḷ alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. e-ṭu : teḷku kaṭitu, teḷku ciṟitu; teḷkukāl, teḷku ciṟi 	(to. e. 425, 413 nac.)
varumoḻi uyirkkaṇam variṉ, kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉmēl uyirēṟi muṭiyum. e-ṭu : teḷku + aṭivu = teḷkaṭivu, teḷku + arumi = teḷkarumi	(to. e. 413 nac.)
iṭittoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaḷ urupuka ḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi iṉcāriyi peṟum. e-ṭu : teḷku + ai ḻū teḷku + iṉ + ai = teḷkiṉi; teḷku + āl ḻū teḷku + iṉ + āl = teḷkiṉāl; accāriyi poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum varum. e-ṭu : teḷkiṉkāl, teḷkiṉ ñāṟci	(to. e. 195 nac.)
iṭinilikaḷi ēlāta viṉikaḷ -
