ṟakarattoṭu kūṭivanta ukarīṟum umīṟumāṉa viṉi muṟṟukkaḷ kaḻinta kālamum varuṅkālamum (ceṉṟu, ceṉṟum; cēṟu, cēṟum), takarattoṭu kūṭiniṉṟa avviraṇṭu īṟṟu viṉiyum iṟappum etirvum (vantu, vantum; varutu, varutum), ṭakarattoṭu kūṭiniṉṟa avviraṇṭu īṟṟuviṉi yum iṟantakālamum (uṇṭu, uṇṭum), miṉīṟum, ēval poruṇmiyil varum aṉittīṟukaḷum, viyaṅkōṭ poruḷaṉavum, ikara īṟum, mār īṟumāṉa viṉikaḷ etir kālamum (uṇmiṉ, uṇ, uṇka - vāḻi - vāḻiyar, cēṟi, uṇmār), pakara īṟṟu viṉi iṟantakālamum etirkālamum (uṇpa), ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉi nikaḻvum etirvum (uṇṇum), etirmaṟiviṉi mukkālamum (uṇṇāṉ) kāṭṭum.	(naṉ. 144 mayili.)
iṭinili, cāriyi, canti, vikāram āvaṉa -
iṭiniliyāvaṉa perumpālum iṉiya ittuṇiya eṉṟaḷattaṟku ariyavāyp patam muṭippuḻik kālamum poruṇmiyum kāṭṭi āṇṭē kāṇappaṭuvaṉa.
cāriyiyāvaṉa aṉ āṉ mutalāka eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ellāp puṇarccikkum potuvāyp perumpālum iṉ ṉoliyē payaṉāka varuvaṉa.
cantiyāvaṉa iṉṉatu vantāl iṉṉatu iṉṉatām eṉa varuvaṉa.
vikāramāvaṉa patattuḷ aṭippāṭum ceyyuḷtoṭiyum oliyum kāraṇamāka valittal melittal mutalāyiṉavāka varuvaṉa. ivi iṭinili mutalāṉavaṟṟāl muṭiyātavaḻi varuvaṉa eṉak koḷka.	(naṉ. 132 mayili.)
iṭinili meymayakkattik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ -
caṅkam eṉiṉum, puṇarcci eṉiṉum, ciyōkam eṉiṉum, mayakkam eṉiṉum, pullal eṉiṉum, kalattal eṉiṉum iṭi nili meymayakkam eṉṉum oruporuṭ kiḷavi. 	(mu. vī. eḻut. 66)
iṭinili meymayakkam  -
oru moḻiyilum irumoḻiyilum ka ca ta pa eṉṟa meykaḷ tammuṉ tāmē mayaṅkum; ra ḻa - eṉṟa iraṇṭu meykaḷum tammuṉ piṟavē mayaṅkum;  ēṉiya paṉṉiraṇṭum tammuṉ tāmum,  tammuṉ piṟavum mayaṅkum.
ka ca ta pa - eṉṟa nāṉku meykaḷumnīṅkalāka ēṉiya patiṉāṉku meykaḷum piṟameykaḷoṭu kūṭum kūṭṭam vēṟṟunili meymayakkamām. raḻa - eṉṟa iraṇṭu meykaḷum oḻittu oḻinta  patiṉāṟu meykaḷum tammoṭu tām kūṭum kūṭṭam uṭaṉili meymayakkamām. ivviru pakuti mayakkamum moḻiyiṭiyē nikaḻum. meyyuṭaṉ uyirum, uyiruṭaṉ meyyum mayaṅkum mayakkattiṟku aḷa villi.	(naṉ. 110)
iṭiyeḻuttu  āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi -
valliṉam pōṉṟu valleṉṟu oliyāmalum melliṉam pōṉṟu melleṉṟu oliyāmalum iṭinikarttatāy olittalāṉum, iṭinikarttatāya miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ piṟattalāṉum iṭiyiṉam eṉpatu kāraṇap peyarāyiṟṟu. mēl yaralavaḻaḷa eṉṟa āṟu meykaḷiyum iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṟa peyarāṉ āḷvataṟku nūlmarapil peyariṭappaṭṭatu. ātaliṉ iṭiyeḻuttu eṉpatu āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi.	(to. e. 21. iḷa., nac. uri).
uyireḻuttukkaḷ vaḷiyit taṭukkāmal veḷiviṭutaliṉ, tāmē olikka iyalkiṉṟaṉa. vaḷiyi naṉku taṭuttaliṉ val leḻuttu melleḻuttukkaḷ tāmē olikka varuvaṉavāy illi. ciṟitaḷavu taṭuttaliṉ, iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷ tāmē ōrāṟṟāṉ olittal kūṭum. olikkum tiṟattil uyireḻuttuk kaṭkum valleḻuttu melleḻuttukkaṭkum iṭippaṭ ṭiruttalāṉē ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - kkaḷ iṭiyeḻuttu eṉap paṭṭaṉa.	(e. ā. pak. 11.)
muḻutum vāytiṟakka uṇṭām uyirukkum, vāy muḻutum mūṭa uṇṭām valliṉa melliṉaṅkaṭkum, vāy ciṟitu mūṭiyum ciṟitu tiṟantum iruttalāl uṇṭām ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - kkaḷ iṭiyāy niṟṟaliṉ iṭiyeḻuttu eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa.	(e. ku. pak. 29)
iṭiyeḻuttu  vēṟupeyarkaḷ -
iṭimi eṉiṉum, iṭikkaṇam eṉiṉum, iṭi eṉiṉum, iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṉum oruporuṭkiḷavi. iṭiyiṉam eṉpatum atu.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 19)
iṭi varum uyirmey -
colliṉ iṭiyil varum uyirmey uyireḻuttākavē koḷḷappaṭum. paṭavē, varaku eṉpatu uyirmeyttoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉappaṭātu, uyirttoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟē peyar peṟukiṟatu.	(to. e. 106 nac. uri)
impar eṉṟa collāṭci -
impar eṉṟa col ‘iṭamuṉ’ eṉṟa poruḷil varuvatu. kālam paṟṟi nōkkiṉ impar eṉpatu ‘kālappiṉ’ ākum.
‘neṭṭeḻut timparum... kuṟṟiyalukaram 	- to. e. 36-kāṭu
‘neṭṭeḻut timpar oṟṟumikat tōṉṟum’ - to. e. 196	-kāṭṭu
‘akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷi’ 	- to. e. 56 - ay
‘kuṟṟeḻut timparum...... ukarak kiḷavi’	- to. e. 267 - uṭūu
eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil impar eṉpatu iṭam paṟṟi varum muṉ eṉṟa poruḷil tolkāppiyattil vaḻaṅkuvatām. ākavē, “impar - umpar eṉpaṉa kāla iṭa vakikaḷāl mayaṅkum” eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟuvatu (cū. vi. pak. 25) poruntātu, ‘impar’ iṭamuṉṉākavē varukiṟatu. 			(e. ā. pak. 62)
imi, noṭi  aḷavātal  -
imi eṉṟatu imittali;  noṭi eṉṟatu noṭittali (noṭi - oli).  iraṇṭum ākupeyarāyk kālatti uṇartti niṉṟaṉa. ‘iyalpu eḻum’ eṉṉum peyareccam imi, noṭi eṉṉum peyarkaḷoṭu muṭintatu. eḻuttoli mutaliya vaṟṟi iyalpu keṭuttu oruvaṉ vēṇṭiyavāṟē eḻuppi ṉum avvāṟu eḻāniṟkum; imiyum noṭiyum iyalpu keṭuttu eḻuppa vēṇṭiṉum, avvāṟē eḻātu iyal pākavē eḻāniṟkum ātaliṉ ‘iyalpu eḻum’ eṉṟum, mēlic cūttirattu eḻuttolikaḷi vēṇṭiyavāṟē eḻuppātu ivvaḷavāṉ eḻuppuka eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.	(naṉ. 100 caṅkara.)
‘iyalpiṉ vikāramum vikārattu iyalpum’ -
‘niṉ’ eṉpataṉ īṟu perumpāṉmiyum iyalpām (naṉ.218) eṉṟa vitippaṭi iyalpākātu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyil ṟakaramākat tiriyum. 
e-ṭu :	‘vaḻipaṭu teyvam niṟpuṟaṅ kāppa’ (tol. cey. 106)
ṇakarīṟu vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil ṭakaramākattiriyum (naṉ. 209) eṉṟa potuvitippaṭi tiriyātu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyil iyalpām.	(naṉ. 255 caṅkara.)
e-ṭu : maṇ koṇarntāṉ
iyalpukaṇam -
varumoḻiyāka vaṉkaṇamākiya ka ca ta pa - mutalākiya moḻikaḷ varumiṭattē tiripukaḷ nikaḻum. meṉkaṇamākiya ña na ma - mutalākiya moḻikaḷ, ya va mutalākiya iṭikkaṇa moḻikaḷ, uyir mutalākiya uyirkkaṇa moḻikaḷ varum vaḻi nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum perumpāṉmiyum iyalpākavē puṇartaliṉ, immūṉṟu kaṇaṅkaḷum iyalpu kaṇam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.	(to. e. 144 nac.)
iyalpukaṇam varumoḻiyāka variṉ tiriyumiṭam -
toṭarmoḻikaḷiṉ muṉ ña na ma eṉṟa meṉkaṇam mutalā kiya moḻikaḷ variṉ melleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	katirñeri, katirññeri; katirnuṉi, katirnnuṉi; katirmuri, katirmmuri.
ciṟupāṉmi ōreḻuttu moḻikaḷum īreḻuttu moḻi kaḷum melleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu : pūñeri, pūññeri; pūnuṉi, pūnnuṉi; pūmuri, pūmmuri;
kāyñeri, kāyññeri; kāynuṉi, kāynnuṉi; kāymuri, kāymmuri.
ciṟupāṉmi, kiññerittār - kinnīṭṭiṉār - kim maṟittār -eṉa ōreḻuttu moḻikaḷum, meyññāṉam - meynnūl - meym maṟantār - eṉa īreḻuttu moḻikaḷum, meymmi - poymmi mutaliya paṇpuppeyarkaḷum mikkē muṭiyum eṉpārum, ivaṟṟi nalintu kūṟap piṟattaliṉ iyalpu eṉpārumuḷar. pūñāṟṟiṉār - pōlvaṉa mikātaṉa.	(to. e. 145 nac. uri.)
iyalpu puṇarcci (1) -
nilimoḻi varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻit tōṉṟal tirital keṭutal eṉṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷ nilimoḻi īṟṟilō varumoḻi mutalilō irumoḻikkum iṭiyilō nikaḻā mal irumoḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum puṇarcci iyalpu puṇarcciyām.
1.	nilimoḻi meyyīṟṟatāka, varumoḻimutalil uyir varuvaḻi, varumoḻi uyir nilimoḻi yīṟṟōṭu puṇarvatu. e-ṭu : avaṉ + aḻakiyaṉ = avaṉaḻakiyaṉ.
2 .	nilimoḻi kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatāka, varumoḻi uyir mutalatāka varuvaḻi, avvuyir akkuṟṟiya lukarattiṉ mēl ēṟimuṭivatu.  e-ṭu : nāku + aritu = nākaritu.
3.	 nilimoḻi uyirīṟṟatāka, varumoḻi uyirmuta lāka, iṭiyē uṭampaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarvatu. e-ṭu : palā + aḻakitu = palāvaḻakitu.
iviyāvum iyalpu puṇarcciyām.
kal + eṟintāṉ = kalleṟintāṉ eṉa, taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟu varumoḻi mutalil uyirvariṉ iraṭṭi muṭivatum iyalpu puṇarcci eṉpar nac.	(to. e. 144 nac. uri.)
iyalpu puṇarcci (2)  -
nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum, iṭiyē tōṉṟal - nili moḻi īṟu tirital - nilimoḻi īṟu keṭutal - varumoḻi mutal keṭutal - varumoḻi mutal tirital - eṉṟa tiripukaḷ iṉṟi iyalpākap puṇarvatu iyalpu puṇarcciyām. ‘uṭalmēl uyir vantoṉṟi’, āl + ili = ālili eṉṟāṟ pōla varuvaṉavum, uṭampaṭumey iṭiyē tōṉṟi maṇi + aḻakitu = maṇiyaḻakitu eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavum iyalpupuṇarcciyuḷ aṭaṅkum. uyirvariṉ, nilimoḻik kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭuvatum atu.		(naṉ. 153, 162, 204, 205)
iyalpupuṇarccikku varum varumoḻikaḷ -
uyiroṭu kūṭiya ña na ma - ya va - mutalāku moḻikaḷum uyireḻuttukkaḷ mutalāku moḻikaḷum iyalpupuṇarc cikku varum varumoḻikaḷām.
viḷa eṉṟa nilimoḻiyoṭu, ñāṉṟatu - nīṇṭatu - māṇṭatu - yātu - valitu - aḻakitu - āṭiṟṟu - iṉitu - īṇṭiṟṟu - uṇṭu - ūṟiṟṟu - eḻuntatu - ēyntatu - aitu - oṉṟiyatu - ōṅkiṟṟu - oḷaviyattatu - eṉa,  immeṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam uyirkkaṇamākiya varumoḻiyip puṇarkka, avi iyal pākap puṇarntavāṟu.	(to. e. 144 nac).
iyalpu, vikāramivaṟṟi uṇarttumpiṟa coṟkaḷ  -
iyalpu eṉiṉum, taṉmi eṉiṉum, cupāvam eṉiṉum okkum. vikāram eṉiṉum, ceyal eṉiṉum, ceyaṟki eṉiṉum, viti eṉiṉum okkum.	(naṉ. 151 caṅkara.)
iyalpoṭu vikārattu iyiyum puṇarppu -
meyyiyum uyiriyum mutalum īṟumākavuṭiya pakāppatam pakupatam eṉṉum iraṇṭu pataṅkaḷum taṉṉoṭu tāṉum piṟitoṭu piṟitumāy alvaḻip poruḷilō vēṟṟumip poruḷilō poruntumiṭattu, nilimoḻiyum varu moḻiyum iyalpākavum tiripuṟṟum puṇarvatu puṇarcciyām.
e-ṭu :	maṇi + aḻaku, nilam + valitu, maṇi + peritu, nilam + aḻakitu - nilimoḻiyīṟṟu uyirum meyyum varu moḻi mutal meyyum uyirum muṟiyē taṉṉoṭu tāṉum piṟitoṭu piṟitum iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.
avaṉ + vantāṉ, poṉ + vaṇṭu, poṉṉaṉ + ki, kiḷi + aḻakitu - pakupatamum pakāppatamum taṉṉoṭu tāṉum piṟitoṭu piṟitum iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.	(naṉ. 151)
iyalum paṭalamum  -
iyal eṉṟatu orucātipporuḷ koṇṭatu eṉpatum, paṭalam eṉpatu palacātipporuḷ koṇṭatu eṉpatum āyiṟṟu. iyal eṉpataṟku utāraṇam inta nūlilē eḻuttiyal eṉpatil eḻuttilakkaṇamē varutalum, pataviyal eṉpatil patattu ilakkaṇamē varutalum, puṇariyal eṉpatil puṇarcciyilakkaṇamē varutalum ām.
paṭalam eṉpataṟku utāraṇam, paṭalavuṟuppik koṇ ṭirukkiṟa kāviyaṅkaḷil, iyalpōla oruvaḻippaṭāmal, pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ katiyic colvatum allāmal mali varuṇaṉi- kaṭal varuṇaṉi - nāṭu nakaram mutaliya pala varuṇaṉikaḷum kalantu varutalum kāṇka.	(naṉ. 17 irāmā.)
iyaṟki aḷapeṭi -
kurīi, āṭūu, makaṭūu - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa iyaṟki aḷapeṭi. 	(naṉ. 91 irāmā.)
‘iyaṟkiya ākum ceyaṟkiya’ -
īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷuḷ neṭili yaṭuttu iṭiyē oṟṟumikka ṭukara ṟukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ, ēṉiya kuṟṟiyalukara īṟukaḷukku vitikkappeṟṟa iṉcāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭivu peṟum iyalpiṉa.
iyaṟki - (īṇṭuc) cāriyi peṟāmi; ceyaṟki - ceytiyi yuṭimi; iyaṟkiya ātal - cāriyi peṟā tiruttal.	(to. e. 198 iḷa. 197 nac.)
e-ṭu :	yāṭu + ai = yāṭṭi; yāṭu + kāl = yāṭṭukkāl
	yāṟu + ai = yāṟṟi; yāṟu + kāl = yāṟṟukkāl
ivaṟṟukku iṉcāriyi vilakkavē, vēṟu cāriyi peṟuṅ kol eṉṟa aiyatti akaṟṟa, avi cāriyi etuvum peṟāta iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(e.ā.pak. 131).
ivaṟṟit tolkāppiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ uyirt toṭarkkum koṇṭu, muyiṟu + ai = muyiṟṟi - eṉac cāriyi peṟātu iṭiyoṟṟu mikkup puṇartalik koṇṭaṉar; piṟkālattē, yāṭṭiṉi - yāṭṭiṉ kāl, yāṟṟiṉi - yāṟṟiṉ kāl, muyiṟṟi - muyiṟṟiṉkāl eṉac cāriyi peṟutalum peṟāmiyum nikaḻkiṉṟavāṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭaṉar.
‘yātta eṉpa yāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē’ (to. po. 602 pērā.) eṉa iṉcāriyi peṟṟami kāṇka.
iyaṟpeyarp puṇarcci -
iyaṟpeyar - peṟṟōr makkaḷukku iṭṭu vaḻaṅkum peyar.
nilimoḻi iyaṟpeyarāka, varumoḻikkaṇ tanti eṉṟa muṟippeyarvariṉ, iyaṟpeyariṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa ‘aṉ’ eṉpatum, tanti eṉṟa varumoḻiyiṉ mutaṟkaṇ akaram ēṟivanta takara oṟṟum keṭa, cāttaṉ + tanti ḻū cātt + anti = cāttanti - eṉṟāṟpōl puṇarum.	(to. e. 347 nac.)
ātaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉpaṉa nilimoḻiyāka, varumoḻiyākat tanti eṉṟa col variṉ, nilimoḻi ‘taṉ’ keṭṭu ā eṉavum pū eṉavum niṟka, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ uḷḷa takaram muḻutum keṭa, ā + nti, pū + nti = ānti, pūnti eṉa muṭiyum.	(348 nac.)
iyaṟpeyar paṇpaṭuttup peruñcāttaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla variṉ, iyaṟpeyarukku aminta ciṟappup puṇarcciyi viṭuttup potup puṇarcci peṟṟu, peruñcāttaṉ + tanti = peruñcāttaṉṟanti - eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum.	(349 nac.)
iṉṉārkku makaṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉṟa uṟavumuṟiyil iyaṟpeyarkaḷ puṇarumvaḻi, nilimoḻi iyaṟpeyar īṟṟiluḷḷa ‘aṉ’ keṭa, ataṉiṭattu ammuccāriyi vantu taṉakkuriya tiripēṟṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum. e-ṭu : cāttaṉ + koṟṟaṉ ḻū cātt + am + koṟṟaṉ = cāttaṅ koṟṟaṉ.	(350 nac.)
itu pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ‘aṉ’ keṭṭu ‘am’ puṇarum eṉṟu kūṟutali viṭa, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ṉakaram varumoḻi valleḻuttuk kēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tiriyum eṉṟal eḷitu. e-ṭu : piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ, antuvañ cāttaṉ, antuvan tāyaṉ, antuvam piṭṭaṉ.	(e. ā. pak. 158).
piṟapeyar iyaṟpeyaroṭu tokkavaḻik koṟṟaṅkuṭi - cāttaṅkuṭi - eṉa aṉkeṭṭu am vantu puṇartalum, cātta maṅkalam - koṟṟa maṅkalam eṉa meṉkaṇattiṉ muṉṉar ammiṉ makaram keṭutalum. vēṭaṉ + maṅkalam, vēṭaṉ + kuṭi eṉpaṉa muṟiyē vēṭṭamaṅkalam, vēṭṭaṅkuṭi eṉa ammuccāriyi peṟutaloṭu nilimoḻi oṟṟu iraṭṭu talum koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. e. 350 nac. uri)
tāṉ pēṉ kōṉ eṉṟa iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tiripiṉṟi iyal pākap puṇarum. tāṉ koṟṟaṉ, pēṉ koṟṟaṉ, kōṉ koṟṟaṉ eṉa iyalpāka muṭintavāṟu.	(351 nac.)
iyaṟṟappaṭum, ēvappaṭum terinili viṉippakutikaḷ  -
terinili viṉippakutikaḷ iruvakippaṭum. iyaṟṟum viṉimutalāṉ iyaṟṟappaṭum terinili viṉippakutikaḷ, ēvum viṉimutalāṉ ēvappaṭum teriniliviṉip pakutikaḷ - eṉpaṉa avi.
e-ṭu :	naṭantāṉ : iyaṟṟappaṭum terinili viṉippakuti; naṭappittāṉ, varuvittāṉ: ēvappaṭum terinili viṉippakuti  	(naṉ. 137, 138 irāmā.)
‘iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa mutal āku irupaḵtu’ -
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ irupattiraṇṭu. aviyāvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum, ka ca ta na pa ma va ya ña eṉṟa oṉpatu meykaḷi ūrnta uyirum, moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukara mum ām.	(to. e. 103 nac.)
iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu mā eṉṟa col puṇartal -
mā eṉṉum col iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ muṭiya uḷḷa eṇkaḷōṭu iyalpāyum tirintum puṇarum.
varumāṟu :	orumā, irumā iraṇṭumā, mummā mūṉṟumā, nāṉmā nāṉkumā, aimmā aintumā, aṟumā āṟumā, ēḻmā eḻumā, eṇmā eṭṭumā, oṉpatiṉmā oṉpatiṟṟumā oṉpatumā. (orumā eṉap puṇarumē yaṉṟi, oṉṟumā eṉa iyalpākap puṇarātu.)	(to. e. 480, 389 nac.)
irā  akaram peṟutalum, peṟāmiyum -
irā eṉpatu iravukkālattik kuṟikkum ākāra īṟṟup peyarum, irāta eṉṟa poruḷ tarum etirmaṟip peyareccamum ām.
iravuk kālattik kuṟikkum irā eṉṟa kālappeyar irupeyarummittokiyil irāappakal eṉa eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭiyākum akaram peṟum. aṅṅaṉamē eḻuvāyttoṭarilum irāak koṭitu eṉa akaram peṟum. āyiṉ vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ peyarkoṇṭu muṭiyiṉum viṉi koṇṭu muṭiyiṉum akarappēṟiṉṟi muṭiyum.
irākkoṇṭāṉ - irāviṭattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa ēḻaṉ poruḷatu; irākkākki - irāviṭattuk kākki eṉa ēḻaṉ poruḷatu; irākkūttu - irāviṭattuk kūttu eṉa ēḻaṉ poruḷatu; irāak kākki - illāta kākki eṉṉum peyarecca etirmaṟi; irāak kūttu - illāta kūttu eṉṉum peyarecca etirmaṟi.
eṉavē, irā eṉpatu peyareccamaṟi ākiyaviṭattu akara eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟum. aḵtu iravuk kālattik kuṟikkum peyarāyavaḻi alvaḻip puṇarcciyil akaram peṟum; vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil atu peṟātu.	(to. e. 223, 227 nac. uri)(e. ā. pak. 137)
irāp peyarkku ‘iṉ’ iṉmi -
irā eṉṉum peyarccol iṉcāriyi peṟātu muṭiyum eṉka. varumāṟu : irāk koṇṭāṉ.	(mu.vī.puṇa. 94)


iru eṉṟa vikuti  -
iru eṉṟa vikuti taṉakkeṉa oruporuḷiṉṟip pakutiyic cārnta pakutipporuḷ vikutiyāy, mēl iṭinili vikuti eṉpaṉa peṟṟuc colli nirappa utavuvatu.
	e-ṭu :	eḻuntiruntāṉ - eḻuntāṉ eṉṉum poruḷatu.
		uṇṭiruntāṉ - uṇṭāṉ eṉṉum poruḷatu.
eḻuntiruntu, uṇṭiruntāṉ eṉpaṉa viṉiyeccamum viṉimuṟṟumāyp pirikkappaṭāta orucol nīrmiyaṉa.		(cū. vi. pak. 41).
irutici puṇartal -
vaṭakku teṟku kuṇakku kuṭakku (kiḻakku, mēṟku) eṉa nāṉku ticikaḷil oruticippeyar nilimoḻiyākavum maṟṟoru ticippeyar varumoḻiyākavum amintu puṇarumvaḻi, iru moḻikkumiṭiyē ē eṉṉum cāriyi varum.
varumāṟu :	vaṭakkē teṟku, kuṇakkē kuṭakku, kiḻakkē mēṟku, vaṭakkē kiḻakku, teṟkē kuṇakku, teṟkē kuṭakku, vaṭakkē kuṇakku - eṉṟāṟpōla varutal kāṇka.	(to. e. 431 nac).
perunticikaḷāvaṉa vaṭakkum teṟkum; ivaṟṟoṭu kōṇat ticikaḷākiya kuṇakku kuṭakku (kiḻakku, mēṟku) eṉpaṉa puṇarumvaḻi, vaṭakuṇakku - vaṭakiḻakku - vaṭakuṭakku - vaṭamēṟku - teṉkuṇakku - teṉkiḻakku - teṉkuṭakku - teṉmēṟku - eṉa, nilimoḻikaḷākiya vaṭakku ‘vaṭa’ eṉavum teṟku ‘teṉ’ eṉavum tirintu varumoḻikaḷoṭu puṇarum.	(to.e. 432 nac.)
tolkāppiyaṉār kālattu vaṭakku ‘vaṭaku’ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. (kiḻakku mēṟku eṉpaṉa kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉṟa peyarkaḷālēyē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa).	(e. ā. pak. 170).

irumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram -
nilimoḻi kuṟṟiyalukaraccollāka, varumoḻi yakara mutal moḻiyāka variṉ, nilimoḻi īṟṟiluḷḷa ukaram keṭa, avviṭattē varum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramām.
e-ṭu : nāku + yātu = nākiyātu; varaku + yātu = varakiyātu; 
kuraṅku + yātu = kuraṅkiyātu
‘muppē piṇiyē varuttam meṉmiyō, ṭiyāppuṟa vanta iḷivaral nāṉkē’ (to. po. 254 pērā.)	(to.e. 35,410 nac.)
irumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram -
nilimoḻi kuṟṟiyalukarīṟṟatāy varumoḻi nāṟkaṇat toṭum alvaḻip poruḷilum vēṟṟumip poruḷilum puṇarumvaḻi, nilimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ yāvum muṟṟiyalukaramāka ōci nirampum. āyiṉ, valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mutalatāka variṉ, muṟṟiyalukaram ākātu arimāttiri oliyiṟṟāya kuṟṟiyalukaramākavē niṟkum.
	e-ṭu : nāku kaṭitu, varaku kaṭitu, eḵku kaṭitu mutaliya coṟṟoṭarkaḷil nilimoḻiyīṟṟeḻuttu muṟṟiyalukaram āyiṟṟu.
cekkukkaṇi, cukkukkoṭu eṉṟa toṭarkaḷil nili moḻi yīṟṟeḻuttu arimāttiri aḷaviṟṟāya kuṟṟiya lukaram ām.	(to. e. 409, 410 iḷa. uri)
nāku + yātu = nākiyātu eṉṟa toṭaril, nilimoḻiyīṟu muṟṟiyalukaramākiviṭavē, ataṉiṭattil kuṟṟiyalikaram varumiṭattu, muṟṟiyalukaram keṭak kuṟṟiyalikaram vantu, nākiyātu eṉṟu muṭiyum eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.	(e.ā.pak. 166)
perumuracu, tirumuracu - eṉpaṉa muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉa eṉpar nac. 	(to.e.36. nac)
irumoḻip potuveḻuttum, vaṭamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttum -
vaṭamoḻiyuḷ ‘accu’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum uyir patiṉāṟa ṉuḷḷum, iṭiyil niṉṟa ēḻāmuyir mutal nāṉkum īṟṟil niṉṟa iraṇṭumāṉa āṟum oḻintu niṉṟa ā ā i ī u ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉṉum pattum,
‘al’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum mey muppattēḻaṉuḷḷum, ka ca ṭa ta pa eṉṉum  aintaṉ varukkattuḷ iṭiyil urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum collappaṭṭu niṟkum mūṉṟum oḻinta ka ṅa ca ña ṭa ṇa ta na pa ma eṉṉum pattum, ya ra la va eṉṉum nāṉkum, ḷavvumāṉa irupattintum tamiḻ moḻikkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvām.
iviyaṉṟi, mēl uyiril oḻinta āṟum, aintu varkkaṅ kaḷilum iṭikaḷil oḻinta patiṉintum, muppatām mey mutalāṉa eṭṭaṉuḷ ḷakaram oḻinta ēḻumāṉa irupat teṭṭum vaṭamoḻikkē uriyavāyt tamiḻmoḻikku varuṅkāl tamakkēṟṟa potuveḻuttukkaḷākat tirintu varum.	(naṉ. 145 mayili.)
iruviḷa eṉṟa peyar -
iruviḷa eṉpatu akara īṟṟup peyarkaḷil oṉṟu. iruviḷa eṉpatu ōli, vēṇāṭṭakattu ōrūr, karuvūriṉakattu orucēriyum eṉpa.
ippeyar vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ, iruviḷakkoṟṟaṉ- eṉa valloṟṟu mikkup puṇarum. iruviḷaviluḷḷa koṟṟaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. iruviḷakkuṟumi eṉpatu iru viḷaviṉatu kuṟumi eṉṉum poruḷatu. avvaḻiyilum, iruviḷak kaṭitu - iruviḷac ciṟitu - iruviḷat tītu -  iruviḷap peritu - eṉa valloṟṟu mikkup puṇarum.	(to.e. 216 nac. uri)
il eṉṉum iṉmic col puṇarcci -
iṉmip poruḷi uṇarttum il eṉpataṉi nili moḻiyākak koṇṭu, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mutal moḻiyākap puṇariṉ, aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟutal- aikāram peṟṟu iyalpākavē puṇartal- ākāramum valleḻuttum peṟutal - cāriyi etuvum peṟātu iyal pākavē muṭital - eṉṟa nāṉku nilikaḷ uḷavām. 
varumāṟu :	il + poruḷ - illip poruḷ, illi poruḷ, illāp poruḷ, il poruḷ - eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(to.e.372nac.)
‘il’ eṉṉum paṇpaṭi niṉṟu varumoḻiyuṭaṉ puṇarvaḻi, aikāramum ākāramum iṭiyē cāriyiyāka varutaliṉ, illip poruḷ - illi poruḷ - illāp poruḷ - eṉpaṉa ‘ilporuḷ’ eṉṉum iyalpu puṇarcci pōl paṇputtokiyē ām.	(naṉ. 233 caṅkara.)
‘il kal’ eṉṟa col muṭivu -
illi kal - eṉṟa viṉimuṟṟut toṭarum, illātatākiya kal- eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum paṇputtokiyum ilkal eṉṟu muṭiyum. viṉimuṟṟut toṭarākiyavaḻi, il kal eṉpataṉ nilimoḻi lakaroṟṟiṉmēl oliyūṉṟip pirittolikka. paṇputtoki ākiyavaḻi, oru tiraṇmiyāka, viṭ ṭicikkātu olikka.	(to. e. 373 iḷa. uri)
illam eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
illam eṉṟa marappeyar, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik- kaṇ, uti - oṭu- cē- vici- eṉṟa marappeyarkaḷip pōla, varumoḻi mutalil vaṉkaṇam variṉ, makaram keṭṭu vaṉkaṇattukku iṉamāṉa melloṟṟu mikkuppuṇarum.
	e-ṭu : illam + kōṭu, tōl = illaṅkōṭu, illan tōl	(to. e. 313 nac.)
‘kalamciti illattuk kāḻkoṇṭu tēṟṟa’	(kali.142)
eṉa attuc cāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu.
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ cāriyi iṉṟi ‘illamoṭu’ (akanā.4) eṉa oṭuurupu ēṟkiyil iyalpākap puṇarum. attuc cāriyi peṟutalē piṟkālattup perumpāṉmi eṉalām.
‘illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppu’ miṭattuppuṇarnili -
	e-ṭu : cāttaṉillikkoṭiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ (poruḷ:cāttaṉ koṭumi ciṟumi  tīmi perumi illātavaṉ) 
ivaṉ kaṇṇil kuruṭaṉ (kaṇ illāmiyāl kuruṭaṉ).
ivaṉ paṇpillāc ciṟiyaṉ (paṇpu illāmiyāl ciṟiyaṉ).
ivi paṇpu uṇarttiṉa.
illi koṭiyaṉ - eṉa mikātu variṉ, atu viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟām.
illāk koṟṟaṉ - eṉa vali mikiṉ, aḵtu īṟu keṭṭa etirmaṟik kuṟippup peyareccamām.
illi eṉṉum kuṟippumuṟṟu, muṉṉar iruntu piṉṉar illāta niliyik kuṟikkum. e-ṭu: vali il kutiri
iṉmi eṉṉum paṇpuppeyar eppoḻutum illā miyi uṇarttum. e-ṭu: kompu il kutiri
īṇṭuk kūṟiya ‘iṉmi’ eṉpatu ‘poruṇmi cuṭṭal’ ākiya uṇmi eṉṉum paṇpuccoṟkuriya etirmaṟic collām.	(to.e.372 ca.pāla.)
ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntā marūumuṭipu -
immarūumuṭipiṟku ilakkaṇa āciriyar viti kūṟār.
arumaruntaṉṉa, nākappaṭṭiṉam, āṟṟūr, cōḻaṉnāṭu, pāṇṭiyaṉnāṭu - mutalāyiṉa muṟiyē arumanta, nāki, āṟi, cōṇāṭu, pāṇṭināṭu - mutalāka vaḻaṅkutal ‘vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tiri’yum ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntā marūumuṭipākum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar āṅkāṅkē kuṟippiṭum ilakkaṇat toṭu poruntā marūumuṭipukaḷ cila varumāṟu:
puḷiyiṉ  kāyiṉip puḷiṅkāy eṉal	(to.e. 130nac.)
ataṉi itaṉi eṉpavaṟṟi atiṉi
itiṉi eṉal 	(to.e. 176 nac.)
uti eṉpataṉi oti eṉal	(to.e.243 nac.)
vēṭakkumariyi vēṭṭuvakkumari eṉal	- to.e. 338 nac.
maṇṇaṅkaṭṭiyi maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi eṉal 	- to.e. 405 nac.
kāṉaṅkōḻiyik kāṉāṅkōḻi eṉal	- to.e. 405 nac.
kallampāṟiyik kallāmpāṟi eṉal	- to.e. 405 nac.
mūvuḻakku eṉpataṉi mūḻakku, mūḻākku eṉal	- to.e. 457 nac.
eḻumā eṉpataṉi ēḻmā eṉal	- to.e. 480 nac.
ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūumuṭipu -
immuṭipiṟku ilakkaṇa āciriyar viti kūṟuvar.
mēl eṉpatu mī eṉa maruviṟṟu (to.e. 250 nac.), kaṇmī- mutaliyaṉa mīkaṇ - mutaliyaṉavāka maruvi vaḻaṅkum (250 nac.), ‘ilmuṉ’ muṉṟil eṉṟākum (to.e. 355 nac.). ‘yāvar’ yār eṉṟum, ‘yātu’ yāvatu eṉṟum ām. 	(to.e. 172 nac.)
ilakkaṇappōli, marūumoḻikaḷ puṇarcci  -
ilmuṉ ‘muṉṟil’ eṉa varum;potui(vi)l ‘potiyil’ eṉa varum. ivvāṟu varum ilakkaṇappōli moḻikaḷum marūumoḻikaḷum nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷuḷ ēṟkum ceyki aṟintu muṭikkappaṭum.	(naṉ. 239 caṅkara.) 
ilakkaṇi -
ilakkaṇi eṉpatu oṉṟi oṉṟākavum, oru poruḷiṉ taṉmiyi maṟaṟoru poruḷiṉ taṉmiyākavum kūṟuvatu.
e-ṭu : 	iyaṟkiyic ceyaṟkiyāk kūṟuvatu; ‘niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpā kummē’ (to. e. 144 nac.) eṉa, iyaṟkit taṉmikku ākkam varuvittal.	(naṉ. 151 irāmā.)
‘ilampaṭu’:puṇarnili muṭipu  -
ilam + paṭu = ilampaṭu. ilam eṉpatu iṉmi eṉṉum peyarpporuṭṭāy niṉṟatu. ilam eṉṉum taṉmip paṉmik kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟuccol vēṟu, itu vēṟu. paṭutal - uṇṭātal, tōṉṟutal, uṟutal, aṭital - mutalākap pala poruḷpaṭum. ilampaṭu (pulavar)- vaṟumip paṭṭa, vaṟumippaṭum (pulavar).
iṉmiyāṉatu uṟṟa - eṉa alvaḻiyāyum,  iṉmiyi  uṟṟa - eṉa vēṟṟumiyāyum ittoṭar poruḷ viriyum.		(to. e. 316 ca.pāla.)
ilampāṭu eṉṟa collamippu -
ilam eṉpatu illāmikkuṟippu uṇarttum uriccol; pāṭu eṉpatu uṇṭātal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum viṉik kuṟippup peyar. ilampāṭu eṉpatu illāmi yuṇṭātal eṉṉum alvaḻip poruḷatu. ilam eṉṟa nilimoḻi pāṭu eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu makarakkēṭum tiripum iṉṟi, ‘ilampāṭu’ eṉṟu iyalpākavē muṭiyum.	(to. e. 316 nac.)

ilēcu -
‘cilvaki eḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākum’nuṟpāvilē miki yākak kāṇappaṭum col ilēcu eṉappaṭum. ilakkaṇa uriyāciriyaṉmār immikic colli vāḷā viṭukkātu, nūl ceyta kālattukkup piṟpaṭṭut tam kālattil vaḻaṅkum ceytikaḷil nūlil kuṟippiṭappaṭāmal viṭupaṭṭavaṟṟi immikiccol peṟappaṭa vippatākak kūṟutal marapu.
e-ṭu :	‘pallavi nutaliya akara iṟupeyar
	 vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal ecca miṉṟē’	(to. e. 174 nac.)
innūṟpāvil ‘eccamiṉṟē’ eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar miki. iḵtu  iṉṟi  ‘vaṟṟoṭu civaṇum’ eṉṟu kūṟiṉum nuṟpā viṉ poruḷ muṟṟum. immikiccollik koṇṭu, “173ām nuṟpāvil iṉcāriyi peṟṟaṉa piṟacāriyi peṟutal koḷka. nilātti, tulātti, makatti eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pallavi nutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mūṉṟām urupu vaṟṟup peṟṟē muṭital koḷka” eṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyar, nuṟpāvil kūṟappaṭāta ceytikaḷik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.
itu pōṉṟa pala ceytikaḷum ilēcu eṉṟa mikic collāl uriyāciriyaṉmārāl koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
‘cīrnili tāṉē ainteḻuttu iṟavātu’	(to.po.353 pērā.)
aci cīrāyavaḻi avi mūṉṟeḻuttiṉ iṟavā eṉpatu ‘tāṉ’ eṉpataṉi ilēcuppaṭuttik koḷvatāl koḷḷap paṭum - eṉṟu pērāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
ivvaṇi, āyiṭi:  uṭampaṭumey peṟāmi -
i + aṇi = ivvaṇi eṉa iṭiyē vakaramey vantu muṭita laṉṟi,  i + aṇi  i + y + aṇi = iyyaṇi eṉa yakara uṭam paṭumey peṟṟu muṭiyātu. cuṭṭiṭiccol ceyyuḷil nīṇṭavaḻi, ā + iṭi eṉa iṭiyē yakaramey vantu muṭita laṉṟi, ā + iṭi ḻū ā + v+ iṭi = āviṭi eṉa vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭiyātu.	(naṉ. 162 mayili. uri)
iṟantakāla iṭinili -
ka ṭa ta ṟa - eṉṉum nāṉku meykaḷum ‘iṉ’ṉum ākiya aintum aimpāl mūviṭattum iṟantakālam kāṭṭum terinili viṉimuṟṟup pakupataṅkaḷil iṭinilikaḷām. ivaṟṟuḷ, kakaram mutaṉili eḻuttāyē varum; takaram mutaṉili eḻuttākāmal varum; ēṉiya ṭakaramum ṟakaramum mutaṉili eḻuttāyum ataṟku iṉamāyum varum.
	e-ṭu : nakkāṉ; urittāṉ; viṭṭāṉ, uṇṭāṉ; uṟṟāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ; uṟaṅkiṉāṉ.
uriyiṟ kōṭalāl, nakkilaṉ  - urittilaṉ - uṇṭilaṉ - niṉṟilaṉ - uṟaṅkilaṉ eṉa etirmaṟikkaṇ ivviṭi nilikaḷ etirmaṟiyik kāṭṭum ‘il’ iṭiniliyoṭu varumāṟu kāṇka.
eñciyatu, pōyatu. pōya - eṉa yakaramum, pōyaṉa - eṉa aṉṉum, pōṉāṉ, pōṉatu - eṉa ṉakaramum ciṟupāṉmi iṟanta kālam kāṭṭum.	(i. vi. eḻut. 47)
eñciyatu tappiyatu nīṅkiyāṉ - eṉa ikaratti aṭutta yakara oṟṟum (eñcu + i + y + a + tu; tappu +i + y + a+tu; nīṅku +i +y + āṉ), pōyatu pōya - eṉa yakara oṟṟum (pō+y+a), pōṉāṉ pōṉatu - eṉa ṉakara oṟṟum (pō +ṉ + āṉ;pō + ṉ + a + tu)ciṟupāṉmi iṟanta kālam kāṭṭum.	(naṉ. 142 irāmā.)
siṉ eṉṟa iṟantakāla iṭiniliyatu vikāramē ikaramum ṉakaramum eṉpar civañā. (142)]
iṟutic ciṉi keṭal, nīṭal -
tolkāppiyaṉār oru colliṉ mutaleḻuttu allāta eḻuttukkaḷic ciṉi eṉavum, mutaleḻuttiṉi mutal - mutaṉili - eṉavum kuṟippiṭum iyalpiṉar. mutaleḻuttu neṭilāyiṉ, atu kuṟukumpōtu ‘ciṉikeṭal’ eṉavum, mutaleḻuttuk kuṟilāyiṉ atu nīḷumpōtu ‘ciṉi nīṭal’ eṉavum kūṟutalumuṇṭu.
iṟuticciṉi keṭalāvatu īṟṟeḻuttākiya neṭiliṉ oru kūṟākiya oru māttiri keṭa atu kuṟilātal. nilā ḻū nila.
kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ nīṭṭam neṭil. aḵtu iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṟṟu. ataṉ cempāti oru māttiri keṭṭu atu kuṟilātalic ‘ciṉi keṭal’ eṉṟār. āṇṭik koṇ ṭāṉ, īṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, ūṇṭik koṇṭāṉ: ivi cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiyavi.	(to. e. 234, 159 nac.)
iṟutippōli vēṟu cila -
iṟutippōliyi muṉ coṉṉa nilitaṭumāṟṟattāl, curumpu - curumpar, arumpu - arumpar (kuṟṟukarattiṟku ‘ar’ pōli), cāmpal - cāmpar, pantal - pantar, kuṭal - kuṭar (lakarattiṟku rakaram pōli), matil - matiḷ (lakarattiṟku ḷakaram pōli) mutaliyaṉavum koḷka.	(naṉ. 122 irāmā.)
‘iṟutimey’ eṉa vīracōḻiyam kuṟippatu -
18 meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkum 18 uyirmey varici uṇṭātal veḷippaṭi. avvuyirmey variciyil avvam mey iṟutiyil koḷḷappaṭum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, ka kā ki kī ku kū ke kē ki ko kō ku k - eṉak kāṇka. ēṉiya meykaḷum ivvāṟē avvav vuyirmey variciyil īṟṟil koḷḷappaṭum. uyirmey variciyil mey iṟutiyil niṟṟal paṟṟi ‘iṟutimey’ eṉappaṭṭatu.	(vī.cō. cantip.2)
iṉ iṭinili varumāṟu -
iṉiṭinili eñciyatu (eñcu + i(ṉ) + a + tu) eṉak kaṭik kuṟintum, pōṉatu (pō + (i)ṉ + a + tu) eṉa mutal kuṟintum varum.	(naṉ. 142 civañā.)
iṉurupu iṉcāriyi peṟāmi -
iṉ eṉṉum aintaṉurupu iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal iṉṉōcittaṉṟu eṉṟu karutippōlum, tolkāppiya ṉār iṉurupu iṉcāriyi peṟātu eṉṟār. avar karuttuppaṭi, ūriṉ nīṅkiṉāṉ - eṉṟu kūṟuvatē muṟi; ūriṉiṉ nīṅkiṉāṉ eṉal piḻi.
āyiṉ, tolkāppiṉārkkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattē ulakavaḻakkil ‘pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ’ eṉpatu pōlavum, ceyyuḷvaḻakkil ‘kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavi’ (akanā.86), ‘akaṭucērpu porunti aḷaviṉiṟ ṟiriyātu’ (malipaṭu.33) eṉavum aruki varuvaṉavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.		(to.e. 131 nac. uri)
iṉurupu, iṉcāriyi : vēṟupāṭu -
iṉ eṉpatu cāriyi āyiṉaviṭattu yātāṉumōr urupu ēṟkum. aḵtu urupāṉaviṭattup piṟitōr urupi ēlātu. itu tammuḷ vēṟṟumi. e-ṭu : viḷaviṉi, viḷaviṉāṉ, viḷaviṟku, viḷaviṉatu, viḷaviṉkaṇ - eṉa iṉcāriyiyiṉ piṉṉar (aintaṉurupu nīṅkalāṉa piṟa) urupukaḷ vantavāṟu.
ūriṉ nīṅkiṉāṉ - eṉa iṉ urupāyavaḻip piṟitōr urupiṉi ēlāmi kāṇka.	(to.e. 119 nac.)
iṉcāriyi uḷvaḻi aiurupu niliyāmi -
peyarccol iṉcāriyi peṟṟaviṭattu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi yurupu tavaṟātu varutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tol. kūṟum. (to.e.157 nac.) āyiṉ tolkāppiyattilēyē ‘cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟu’ (1) (marapiṉiyuṭiya mūṉṟu), ‘āyiru tiṇiyiṉ icikkumaṉa’ (co.1) (tiṇiyiṉiyum icikkum) eṉa, iṉcāriyi peṟṟu iraṇṭaṉurupu viriyā malēyē coṟṟoṭar amintiruttalik kāṇalām.

‘maṟaṅkaṭinta aruṅkaṟpiṉ
 cilcolliṉ palkūntal... tuṇit tuṇiviyar’	(puṟanā.166)
kaṟpiṉiyum colliṉiyum kūntaliyumuṭiya tuṇivi yar - eṉa ilakkiyattilum iṉcāriyi vantu aiurupu viriyāta poruṭ puṇarcciyiyum kāṇkiṟōm.	(to. e. 157nac. uri)
iṉcāriyi varum iṭaṅkaḷ  -
a) urupu puṇarccikkaṇ
1.	a, ā, u, ū, ē, oḷa - eṉṟa āṟu īṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupēṟku miṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟum.
e-ṭu	: viḷaviṉi, palāviṉi, kaṭuviṉi, taḻūviṉi, cēviṉi, veḷaviṉi,	(to.e.173 nac.)
2. 	ñ, n - īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟum. varumāṟu : uriñiṉi, veriniṉi	        (182)
3.	makarīṟṟup peyarkaḷ cila urupēṟkumiṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟum. e-ṭu : urumiṉi, tirumiṉi	                    (186)
4.	tev eṉṟa uriccol paṭuttalōciyāṉ peyarāki urupēṟku miṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟum. varumāṟu : tev viṉi, tevviṉoṭu; tevviṟku..... tevviṉkaṇ    (184)
5.	aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ - eṉṟa ṉakara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupēṟku miṭattu iṉcāriyiyum peṟum. varumāṟu : aḻaṉiṉi, puḻaṉiṉi   (193)
6.	oṟṟu iraṭṭum neṭiltoṭar uyirt toṭar nīṅkalāṉa piṟa toṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupēṟku miṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟum. e-ṭu : nākiṉi, nākiṉoṭu; varakiṉi, varakiṉoṭu (eḵkiṉi, paṭṭiṉi, pañciṉi, cālpiṉi)        (195)
iviyellām aintaṉurupu ēṟkumiṭattē iṉcāriyi peṟa māṭṭā.	 (131)
ā) poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ
1.	kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ kuṟi eṉṉum col varumiṭattu iṭiyē iṉcāriyi varum. e-ṭu : uḻakkiṉ kuṟi, āḻākkiṉ kuṟi, kaḻañciṉ kuṟi
ivi ummittokip poruḷaṉa. uḻakkum ataṉiṉ kuṟiyum mutalākap poruḷ koḷka. uḻakkiṟkuṟi, āḻākkiṟkuṟi, kaḻañciṟkuṟi: ivi āṟām vēṟ ṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci	(to.e.167 nac.)
2.	paṉi eṉṟa aḷavuppeyar muṉṉum, kā eṉṟa niṟip peyar muṉṉum kuṟi eṉṟa col vantu ummit tokippaṭap puṇarumiṭattu iṉcāriyi varum. varumāṟu : paṉiyiṉ kuṟi, kāviṉ kuṟi	        (169)
3.	maka eṉṟa col makaviṉki eṉṟāṟpōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(218)
4.	paṉi eṉṟa col paṉiyiṟceṉṟāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(241)
5.	vaḷi eṉṟa col vaḷiyiṟ ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(242)
6.	āṭū eṉṟa col āṭūviṉki eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉ cāriyi peṟum. 	(271)
7.	makaṭū eṉṟa col makaṭūviṉki eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉ cāriyi peṟum.	(271)
8.	cē eṉṟa peṟṟattiṉ peyar cēviṉ vāl eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(271)
9.	maḻi eṉṟa col maḻiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(287)
10.	veyil eṉṟa col veyiliṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(377)
11.	iruḷ eṉṟa col iruḷiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(402)
12.	vaṇṭu eṉṟa col vaṇṭiṉkāl eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(420)
13.	peṇṭu eṉṟa col peṇṭiṉki eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟum.	(420)
14.	pattu nilimoḻiyāka, oṉṟu - mūṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟāṉa eṇkaḷ - ivi varumoḻiyākappuṇarumvaḻi, pati ṉoṉṟu - patiṉmūṉṟu - patiṉāṉku - patiṉintu - patiṉāṟu - patiṉēḻ- patiṉeṭṭu- eṉa iṉcāriyi iṭiyē peṟum.	(433)
15.	pattu āyirattoṭu patiṉāyiram eṉavum, oṉpatu āyirat tōṭu oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉavum puṇarum. 	(435, 470)
16.	pattu nilimoḻiyāka, niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varumoḻi yāka variṉum, oṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka avi variṉum iṭiyē iṉcāriyi varum. 
	e.ṭu :	patiṉkalam, patiṉkaḻañcu; oṉpatiṉkalam, oṉpatiṉ kaḻañcu	(436, 459)
17.	orupaḵtu, irupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa āyirattoṭum niṟip peyar aḷavuppeyarkaḷoṭum puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyē iṉcāriyi varum.
varumāṟu :	orupatiṉāyiram, irupatiṉāyiram, orupatiṉ kaḻañcu, orupatiṉmaṇṭi, irupatiṉ kaḻañcu, irupatiṉ maṇṭi	(476, 477)
	18.	ā, mā- eṉṟa peyarkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattu varum iṉcāriyiyiṉ ikaram keṭutalumuṇṭu.
varumāṟu : āviṉi, āṉi; māviṉi, māṉi	 (120)
patiṉkalam - patiṟṟukkalam eṉṟāṟpōla, iṉcāriyi ‘iṟṟu’ eṉat tiritalum koḷka.	(121 nac. uri)
‘iṉtokuti mayaṅkiyal moḻi’ -
cevikku iṉitākac coṟṟiraḷiṭattu nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumāy oṭṭi niṉṟāṟpōla iṇintu niṉṟum poruḷuṇarttātu, pirintu niṉṟu piṉṉarcceṉṟu oṭṭip poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷ.
itu pā eṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻa, poruḷoṭṭāmal cāṉṟōr coṟkaḷic cērttaliṉ nikaḻvatu.
e-ṭu : ‘irumputirit taṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ
 paralaval aṭiya irali teṟippa’	(akanā. 4)
‘maruppiṉ irali’ eṉap poruḷ poruttamuṟa oṭṭa vēṇṭiyatu, ‘maruppiṟ paral’ eṉat taḻāat toṭarāy īṟu tirintu puṇarntatu.
‘karuṅkāl ōmik kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉi’ 	(akanā. 3)
‘ōmicciṉi’ eṉa oṭṭiniṉṟu poruḷuṇartta vēṇṭiya toṭarukku iṭiyē ‘kāṇpiṉperu’ eṉpaṉa iṉṉō cikku vantu, ‘ōmik kāṇpiṉ’ eṉat taḻāat toṭarāy nilimoḻi varumoḻikkuriya puṇarcci peṟṟu valloṟṟu mikkup puṇarntatu.
‘teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi aruḷāl’	(cilap 3 : 1)
‘teyva vari’ eṉa nilimoḻi varumoḻiyāyp puṇara vēṇṭiyatu, ‘teyvamāl’ eṉat taḻāat toṭarāy, nilimoḻi varumoḻikkuriya puṇarcci peṟṟu makarīṟu kuṉṟip puṇarntatu.
ivvāṟu meypiṟitātal - mikutal - kuṉṟal - eṉṟa mūvakit tiripum peṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaṇ puṇarntu varum taḻāat toṭarkaḷē ‘iṉtokuti mayaṅkiyal moḻikaḷ’ ām.	(to. e. 111 nac. uri)
iṉṟi eṉṟa viṉiyeccam īṟu tirital -
iṉṟi eṉṟa viṉiyeccam ‘viruntiṉṟik kaḻinta pakal’ eṉṟāṟ pōla, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. aḵtu īṟu tirintu ‘iṉṟu’ eṉa viṉimuṟṟup pōla amintavaḻi, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam mikātu iṉṉōci payattaliṉ ceyyuḷil payila vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.
e-ṭu : ‘uppiṉṟu puṟkiuṇkamā koṟkiyōṉē’
‘vaḻuviṉṟu niliiya iyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum’	(to.po. 111. nac.)
‘poṟiyiṉṟu perukiya paruvaraṟ kaṇṇum’ 	(to.po. 151 nac.)
‘kāyviṉṟu avaṉvayiṉ poruttaṟ kaṇṇum’	(to.po. 151 nac.)
‘tāviṉṟu uriya tattam kūṟṟē’	(to.po. 241 nac.)
eṉat tolkāppiyattil iṉṟi eṉpatu ‘iṉṟu’ eṉṟu ceyyuḷiṉpam karuti vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.	(to. e. 237 nac.)
iṉṟi eṉṟa colliluḷḷa iraṇṭu ikaram cevikku iṉimi yāka illātatāl, oṉṟu ukaramāka māṟiyirukkavēṇṭum. eṉavē, aṉṟi eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa ikaram ukaramākat tiriya vēṇṭuvatilli. at tiripu caṟṟup piṟkālattatu eṉpa. atuvaṉṟi eṉpatu atāaṉṟu eṉat tirintatō eṉpatu nōkkattakkatu.
iṉṉici aḷapeṭi -
poruḷ vēṟupāṭō yāppamitiyō karutātu, kuṟ ṟeḻuttu varavēṇṭiya iṭattil kuṟil neṭilāki aḷa peṭuttirukkum iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa avvaki aḷapeṭiyi, atu ceyyuḷici niṟikka vantataṉmiyiṉ, iṉṉōci oṉṟaṟkākavē aḵtu amintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttāṉ, iṉṉici aḷapeṭi eṉpa.
pāṭṭukkaḷil iṉṉōci amintiruntalē vēṇṭum ātalāṉum, iṉṉōci illāviṭattu ōci citintatu eṉavē paṭum ātalāṉum, iṉṉici aḷapeṭi eṉṟu vēṟu peyariṭṭu oru cār uyiraḷapeṭiyip pakuttuk kōṭal vēṇṭuvatiṉṟu eṉpar oru cārār.
‘keṭuppatum... eṭuppatum ellām maḻi’ (kuṟaḷ 15) eṉiṉum ceyyuḷōci keṭātu amiyumāyiṉum, ‘keṭuppatuum... eṭuppatuum ellām maḻi’ eṉak kuṟil neṭilāki aḷapeṭut tataṉāl vēṟu payaṉiṉṟi iṉiya ōciyoṉṟē payaṉāta liṉ, ittakiyaṉa iṉṉiciyaḷapeṭi eṉṟē peyar peṟal vēṇṭum eṉpatu cilar karuttu. cilar ivaṟṟik kuṟṟeḻuttaḷapeṭiyil aṭakkuvar.	(e. ā. pak. 42)  (naṉ.91 uri)
iṉṉiṉ ikaram keṭutal -
iṉcāriyiyiṉ ikaram, ā eṉṟa colliyaṭuttu ac cāriyi varumiṭattuk keṭutalumuṇṭu. mā eṉṟa coṟkum itaṉik koḷpa. 
va-ṟu :	ā+iṉ+ai - āviṉi, āṉi; mā+iṉ+ai - māviṉi, māṉi; ā+iṉ+kōṭu - āviṉ kōṭu, āṉ kōṭu; mā+iṉ+kōṭu - māviṉ kōṭu, māṉkōṭu
nilimoḻiyīṟṟu neṭilukkumuṉ kuṟili mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷ varumiṭattup puṇarccikkaṇ akkuṟil keṭutalu muṇṭu ātaliṉ, ā eṉṟa colliṉ muṉ vanta iṉcāriyi yiṉ ikaram keṭṭatu. nīir - nīr, mūuḻakku = mūḻakku, pō + iṉ +āṉ= pōṉāṉ - eṉap piṟa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ muṉṉum vanta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ keṭutalik kāṇalām. (e. ā. pak. 99)
iṉa eḻuttu  -
a ā, i ī ai, u ū oḷa, e ē, oō- ivi tammuḷ iṉamām. ka ṅa, ca ña, ṭa ṇa, ta na, pa ma, ṟa ṉa - eṉa valliṉam melliṉam tammuḷ iṉamām. iṭiyiṉattiṟku iṉamiṉṟu. iṅṅaṉam iṉam aṭittal aḷapeṭiyilum puṇarcciyilum payaṉpaṭum.	(naṉ. 71)
iṉoṟṟu mikutal -
tolkāppiyaṉār ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa - kkaḷukku ṅa ña ṇa na ma 
ṉa-kkaḷi yāṇṭum iṉaveḻuttu eṉṟō iṉoṟṟu eṉṟō kūṟiṉarallar. muṉṉar varum valleḻuttu miku taliyē iṉoṟṟumikutal eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
e-ṭu :	nūṟu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu iṉoṟṟu mikum eṉṟu kūṟiyatu pōlvaṉa. varumāṟu : nūṟu + oṉṟu ḻū nūṟṟu + oṉṟu = nūṟṟoṉṟu 	(to.e.472nac.) (e.ā.pak.167)
iṉam eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇam  -
mārpu mutaliya tāṉam, itaḻacivumutaliya muyaṟci, māttiri eṉṟa aḷavu, poruḷ, olivaṭivu, varivaṭivu - ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum palavum ottu varutal iṉam aṭit taṟkuk kāraṇam. poruḷāvatu pālaṉ viruttaṉ āṉāṟ pōlak kuṟiyataṉ vikāramē neṭil ātaliṉ, iraṇṭaṟkum poruḷ oṉṟu eṉṟu mutalnūlāl niyamikkappaṭṭa poruḷ.	(naṉ. 72 caṅ.)
iṉam ottal -
 iṉam ottalāvatu piṟappum  puṇarcciyum ōciyum vaṭivum ottal.
aā, iī, uū, eē, oō eṉṟa uyirkaḷum, kaṅa,caña, ṭaṇa, tana, pama, ṟaṉa - eṉṟa meykaḷum iṉam ottaṉavām.
aā- eḻuvāyttoṭarp puṇarcciyum, piṟappum, ōciyum, vaṭivum okkum. iīai - vaṭivu ovvā; ēṉiya okkum.
uūoḷa - uū vaṭivu okkum. oḷa vaṭivu ovvātu; ēṉiya okkum.
eē, oo - vaṭivum ēṉiyavum perumpālum okkum.
kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, pama, ṟaṉa -  piṟappu okkum; ēṉiya perum pālum ovvā. puṇarccikkaṇ valliṉattukku iṉamel leḻuttāyp puṇarcci okkum. (to.e.41 nac. uri)
iṉi eṉṟa iṭiccol puṇarcci -
iṉi eṉpatu ippoḻutu eṉṉum kālatti uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccol. aḵtu iṉikkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōl, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.		(to. e. 236 nac.)
iṉi + iṉi - iṉṉiṉi eṉṟām. aḵtu iṉṉiṉik koṇ ṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkup puṇarum.	
‘iṉi’ peyarccol niliyatu.	(246 nac. uri)
ī
īkāra īṟṟu alvaḻip puṇarcci -
īkārīṟṟup peyar, alvaḻikkaṇ eḻuvāyttoṭarāyiṉ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikkum, meṉ kaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum variṉ iyalpākavum, uyirkkaṇam variṉ uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	 īk kaṭitu, ī naṉṟu, ī yātu, ī yaṭintatu (to. e. 249 nac.)
(ikkālattu, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum, eḻuvāyt toṭari iyalpupuṇarcciyākavē koḷḷum vaḻakkam mikkuḷatu.)
īkāra īṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcciyuḷ iyalpāvaṉa -
alvaḻippuṇarccikkaṇ nī eṉṟa muṉṉili orumippeya rum,pī eṉṟa iṭakkarppeyarum, mēliṭatti uṇarttum mī eṉṟa peyarum iyalpākap puṇarum. ‘mī’ valimeli mikutalu muṇṭu.	(naṉ. 178)
varumāṟu :	nī kuṟiyi, pī kuṟitu, mīkaṇ; mīkkaṇ, mīntōl	(to. e. 250, 251 nac.)
‘mī’ melleḻuttu mikutal tolkāppiyaṉār kālat tukkup piṟpaṭṭatu.
īkāra īṟṟuc ciṟappu vitikaḷ  -
ā - eṉṟa col muṉ varum pī eṉṟa īkāra īṟṟuc col, īkāram kuṟukip pakaram mikku āppi - eṉap puṇarum. ip puṇarmoḻitāṉ nilimoḻiyāka niṟpa, nāṟkaṇam variṉum, alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	āppi +kuḷirum, naṉṟu valitu,  aritu = āppi kuḷirum, āppi naṉṟu, āppi valitu, āppi yaritu
pī nī mī - eṉpaṉa alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum. e-ṭu : pīkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu; nī kuṟiyi, ciṟiyi, tīyi, periyi; mīkaṇ, cevi, tali puṟam.
mī eṉṟa collukku valleḻuttu mikutalum melleḻuttu mikutalumākiya puṇarcci orōvaḻi uṇṭu. e-ṭu : mīkkūṟṟu, mīntōl	(naṉ. 178)
īkārīṟṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci -
īkārīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikum; piṟa kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	īkkāl, īcciṟi, īttali, īppuṟam; īmāṭci, īvaṉmi, īyāṭṭam,
vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ nī eṉṟa muṉṉilip peyar niṉ eṉat tirintu varumoḻiyōṭu iyalpākap puṇarum. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcciyil niṉ eṉpatu (vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattu) niṟ eṉat tirintu puṇarum. uyirkkaṇam variṉ ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭum.
e-ṭu :	niṉkaṭami, niṉnā(ṉā)ṭu, niṉyāḻ, niṉṉaḻaku, niṟ puṟaṅkāppa	       (to. e. 252, 253 nac.)
īm  kam urum:  puṇarumāṟu -
immūṉṟu collum alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum, mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar pōla, yakaram allāta mey varumoḻi mutalil variṉ, ukarac cāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum; īmum kammum vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukarac cāriyiyē aṉṟi akarac cāriyiyum peṟum.
e-ṭu :	īmuk kaṭitu, kammuk kaṭitu, urumuk kaṭitu - alvaḻi; īmukkaṭumi, kammukkaṭumi, urumukkaṭumi - vēṟṟumi; īmakkuṭam, kammakkuṭam  - vēṟṟumi	(naṉ. 223)
‘īm’ puṇarumāṟu -
īm - cuṭukāṭu. ‘īm’ nilimoḻiyāka, alvaḻip puṇarcci yilum vēṟṟumiyiṉ kuṇavēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum variṉ ukara māttiram peṟṟum puṇarum. vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccik kaṇ ukaram keṭa iṭiyē ammuccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum. iruvaḻiyiṉum uyirkkaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	īmuk kaṭitu, īmu māṇṭatu, īmu valitu - alvaḻi; īmuk kaṭumi, īmu māṭci, īmu vaṉmi - vēṟṟumi yil kuṇavēṟṟumip puṇarcci; īmak kuṭam, īma neruppu, īma viṟaku - poruṭpuṇarcci; īmaṭintatu, ī maṭivu - iruvaḻiyilum uyirkkaṇam. 	(to. e. 328, 329 nac.)
īraḷapu  icittal -
tamiḻil uyirneṭṭeḻuttum uyirmeyneṭṭeḻuttum ovvoṉṟum iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṟṟāka olikkum.
(to. e. 4, 10 nac.)
‘īriyal maruṅkiṉum icimi tōṉṟum’ poruḷ -
piṟarellām ‘īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōtiṉar. 
muppāṟ puḷḷiyākiya āyta eḻuttiṉatu icimi (eḻut tāka icikkum taṉmi) uyiriṉatu maruṅkākavum oṟṟiṉatu maruṅkākavum tōṉṟi varum.
īr iyal - uyiriyalpum oṟṟiyalpum. uyiriyalpāvatu, icittuc ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ alaku peṟṟu varutal; oṟṟiyal pāvatu, alaku peṟātu acikku  uṟuppāki varutal.
e-ṭu:	‘aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka aḵtuṭampu’ 	(kuṟaḷ 943)
	‘tōṉṟiṉ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka aḵtilār’ 	(kuṟaḷ. 236)
muṟiyē ivvīriṭattum āytam uyiriyal maruṅkāy alaku peṟṟum, oṟṟiyal maruṅkāy alaku peṟātu acikku uṟuppāyum niṉṟavāṟu.	(to. e. 39 ca.pāla.)
īreḻuttumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
ku cu tu pu - eṉṟa nāṉkaṉiyum īṟṟeḻuttākavum, neṭṭeḻuttu oṉṟi mutaleḻuttākavum koṇṭa īreḻuttumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum,
	e-ṭu :	 nākukāl, nākuñāṟci, nākuvāl, nākacivu - vēṟṟumi; nāku kaṭitu, nāku naṉṟu, nāku valitu, nākaritu - alvaḻi	(to. e. 412, 425 nac.)
āṟu eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar, niṟi aḷavup peyarkaḷākiya vaṉkaṇam variṉ, alvaḻikkaṇ mutal kuṟuki iyalpākap puṇarum (440); uyir mutal moḻi variṉ, neṭil kuṟukātu iyalpākap puṇarum. va-ṟu : aṟukalam, aṟu kaḻañcu; āṟakal, āṟuḻakku. 	(to.e. 449, 458 nac.)
avveṇṇuppeyar āyiram varumoḻiyāyiṉ iyalpā yum neṭil kuṟukiyum puṇarum. varumāṟu : āṟāyiram, aṟāyiram		(to.e. 469 nac.)
yātu eṉṉum viṉāppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟṟu vēṟ ṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ puṇarum.
e-ṭu : yātaṉ kōṭu	(to.e. 422 nac.)
īreḻuttorumoḻiyām kuṟṟukara īṟṟuppeyar ciṟu pāṉmi ammuccāriyi peṟṟu, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ puṇarum. e-ṭu : ēṟaṅkōḷ (cīvaka. 489)	(to.e. 417 nac.)
ciṟupāṉmi urupupuṇarccikkaṇ yāṭṭiṉi eṉa iṉcāriyi peṟuvatupōlap poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum yāṭṭiṉ kāl eṉṟāṟ pōla iṉcāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu. 	(to.e. 412 nac.)
ṭu ṟu - eṉpaṉavaṟṟi īṟṟiluṭiya īreḻuttumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum iṉoṟṟu iṭiyē mika, varumoḻikkaṇ vanta valleḻuttu iṭiyē mikum. poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ iyalpukaṇam variṉum iṉoṟṟu iṭiyē mikum.
e-ṭu :	yāṭṭi, yāṭṭoṭu; pāṟṟi, pāṟṟoṭu; (to. e. 196 nac.) yāṭṭukkāl, yāṭṭuniṇam, yāṭṭuvāl, yāṭṭataḷ; ēṟṟukkāl, ēṟṟuniṇam, ēṟṟuvāl, ēṟṟataḷ	(to.e. 411)
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ yāṭṭiṉi, pāṟṟiṉi - eṉṟāṟpōla iṉcāriyi peṟutalum koḷka.	(to.e. 197)
īrēval -
cey eṉṟa ēvalviṉiyi aṭuttu vi-pi- eṉṟa vikutikaḷuḷ oṉṟu varumāyiṉ, ceyvi eṉṉum ōrēvalmēl ōrēval;  ivvikuti mīṇṭum iyiyiṉ ceyvippi eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu īrēval nikaḻum.
e-ṭu :: naṭa - naṭappi - naṭappippi; vā - varuvi- varuvippi 
cey eṉṉum ēvalviṉikkaṇ, ēval viṉimutal oṉṟu; iyaṟṟum viṉimutal oṉṟu. ceyvi eṉpataṉkaṇ, ēval viṉimutal iraṇṭu; iyaṟṟum viṉimutal iraṇṭu. ceyvippi eṉpataṉkaṇ, ēval viṉimutal mūṉṟu; iyaṟṟum viṉimutal mūṉṟu.
naṭa - naṭattu - naṭattuvi - naṭattuvippi; varuntu - varuttu - varuttuvi- varuttuvippi - eṉavum varum.	(naṉ. pata. 11 irāmā.)
īroṟṟu uṭaṉili  -
coṟkaḷil yaraḻa eṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷi aṭuttuk kacatana pamañaṅa- eṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷum melleḻuttukkaḷum vara, ivvāṟu vevvēṟu oṟṟukkaḷ iraṇṭu iṇintu varunili īroṟ ṟuṭaṉiliyām.
e-ṭu :	vēykka, vāycci, vāyttal, vāyppu; kāyṅkaṉi, tēyñcatu, kāyntaṉam, kāympuṟam; pīrkku, nērṅkal; vāḻkki, vāḻntaṉam
ceyyuṭkaṇ lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ tirinta ṉakaraṇakaraṅkaḷi aṭutta makaram īroṟṟāy vantu māttiri kuṟukum.
varumāṟu :	pōlum ḻū pōlm ḻū pōṉm; maruḷum ḻū maruḷm ḻū maruṇm.	(to. e. 29, 48, 51 nac.)
maruṇm pōlvaṉa tolkāppiyattiṟkup piṟkālattaṉa.
īroṟṟuṭaṉili orumoḻiyilum irumoḻiyilum varum; colliṉ iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum varum eṉpatu peṟṟām.
īroṟṟuṭaṉili, mūvoṟṟuṭaṉili paṟṟi vīracōḻiyam kuṟippaṉa -
īroṟṟuṭaṉili, mūvoṟṟuṭaṉili eṉpaṉa moḻi yiṭit tōṉṟum iṭinili meymmayakkaṅkaḷ. vantār eṉpuḻi, tā eṉṟa eḻutti t-ā eṉṟu pirikkumiṭattē, vantār eṉa īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyām. cārntār eṉpuḻi, tā eṉṟa eḻutti avvāṟu pirikkumiṭattē, cārntār eṉa mūvoṟṟuṭa ṉiliyām.	(vī. cō. cantip. 4)
īroṟṟuttoṭar iṭittoṭar ākāmi -
yaraḻa - eṉṉum mūṉṟu oṟṟukkaḷiyum aṭuttuk kacatapaṅa ñanama-kkaḷ īroṟṟāka vara, avaṟṟi aṭuttuk kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu amiyum. appoḻutu kuṟṟiyalukarattukku muṉṉē vanta eḻuttu īroṟṟut toṭaril valleḻuttākavō melleḻuttākavō irukkum. aṭutta eḻuttik koṇṭē kuṟṟiyalukarattukkup peyariṭappaṭum ātaliṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaratti aṭutta eḻuttu īroṟṟut toṭaril iṭiyeḻuttāka vārāmiyāl, īroṟṟi iṭiyē koṇṭa kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc col iṭittoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram ākamāṭṭātu.
e-ṭu :	moympu, noympu, moyttu, poyttu; ārkku, īrkku; vāḻttu, vāḻntu.
iraṇṭu oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ toṭarntuniṟkum colliṉ kaṇ, iṭiyiṉa oṟṟu muṉniṉṟāl, mēl iṭiyiṉam toṭarntu nillā; valliṉamum melliṉamumē toṭarntu niṟkum. ātaliṉ collil iṭiyiṉa oṟṟu iruppiṉum, kuṟṟiyalukaratti aṭutta (īṟṟayal) eḻuttu valloṟ ṟākavō melloṟṟākavō iruttaliṉ, avval loṟṟu melloṟṟup peyarālēyē kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟippiṭap peṟum eṉpar nac.	(to. e. 407)
īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi -
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷil īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarattukku muṉṉāka valloṟṟō melloṟṟō vara, ataṟku muṉṉāka iṭiyoṟṟu oṉṟu vara amiyum coṟkaḷ īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭum.  e-ṭu : moympu, eyppu, ārntu, ārttu, vāḻntu, vāḻttu.	(to. e. 407 nac.)
īṟṟayal aci paṟṟik kuṟṟiyalukarattik kaṇakkiṭumāṟu nirampāmi -
atu itu mutaliya muṟṟukaratti nīkkavēṇṭit taṉikkuṟili ṉākiya aciyi viṭuttu,
	taṉineṭṭeḻutti aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - kāṭu; taṉineṭiloṟṟi aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - kāṭṭu;	kuṟṟoṟṟi aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - kaṇṭu; kuṟiliṇiyi aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - paracu; kuṟiliṇi oṟṟi aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - vaṟaṇṭu; kuṟil neṭil aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - palācu; kuṟil neṭil oṟṟi aṭutta kuṟṟiyalukaram - kiṭēccu
eṉa acipaṟṟik kuṟṟiyalukaram varumiṭam ēḻ eṉṟu koḷḷiṉ, piṇṇākku-cuṇṇāmpu - paṭṭāṅku - viḷi yāṭṭu- iṟumpūtu - mutaliya coṟkaḷ ivaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkā. piṇṇākku - cuṇṇāmpu -paṭṭāṅku - viḷiyāṭṭu-eṉpaṉavaṟṟi īṟṟayalaci paṟṟi neṭṭoṟṟiṟutik kuṟṟiya lukaramākavum,iṟumpūtu eṉpataṉi neṭiliṟutik kuṟṟiya lukaramākavum aṭakkik koḷḷiṉum, pōvatu - varuvatu - oṉpatu - mutaliyaṉa ēḻvakiyuḷ oṉṟaṉuḷḷum aṭaṅkāmi yiṉ, aciyikkoṇṭu kuṟṟiyalukaratti  ēḻ vakiyuḷ aṭakkutal nirampāta ilakkaṇamām.	(cū. vi. pak. 29)
īṟṟiṉ varum uyirmeykaḷ -

mēli aṭṭavaṇiyil īṟṟil varum uyirmeykaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭuṭiyavi-141, eṭuttukkāṭṭillātavi-20 āka 161ām.
ekaram enta meyyoṭum īṟṟil vārātu. okaram nakaramey yuṭaṉēyē varum (no). oḷakāram kakara vakara meykaḷuṭaṉē varum(ku,veḷa).
uyirkaḷuḷ oḷakāram nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya patiṉoṉṟum moḻi yiṟutiyil varum.	(to. e. 77 nac.)
īṟṟu uyirmey -
colliṉ īṟṟu uyirmey uyirīṟākavē koḷḷappaṭum. uyirmeyyāvatu meyyoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉu māka olikkum kalappeḻuttu ātaliṉ, ataṉ mutaloli meyyoli, īṟṟoli uyiroliyām. ākavē uyimey moḻimutalil variṉ meyyeḻuttākavum, moḻiyiṟuti yil variṉ uyireḻuttākavum koḷḷappaṭum.
‘pala’ eṉpatu p + a + l + a eṉṟu amitaliṉ, īṟṟuyir mey uyirīṟākavē puṇarccikkaṇ koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.		(to. e. 106 nac.)
īṟṟu nili -
uyir 12, ñ ṇ n m ṉ y r l v ḻ ḷ - eṉṟa mey 11, kuṟṟiya lukaram - eṉṟa 24 eḻuttukkaḷum moḻiyīṟṟil varuvaṉavām. uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aḷapeṭiyil taṉittu īṟāka varum. ā a, ī i, ūu, ēe, ōo - eṉa varumāṟu kāṇka. ekaram aḷapeṭikkaṇ taṉittu īṟātalaṉṟi, meyyoṭu kūṭi īṟāka vārātu. okaram nakarattoṭu kūṭi ‘no’ eṉavum, oḷakāram kakarattoṭum vakarattoṭum kūṭik ku- veḷa- eṉavum īṟām.	(naṉ. 107, 108)
īṟṟuviṉāp puṇarcci -
nilimoḻi īṟāka varum ā ē ō-eṉṉum viṉāviṭic coṟkaḷum, mutalākavarum yā eṉṉum aḵṟiṇippaṉmi viṉāppeyarum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi iyalpām.
	e-ṭu :	uṇkā + koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā = uṇkā koṟṟā....... uṇkē + koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā = uṇkē koṟṟā......... uṇkō + koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā = uṇkō koṟṟā.......... yā + kuṟiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya = yā kuṟiya..............
		yā iyalpu paṟṟi uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. 	(naṉ. 159 mayili.)
‘īṟu āku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyal’ -
neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikaḷ yāṉ yām nām nī tāṉ tām eṉpaṉa. nīir eṉpatum attakiyatē. ivimuṟiyē eṉ em nam niṉ taṉ tam num-eṉṟu neṭumutal kuṟukiniṟkum. ivi nāṉkaṉurupākiya ku eṉpataṉoṭum, āṟaṉurupuka ḷākiya atu a eṉpavaṟṟoṭum puṇarumvaḻi, akarac cāriyi peṟṟu, eṉa ema nama niṉa taṉa tama numa - eṉṟāki, eṉa + ku = eṉakku, eṉa + atu = eṉatu, eṉa + a = eṉava eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum. atu urupiṉ akaram keṭṭu viṭum. eṉava eṉpuḻi vakaramuṭampaṭumey.	(to. e. 161 nac.)
taṉātu - eṉātu - eṉpaṉavum taṉa + atu, eṉa + atu - eṉṟē pirittuk koḷḷappaṭavēṇṭuvaṉavām. nilimoḻi yīṉum varumoḻi mutalum ākiya iraṇṭu akaraṅkaḷ ‘ā’ eṉa oru neṭil āyiṉa. ‘ātu’ urupu aṉṟu. ‘ātu’ neṭu mutal kuṟukum moḻikaḷilēyē kāṇappaṭuvatum nōkkat takkatu.	(e. ā. pak. 96)
īṟu iyal maruṅku -
nilimoḻi īṟṟeḻuttu varumoḻi mutaleḻuttoṭu puṇarntu naṭakkumiṭam. nilimoḻiyīṟu uyirākavō meyyākavō irukkalām; varumoḻi mutalum avvāṟē irukkalām. īṟu uyir meyyāyiṉ uyirākavum, mutal uyirmeyyāyiṉ meyyākavum koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. e. 39, 171 nac.)
īṉ puṇarumāṟu -
īṉ ivviṭam eṉṟu poruḷpeṟum ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccollām. ivviṭic col peyarttaṉmi peṟṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarva tām. varumoḻiyil vaṉkaṇam mutaleḻuttāka variṉ, īṟkoṇṭāṉ-eṉṟāṟpōla ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu puṇarum; īṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟa iyalpu puṇarcciyumuṇṭu.	(to. e. 333 nac.)
u
u -
ukaram tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil aintām uyireḻuttu. iḵtu aṅkāppōṭu itaḻkuvittalāṉ piṟappatu; ūkārattiṟku aḷapeṭiyeḻuttāka varuvatu(ūuṅku); yakara rakara lakara mutal āriyaccoṟkaḷ vaṭacoṟkaḷākat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum pōtu, avaṟṟi iyakkum moḻimutaleḻuttāka varuvatu (uyuttam, urōmam, ulōkam); āriyaccollil val leḻutti yaṭuttu makaram vakaram varuvaṉavaṟṟi vaṭa collākkumiṭattu avvalleḻutti ūrntu, olit tali eḷimiyākkuvatu(pakvam - pakkuvam; patmam-patumam); āriyaccoṟkaḷil moḻimutaṟkaṇ •, ° - varumiṭaṅkaḷil avaṟṟi vaṭacollākkukiyil av veḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṭattē tāṉ vantu olippatu(cutti, °vāmi - cuvāmi); tamiḻccoṟkaḷil iṭiyilum īṟṟilum cāriyiyāka varuvatu (pal-pallu,avaṉ + ku(avaṟku)-avaṉukku); oḷakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarum, ñaṇanamala vaḷaṉa- oṟṟīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkaḷum varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyē cāriyiyāka varuvatu (kuvuk kaṭitu, uriñuk kaṭitu, maṇṇuk kaṭitu, porunuk kaṭitu, tirumuk kaṭitu, colluk kaṭitu, tevvuk kaṭitu, tuḷḷuk kaṭitu, miṉṉuk kaṭitu); cēymi aṇmikku iṭippaṭṭa niliyik kuṟikkum cuṭṭāka varuvatu(uvaṉ, uppakkam); toḻiṟ peyar vikutiyākavum, paṇpuppeyar vikutiyākavum, viṉi yecca vikutiyākavum nikaḻvatu(varavu, celavu; maḻavu, kuḻavu; ceytu(cey + t + u)).
ukkuṟaḷ meyviṭṭōṭal mutaliyaṉa -
nilimoḻiyīṟṟil kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟka varumoḻi mutalil uyir variṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ēṟiya meyyi niṟuttit tāṉ keṭṭuviṭum; varumoḻi mutalil yakaram variṉ, tāṉ ikaramākat tiriyum. muṟṟiyalukaramum nilimoḻiyīṟṟil orōvaḻik keṭum.
e-ṭu :	kāṭu + aritu ḻū kāṭ + aritu = kāṭaritu; kāṭu + yātu ḻū kāṭi +  yātu =  kāṭiyātu: kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu
	katavu + aḻakitu ḻū  katav + aḻakitu = katavaḻakitu: muṟṟiyalukarīṟu.
atu+aṉ+ai ḻū at+aṉ+ ai = ataṉi: ituvumatu.
orōvaḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭātu niṉṟu varumoḻi yōṭu uṭampaṭumey peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	ātu +  umḻū ātu + v +  um = ātuvum (naṉ. 300) - iṭiyē vakara uṭampaṭumey vantatu.	(naṉ. 164)
ukarīṟṟu alvaḻip puṇarcci -
ukarīṟṟup peyar eḻuvāyttoṭarātaṟkaṇ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum variṉ iyalpākavum, uyirkkaṇam variṉ uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum puṇarum.
e-ṭu : kaṭuk kuṟitu, kaṭu naṉṟu, kaṭu valitu, kaṭu varitu		(to. e. 254 nac.)
ukarīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ eḻuvāyttoṭarātaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	atu kuṟitu, atu naṉṟu, atu valitu, atu vaḻakiyatu	(to.e. 257 nac.)
ceyyuḷil	atu + aṉṟu = atāaṉṟu eṉavum, atu + ai + maṟṟu + amma = atimaṟṟamma eṉavum puṇarum.	(to.e. 258 nac.)
varum,mikum, tokum, peṟum, paṭum - mutaliyaṉa aciyi nirapputaṟkāka varūum, mikūum, tokūum, peṟūum, paṭūum -mutalaṉavāka nīṇṭu ukara aḷapeṭi peṟutalu muṇṭu.
‘oḷeṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum’	(to.e.152 nac.)
‘mutti varūum kālam tōṉṟiṉ’	(164)
‘tammiṭi varūum .... nummiṭi varūum’	(191)

‘makkaḷ muṟitokūum maruṅki ṉāṉa’	(350)
‘takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa’	(399)
‘oṟṟunili tiriyātu akkoṭu varūum’	(418)
‘muṟṟiṉ varūum iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē’	(433)
‘niṟiyum aḷavum varūuṅ kāli’	(436)
‘nuṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kāli’	(471)
‘laṉeṉa varūum puḷḷi iṟutimuṉ’	(481)
‘aā veṉa varūum iṟuti’	(to.co. 9 nac.)
‘yāteṉa varūum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi’	(32)

‘iai āyeṉa varūum mūṉṟum’	(225)
‘irīr miṉeṉa varūum mūṉṟum’	(226)
‘ōrāṅku varūum viṉiccol kiḷavi’	(243)
‘vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉāvuṭi viṉiccol’	(246)
‘avaṟṟiṉ varūum eṇṇiṉ iṟutiyum’	(292)
‘uṟutava naṉieṉa varūum mūṉṟum’	(301)
‘pēm nām urumeṉa varūum kiḷavi’	(365)
mutaliyaṉa kāṇka.
īṟṟu ḻakarukaram nīṇṭu aḷapeṭuttu ukarīṟṟuc collā talumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :eḻu - eḻūu, kuḻu - kuḻūu, taḻu - taḻūu, paḻu – paḻūu		to. e. 261 nac.)
ukarīṟṟuc cuṭṭupeyarp puṇarcci -
ukarīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ atu itu utu eṉpaṉa. avi eḻuvāyt toṭarkkaṇ iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :atu kuṟitu, atu naṉṟu, atu valitu (to.e. 257nac.)
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ atu mutaliya cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ iṟuti ukaram keṭṭu aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupukaḷoṭu puṇarum. nāṉkaṉurupiṟkuc cāriyi ṉakaram ṟakaram ākum.
varumāṟu :	atu + ai ḻū atu + aṉ + ai ḻū at + aṉ + ai = ataṉi; atu + ku ḻū atu + aṉ + ku ḻū at + aṟ + ku = ataṟku
ataṉāṉ, ataṉiṉ, ataṉatu, ataṉkaṇ; itaṉi, utaṉi, itaṉāṉ, utaṉāṉ, itaṟku , utaṟku - mutalāka oṭṭip puṇarkka.
vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum atu mutaliya coṟkaḷ īṟṟu ukaram keṭṭu aṉcāriyi peṟṟu nāṟkaṇattoṭum iyalpākap puṇarum,
e-ṭu :ataṉkōṭu, ataṉmayir, ataṉvāl, ataṉaḻaku (to.e. 263 nac.)


ukarīṟṟu vēṟṟumip puṇarcci -
ukarīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikkum, ēṉikkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum .
e-ṭu : kaṭukkāy, kaṭunaṉmi, kaṭuvalimi, kaṭuvarumi		(to. e. 259 nac).
eru, ceru eṉṟa coṟkaḷ ammuccāriyi peṟum. ceru eṉṟa colliṉ cāriyi ammiṉ makaram keṭa, akaram māttiram ceru eṉpataṉoṭum puṇara, vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikkum, ēṉiya kaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :eruvaṅkuḻi, eruvañcēṟu, eruvantātu, eruvampūḻi
eruva ñāṟci, eruva vaṉmi; eruva varumi;
	ceruvakkaḷam, ceruvaccēṉi, ceruvattāṉi ceruvappaṟi; ceruva naṉmi, ceruva vaṉmi, ceruva veḻucci.	(to.e. 260 nac.)
ciṟupāṉmi erukkuḻi, cerukkaḷam. eṉac cāriyi peṟātu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, eruṅkuḻi - eṉa mel leḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.
ciṟupāṉmi, urupupuṇarccikkup payaṉpaṭum iṉcāriyi vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkup payaṉpaṭutalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	eruviṉi,  eruviṉ kuḻi; ceruviṉi, ceruviṉ kaṭumi	(to.e. 260 nac. uri)
oṭumarappeyar, utimarappeyar pōla, vaṉkaṇam variṉ melleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :oṭuṅkōṭu, oṭuñcetiḷ, oṭuntōl, oṭumpū(to.e. 262  nac. uri)
		ciṟupāṉmi oruvaṅkāṭu eṉṟāṟpōla ammup peṟutalumuṇṭu.	(nac. uri)
	eḻuviṉpuṟam, koḻuviṉ kūrmi - eṉa iviyum iṉcāriyi peṟṟaṉa.		utu + kāṇ = utukkāṇ-eṉa valleḻuttu mikkē varutalum koḷka.	        (263 nac. uri)
ukaraccuṭṭu  -
ukaram etirmukamiṉṟip piṉniṟkum poruḷiyum cuṭṭum eṉak kāṇka. ukaram avvāṟu cuṭṭiniṟpati ‘ūḻiyum uppakkam kāṇpar’ (kuṟaḷ 620) eṉpataṟkup ‘piṉ pakkamāy mutuku kāṭṭak kāṇpar’ eṉap poruḷ kūṟuvatāl kāṇka.	(naṉ. 66 irāmā.)
ukaram naṭuviluḷḷa poruḷiyum (‘avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ’) mēluḷḷa poruḷiyum (umpar) kuṟittu varutalum koḷka.
ukaraccuṭṭup puṇarcci -
ukaraccuṭṭu vaṉkaṇam variṉ vanta valleḻuttu mikkum, ñanama mutaṟkaṇ varum coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyāka variṉ avvanta melleḻuttu mikkum, yakara vakaraṅkaḷ variṉum uyirvariṉum vakaram mikkum, ceyyuḷil ukaram nīṇṭum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	ukkoṟṟaṉ, uccāttaṉ, uttēvaṉ, uppūtaṉ; uññāṇ, unnūl, ummaṇi; uvyāḻ, uvvaṭṭi; uvvaracu; ūvayiṉāṉa.	(to. e. 255,256 nac.)
ukaraccuṭṭiṉmuṉ vakaram vantuḻi, uyirmutal variṉ, taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟāy vakaram iraṭṭum.
u + aracu ḻū uv + aracu ḻū uv + v + aracu = uvvaracu

ukaram kuṟukuiṭaṉ āṟu -
īreḻuttorumoḻi, uyirttoṭarmoḻi, iṭit toṭar moḻi, āytattoṭarmoḻi, vaṉṟoṭar moḻi, meṉṟoṭar moḻi-eṉṟa āṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum ukaram kuṟukik kuṟṟukaramākum.
e-ṭu :	nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku - eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(to. e. 406 nac.)
ukaram nakarattoṭu navilāmi -
ukaram tāṉē niṉṟum piṟameykaḷoṭu niṉṟum payilva taṉṟi, nakarattoṭu payilātu. nakaram, poruna - .nā - poruni - nī - porunū, (nū) -nē - ni - no - nō - eṉṟu piṟuyirka ḷoṭu kūṭi īṟāmāṟu kāṇka.
(poruni, porunū - oppiṭṭu; nū - eḷ)
nakarīṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyē ukaraccāriyi peṟṟup porunuk kaṭitu eṉṟāṟpōla varum. eṉavē, iyalpāka orumoḻikkaṇ ukaram nakarattoṭu kūṭi moḻi yiṟutikkaṇ vārātu eṉpatu.	(to. e. 74  nac)
ukaram niṟital -
taṉimoḻi īṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram, varumoḻi nili moḻiyoṭu puṇarntuvarum alvaḻi vēṟṟumip poruḷ nilikaḷil, irumoḻikkaṇṇum iṭivarutaṟ kaṇ māttiri mikku muṟṟiyalukaramāka olikkum. aṅṅaṉam olikkuṅkāl, valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi īṟṟuk kuṟṟiya lukaram māttiram, varumoḻiyāy valleḻuttu varuvaḻi, arimāttiri aḷaviṟṟākavē olikkum. atuvum nili moḻiyīṟṟil kakarukaram(ku) niṟka, varumoḻi mutalil kakara varukkam varumvaḻiyē tāṉ ari māttiri aḷaviṟ ṟātalu muṇṭu. (‘niliyum’ eṉpatu nac. pāṭam)
	e-ṭu	:	uṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ - oru māttiri; cekkuk kaṇi - ari māttiri; cukkuk koṭu - ari māttiri.		(to.e. 409, 410 iḷa. uri) ( e. ā. pak. 163, 165)
ukaram vakarattoṭu vārātu eṉal -
kaṉavu, aravu, nilavu, iravu, iṟavu, putavu - mutaliya coṟkaḷ muṟiyē kaṉā, arā, nilā, irā, iṟā, putā - mutaliya ākāra īṟṟuc coṟkaḷē.
ivi ceyyuṭkaṇ ākāram kuṟuki ukaram peṟṟuk kaṉavu, aravu - mutaliyavāka varum. puṇarvu, cārvu, cērvu, cōrvu, aḷavu, taḷarvu - mutaliya vukarīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ toṭakkattil puṇarpu, cārpu, cērpu, cōrpu, aḷapu, taḷarpu mutalākap pukarīṟṟuc coṟkaḷē eṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum, ivvāṟu pakaratti vakaramāka olikkum iyalpiṉāṉē, vaṭa coṟ kaḷilum āriyacoṟkaḷilum uḷḷa pakaram vakaramāka olippa tāyiṟṟu, kōpam - kōvam, āpattu - āvattu, tapam - tavam, ātapaṉ-ātavaṉ, pātapam- pātavam eṉa varutal kāṇap paṭum.
ukaram vakarattoṭu varum eṉpār katavu,kaṉavu mutaliya ukaravikuti peṟṟavaṟṟiyum iyalpāṉa coṟkaḷākavē koṇṭaṉar.	(e. ā. pak. 70)
‘ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi iṟuti’ -
ñaṇanamalavaḷaṉa - eṉṟa eṭṭu meykaḷiyum iṟutiyāka vuṭiya mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭum meṉkaṇattoṭum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattoṭum puṇarumvaḻi ukaraccāriyi peṟum; varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ yakaramō uyireḻuttō variṉ ukaraccāriyi peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	maṇ: maṇṇuk kaṭitu; maṇṇu māṇṭatu; maṇṇu valitu, maṇ yātu; maṇṇeḻuntatu.
maṇ -kaḻuvutal eṉṉum poruḷatu. (to. e. 163 nac.)
uccakāram irumoḻikku urittātal -
moḻiyiṟutiyil muṟṟiyalukaramāka varum ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakarameyyākiya cu eṉpatu tamiḻil iraṇṭu coṟkaḷilēyē varum. aviyāvaṉa ucu, mucu - eṉpaṉa. ucu-uḷu; mucu- kuraṅkiṉ vaki.
pacu eṉpatu āriyac citivātaliṉ tamiḻc collākak koḷḷappaṭṭilatu. 
tamiḻiluḷḷa ēṉiya coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟil varum cukaraṅkaḷ ellām kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷām.	(to. e. 75 nac.)
uccakāramoṭu nakāram civaṇal -
tamiḻil cu eṉṟa muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ ucu, mucu, eṉṟa iraṇṭē. atupōla, nakarameyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum iraṇṭē uḷa. aviyāvaṉa porun, verin - eṉpaṉa. porun - oppiṭutal; verin - mutuku.	(to. e. 79 nac.)
uṭampaṭumey -
nilimoḻiyīṟṟu uyiriyum varumoḻimutal uyiriyum, uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkam iṉmiyiṉ, iṇittu vittaṟku iṭiyē varum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉappaṭum. ‘uṭampaṭumey vēṇṭum’ eṉṉātu ‘uṭampaṭumey variyār’ eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutaliṉ, uṭampaṭu mey yāṇṭum peṟṟē varal vēṇṭum eṉṟa variyaṟi illi. itaṉi iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, ilakkaṇa viḷakka āciriyar mutaliyōr kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. itu kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷa moḻikaḷilum koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.
i ī ē ai - muṉ yakaramum, aāuūōoḷa muṉ vakaramum varukiṉṟaṉa. ākāra ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ orōvaḻi māyiru ñālam - kōyil - eṉṟāṟpōla, yakara uṭampaṭumey varutalum koḷka.
‘kōmā muṉvariṉ yakaramum kutikkum’ eṉṟu muttuvīriyam moḻikiṟatu. 	(puṇariyal 24)
iṭiyaṇṇattil piṟappaṉavākiya iīeēai- ivaṟṟukku muṉ, iṭiyaṇṇattil piṟappatākiya yakaramum, itaḻil piṟappaṉavākiya u ū o ō oḷa - ivaṟṟukkumuṉ itaḻil piṟappatākiya vakaramum uṭampaṭumeyyāka varukiṉṟaṉa.
aā- iraṇṭum ivviṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉum piṟappaṉa alla āyiṉum, vāy aṅkāntu olikkap piṟakkum avvoli itaḻiṭiyēyaṉṟi olippatu iyalātākaliṉ, avveḻut tukkaḷiṉ muṉ itaḻil tōṉṟum vakaram varuvatāyiṟṟu.
a ā i ī e ē ai ō - ivvīṟukaḷukku muṉ yakaram varum eṉavum, uūōoḷa- ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ vakaram varum eṉavum captamaṇi tarpaṇam (55) kūṟukiṟatu. teluṅku moḻiyilum akarākāra īṟukaḷukku muṉ yakaram varukiṟatu.
nilimoḻiyīṟu tālavyamāṉāl yakaramum, ōṣṭra°vara māṉāl vakaramum uṭampaṭumeyyāka varum eṉṟu kēraḷapāṇinīyam kūṟukiṟatu.
tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, maliyāḷam eṉṟa nāṉku moḻi kaḷilum ākāra oḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar yakaram varutal aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ yakaram varumoḻi nōkki vantatu eṉal amiyum. vaṭamoḻiyil ikara īkāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar ikara īkāram allāta piṟa uyir variṉ, anta ikara īkāraṅkaḷ yakaram ākum; ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ ivvāṟē vakaram ākum.
e-ṭu :	tati + atra = tatyatra;  natī + ēṣā = natyēṣā; matu + atra = matvatra; vatu + āṉaṉam = vatvāṉaṉam
tamiḻil i ī u ū keṭāmal niṟka, muṟiyē yakara vakaram varum. vaṭamoḻiyil avi keṭa yakara vakaram varum. iḵtoṉṟē vēṟupāṭu.	(e. ā. pak. 107, 108, 109)
‘ṉakāra viṟuvāy’ - to.e.9; ‘avviyal niliyum’ - e.12; ‘āyiru tiṇiyiṉ’ - co. 1; ‘āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa’ - e.117; ‘āviṉ iṟuti’ - 120; ‘illā vellā meyyum’ - 17; ‘noṭiyeṉa vavvē’ - 7; ‘kūṭṭi yeḻuutal’ - 6; ‘ī yākum’ - co. 123; ‘uruvāki’ - e.17; ‘ammūvāṟum’ - 22; ‘ē oḷa veṉṉum’ - e.173; ‘uḷavē yavvum’ - co. 68; ‘mūppē yaṭimi’ - 57; ‘uyartiṇippeyarē yaḵṟiṇi’ - 117;  ‘ari yaḷapu kuṟukal’ - e.13; ‘ō oḷa veṉa vicikkum’ - 87
a u ū ō oḷa - ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar vakaramum, i ī ē ai - ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar yakaramum, āviṉ piṉṉar yakaravakaraṅ kaḷum varum eṉpatik kāṇkiṟōm.	(e. ku. pak. 144)
‘uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal variyār’ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār potuppaṭa ōtiṉārēṉum, uyirkaḷi  uṭampaṭuttaṟkuriyaṉa iṭappiṟaviyāṉ avvuyirōṭu otta  iṭiyeḻuttu eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷḷum moḻi mutaṟkaṇ varutaṟkuriyaṉa yakara vakaraṅkaḷē yātaliṉ aviyē īṇṭikku varappeṟum eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ḷum perumpālum iīēai - muṉ yakaramum, ēṉi uyir kaḷiṉ muṉ vakaramum varum eṉpatu ēṟpuḻikkōṭal eṉpataṉāl peṟappaṭum.	(cū. vi. pak. 42)
uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkam illātatiṉālē, avviraṇṭum uṭaṉpaṭutaṟporuṭṭu iṭiyē varum meyyiṉi uṭampaṭumey eṉṟār. iṉi, varum uyirkku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉiṉum amiyum. inta uṭampaṭumeyyiṉ tōṟṟam, tōṉṟal vikāram pōlākātu, iṭiviṭṭa ulōkaṅkaḷi iṇikkum paṟṟācu pōla varutaliṉ, iyalpu puṇarcci.	(naṉ. 162 irāmā.)
nilimoḻiyīṟṟuyir i ī ai - eṉa iruppiṉ yakara uṭampaṭu meyyum, ēṉiya uyirīṟukaḷiṉ muṉ vakara uṭampaṭu meyyum, ēkāra īṟṟiṉ muṉ ivviraṇṭum iṭiyē varum.
e-ṭu :	maṇi + aḻakitu = maṇiyaḻakitu; ā + aḻakitu = āvaḻakitu; cē + aḻakitu = cēyaḻakitu, cēvaḻakitu   	(162 irāmā.)
‘uṭampaṭumey aṉṟu’ eṉak kūṟum vakaram -
‘viṇvattuk koṭkum’, ‘celvuḻic celka’, ‘cārvuḻic cārnta  takiyaṉ’- eṉa meyyīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyir varuṅkāl (viṇ + attu, cel + uḻi,  cār + uḻi), iṅṅaṉam ‘uṭampaṭu mey aṉṟu’ eṉak kūṟum vakaram tōṉṟiṉa. (naṉ. 163 caṅkara.)
uṭampaṭumey uyir īṟṟukkē varutal -
‘viṇvattuk koṭkum’ eṉa meyyīṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum uyir variṉ uṭampaṭumey peṟum eṉpārumuḷar. uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutalvariṉ, uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkam iṉmi yiṉ puṇarcciyiṉṟi viṭṭicittu niṟkum ātaliṉ, uṭam paṭāta avviraṇṭum uṭampaṭutaṟporuṭṭāka iṭiyē varum meyyi uṭampaṭumey eṉpa ātaliṉ, varum uyirēṟi oṟṟumippaṭṭup puṇartaṟkuriya meyyīṟṟiṉ vaḻittōṉṟum meyyi uṭampaṭumey eṉpatu porun tātu. uṭaṉpaṭal ‘uṭampaṭal’ eṉa marīiyiṟṟu. ‘uṭampā ṭilātavar vāḻkki’ (kuṟaḷ 890) eṉavarutal kāṇka. uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkam iṉmiyiṉ, varum uyirkku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey uṭampaṭumey  eṉap poruḷ kūṟutalum oṉṟu.	(naṉ. 162 caṅkara.)
uṭampaṭumey ceyki ōttil kūṟappaṭāmi -
nilimoḻi uyirīṟāka varumoḻi uyir mutalāka variṉ, uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkam iṉmiyāṉ, avvīruyir kaḷiyum iṇittu uṭampaṭuttaṟku iṭiyē aṭuttuvarum meykaḷākiya yakara vakaraṅkaḷ uṭampaṭumeykaḷām. ākavē, viṇ+ attu = viṇvattu - eṉa nilimoḻiyīṟṟil mey niṟpuḻi iṭiyē varum vakaram eḻuttuppēṟēyaṉṟi uṭampaṭumey aṉṟām.
tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāram mutal nāṉku iyalkaḷ karuvi ōttu; aṭutta aintu iyalkaḷum ceyki ōttu.
uṭampaṭumey irumoḻikaḷiyum uṭampaṭuvittaṟku varum karuviyātaliṉ, tolkāppiyaṉār uṭampaṭumeyyik karuvi ōttu nāṉkaṉuḷ nāṉkāvatākiya puṇariyalil kūṟi ṉār. itu puṇarmoḻic ceykiyāyiṉ, ceyki ōttil kūṟap paṭṭirukkum.	(cū. vi. pak. 42)
ātaliṉ, uṭampaṭumey peṟutal iyalpupuṇarcci vakiyuḷ oṉṟu.
uṭampaṭumey puṇarpu eḻuttukkaḷ -
uyirāṉ muṭinta collum uyirāl toṭaṅkiṉa collum tammuḷ puṇaruṅkāl, avviraṇṭu uyir naṭuvē oṟṟicittal vēṇṭum icippaṭum eḻuttē puṇarpeḻuttu eṉappaṭum.
i ī e ai - eṉṉum nilimoḻi uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ varu moḻip paṉṉīruyirum puṇaril, yakara uṭampaṭumeyyām. aāuūēōoḷa- eṉṉum nilimoḻi uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ varumoḻip paṉṉīruyirum puṇaril vakara uṭampaṭumey yām. ēkāra īṟṟiṉ muṉ paṉṉīruyirum puṇaril ivviru vitiyum peṟum.	(to. vi. 20 uri)
‘uṭalmēl uyirvantu oṉṟu’tal  -
nilimoḻi īṟu meyyeḻuttāka varumoḻi mutal uyireḻuttāyiṉ, varumoḻimutal uyir nilimoḻi yīṟṟumeyyi ūrntu uyirmeyyāyp puṇarum. iḵtu iyalpu puṇarcciyām.
e-ṭu : āl+ ili = ālili(naṉ. 204)
uṭaṉnili meymmayakkam -
coṟkaḷil rakaraḻakara oṟṟukkaḷ nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya patiṉāṟu oṟṟukkaḷum tammuṉ tāmē vantu collamiyum niliyil avaṟṟiṉ cērkki uṭaṉili meymmayakkam eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu :	kākki eṅṅaṉam, pacci, maññi, paṭṭi, kiṇṇam, tatti, venney, uppu, ammi, veyyar, elli, tevvar, kaḷḷi, koṟṟi, kaṉṉi - eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(to. e. 30 nac.)
raḻa - allāta meykaḷ orumoḻiyilum irumoḻiyilum tammoṭu tām mayaṅkum mayakkam uṭaṉili mayakkamām.
e-ṭu :	pakkam, taccaṉ, paṭṭam, cattam, kappam, ceṟṟam, aṅṅaṉam, aññāṉam, kiṇṇam, vennīr, kammam, kaṉṉam, veyyar, vellam, tevvar, kaḷḷam.	(naṉ. 118)
uṭaṉnili moḻi -
nilimoḻiyōṭu ōci iyintu niṟṟaliyuṭiya varumoḻi. e-ṭu : mīkkōḷ, mīppal, mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl - ivaṟṟuḷ kōḷ, pal - mutaliya varumoḻikaḷ uṭaṉnili moḻikaḷām.		(to. e. 251 nac. uri)
uṭukki : collamippu -
uṭukki - uṭuttutal eṉṉum toḻiliyum uṭukkappaṭum āṭiyiyum kuṟikkum. uṭukki eṉpatu uṭuttutal eṉṉum puṭipeyarcciyi uṇarttuṅkāl, ‘ki’ vikuti yuṭiyatu. (uṭu+k+ki).aḵtu uṭukkappaṭum āṭiyi uṇarttuṅ kāl, ‘ai’ vikutiyuṭiyatu (uṭu+k+k+ai)	(cū. vi. pak. 33)
kivikuti toḻiṟpeyari uṇarttuvatu; aivikuti ceyappaṭu poruḷi uṇarttuvatu.
uṭipporuḷ nīravāy varum mutaṉili  -
karumiyaṉ - cemmiyaṉ - ‘poruttam iṉmiyēṉ poymmi uṇmiyēṉ’ (tiruvā. 5:93) - eṉavum, valicci -pulicci - eṉavum, kaṟuppaṉ - civappaṉ- aḷiyaṉ- aṉpaṉ- eṉavum varum paṇpup pakupataṅkaṭkuk karumi cemmi iṉmi uṇmi eṉṟa paṇpup peyarkaḷum, valimi - pulimi - eṉṟa cātippaṇpu uṇara niṟkum peyarkaḷum, kaṟuppu  civappu aḷi aṉpu - eṉṟa palvēṟu  vakippaṭṭa paṇpuppeyarkaḷum neṟippaṭa vārātu uṭipporuḷ nīravāki varum mutaṉiliyākak koḷḷap paṭum.	(i. vi. 45 uri)
‘uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ’ ottal  -
ēṉi urupukaḷ varumvaḻi vēṇṭum cāriyi palavaṟṟiyum mūṉṟaṉurupākiya oṭu vēṇṭātu. varum.
makara īṟṟu moḻikaḷ, iraṇṭaṉurupu nāṉkaṉurupu mutaliya vaṟṟoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, iṭiyē  attuccāriyi peṟṟē varum. oṭuurupukku ivviti vilakkākum.
e-ṭu	: maram + ai = maratti; maram + ku = marattukku; maram + oṭu = marattoṭu, maramoṭu
kēraḷa pāṇiṉīyattuḷḷum, taṉatti, taṉattoṭu taṉa moṭu, taṉattiṟku mutaliya utāraṇaṅkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
urumiṉi, vaḷattiṉi, kālattiṉi - urumiṟku, vaḷattiṟku, kālattiṟku, eṉac cāriyi peṟṟē varuvaṉa, oṭu urupiṟku, urumiṉoṭu urumoṭu - vaḷattiṉoṭu vaḷamoṭu - kālattiṉoṭu kālamoṭu - eṉac cāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmiyum varutal koḷḷappaṭum.
kāmamoṭu (muruku aṭi 134) cīṟṟamoṭu (patiṟ. 16) ‘kālamoṭu’ (to.co.250 cēṉā.) eṉap paṇṭiya ceyyuḷkaḷilum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvilum kāṇalām.
palavaṟṟoṭu eṉpatu pōlavē, palavoṭu eṉa varutalum tolkāppiyar karuttām. neñcamoṭu (to. po. 40, 113, 204 nac.), pakkamoṭu (41), karaṇamoṭu (142), ārvamoṭu (146), uḷḷamoṭu (146,147), cuṟṟamoṭu (192) - eṉat tolkāp piyattum makara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ pala cāriyi iṉṟi varutal kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āyiṉ pala eṉpatu palavoṭu eṉṟu ataṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭāmi nōkka, palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa atu cāriyi peṟṟē varutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu nac. karuttu.	(eḻu. 132 nac.) (e. ā. pak. 103,104)
palla, pala, cila, uḷḷa, illa - eṉṟa peyarkaḷ oṭu urupēṟkumiṭattu vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟē varum.
varumāṟu: pallavaṟṟoṭu, palavaṟṟoṭu, cilavaṟṟoṭu, uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu, illavaṟṟoṭu.	(to. e. 132, 174, nac.)
uṇṇā kutiri  uṇṇāk kutiri , uṇṇākiṭantaṉa   -uṇṇāk kiṭantaṉa : vēṟupāṭu  -
uṇṇā kutiri(kaḷ) eṉpuḻi, ‘uṇṇā’uṇṇātavi eṉa viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarākap poruḷpaṭum. uṇṇā taṉavākiya kutiri(kaḷ) eṉka. āṇṭu vali mikātu. ‘uṇṇā’ muṟṟāya vaḻiyum atu.
uṇṇā kiṭantaṉa eṉpuḻi, ‘uṇṇā’ uṇṇātaṉavāy eṉa muṟṟeccamākap poruḷpaṭum. uṇṇātaṉavāyk kiṭantaṉa eṉka. āṇṭum vali mikātu. 
etirmaṟip peyareccamum ceyyā eṉṟum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamumāka ‘uṇṇā’ eṉpatu niṟpiṉ, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikappeṟum, uṇṇāk kutiri- uṇṇāta kutiri; uṇṇāk kiṭantaṉa- uṇṭu kiṭantaṉa.	(naṉ. 171 caṅkara.)
uṇmi ceppum uṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉ puṇarcci -
uṇmi cepputalāvatu, oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇiyum uṇṭāy niṟkiṉṟa taṉmi yākiya paṇpi uṇarttutal. itu ‘poruṇmi cuṭṭal’ eṉavumpaṭum.	(to. co. 66 cēṉā.)
uṇṭu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka varumoḻi mutalil vaṉkaṇam variṉ, uṇṭu eṉpatu iyalpākap puṇartalum, uṇṭu eṉpataṉ iṟuti keṭṭu ṇakaram ḷakara oṟṟāki uḷ eṉa niṉṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartalum eṉṟa iru nilimiyum peṟum.
	varumāṟu : uṇṭu+poruḷ = uṇṭu poruḷ, uḷ poruḷ.
uṇṭu eṉpataṉ muṉ iyalpukaṇam varuvuḻik kēṭum tiripum iṉṟi, uṇṭu ñāṉam - uṇṭu vaṭṭu - uṇṭu aṭi (uṇṭaṭi) - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarum.	(to. e. 430 nac.)
uttēcam, vitēyam  eṉpaṉa -
ovvoru nuṟpāviṉ tōṟṟattukkum uttēciyamum vitēyamum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. uttēciyam nutaliyatu (karutiyatu). vitēyam - uṇarttiyatu. terinta poruḷi nutalit teriyāta poruḷik kūṟutalē muṟiyātaliṉ, nutaliya poruḷākiya uttēciyam māṇākkarkkut terintatāyum, uṇartta vēṇṭiya poruḷākiya vitēyam teriyātatāyum irukkum. potu iyalpāyt terintatu nutaliya poruḷāy varuvatum, ciṟappiyalpāyt teriya vēṇṭiyatu uṇartta vēṇṭiya poruḷāy varuvatum ām.
	e-ṭu : kōyil cāttaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟa toṭaril, kōyil eṉpatu nutaliya poruḷākiya uttēciyam; ‘cāttaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu’ eṉpatu uṇarttiya poruḷākiya vitēyam. ‘kōyil’ terintatāyiṉum, iṉṉārāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟu teriyātāṉukku uṇarttiya ceyti teriyātatām.
‘eḻutteṉappaṭupa akara mutala ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu’ - itaṉkaṇ, eḻuttu eṉpatu terinta poruḷ; muppaḵtu eṉṟa eṇ teriyāta poruḷ. eḻuttu eṉpataṉip potu viyalpāl uṇarnta oruvaṉukkut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum eḻut tukkaḷ akara mutala ṉakara iṟuvāy aminta muppatē - eṉṟuṇarttuvatē cūttirak karuttu ātaliṉ, eḻuttu - uttēciyam, muppaḵtu- vitēyam - piṟavum aṉṉa.	(e.ā.pak. 4,5)
uti eṉṟa marappeyarp puṇarcci -
uti eṉṟa marappeyar varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ otta melleḻuttu iṭiyē mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : utiṅkōṭu, utiñcetiḷ utintōl, utimpū.
piṟkālattu ammuccāriyi peṟṟu nāṟkaṇattoṭum puṇarum niliyum ēṟpaṭṭatu.
	e-ṭu : utiyaṅkōṭu, utiyamaram, utiyavaṭṭi, utiyāram (utiyavāram)	(to. e. 243 nac.)
utukkāṇ: puṇarumāṟu -
utu eṉpataṉoṭu kāṇ eṉṟa col puṇarumvaḻi, valloṟṟu mikku utukkāṇ - eṉa muṭiyum.	(to. e. 263 nac. uri)
(itaṟku utaṉik kāṇpāyāka eṉṟu iraṇṭu collāyk koṇṭu poruḷ kūṟalumuṇṭu. itaṉi oṭṭi niṉṟa iṭic collāy uṅkē eṉṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruṇarttuvatu eṉṟu kūṟalumuṇṭu. pari. kuṟaḷ 1185
‘uppakāram iruvayiṉ niliyal’ -
pakarameyyi ūrntu varum muṟṟukarac col oṉṟē. aḵtu ēval viṉi, toḻiṟpeyar ākiya iraṇṭiṭattum niṟkum poruṇmi yuṭittām. 
atu ‘tapu’ eṉṉum col. atu ‘keṭuvāyāka’ eṉa ēvaṟ poruṭṭāka varuvatu; peyarāmiṭattut ‘tavaṟu’ eṉpatu poruḷ. aḵtu ikkālattut tappu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.	(to. e. 76. ca.pāla.)
uppakāram oṉṟu -
pu eṉṟa muṟṟiyalukaratti iṟutiyāka  uṭiya col ‘tapu’ eṉṟa oṉṟē ākum ataṉip paṭuttuk kūṟiṉ, ‘nī cā’ eṉṟa taṉviṉip poruḷpaṭum; eṭuttu olippiṉ, ‘nī oṉṟaṉic cāvappaṇ’ eṉṟu piṟaviṉip poruḷpaṭum. (to. e. 76 nac.)
uppakāramoṭu ñakāriyum aṟṟu’ ātal -
pu eṉṟa muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc col tamiḻil ‘tapu’ eṉṟu oṉṟēyāy iruttal pōla, ñakāra oṟṟīṟṟuc collum ‘uriñ’ eṉa oṉṟēyāy uḷḷatu. āyiṉ tapu eṉpatu paṭuttukkūṟat taṉviṉiyākavum eṭuttukkūṟap piṟaviṉi yākavum poruḷ paṭutal pōla, uriñ eṉṟa col eṭuttal paṭuttal oli vēṟṟumiyāṉ poruḷ vēṟṟumi tārātu.	(to. e. 80 nac.)
uppum nīrum pōla  -
uyirmeyyil, arimāttiri aḷavu koṇṭa mey yoli, oru māttiriyum iraṇṭu māttiriyum koṇṭa uyiroliyil, uppāṉatu nīril karintu taṉṉaḷavu keṭutal pōla, karintu pōkavē, uyiriṉ māttiriyē uyirmeyyiṉatu māttiriyāka olikkappeṟum.	(to. e. 18 nac. uri)
upacarkkaṅkaḷ  -
pira	:	pirayōkam	ā	:	ākāram
parā	:	parāpavam	ni	:	nivācam
apa	:	apakīrtti	ati	:	atimaturam
cam	:	caṅkati	api	:	apivirutti
anu	:	anupavam	cu	:	cutiṉam
ava	:	avamāṉam	uṟ	:	uṟpātam
nir	:	nirkkuṇam	pirati	:	piratikūlam
tur	:	turkkuṇam	pari	:	paripākam
vi	:	vikāram	upa	:	upayōkam
ippatiṉeṭṭum vaṭamoḻikaḷukku mutalaṭuttu vevvēṟu poruḷi viḷakkivarum upacarkkaṅkaḷākum. 	(to. vi. 86 uri)
um, keḻu  eṉṟa cāriyip puṇarcci  -
um eṉpatum, keḻu eṉpatum cāriyiyākavum varum.
‘vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum pōlum’ - ummiṉ makaram varumoḻi nōkki nakaroṟṟākavum tiriyāmal iyalpākavum amintatu. itaṟku ‘vāṉavari villiṭit tiṅkaḷ’ eṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittup poruḷ ceytal vēṇṭum.
‘mānitik kiḻavaṉum pōṉm’ (akam. 66) mānitik kiḻavaṉip pōlum eṉṟu poruḷ ceyka.
‘kalkeḻu kāṉavar’ (kuṟun. 71) kallik keḻīiyiṉa kāṉavar eṉṟu poruḷ ceyka.
‘kāṉkeḻu nāṭu’ (akanā. 93) kāṉik keḻīiyiṉa nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ ceyka.
‘pūkkēḻt toṭali’ (aka. 28) ‘tuṟikēḻ ūraṉ’ (aiṅ. 11) ‘ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi’ (aka. 80) - keḻu eṉpatu kēḻ eṉat tirintu niṉṟatu.	(to. e. 481 nac. uri)
‘ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi’ puṇarumāṟu -
iṭiyē um eṉṟa eṇṇiṭiccol maṟiya, ummit tokiyāy niṟkum irupeyarkaḷ kūṭiya tokiccoṟkaḷ, nilimoḻi ākāra īṟṟuc collāyiṉ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattil toṭaṅkiṉ, iṭiyē eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭiyākiya akaramum valleḻuttum mikkup puṇarum. iyalpukaṇam varumoḻiyāka variṉum akaram peṟum.
	e-ṭu : uvā+patiṉāṉku = uvāappatiṉāṉku (vaṉkaṇam)irā + pakal = irāappakal (vaṉkaṇam)
	iṟā + vaḻutuṇaṅkāy = iṟāa vaḻutuṇaṅkāy (iyalpukaṇam)
ivi muṟiyē - uvāvum patiṉāṉkum eṉavum, iravum pakalum eṉavum, iṟāvum vaḻutuṇaṅkāyum eṉavum poruḷ paṭṭavāṟu.	(to. e. 223 nac. uri)
uyttuviṭum, kāṭṭiviṭum : collilakkaṇam  -
uyttu eṉṟa viṉiyeccattoṭum kāṭṭi eṉṟa viṉi yeccattoṭum viṭu eṉṟa vikuti puṇarntu mutaṉilit taṉmippaṭṭu uyttuviṭu kāṭṭiviṭu eṉṟa mutaṉili kaḷāki, piṉṉar um mutaliya vikutikaḷoṭum cērntu, uyttuviṭum kāṭṭiviṭum - mutaliya viṉiccoṟkaḷi uṇṭākkum. ic coṟkaḷip parimēlaḻakar mutalāyiṉār ‘oru col’ eṉṟami, ivi uyttuviṭu kāṭṭiviṭu - eṉṟu mutaṉilikaḷōṭu iṇintu mēl vikutikaḷoṭu cērntu colli uṇṭākku taliṉālēyām. iṅṅaṉam viṭu pōṉṟa vikutikaḷ pakutikaḷōṭu iṇintu pakutit taṉmip paṭṭu mēlvarum vikutikaḷōṭu iṇital vaṭanūlārkkum uṭaṉpāṭām.	(cū. vi. pak. 42)
uyartiṇippeyarp puṇarcci -
uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟumāka varum uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ, varumoḻiyil nāṟkaṇaṅkaḷum variṉum, alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum perumpāṉmiyum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu	:	nampi kuṟiyaṉ; nampiki
			avaḷ kuṟiyaḷ; avaḷki
	kapila paraṇar, maruttuvamāṇikkar - nilimoḻi ṉakara īṟu keṭṭu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa.
	ācīvakap paḷḷi, nikkantak kōṭṭam - nili moḻi ṉakarīṟu keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarntaṉa.
	īḻavak katti, vāṇikat teru - nilimoḻi rakara īṟu keṭṭu, valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarntaṉa.
	pirama kōṭṭam, piramak kōṭṭam - nilimoḻi ṉakarīṟu keṭṭu, valleḻuttu uṟaḻntatu.
	palar + caṅkattār, palar + aracar - palcaṅkat tār, pallaracar - ivi rakarīṟum ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa akaramum keṭṭup puṇarntaṉa.
ikara īṟṟu uyartiṇip peyar vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyi lum  mikku muṭital perumpāṉmi.
e-ṭu :	eṭṭippū, nampippēṟu - āṟām vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci; nampittuṇi, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ-irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki;
	aikāra īṟu ciṟupāṉmi alvaḻikkaṇ mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu:	naṅkip peṇ, naṅkiccāṉi 												  (to. e. 153, 154 nac. uri)
		āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ, makaṭūuk kuṟiyaḷ - ūkāra īṟu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟa alvaḻip puṇarcci.		(to.e. 265 nac. uri)
	āṭūukki, 	 makaṭūukki - ūkāra īṟu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟa vēṟṟumip puṇarcci  (to.e. 267 nac. uri)
		āṭūviṉki, makaṭūviṉki - ūkārīṟu iṉcāriyi peṟṟa vēṟṟumip puṇarcci 	(to.e. 271 nac. uri)
uyartiṇippeyarpuṇarcciyiṭattu vikārappaṭutal  -
āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ, makaṭūuk kuṟiyaḷ (ciṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaḷ,tīyaṉ tīyaḷ, periyaṉ periyaḷ - eṉa ēṉi vaṉkaṇamum koḷka) eṉavum, āṭūukki, makaṭūukki (cevi, tali, puṟam - eṉa ēṉi vaṉkaṇamum koḷka) eṉavum, eṭṭippū eṭṭip puravu, kāvitippū kāvitippuravu, nampippū nampippēṟu - eṉavum uyartiṇip peyarmuṉ cila mikkaṉa.
	makkaṭ kuṇam, makkaṭcuṭṭu, makkaṭṭali, makkaṭ puṟam - eṉat tirintaṉa.
	kapilaparaṇar, palacāṉṟār - eṉa īṟu (ṉ,r) keṭṭu iyalpāyiṉa.
ācīvakap paḷḷi, kaṇakkāyap paḷḷi, īḻavak katti, kōlikap puṭavi, vaṇṇārap peṇṭir - eṉa nilimoḻi īṟu (r, r,r, ṉ, m ) keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
vācutēva kōṭṭam vācutēvak kōṭṭam, pirama kōṭṭam piramak kōṭṭam - eṉa nilimoḻi īṟu(ṉ) keṭṭu varumoḻi valliṉam iyalpum mikalumāka vikaṟpam āyiṟṟu.
pārppaṉakkaṉṉi, cēri, tōṭṭam, piḷḷi, marapu, vāḻkki - eṉa, (pārppāṉ eṉṉum) nilimoḻi īṟṟayal kuṟuki akaram mikkatu; vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi valliṉam mikalum, ēṉiya meykkaṇam varuvaḻi iyalpātalum koḷka.	(naṉ. 158 mayili.)
uyartiṇippeyar, viravuppeyar  ku, kaṇ urupoṭu puṇartal -
uyartiṇippeyar kuvvurupu aṭuttavaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum, kaṇ urupu aṭuttavaḻi mikāmiyum  uṭittu. viravuppeyar niliyum atuvē.
e-ṭu	: nampikku; nampikaṇ; naṅkikku; naṅkikaṇ; avaṉukku; avaṉkaṇ; avaḷukku; avaḷkaṇ - eṉa uyartiṇip peyar ku, kaṇ - ivaṟṟoṭu puṇarntavāṟu.
	koṟṟikku; koṟṟikaṇ kōtikku; kōti kaṇ; tāykku; tāykaṇ; makaṉukku; makaṉkaṇ - eṉa viravuppeyar ku, kaṇ ivaṟṟoṭu puṇarnta vāṟu. 	(to. e. 114 nac. uri)
uyartiṇi viṉiccol muṭipu -
uyartiṇi viṉiccol iyalpāyum tirintum muṭivaṉa uḷa.
uṇku, uṇṭu, varutu, cēṟu, uṇpal, uṇṭēṉ, uṇpēṉ - eṉṉum taṉmiviṉikaḷ koṟṟā - cāttā- tēvā- pūtā - ñeḷḷā - nākā - māṭā - yavaṉā - vaḷavā - ātā - eṉṟa nāṟkaṇattoṭum puṇarumvaḻi iyalpākum.
uṇṭīr+ cāṉṟīr, uṇṭīr+ pārppīr - eṉa muṉṉilik kaṇṇum, uṇpa, uṇṭār + cāṉṟār pārppār - eṉappaṭarkkikkaṇṇum iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.
uṇṭaṉeñ cāṉṟēm - makaram tirintu puṇarntatu.
uṇṭē nām - makaram keṭṭup puṇarntatu.
(to. e. 153 nac. uri)
uyir: āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi  -
akaram mutal oḷakāram iṟuvāyuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭu eḻuttuk kaḷiyum uyir eṉṟa potuppeyarāṉē vaḻaṅkuvatu āṭciyām. uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, mey patiṉeṭṭaṉiyum iyakki uyirmeyyām niliyil varivaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ, uyir eṉṟa peyar kāraṇam paṟṟiyatāyiṟṟu. meykku uyirāy niṉṟu meykaḷi iyakkum niliyum taṉittu niṟkum niliyum uyireḻuttukku uṇṭu, iṟi oṉṟēyāy niṟkum taṉmiyum, palluyirkkum tāṉēyāy avaṟṟiṉ aḷavāy niṟkum taṉmiyum pōla.	(to. e. 8 nac. uri)
uyir iyal  -
uyiriṉ māttiriyum, peyarum, oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇikki yum uyiriṉ iyalpām. a,ka - oru māttiri, akaram eṉṟa peyar, oṉṟu eṉṟa eṇ - eṉpaṉa ivaṟṟil uyiriṉ ilakkaṇamām.	(to. e. 10 nac. uri)
uyiriyal tiriyāmi -
uyirāṉatu meyyoṭu kūṭi uyirmey ākiya kālattum taṉ māttiriyum kuṟiyum eṇṇum tiriyātiruttal.
a eṉpuḻi niṉṟa oru māttiriyum, kuṟil eṉṟa peyarum, oṉṟu eṉṟa eṇṇum, avvakaram kakarameyyi  ūrntu ka eṉa niṉṟavaḻiyum ottal pōlvaṉa; ā eṉpuḻi niṉṟa aḷavum kuṟiyum oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇum kā eṉa niṉṟa vaḻiyum ottal pōlvaṉa.	(to. e. 10 iḷa., nac. uri)
uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷavi -
uyireḻuttukkaḷi īṟāka uṭiya muṉṉili viṉic coṟkaḷ, muṉniṉṟāṉ toḻil uṇarttuvaṉavum avaṉit toḻiṟ paṭuppaṉavum eṉa iruvakiya,
i, ai - eṉpaṉa muṉniṉṟāṉ toḻili uṇarttuvaṉa.
e-ṭu : uṇṭi, uṇṭaṉi.
muṉniṉṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa, akaram mutal oḷakāram iṟuvāy ekaramnīṅkalākap pakuti māttiriyāy niṉṟu eṭutta lōciyāṉ muṉṉili orumi ēval poruṇmi uṇarttuvaṉa. avi naṭa, vā, maṭi, cī, viṭu, kū, ē, vi, no, pō, veḷa eṉa varum.	(to. e. 151 nac. uri)
uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyiṉ puṇarcci -
muṉniṉṟāṉ toḻili yuṇarttum ikara īṟum aikāra īṟum vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. e-ṭu: niṟṟi koṟṟā, uṇṭaṉi koṟṟā.
muṉniṉṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttum uyirīṟukaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇarvaṉavum, uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
e-ṭu : koṇā koṟṟā, eṟi koṟṟā - iyalpu
	naṭa koṟṟā, naṭak koṟṟā – uṟaḻvu (to. e. 151 nac. uri)
uyirttoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram  -
īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaratti aṭutta eḻuttu uyirmeyyāka iruppiṉ accollīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram uyirttoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉappaṭum. aḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam (meṉkaṇam, iṭikkaṇam- ivi) varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varumiṭattu iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : varaku + kaṭitu = varaku kaṭitu (varaku ñāṉṟatu, varaku yātu)
vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpākavum iṉcāriyi peṟṟum puṇarum;ciṟupāṉmi valliṉamey iraṭṭum.
	e-ṭu : varaku +katir = varakukatir, varakiṉ katir; veruku + kaṇ = verukkukkaṇ; erutu+ kāl = eruttukkāl.
uyirttoṭark kuṟṟukaram ṭaṟa meykaḷi ūrntu varu miṭattu, vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam  variṉ, avvalliṉa meykaḷ iraṭṭa valleḻuttu mikum; ēṉikkaṇamvariṉ, avviraṭṭutalōṭu iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : muruṭu+ kāl, ñāṟci, yāppu, aṭi = muruṭṭukkāl, 	muruṭṭuñāṟci, muruṭṭiyāppu, muruṭṭaṭi
		muyiṟu+ kāl, niṟam, vaṉmi, aṭi = muyiṟṟuk kāl, muyiṟṟuniṟam, muyiṟṟuvaṉmi, muyiṟṟaṭi.
ciṟupāṉmi vēṟṟumikkaṇ iraṭṭāmiyum, alvaḻik kaṇ iraṭṭutalum, ciṟupāṉmi iruvaḻiyum piṟa oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum uḷa. e-ṭu : nāṭu kiḻavōṉ eṉavum, kāṭṭaraṇ eṉavum, verukkukkaṇ eruttumāṭu eṉavum muṟiyē kāṇka.	(naṉ. 182, 183)
uyirttoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
uyirttoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ valliṉam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum
  e-ṭu  :: varaku kaṭitu, kiṭantatu kutiri, karitu kutiri 		(to. e. 425 nac. uri)
irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki vaṉkaṇam variṉ iṉoṟṟu mikku valleḻuttup peṟutalum, iyalpukaṇam varuvaḻi iṉoṟṟu mikutalum uḷa.
e-ṭu :	karaṭu + kāṉam = karaṭṭuk kāṉam; kuruṭu  + kōḻi = kuruṭṭuk kōḻi; tiruṭu  + puliyaṉ = tiruṭṭup puliyaṉ; veḷiṟu + paṉi = veḷiṟṟup paṉi; eyiṟu + pal = eyiṟṟup pal
ivi vaṉkaṇam puṇarntaṉa.
vaṟaṭu + āṭu = vaṟaṭṭāṭu; kuruṭu  + erutu = kuruṭṭerutu.
	ivi iyalpukaṇam puṇarntaṉa. (to.e. 425 nac. uri)
vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṭu ṟu īṟākiya coṟkaḷ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ iṉoṟṟum valleḻuttum mikum; iyalpukaṇam variṉ iṉoṟṟu mikum.
e-ṭu :	muyiṟu+ kāl = muyiṟṟukkāl; kayiṟu + puṟam = kayiṟṟuppuṟam; vayiṟu + tī = vayiṟṟut tī; pakaṭu + kāl = pakaṭṭukkāl; akaṭu + tī = akaṭṭuttī; mukaṭu  + pakuti = mukaṭṭuppakuti
ivi vaṉkaṇam puṇarntaṉa.
	pakaṭu + ñāṟci = pakaṭṭuñāṟci; muyiṟu + ñāṟci = muyiṟṟuñāṟci ivi iyalpukaṇam puṇarntaṉa. 
ku cu tu pu - eṉṟa īṟukaḷ iyalpākap puṇarum
	e-ṭu : varakukāl, varakukatir, varakuciṉi, varakutāḷ, varaku patar, varakuñāṟci. (piṟa īṟukaḷum koḷka.)
urupupuṇarcci pōla iṉcāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu : varakiṉi - varakiṉ katir    (to.e. 412 nac. uri)
uyir mayaṅkiyal  -
itu tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattu ēḻām iyal. puṇarcci paṟṟiya akattōttiṉ mutal iyal itu. itaṉkaṇ akaram mutal oḷakāram īṟākiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirkaḷiyum īṟāka uṭiya coṟkaḷ, alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum, perumpāṉmi vaṉkaṇattoṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷoṭum puṇarum ceyki nili 93 nuṟpākkaḷil virittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikara aikāra īṟṟu alvaḻi  muṭipum, iyalpukaṇattiṉ potuppuṇarcci muṭipum tokimarapiṉuḷ kūṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ (to.e.158, 144 nac.), ēṉiyavē uyirmayaṅkiyalil kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
uyirmey  -
uyirum meyyum iṇintu tōṉṟum cārpeḻuttu uyir meyyām. mey uyiroṭu kūṭumiṭattu - akarattoṭu kūṭumvaḻip puḷḷi nīṅkiya taṉ vaṭivē vaṭivākavum, ākārat toṭu kūṭumvaḻip puḷḷi nīṅkiya vaṭivoṭu kālpeṟṟum, ikara īkāraṅkaḷoṭu kūṭumvaḻi mēl vilaṅkum - ukara ūkāraṅkaḷoṭu kūṭumvaḻik kīḻvilaṅkum - ekara ēkāra aikāraṅkaḷōṭu kūṭumvaḻi avvavaṟṟik kuṟikkum kompum - okara ōkāraṅkaḷoṭu kūṭumvaḻik kompum kālum, oḷakārattoṭu kūṭumvaḻi ataṟkeṉa uriya kompoṭu kūṭiya kālum  peṟṟu vaṭivu  tirintum, meyyiṉmēl ēṟiya uyireḻut tiṉ māttiriyē taṉakkuriya māttiriyāy, uyir vaṭivattip peṟātu, uyirmey eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ mey yoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉumāy olikkum cār peḻuttu uyirmeyyeḻuttām. paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum patiṉeṭṭu meyyuṭaṉ porunta 216 uyirmey yeḻuttut tōṉṟum.
