(naṉ.89)
uyirmey: ilakkaṇam  -
appoṭu peyta uppē pōla, uyiroṭu puṇarttiya mey taṉṉaḷavu tōṉṟātu oṉṟāy niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟumāy, oṟṟu muṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāy olittu niṉṟa neṟiyāṉ iraṇṭumāy niṟṟaliṉ, ‘eṇ’ atikārattuḷ uyirmeyyi oṉṟākac coṉṉa āciriyar ‘īṟu’ atikārattuḷ iraṇ ṭāka vittu ilakkaṇam kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟṟāṉ uyirmey eṉṉum col, māttiri vakiyāṉ ummittokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyākavum, olivaki yāṉ ummittokiyākavum koḷḷappaṭum.	(naṉ. 109 caṅkara.)
uyirmey īṟu  -
coṟkaḷiṉ īṟukaḷi uyirīṟu meyyīṟu eṉṟu iraṇṭākap pakuttuk kāṇumiṭattu, uyirmey, meyyoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉumāka olittal eṉṟa toṭarpu paṟṟi, uyirmey īṟu uyirīṟākavē koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. e. 106 nac.)
uyirmey īṟu uyirīṟē -
moḻikaḷiṉ īṟṟilē niṉṟa uyirmeyyi uyirīṟu eṉalām;  eṉṉi? ‘meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu uyirtōṉṟu niliyē’ (to.nūṉ. 18) eṉṟār ākaliṉ. ‘uyirmey īṟum uyirīṟṟu iyaṟṟē’ (to. puṇa. 4) eṉpataṉāl, pirittāl uṭal muṉ uyir piṉṉām ātalāṉ eṉak koḷka. aṉṟiyum, vāḷum kūṭum iruppiṉ, “vāḷik koṭuvā” eṉṉum attuṇi yallatu, “kūṭṭik koṇṭuvā” eṉpatu illi. ātalāṉ iṅṅaṉam collappaṭṭatu.	(nēmi. eḻut. 8 uri)
uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷciṟappila eṉpatu  -
eḻuttatikāram ceyki paṟṟiyatu ātalāṉum, ceykikkup payaṉpaṭuvaṉa uyirum meyyumākiya mutaleḻuttukkaḷē ātalāṉum, uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷip pirittu moḻi mutaṟ kaṇvariṉ meyyeḻuttu eṉavum, moḻiiṟutikkaṇ variṉ uyireḻuttu eṉavum pakuttuk koḷḷutalāṉ uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷ ciṟappila eṉpatu.	(e. ā. pak. 5)
uyirmey: oṟṟumi nayam, vēṟṟumi nayam  -
oṟṟuminayam eṉpataṉkaṇ pala eḻuttukkaḷ orē iṭattil piṟappatum, pala eḻuttukkaḷ oliaḷavāṉ oṉṟāka iruttalum koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
u ū o ō ōḷa- eṉpaṉa pala eḻuttukkaḷ orē iṭattil piṟappataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. kākki, kōṅku - eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa meykaḷ ōriṭattil piṟantu oliyaḷavāṉ arimāttiri olittal eṉṉum taṉmiyil oṉṟu paṭṭami oṟṟuminayamām.
toṭarum eḻuttukkaḷ vevvēṟiṭattil piṟattal vēṟṟumi nayam eṉṟa karuttil, vēyka ūrka vīḻka - eṉa yaraḻa-kkaḷ muṉpu kakaram varutal vēṟṟuminayam eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(e.ā)
uyirmeyyiṉit taṉi ōreḻuttākak kōṭal oṟṟumi nayam.
‘lakaram ṟakaroṟṟu ākalum urittē’ - pala eṉpataṉ lakaruyirmey ‘lakaram’ eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭatum,
‘āyiṭi varutal ikāra rakāram’ - ‘rakāram’ eṉpatu uyir-meyyāyt toḷḷāyiram eṉpataṉkaṇ varutal kuṟikkap paṭṭatum,
‘muṉṉar tōṉṟum lakāra makāram’ - ‘polam’ eṉpataṉ lakaruyirmey ‘lakāram’ eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭatum
eṉṉum ivi oṟṟumi nayamām.	(to. e. 214, 463, 356 nac.) (e.ā.pak. 137)
iṉi, uyirmeyyiṉi meyyeḻuttākak kōṭal vēṟṟumi nayam ‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’ (to. e. 19 nac.) - iṅkuk kakaram mutaliya uyirmeykaḷ kakaroṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭutal vēṟṟumi nayam.
uyirmeyyiṉi ōreḻuttākak koṇṭu māttiri kōṭal oṟṟumi nayam. avviṭattu ‘uyirmey’ ummittokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittoki. uyirmeyyiṉi oli paṟṟi īreḻuttāka eṇṇutal vēṟṟumi nayam. vēṟṟumi koḷḷu miṭattē ‘uyirmey’ ummittokiyām. 	(to. e. 17 iḷa. uri)
uyirmeykku māttiri koḷḷuṅkāl, uppum nīrum pōla oṉṟēyāy niṟṟal oṟṟuminayam. ataṉi vēṟupaṭuttuk kūṟuṅkāl viralum viralum cēra niṉṟāṟpōla vēṟāy niṟṟal vēṟṟuminayam.	(to. e. 18 nac. uri)
uyirmey cārpeḻuttātal  -
uyirmey eṉpataṉi oṟṟuminayam koḷvaḻi ummit toki nilikkaḷattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittokiyāka vum, vēṟṟuminalam koḷvaḻi ummittokiyākavum koḷka. uyirum meyyum kūṭukiṉṟa kūṭṭattiṉi ‘ellā meyyum’ eṉa meymēl vittuk kūṟiyatu, atu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭutal nōkkippōlum. iṅṅaṉam varutalāṉ ‘uyir mey’ cārpeḻuttu eṉa mutaleḻut tiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.	(i. vi. 18 uri)
uyirmeynnili tam iyal mayakkam kiḷattal  -
uyirmey eḻuttil paṉṉīruyirum, meyyiṉ taṉmiyākiya vaṉmi - meṉmi - iṭimi - eṉpavaṟṟil, tammuṭiya kuṟumi neṭumi eṉṟa taṉmikaḷ iṇintaṉavākak kūṟutal. uyirmeyk kuṟil neṭilkaḷi valleḻuttu melleḻuttu iṭiyeḻuttu - eṉṟu kuṟippiṭutal ik karuttup paṟṟiyē.	(to. e. 47 nac. uri)
uyirmey piṟakkumāṟu -
vāyitaḻkaḷiṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu kōṇaṅkaḷil veḷivarukiṉṟa uyirppu iciyiṉi, nāvāṉatu aṇpalloṭum aṇṇat toṭum uṟṟum, uṟaḻntum, itaḻāṉatu palloṭum itaḻoṭum iyintum, taṭippaṭuttiyum veḷippaṭuttum nilimiyāl varuvaṉa uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷām.	(to.e. pak. 10. ca. pāla.)
uyirmey: peyarkkāraṇam  -
paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meyyoṭum taṉittaṉik kūṭa, meyyoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉumāy amiyum kalappeḻuttu uyirmeyyām. ākavē, uyirmey eṉpatu olippu vakiyāl ummittoki; māttiri kōṭaṟkaṇ ummit toki aṉmoḻi eṉka.	(to. e. 17 nac. uri)
uyirmey mayakku -
meyyoṭu mey mayaṅkutalaṉṟi, uyiruṭaṉ meyyum mey yuṭaṉ uyirum māṟi uyirum meyyum mayaṅkum mayakkattiṟku variyaṟi iṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu mayaṅkum. iṉi ‘uyir mey mayakku aḷaviṉṟē’ eṉpataṟku uyirmey muṉ uyirmey mayaṅkutal variyaṟi iṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar. iṅṅaṉam uyirmeyyi iraṇṭeḻut tākap pirittu mayakkavitiyum kūṟum āciriyar ataṉiyē oṉṟāka vittu mayakkaviti kūṟār ātalāṉum, kūṟiṉum iṭinili mayakkam muḻutum ic cūttirattuḷ aṭaṅkāmi yāṉum atu poruntātu.	(naṉ. 110 caṅkara.)
uyirmeyyiṉatu māttiri  -
uyirmey meyyum uyirum iṇintu piṟappatōr eḻuttu eṉiṉum, uppu nīril karintu taṉṉaḷavu keṭutalpōla, meyyiṉ oli uyiroliyil karintuviṭuvatāl, uyir meykku māttiri uyiriṉatu māttiriyēyām. ākavē, uyirmeykkuṟil oru māttiriyum, uyirmeyneṭil iru māttiriyum peṟum eṉpatu. olivakiyāṉ ummit tokiyākum ‘uyirmey’, māttiri vakiyāṉ ummit tokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyām.	(to. e. 17 nac. uri)
uyirmeyyiṉip pirittuk kāṇṭal  -
uyirmeyyil mey, taṉṉoṭu kūṭiniṉṟa uyir puṇarcci yiṭattup pirintu vēṟu niṉṟatāyiṉ, tāṉ muṉṉarp peṟṟa puḷḷi vaṭivē peṟum. 
	e-ṭu : ālili - āl+ili; ataṉi - atu+aṉ+ai	(to. e. 139 nac.)
uyirmey mutaliya oṉpatum cārpeḻuttu āmāṟu  -
uyirmey eṉpatu cārpeḻuttu āmāṟu ‘uyirmey cārpeḻuttātal’ eṉṟa talippil kāṇka.
kaḵṟītu - muḵṭītu - eṉpaṉavaṟṟi meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci eṉṟa taṉāṉum,  ‘āytappuḷḷi’ eṉac cūttiram ceytata ṉāṉum, itaṉiyum uṭaṉkūṭṭi oṟṟaḷapeṭi patiṉoṉṟu eṉṟataṉālum, āytam oṟṟiṉpāṟpaṭuvatēṉum, uyir ēṟātu ōcivikāramāy iṭam paṟṟi nikaḻvatoṉṟu ākaliṉ, cārpeḻutteṉa oṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.
kōṭṭu nūṟum mañcaḷum kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cevvaṇṇam pōla, uyiraḷapeṭi neṭilum kuṟilum kūṭiya kūṭṭattil piṟantu piṉ piḷavupaṭātu olikkiṉṟa oṉṟu; eḷ āṭṭiya vaḻiyallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭātavāṟu pōla, neṭilum kuṟilum kūṭi olikkum kūṭṭattallatu atu pulappaṭṭu nillātu. iyaṟki yaḷapeṭiyum ceyyuṭkup pulavar ceytukoṇṭa ceyaṟki yaḷapeṭiyum eṉa iraṇṭu tiṟattatāy, alaku peṟātum alaku peṟṟum atu niṟpatu. ātaliṉ uyiraḷapeṭi cārpeḻutteṉa uyiriṉ vēṟā yiṟṟu.
oṟṟaḷapeṭi orumāttiriyāy alaku peṟutalāṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum cārpeḻuttu eṉa oṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅkōlākātu cantaṉak kōlēyām. atupōla, uyiriṉatu kuṟukkamum uyirēyām eṉiṉum, puṇarcci vēṟṟumiyāṉum. poruḷ vēṟṟumi yāṉum, cīrum taḷiyum citiyumiṭattē alakukāriyam peṟāmiyāṉum kuṟṟikaramum kuṟṟukaramum cārpeḻuttu eṉa uyiriṉ vēṟāyiṉa.
ki - pi - mi - eṉpaṉavum, ku - veḷa - eṉpaṉavum poruḷic cuṭṭiyavaḻik kuṟukum. iṅṅaṉam (oru māttiri yum oṉṟari māttiriyumāka) aḷavu kuṟukutalāṉum, cīrum taḷiyum citiyumiṭattē alaku peṟāmiyāṉum aikāra oḷakārak kuṟukkaṅkaḷ cārpeḻuttu eṉa, uyiriṉ vēṟāyiṉa.
kāl māttiriyāka aḷavu kuṟukiyolikkum makarakkuṟukka mum cārpeḻuttu eṉa oṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟṟāṉ uyirmey mutalāya oṉpatum cārpeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭaṉa.	(i. vi. 18, 17, 19, 20, 16, 21, 22 uri)
uyirmey mutaliyaṉa cārpeḻuttu ākāmi -
tamakkeṉap piṟappiṭam oṉṟu taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil iṉṟit tām cārnta eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamē tam piṟappiṭa māyk koṇṭu olikkum kuṟṟiyalikaram- kuṟṟiyalukaram - āytam - eṉṟa mūṉṟumē cārpeḻuttām.
taṉi eḻuttukkaḷākiya kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum taṉittō, akaruyiric cārntō iyaṅkum āṟṟaliṉṟi moḻiyiṭip paṭuttē uṇarappaṭa vēṇṭuta liṉ cārpeḻuttāyiṉa. ēṉiya uyirmey mutaliyaṉa cārpeḻuttu ākā.
āl eṉpuḻi uyir muṉṉum meypiṉṉum mayaṅkiṉāṟ pōla, lā eṉpuḻi mey muṉṉum uyir piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉavē allatu, uyirum meyyumākiya tam taṉmi tirintu vēṟāyiṉa alla;uyirmey ākiya kālattum, kuṟiṉmi neṭiṉmi eṉṟa uyirttaṉmiyum vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi eṉṟa meyttaṉmiyum tam iyalpiṉ tirivupaṭavilli. ‘uṭalmēl uyirvantu oṉṟuvatu’ ‘poṉmaṇi’ pōla iyalpupuṇarcciyēyām. puṇarcciyil meyyiyum uyiriyum pirittuk koḷvar. ‘tuṇaṅki’ eṉpatu mey mutal uyirīṟu eṉavum, ‘varaku’ eṉpatu uyirt toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉavum kūṟumiṭattē, uyir mey uyirākavum meyyākavum pakuttuk koḷḷappaṭu kiṟatu. ātaliṉ kalappeḻuttākiya uyirmey cārpeḻuttu ākātu.
aḷapeṭi, neṭṭeḻuttōṭu iṉamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttu ottu niṉṟu nīṇṭicippatoṉṟu āyiṉum, moḻik kāraṇamāy vēṟuporuḷ tārātu iciniṟittal māttiri payattatāy niṟṟaliṉ vēṟeḻuttu eṉṟu vittu eṇṇappaṭātatāyiṟṟu.
aikārak kuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarakkuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉpaṉa oru kāraṇam paṟṟik kuṟukiṉa ātaliṉ, ciṟumaram peruttuḻiyum perumaram ciṟuttuḻiyum vēṟoru maram ākātatu pōla, vēṟeḻuttu eṉappaṭā.
vaṭaveḻuttuḷ uyireḻuttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vitta iraṇṭum, oṟṟeḻuttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vitta iraṇṭum, valleḻuttiṉ muṉ melleḻuttu vantu mayaṅkuḻi av valleḻuttōṭu oppa iṭiyē tōṉṟum eṉappaṭṭa viyama eḻuttum eṉṉum ivaṟṟiyē cārpeḻuttāka vaṭanūlār koṇṭaṉar.
kālmāttiri peṟum vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram māttirik kuṟukkam paṟṟit taṉiyeḻuttākak koḷḷappaṭa villi. ivaṟṟi nōkkac cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟēyām eṉpatu.	(cū. vi. pak. 29, 30)
uyirmey mutaliyaṉa cārpeḻuttātal  -
cārpeḻutteṉa mūṉṟē koṇṭār tolkāppiyaṉār. avar koṇṭa kuṟṟiyalikaram - kuṟṟiyalukaram - āytam - eṉṟa mūṉṟum nīṅkalāṉa uyirmey mutaliya ēḻum cārpil tōṉṟutalāṉum, mutaleḻuttām taṉmi avaṟṟiṟku iṉmiyāṉum, mutalum cārpu maṉṟi mūṉṟāvatoru pakuti collalāvatu iṉmiyāṉum, uyirmey mutaliya pattum cārpākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatu.	(naṉ. 59 mayili.)
uyir meyyōṭu iyital  -
 uyirmey eṉṟa kalappeḻutti uṇṭākka uyirāṉatu meyyoṭu poruntum. aṅṅaṉam poruntiṉum atu taṉ māttiriyum peyarum eṇṇum tirintu nillātu.
a eṉpuḻi niṉṟa oru māttiriyum, kuṟil eṉṟa peyarum, oṉṟu eṉṟa eṇṇum ka eṉpuḻiyum okkum. ā eṉpuḻi niṉṟa iru māttiriyum, neṭil eṉṟa peyarum, oṉṟu eṉṟa eṇṇum kā eṉpuḻiyum okkum piṟavum aṉṉa.	(to. e. 10. nac. uri)
uyirmey vaṭivu meyyiṉ vēṟātal  -
puḷḷimāttiramē peṟum meypōlaṉṟi, uyirmeykaḷ kompum, kālum, kaṭṭum, vīccum eṉa ivi vēṟupaṭutaliṉ, varivaṭivu palavāka vēṟupaṭṭu varuvaṉavām. 	(nēmi. eḻut. 7 uri)
ēṉi uyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttal - 			(iḷa., nac. uri) (to. e. 17)
uyirviṉāvuṭaṉ yāviṉāvum kūṭṭiyurittal -
ā e ē ō eṉṟa uyirviṉāvuṭaṉē yā eṉṉum uyirmey viṉāvik kūṭṭiyurittami mayaṅkak kūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟam ākātu. eṉṉiyeṉil, itu tokivakiviripaṭac ceykiṉṟa nūl ākalāṉum, mutalviṉā (e, yā) eṉṉum poruḷ oppumiyāṉum eṉpatu.	(naṉ.66 mayili.)
uyiratu kuṟukkam uyirē -
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅkōl ākātu cantaṉak kōlē āmāṟu pōla, uyirkaḷiṉ kuṟukkamākiya kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram aikārak kuṟukkam eṉpaṉa uyirēyām.
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉa puṇarcci vēṟṟumiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumiyum paṟṟi vēṟeḻuttukkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ākiyōratu karuttām.	(to. e. 2 iḷa., nac. uri)
uyiratu kuṟumi neṭumi aḷaviṟ kōṭal  -
uyir oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟāka olippiṉ kuṟil, atē uyir iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṟṟāka olippiṉ neṭilām. ākavē, oliyiṉ kuṟukkam nīṭṭam ivaṟṟik koṇṭē kuṟilum neṭilum tōṉṟiṉa. eṉavē, puṇarcciyiṭattuk kuṟiliṇiyi neṭilākak koḷḷalām. uyirmeykkum iḵtu okkum.
	e-ṭu	:	kōḷ+ nimirntatu	=	kōṇimirntatu
			kuṟaḷ + nimirntatu	=	kuṟaṇimirntatu
taṉineṭili yaṭutta oṟṟukkaḷukkuk kūṟum puṇarcciviti kuṟiliṇi oṟṟukkum, ciṟupāṉmi kuṟilneṭil oṟṟukkum (varāl + nimirntatu = varāṉimirntatu; kōl + nimirntatu = kōṉimirntatu) koḷḷappaṭutaṟku ituvē amitiyām.		(to. e. 50 iḷa. 161 uri)
uyiraḷapeṭi (1) -
eḻuttup pala āyiṉa olivēṟṟumiyāṉaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉam ātaliṉ neṭilatu vikāramāy ōroliyāyp piṟappatē aḷapeṭi eṉpār ‘neṭil aḷapeḻum’ eṉṟum, ‘avaṟṟa vaṟṟu iṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum nīrum nīrum cērntāṟpōla neṭ ṭeḻuttoṭu kuṟṟeḻuttu ottuniṉṟu nīṇṭicippatē aḷapeṭi eṉpār, `kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum neṭṭeḻut, timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē’ (eḻut. 41) eṉṟār. ippeṟṟi aṟiyātār, neṭilum kuṟilum viralum viralum cēra niṉṟāṟpōla iṇintu niṉṟu aḷapeṭukkum eṉat tamakku vēṇṭiyavāṟē kūṟupa. neṭi lum kuṟilum avvāṟu niṉṟu aḷapeṭukkum eṉṟal poruntāmikku `eḻutteṭi’ eṉṉātu aḷapeṭi eṉ ṉum kuṟiyīṭē cāṉṟātal aṟika. aṟṟēl, ōr eḻuttiṉiyē iraṇṭu māttiriyum oru māttiriyumākap pirittu acittu ataṉāl cīrceytu taḷiyaṟuttal poruntātu eṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; ‘eḻuttu vakiyāṉ’ eṉṉātu,
‘māttiri vakiyāṉ taḷitama keṭānili
yāppaḻi yāmieṉṟu aḷapeṭi vēṇṭum’
eṉak kūṟupa ātaliṉ, eḻuttiṟku māttiri kōṭalum acittalum cīrceytalum taḷiyaṟuttalum ōcipaṟṟi yallatu eḻuttup paṟṟi alla eṉka.	(naṉ. 91 civañā.)
uyiraḷapeṭi (2) -
uyiruḷ neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻum aḷapeṭukkum. avi aḷapeṭukku miṭattut taṉinili - mutaṉili - iṭinili - iṟutinili- eṉṉum nāṉkaṉōṭum uṟaḻa 7o 4 =28 ām.
eṉṉi?
‘kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum
neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē’
‘ai oḷa eṉṉum āyīr eḻuttiṟku
ikara ukaram iciniṟivu ākum’	(to. e. 41, 42.)
eṉṟārākaliṉ.
	varalāṟu : āa, īi, ūu, ēe, aii, ōo, oḷu - eṉpaṉa taṉinili aḷapeṭi.
		māari, vīiram, kūuri, ēeri, tiiyal, ōori, oḷuvi - ivi mutaṉili aḷapeṭi
	paṭāaki, parīiyam, kaḻuumaṇi, parēeram, vaḷiiyam, urōocam, anuukam - ivi iṭinili aḷapeṭi. 
	palāa, kurīi, kaḻuu, vilēe, virii, ulōo, anuu - ivi iṟutinili aḷapeṭi. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(nēmi. eḻut. 3 uri)
uyiraḷapeṭi irupattoṉṟu ātal -
ceppalōci mutaliya ōci kuṉṟātu neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻum moḻi mutaliṭi kaṭikaḷil niṉṟu aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl, oḷakāram moḻi iṭikaṭikaḷil varappeṟāmiyāl, avviṭaṅkaḷil aḵtu oḻiya niṉṟu aḷapeṭukkum aḷapeṭi pattoṉpatōṭu, iṉṉici niṟippavum collici niṟippavum aḷapeṭukkum aḷapeṭi iraṇṭum kūṭṭi, uyiraḷapeṭi eḻumūṉṟāy varumāṟu kāṇka.	(naṉ. 91 civañā.)
uyiraḷapeṭi cārpeḻuttātal  -
kōṭṭu nūṟum mañcaḷum kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cevvaṇṇam pōla, neṭilum kuṟilum kūṭiya kūṭṭattil piṟantu piṉ piḷavu paṭātu olikkiṉṟatu oṉṟu ākalāṉum, eḷḷāṭṭiya vaḻiyallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭāvāṟu pōla neṭilum kuṟilum kūṭiya kūṭṭat tallatu pulappaṭātu niṟpatu oṉṟu ākalāṉum, atutāṉ iyaṟki yaḷapeṭiyum ceyyuṭkup pulavar ceytukoṇṭa ceyaṟki yaḷapeṭiyumāy alaku peṟātum peṟṟum niṟpatu oṉṟu ākalāṉum, cārpeḻuttu eṉa uyiriṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.	(i.vi. 19)
uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyir vantu puṇarumāṟu paṟṟi vīracōḻiyam kuṟippiṭuvatu -
ikara īkāra aikāra uyirīṟṟu nilimoḻi muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya varumoḻi puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyē yakāra oṟṟu vantu tōṉṟum. ivi allāta maṟṟa uyirīṟṟu nilimoḻi muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya varumoḻi puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyē vakāra oṟṟu vantu tōṉṟum. ēkāra īṟṟu nili moḻi muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya varumoḻi puṇarumiṭattu yakāra vakāra oṟṟukkaḷ iraṇṭum vantu tōṉṟum. orō viṭattu nilimoḻiyiṉatu īṟṟil niṉṟa patamāvatu, uyir meyyāvatu, uyirāvatu, oṟṟāvatu aḻintu puṇarum.	(vī.cō. cantip. 13)
vīracōḻiyattilum ataṉ uriyilumē, uṭampaṭumeyyām eḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟiyum, nilimoḻi iṉṉa īṟṟukku iṉṉa uṭampaṭumey eṉpatu paṟṟiyum mutaṉmutalāka variyaṟi kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉi yoṭṭiyē naṉṉūl vitikkum.
nilimoḻiyīṟu keṭṭu muṭivatu nēminātattil viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
	varumāṟu : orupatu + oṉṟu ḻū orupatu+āṉ + oṉṟu = orupāṉoṉṟu - nilimoḻiyil patu eṉṟa patattiṉ ‘atu’ keṭṭatu.
	vāḻiya + cāttā =vāḻicāttā - nili moḻi yil yakara uyirmey keṭṭatu.
	paṉi + kāy ḻū paṉi + am + kāy ḻū paṉ + am + kāy =paṉaṅkāy - nilimoḻiyil īṟṟu aikāruyir keṭṭatu.
	maram+aṭi ḻū mara+aṭi =maravaṭi - nili moḻiyīṟṟu makaramey keṭṭatu. (vakaram : uṭampaṭumey)	(nēmi. eḻut. 19, 13 uri)
uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ vaṉkaṇa mutal moḻi -
iyalpiṉālum vitiyiṉāṉum iṟutiyāka niṉṟa (nili moḻiyīṟṟu) uyirkaḷiṉ muṉṉar (varumoḻi mutalil) varum kacatapa-kkaḷ perumpālum mikum. 
viti uyirīṟāvaṉa:muṉṉiya uyirīṟum meyyīṟum oḻiya uyirīṟāy niṟpaṉavum, yātāṉumōr uyir iṟutikkaṇ tōṉṟi niṟpaṉavum ām.
e-ṭu:	nampik koṟṟaṉ - uyartiṇikkaṇ iyalpu ikara īṟu vali mikkatu; āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ - uyartiṇikkaṇ viti ukarīṟu valimikkatu; cāttip peṇ - viravut tiṇikkaṇ iyalpu  ikarīṟu vali mikkatu; tārāk kaṭitu, oṟṟikki - aḵṟiṇikkaṇ iyalpu ākāra īṟum viti aikārīṟum vali mikkaṉa.
	vaṭṭak kal, tāḻakkōl - aḵṟiṇikkaṇ viti akarīṟukaḷ vali mikkaṉa.
ivi alvaḻip puṇarcci.
	nampippū, āṭūuk ki - uyartiṇikkaṇ iyalpu ikarīṟum viti ukarīṟum vali mikkaṉa. viḷakkōṭu, kaṭukkāy, āṭṭukkāl - aḵṟiṇikkaṇ iyalpu akara ukara īṟum vitik kuṟṟiyalukarīṟum vali mikkaṉa. ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci.
āṭik koṇṭāṉ, āṭāk koṇṭāṉ, āṭūuk koṇ ṭāṉ, āṭeṉak koṇṭāṉ, āṭak koṇṭāṉ, uṇ pākkuc ceṉṟāṉ, pūttuk kāyttatu, poḷḷeṉap parantatu, cālap pakittatu, iruḷiṉṟik kaṇṭāṉ - pala vakit teri nili kuṟippu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷiṉ uyirīṟukaḷ valimikkaṉa.
maṟṟic cāti, kaṭik kamalam, coṉṟik kuḻici, kaṅkik kari- iṭi uri tici vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ uyirīṟukaḷ valimikkaṉa.
nok koṟṟā, tuk koṟṟā - uyirīṟṟu ēval muṉ vali mikkaṉa.	(naṉ. 165 caṅ.)
uyirīṟṟup puṇariyal eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu -
potuvakiyāl puṇarum iyalpum, pala ceytikaḷi uḷḷaṭakkit tokkup puṇarum iyalpum, kuṟṟukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ niṉṟu puṇarum iyalpum ākiya piṟa puṇarcci iyalpukaḷum ivviyalil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. talimi paṟṟi ‘uyirīṟṟup puṇariyal’ eṉṟa vicēṭaṇam koṭukkappaṭṭatu. ivvicēṭaṇam, muṟkūṟiya piṟa puṇarcci iyalpukaḷi uṇarttutalōṭu iyipu nīkkātu, uyirīṟu puṇartal ākiya taṉṉōṭu iyipiṉmi māttiri nīkkiyatu. ik kuṟiyīṭu ‘ā tīṇṭu kuṟṟi’ eṉpatu pōlat talimi paṟṟiya aṭiyaṭuttu vantatu.	(i. vi. eḻut. 53 uri)
uyirīṟṟu marappeyarp puṇarcci  -
uyirīṟṟu marappeyar muṉ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi, potuviti yāl iṭiyē valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi, avvalleḻut tukku iṉamāṉa melleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu. itu vēṟṟumip puṇarcci.
	e-ṭu : palākkāy, ilantikkaṉi : valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
		viḷaṅkāy, kaḷaṅkaṉi, māṅkompu, māmpaḻam: iṉamelleḻuttu mikkaṉa.	(naṉ. 166)
uyireḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇam -
uyireḻuttu utāṉaṉ eṉṉum mēleḻum ōcikkāṟṟi ṉāl tōṉṟi, vāyuṟuppukkaḷiṉ oṟṟutal toḻiliṉṟi itaḻkaḷiṉ kōṇattāl miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyālē, kaṇṭatti ṉiṉṟu tam nili tiriyāmal uyirppoṭu piṟantu cevip pulaṉākum; taṉittum, meyyiṉi ūrntum, collāyum, colluṟuppāyum amintu poruḷ kuṟittu niṟkum; meyyeḻuttukkaḷ uyirmeyyeḻuttu āvataṟkut tuṇi yāy avaṟṟi iyakki avaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭamē iṭamākat tōṉṟi varum; taṉittu icikkuṅkāl, kuṟumi neṭumi koḷḷum;uyirmeykkut tuṇiyākuṅkāl, vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi koḷḷum;  taṉittum meyyiṉi ūrntum ceyyuṭkaṇ pala ōcikaḷi nikaḻtti alaku peṟum; paṇṇicikkum vaṇṇattiṟkum kāraṇamāki niṟkum. akaram tavirtta ēṉiya uyireḻuttukkaḷ mey yolikaḷic ciṟitu tiriyac ceyyum. yāvum avaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟumi neṭumikaḷik kaṟpittalic ceyyum. uyireḻut tukkaḷ moḻi mutaṟkaṇ taṉittum, iṭiyilum īṟṟilum aḷapeṭiyākavum, vikāramākavum varum; tammuṉ tām toṭara nēriṉ, uṭampaṭumey peṟṟut toṭarum; uyir meykkut tuṇi yāki varuṅkāl, varivaṭiviṉ kaṇ tam kūṟukaḷi niṟuttit tam varivaṭivi iḻantuviṭum.		(to. e. pak. 18, 19 ca. pāla.)
uyireḻuttup piṟakkumāṟu -
vāyuṟuppukkaḷiṉ ceyaṟpāṭiṉṟi avi camaniliyil niṟka, vāy aṅkāppa,miṭaṟṟu eḻu vaḷiyiciyāka veḷippaṭum uyirppu iciyē akaram eṉṉum aṭippaṭi eḻuttām. ivakaram ellā eḻuttukkaḷukkum mūlamākavum tuṇi yākavum akaniliyil eḻuttām. aḵtāvatu akara oliyiṉ tiripukaḷē palvēṟu eḻuttukkaḷākac cevippulaṉām. akara eḻuttē vāyitaḻkaḷiṉ kōṇattālum, nāviḷimpiṉ virivālum curukkattālum, ēṉiya patiṉoru vakiyākiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttukkaḷāka nikaḻkiṟatu.	(to.e. pak. 10. ca. pāla.)
uralāṇi iṭṭāṟpōlac ceṟital  -
palakālum payaṉpaṭuttiyatāl uṭkuḻi tēyntu āḻnta paḷḷamāṉa uralil paḷḷatti maṟikkumpaṭi iṭum marāppu uralāṇi eṉappaṭum. avvuralāṇi paḷḷattil aḻuttamāka iṇin tirukkum. atupōla, y eṉṟa mey tōṉṟa, aṭinā mēlvāyi uralāṇi yiṭṭāṟpōlac ceṟiyum eṉpatu. 	(to. e. 99. nac. uri)
uyirik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ  -
accu eṉiṉum, āvi eṉiṉum, curam eṉiṉum, pūtam eṉiṉum, uyir eṉiṉum oru poruṭkiḷavi.	(mu. vī. e. 7)

urupiyal nuvalum ceyti  -
tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattiṉ āṟām iyalākiya urupiyal 30 nuṟpākkaḷi uṭiyatu. avi nuvalum ceyti- kaḷāvaṉa:
a ā u ū ē oḷa - eṉṟa āṟu īṟṟup peyarkaḷum vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷi ēṟkumpōtu potuvāka iṉcāriyi peṟum. cila akarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, yā eṉṟa ākāra īṟṟup peyarum vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟum. cuṭṭu mutal ukarīṟu aṉcāriyi peṟum. cuṭṭu mutalākiya aikāra īṟu vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟum. yāvi eṉpatum vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟum. nī eṉpatu niṉ eṉṟākum. ōkāra īṟu oṉcāriyi peṟum. aā īṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻaṉurupu ēṟkumpōtu 
attuc cāriyiyum peṟum. ñ n - īṟukaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟum. makarīṟu attum iṉṉum peṟum. ellām eṉpatu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ vaṟṟum, uyartiṇikkaṇ nammum peṟum. yāṉ, yām, nām, tāṉ, tām - eṉpaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukum. ellārum eṉpatu tammuccāriyiyum, ellīrum eṉpatu nummuccāriyiyum peṟum. aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ - eṉpaṉa attum iṉṉum peṟum. ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟum. kuṟṟiyalukara īṟukaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟum, avaṟṟuḷ eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ aṉcāriyi peṟum. aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉpaṉavum, yātu eṉpatum aṉcāriyi peṟum. ticip peyarkku muṉ ēḻaṉurupu variṉ iṉcāriyi peṟutalum, peṟāmal puṇartalum eṉa irutiṟaṉum uḷa.
iṉṉa ceytikaḷ nilimoḻikaḷ urupukaḷi varumoḻi yākak koṇṭu puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻvaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
uyirīṟṟuḷ ikarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, nī eṉṉum peya roṉṟum oḻinta īkārīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, avi ivi uvi yāvi- eṉṟa nāṉkum oḻinta ēṉiya aikārīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, tāṉ yāṉ - eṉṟa iraṇṭum oḻinta ēṉiya ṉakarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, ēḻ eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar oḻinta ēṉiya ḻakarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum - ivi yellām urupoṭu kūṭumiṭattuc cāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum puṇarum.
aāuūēōoḷa - eṉṟa ēḻ uyirīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, ñ n mv- eṉṟa nāṉku meyyīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, oṟṟu iṭi mikum īreḻuttoru moḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaḷ oḻinta ēṉiya peyarkaḷum, toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupoṭu kūṭumiṭattuc cāriyi peṟṟē varum.
num tām yām nām - eṉṟa makarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟa ṉakarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, iṭi oṟṟumikum īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟup peyarkaḷum cāriyi peṟāmal varum.
urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇṇum cellumiṭattu avi akattōttukkaḷil māṭṭēṟṟāṉ kuṟikkappaṭum.		(to. e. 173 - 202 nac.) (e. ā. pak. 133)
urupiṉ muṭivaṉa poruṭpuṇarcciyilum ottal  -
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ ellām eṉpatu aḵṟiṇi āṉakāli aṟṟuccāriyiyum urupiṉmēl ummum, uyartiṇi āṉa kāli nammuc cāriyiyum urupiṉmēl ummum peṟum eṉpār, vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum avvāṟē, ellāvaṟṟuk kōṭum - ellā naṅkiyum - eṉavum,
tāṉ tām nām - eṉpaṉa mutal kuṟukum eṉavum, yāṉ yām - eṉpaṉa eṉ em eṉavum nī eṉpatu niṉ eṉavum nīyir eṉpatu num eṉavum ākum eṉavum kūṟuvār, īṇṭum avvāṟē, taṉki taṅki naṅki - eṉavum, eṉki eṅki -eṉavum, niṉki numki - eṉavum,
ā mā kō - eṉpaṉa ṉakarac cāriyi peṟum eṉpār, īṇṭum avvāṟē āṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu kōṉkuṇam - eṉavum,
cuṭṭu mutal vakaram aṟṟuccāriyi peṟum eṉpār, īṇṭum avvāṟē aṟṟup peṟṟu avaṟṟukkōṭu - eṉavum,
muṟiyē koḷḷavippatu.	(naṉ. 237 mayili.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu attuccāriyi peṟuvaṉa -
akara ākāra īṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻaṉurupu ēṟkumiṭattu iṭiyē attuccāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu. makarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṟa ṉakarīṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupukaḷ ēṟkumiṭattu attuccāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu	: viḷa +kaṇ ḻū viḷa + attu + kaṇ = viḷavattuk kaṇ; palā+ kaṇ ḻū palā + attu + kaṇ = palā vattukkaṇ (to. e. 181 nac.); maram + ai  ḻū maram + attu + ai = maratti (to.e. 185 nac.); aḻaṉ + ai ḻū aḻaṉ + attu + ai  = aḻatti; puḻaṉ + ai ḻū puḻaṉ + attu + ai = puḻatti. 	(to.e. 193 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu aṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa -
atu itu utu - aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu - yātu - oṉṟu mutal pattu īṟāya eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ - ākiyavi aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum. 
varumāṟu:	atu + aṉ + ai = ataṉi; itu + aṉ +  ai = itaṉi; utu + aṉ +  ai = utaṉi 	(to. e. 176 nac.)
		aḵtu +  aṉ +  ai = ataṉi; iḵtu + aṉ + ai = itaṉi; uḵtu + aṉ +  ai = utaṉi 	(to. e. 200 nac.)
 (iṭiyē uḷḷa āytam keṭum eṉka) 
		yātu + aṉ + ai = yātaṉi 	(to.e. 200 nac.)
		oṉṟu + aṉ + ai = oṉṟaṉi	(to.e. 198 nac.)
		ēḻ + aṉ + ai = ēḻaṉi	(to.e. 194 nac.)
		pattu + aṉ + ai = pattaṉi	(to.e. 198 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu āṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa -
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ nilimoḻi yāy niṟka, pattu allatu paḵtu varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, pattu paḵtu - eṉṟa colliṉ pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalāka eñciyuḷḷa attu aḵtu keṭa, āṉcāriyiyum urupum puṇarum nilimiyum uṇṭu.
	varumāṟu :oru paḵtu+ ai ḻū  orup + āṉ + ai = orupāṉi
		eṇpaḵtu  + ai ḻū eṇp + āṉ + ai = eṇpāṉi
		orupatu + ai ḻū orup + āṉ + ai = orupāṉi
		oṉpatu + ai ḻū oṉp + āṉ + ai = oṉpāṉi	
		irupatu + ai = irupāṉi, muppatu + ai = muppāṉi.... mutalāyiṉa koḷka.				(to. e. 199 nac. uri)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu iṭioṟṟu iraṭṭittu urupu ēṟpaṉa -
ṭu ṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷum, ciṟupāṉmi uyirt toṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷum iṭiyē oṟṟu mikku iṉcāriyi peṟāmal urupēṟkum. piṟkālattē iṉcāriyi peṟum marapum ēṟpaṭṭatu.
e-ṭu :	yāṭu + ai  = yāṭṭi., yāṭṭiṉi; pāṟu + ai = pāṟṟi, pāṟṟiṉi; kuruṭu + ai = kuruṭṭi, kuruṭṭiṉi; muyiṟu + ai  = muyiṟṟi, muyiṟṟiṉi 	(to. e. 196, 197 nac. uri)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu iṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa -
aāuūēoḷa- ñ n mv ṉ - kuṟṟukaram - eṉpavaṟṟi īṟākak koṇṭa peyarkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟum.
e-ṭu	: viḷaviṉi, palāviṉi, kaṭuviṉi, taḻūviṉi, cēviṉi, veḷaviṉi	(to. e. 173 nac.)
		uriñiṉi, poruniṉi	-	(to.e. 182 nac.)
		urumiṉi		-	(to.e. 186 nac.)
		tevviṉi		-	(to.e. 184 nac.)
		aṉiṉi, puḻaṉiṉi		-	(to.e. 193 nac.)
		nākiṉi, varakiṉi		-	(to.e. 195 nac.)
	ōkāra īṟu :	kōviṉi, cōviṉi, ōviṉi - 180 nac. uri
urupiyal puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ uyirīṟṟuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra īṟukaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟṟum peṟātum urupēṟkum.
	e-ṭu : kiḷiyiṉi kiḷiyi; tīyiṉi, tīyi; tiṉiyiṉi, tiṉiyi puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ ṇakara yakara rakara lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum urupēṟkum.
	e-ṭu : maṇṇiṉi, maṇṇi; vēyiṉi, vēyi; nāriṉi, nāri;  kalliṉi, kalli; muḷḷiṉi, muḷḷi	(to.e. 202 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu oṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa -
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ ōkāra īṟṟup peyarkaḷ cila oṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum. e-ṭu : kō+oṉ+ai = kōoṉi		(to. e. 180 nac.)
itu piṟkālattu‘ṉ’ cāriyi āyiṟṟu. cō - mutaliya ōkāra īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ oṉcāriyi peṟum vaḻakku iruppatākat teriyavilli.
urupu ēṟkumpōtu tammuc cāriyi peṟuvatu  -
urupēṟkumiṭattu ellārum eṉpatu tammuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟṟu ummiyi iṟutikkaṇ koṇṭu, ellār tammiyum - ellārtammoṭum - ellārtamakkum - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(to. e. 191 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu toṭakkam kuṟukum peyarkaḷiṉ nili -
yāṉ yām nām nī tāṉ tām - eṉpaṉa toṭakkam kuṟuki muṟiyē eṉ em nam niṉ taṉ tam - eṉat tirintu, eṉṉi emmi nammi niṉṉi taṉṉi tammi - eṉṟāṟ pōla urupēṟkum.	(to. e. 179, 188, 192 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu nammuccāriyi peṟuvatu  -
ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar uyartiṇiyik kuṟikku miṭattu, nammuccāriyiyum urupiṉmēl ummum peṟum.
	varumāṟu :  ellānammiyum, ellānammoṭum (to. e. 190 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu nummuccāriyi peṟuvatu -
ellīrum eṉpatu nummuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟṟu ummi iṟutikkaṇ aṭiya, ellīrnummiyum, ellīrnummoṭum eṉṟāṟ pōla varum.	(to. e. 191 nac.)
urupu ēṟkumpōtu vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟuvaṉa -
palla pala cila uḷḷa illa - eṉṉum akara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ, yāviṉā, avi ivi uvi- eṉpaṉa, yāvi eṉpatu, ellām eṉṉum makarīṟṟup peyar, av iv uv - eṉṟa vakarīṟṟup peyarkaḷ - eṉpaṉa vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum.
	varumāṟu : pallavaṟṟi, palavaṟṟi, cilavaṟṟi, uḷḷa vaṟṟi, illavaṟṟi (to. e. 174 nac.); yāvaṟṟi	(to.e. 175 nac.); aviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟi (to.e. 177 nac.); yāvi + vaṟṟu + ai = yāvaṟṟi (to.e. 178 nac.); ellā vaṟṟiyum (to.e. 189 nac.); avaṟṟi, ivaṟṟi, uvaṟṟi (to.e. 183 nac.)
eṉa, akara ākāra aikāra makara vakara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ cila vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟṟaṉa.
urupukaḷ nāṟpatu āmāṟu -
peyar - ai - oṭu - ku - iṉ- atu - kaṇ- viḷi- eṉṟa eṭṭum, oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu pala - eṉṟa aimpāṟ peyaro ṭum uṟaḻa, vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ nāṟpatām. (nampi- nampiyi- nampiyoṭu- nampikku- nampiyiṉ- nampiyatu- nampikaṇ - nampiyē - eṉa ‘oruvaṉ’ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu āṇpāṟ peya rōṭu eṭṭu urupukaḷum vantaṉa. ēṉiya nāṉku pālkaṭkum ivvāṟē oṭṭik koḷka.)	(naṉ. 242)
urupukaḷ nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu puṇartal -
orupeyar urupēṟkiyil peyar nilimoḻiyākavum urupu varumoḻiyākavum irukkum. peyar avvurupiṉi ēṟṟapiṉ urupu peyariṉatu oru kūṟākit tāṉum peyaroṭu cērntu  nilimoḻiyāki varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum puṇarcci vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
	e-ṭu : nampiyik koṇarntāṉ, maliyoṭu porutatu, ūrkkuc ceṉṟāṉ.
ivvāṟu urupu nilimoḻi īṟāki varumoḻiyoṭu tirintum iyalpākavum puṇarntavāṟu. ivvakiyāka urupu virintu nilimoḻiyiṉ īṟāki varumoḻiyoṭu puṇar taṟkut tolkāppiyattil taṉiyē viti illi.	(to.e. 202 nac.)
urupu tirintu uyirttal -
varivaṭivu tirintu tōṉṟutal eṉpatu poruḷ. meykaḷ uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭumiṭattē, akarattoṭu kūṭiyavaḻip puḷḷi nīṅkiya tam paṇṭi vaṭivē vaṭivamāyum, ēṉiya uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭumvaḻit tam meyvaṭivil ciṟitu tiripu  koṇṭu mēlvilaṅku kīḻvilaṅku peṟṟum, kompu peṟṟum, kompum kālum peṟṟum, kālpeṟṟum varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭutal.
	varumāṟu : kā, ṅā - kālkaḷ peṟṟaṉa. ti, tī - mēl vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. pu, pū - kīḻ vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke, kē, ki - kompu peṟṟaṉa. ko, kō, ku - kompum kālum peṟṟaṉa.	(to. e. 17 nac. uri)
urupu puṇarcci  -
nilimoḻiyākiya peyarkaḷ, urupukaḷi varumoḻiyākak koṇṭu iṭiyē, īṟukaṭku ēṟpa iṉ aṉ attu vaṟṟu āṉ oṉ- mutaliya cāriyikaḷ peṟṟum peṟāmalum puṇarum puṇarcci urupupuṇarcciyām.
	e-ṭu : maram + attu + ai = maratti - attuccāriyi (to. e. 185 nac.); ā + ai = āṉi- (i)ṉcāriyi	(to.e. 120 nac.); poṉ + ai = poṉṉiṉi, poṉṉi; iṉcāriyi peṟṟum peṟātum urupoṭu puṇarntatu. (to.e. 202 nac.); mali + oṭu = mali yoṭu; cāriyi peṟātum īṟu tiriyātum urupoṭu puṇarntatu. (to.e. 202 nac.)
urupukaḷiṉ puṇarcci iṭiccoṟ puṇarcciyē eṉiṉum perumpālum vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpil amiyum. īṇṭu urupu eṉṟatu, eḻuvāyum viḷiyum oḻinta ēṉiya āṟu urupukaḷiyēyām.
urupoṭu puṇarum nilimikkaṇ, yāṉ yām nām nī nīr tāṉ tām -eṉṟa mūviṭap peyarkaḷ muṟiyē eṉ em nam niṉ num tam tam - eṉa neṭumutal kuṟuki varutal vēṟṟumip poruḷ nōkkam paṟṟi nikaḻvatātaliṉ urupupuṇarccikkuc ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭum. itu poruṭpuṇarccik kumokkum.	(naṉ. 242 caṅkara.)
urupupuṇarcci ciṟappu viti -
ellām eṉpatu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ vaṟṟuc cāriyiyum, uyartiṇikkaṇ nammuccāriyiyum peṟṟu urupēṟkum; urupiṉi aṭuttu ummi peṟum.
varumāṟu : ellāvaṟṟiyum, ellānammiyum
ellārum ellīrum - eṉpaṉa ellār ellīr eṉa muṟiyē niṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ muṟiyē tam num eṉṟa cāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇ ummi peṟṟu muṭiyum.
varumāṟu : ellārtammiyum, ellīrnummiyum
mūviṭap peyarkaḷ neṭumutal kuṟuki, eṉ em nam niṉ num taṉ tam eṉa niṉṟu urupoṭu puṇarum. nāṉkaṉurupu ēṟkumiṭattu im mutal kuṟukiya peyarkaḷ akarac cāriyi peṟum. nāṉkaṉurupum āṟaṉurupum ēṟkumiṭattu, eṉ em- mutaliya taṉikkuṟili aṭutta oṟṟu īṟāka aminta iccoṟkaḷ potuvitippaṭi īṟṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭamāṭṭā.
varumāṟu : eṉṉi, eṉṉāl, eṉṉiṉ, eṉkaṇ 
em, nam - mutaliyavaṟṟoṭum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. 
yāṉ + ku ḻū eṉ + ku ḻū eṉ + a + ku ḻū eṉa + ku  = eṉakku yāṉ + atu ḻū eṉ + atu =  eṉatu  piṟavaṟṟōṭum ivvāṟē oṭṭik kāṇka ā mā kō - eṉṟa peyarkaḷ ṉakarac cāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum urupēṟkum.
	varumāṟu : āṉi, āvi; māṉi, māvi: kōṉi, kōvi (vakaram : uṭampaṭumey)
orupatu mutal eṇpatu īṟākiya eṭṭu eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum oṉpatu eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarum urupoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, iṭiyē āṉcāriyi variṉ, ‘patu’ eṉpatil pakaramey nīṅkalāka ēṉiya keṭap puṇarntu muṭiyum.
varumāṟu: 	orupatu+āṉ+ ai ḻū orup + āṉ+ ai = oru pāṉi; eṇpatu+ āṉ + ai ḻū  eṇp + āṉ + ai =  eṇpāṉi; oṉpatu + āṉ + ai ḻū oṉp + āṉ + ai = oṉpāṉi
āṉcāriyi iṭiyē vārātoḻiyiṉ, orupati - orupaḵti, eṇpati - eṇpaḵti, oṉpati - oṉpaḵti - eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum.
av, iv, uv - eṉṟa cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ urupēṟpuḻi aṟṟuc cāriyi peṟṟu, avaṟṟi -ivaṟṟi - uvaṟṟi - eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum.
aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu - eṉṟa cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ urupēṟpuḻi, āytam keṭṭu aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum.
varumāṟu :	ataṉi, itaṉi, utaṉi - eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum. atu  itu utu - eṉpaṉavum urupēṟ puḻi ataṉi - itaṉi - utaṉi - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum. 	(naṉ. 245 - 251)
urupupuṇarcci poruṭpuṇarccikkum porunti varutal -
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyiyi (avvurupu tokka) poruṭpuṇarccikkum koḷvar. āyiṉ atu tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭu aṉṟu. urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭpuṇarccikkum okkumāyiṉ, avar māṭṭeṟintē kūṟuvār.
‘nīeṉ orupeyar urupiyal niliyum’	(to. e. 253 nac.)
‘cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum’	(263)
‘urupiyal niliyum moḻiyumā ruḷavē’	(294)
eṉa māṭṭeṟintu, māṭṭēṟṟāṉ atikāravalleḻuttu vilakkappaṭātu ātaliṉ, ataṉi nīkka ‘valleḻuttu iyaṟki’ eṉpār.
e-ṭu : nī + ai ḻū niṉ + ai = niṉṉi (to. e. 179 nac.)
		nī + ki ḻū niṉ + ki = niṉki	(253)
	atu + ku ḻū atu + aṉ + ku ḻū at + aṉ + ku = ataṟku;	(176)
	atu + ki ḻū atu + aṉ + ki ḻū at + aṉ + ki = ataṉki	(263)
	kō + ai ḻū kō + oṉ + ai = kōoṉi	(180)
	kō + ki ḻū kō +  oṉ + ki = kōoṉki      (294)
urupupuṇariyal urippaṉa -
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ nilimoḻiyoṭum varumoḻi yoṭum puṇarumāṟum, iṭiyē cāriyi peṟuṅkāl varum tiripukaḷum, vikuti patam cāriyi urupu - ivi potu vitiyāṉ puṇarvaṉavum, iraṇṭām mūṉṟām vēṟṟumip puṇarccikaḷiṉ ciṟappuvitiyum, eḻuttatikārap puṟaṉaṭiyum urupupuṇariyalil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. naṉṉūl eḻuttati kārattu iṟutiyiyalām aintāvatu itu. itaṉkaṇ 18 nuṟpākkaḷ uḷa.	(naṉ. 240-257)
urupupuṇariyalil ticippeyarp puṇarcci -
urupupuṇariyalil, ticippeyarkaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum, aikāram peṟṟum, palavāka vikārappaṭṭum puṇarum.
e-ṭu	: a)	 vaṭakkiṉkaṇ, vaṭakkaṇ;teṟkiṉkaṇ, teṟkaṇ; kuṇakkiṉkaṇ, kuṇakkaṇ; kuṭakkiṉkaṇ, kuṭakkaṇ; kiḻakkiṉkaṇ, kiḻakkaṇ; mēṟkiṉkaṇ, mēṟkaṇ - ivi iṉcāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum uru poṭu puṇarntaṉa.
ā)	kiḻakkiṉkaṇ - kīḻi; mēṟkiṉkaṇ - mēli; - ivi aikāraccāriyi peṟṟaṉa.
i)	kīḻcār, kīḻpuṭi; mēlcār, mēlpuṭi; teṉcār, teṉpuṭi; vaṭacār, vaṭapuṭi - ivi  cāriyi iṉṟip pala vikārappaṭṭu urupoṭu puṇarntaṉa.(to. e. 201 nac. uri)
urum eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu -
urum eṉṟa peyar, alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukarappēṟum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇa mum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukarappēṟum eytip puṇarum. uyirkkaṇamum yakaramum variṉ ukarappēṟiṉṟi iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	urum + kaṭitu, kaṭumi = urumuk kaṭitu, urumuk kaṭumi; urum + nīṇṭatu, nīṭci = urumu nīṇṭatu, urumu nīṭci; urum + valitu, vaṉmi = urumu valitu, urumu vaṉmi; urum + aṭintatu, aṭivu = urumaṭintatu, urumaṭivu; urum + yātu, yāppu = urum yātu, urumyāppu.	(to. e. 328 nac. uri)
‘uruviṉum iciyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum’ kuṟippumoḻi -
niṟattiṉkaṇṇum ōciyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi āytam tōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittaliyuṭiya coṟkaḷ.
e-ṭu :	‘kaḵḵ ṟeṉṉum kallatar attam’  - karumi :niṟam
	‘cuḵḵ ṟeṉṉum taṇtōṭṭup peṇṇi’
		- curr:ōci. iccoṟkaḷil āytam aḷapeṭut tuḷḷatu.	(to. e. 40 nac. uri).
uritta vitiyiṉ ōrntu oppaṉa koḷal’ -
vitikaḷuḷ ivviti itaṟkup poruntum, itaṟkup porun tātu eṉa uyttuṇarntu evviti etaṟkup poruntumō, avvitiyi ataṟkuk koḷka.
vikutip puṇarcci: ‘ṟavvoṭu ukara ummi’ (naṉ. 145) - ceṉṟu eṉpuḻi iṟantakālamum, cēṟu eṉpuḻi etirkālamum poruntum.
patappuṇarcci :‘alvaḻi i ai’ (176) - āṭi tiṅkaḷ- eṉa iyalpām eṉṟum, paruttik kuṟitu - eṉa mikum eṉṟum koḷḷaṟka. parutti kuṟitu - eṉa eḻuvāykkaṇ iyalpām eṉavum, āṭit tiṅkaḷ - eṉap paṇputtokikkaṇ mikum eṉavum koḷka.
cāriyip puṇarcci: ‘patamuṉ vikutiyum’ (243)- nāṭṭiṉiṉ nīṅkiṉāṉ- eṉa ‘iṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku’ iṉcāriyi varum eṉavum, nāṭṭukkaṇ iruntāṉ - eṉak kaṇṇurupiṟku iṉcāriyi vārātu eṉavum koḷḷaṟka. nāṭṭiṉ nīṅkiṉāṉ - eṉa iṉ urupiṟku iṉcāriyi vārātu eṉṟum, nāṭṭiṉkaṇ iruntāṉ- eṉak kaṇṇuru piṟku iṉcāriyi varum eṉṟum koḷka.
urupupuṇarcci: ‘oṟṟuyir mutalīṟṟu’ (242)- nampikaṇ- eṉak kaṇṇurupu (valloṟṟu) mikātu eṉavum, nampikku- eṉak kuvvurupu ‘maṉ’ eṉṟa mikiyāl vali mikum eṉavum koḷka.
ivvāṟu poruḷ kāṇāmal, mēṟkāṇum nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc coṉṉaviti maṟṟoṉṟaṟkum koḷka eṉap poruḷ koḷvārumuḷar.	(naṉ. 254 caṅkara.)
‘uripporuṭ kiḷavi’ puṇarcci -
uripporuṭkiḷaviyāvatu etirmukamākkum poruḷi yuṭiya amma eṉṉum iṭiccol. iḵtu amma koṟṟā eṉa iyalpāyum ammā koṟṟā eṉa nilimoḻi īṟu nīṇṭu iyalpāyum puṇarum.	(to. e. 210, 212 nac.)
amma eṉṉum uripporuṭkiḷaviyiṉ nīṭṭam viḷiyiṉ - kaṇṇēyē varum.	(to. co. 153 cēṉā.)
kēḷ eṉṟu collutaṟkaṇ varuvatu amma eṉṉum iṭiccol.	(e. ku. pak. 200)
uriyacik kiḷavi -
tāṉ kūṟum poruḷik kōṭaṟku oruvaṉi etirmukamāk kum col. atu kēḷ mutaliya muṉṉili ēval orumic col.	(to. e. 34 nac. uri)
kēṇmiyā eṉpataṉkaṇ miyā eṉpatu uriyacic col.		(to. e. 34. iḷa. uri)
‘āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷavi’	(to.e. 204 nac.)
‘amma eṉṉum uripporuṭ kiḷavi’	(210)
‘antil āṅka acinilik kiḷavi’	(to. co. 267 cēṉā.)
eṉpaṉavaṟṟi nōkkiṉ, miyā eṉpatē uriyacik kiḷavi;kēḷ eṉpatu uriyacik kiḷavi ākātu.
‘ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum’ (to. e. 244 nac.) eṉa āciriyar kūṟutaliṉ, miyā eṉpatē uriyacik kiḷavi. 			(e.ā.pak. 36)
	varumāṟu :	kēṇmiyā koṟṟā - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarum.			(to. e. 224 nac.)
uriyacikkiḷavip puṇarcci -
kaṭṭurikkaṇṇē acitta niliyāy varum āṅka eṉṉum iṭiccol uriyacik kiḷaviyām. kaṭṭuri - puṉinturi;acittal - cērttutal.
‘āṅkak kuyilum mayilum kāṭṭik
kēḷvaṉi viṭuttup pōki yōḷē’
eṉpuḻi, āṅka eṉpatu aṅṅaṉē eṉap puṉinturittu niṉṟatu. ciṟitu poruḷ uṇarttuvaṉavaṟṟi uriyaci eṉpa.	(to. co. 279 nac. uri)
akarīṟṟu āṅka eṉṉum uriyaci iṭiccol vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi mikkup puṇarum. āṅkak koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(to. e. 204 nac.)

uriyiṟ kōṭal -
uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpatu uttivakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. tol kāppiyam kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ iḵtu iṭam peṟṟilatu. iv vuttivakiyi uriyāciriyaṉmār eṭuttāṇṭuḷḷaṉar. nūṟpā vāyilāka nērāka uṇarttappaṭāta iṉṟiyami yāta ceytikaḷi urivāyilāka veḷiyiṭuvatu ivvutti vaki kuṟippiṭum ceytiyām.
	e-ṭu :	makaram taṉ arimāttiriyiṉ kuṟukum eṉpatē nūṟpāc ceyti. atu kālmāttiriyākak kuṟukum eṉpatu uriyiṟkōṭal.         (to.e. 13 nac. uri)
‘puḷimarak kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi’ eṉpatutāṉ nūṟpāc ceyti. uyir varuvaḻip puḷiyavili eṉa cāriyi makaram keṭutalum, puḷiyili eṉa ammuc cāriyi muḻutum keṭuvatum uriyiṟ kōṭal.	(to.e. 130 nac.)
‘ē eṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum’ eṉpatē nūṟpāc ceyti. ēek koṭṭil - ēe nekiḻcci - eṉa ekarappēṟu yāṇṭum koḷḷātu ēṟpuḻik koḷka eṉpatu uriyiṟkōṭal.	(to.e. 227 nac.)
tev eṉṟa col toḻiṟpeyar pōla ukaram peṟum eṉpatē nūṟpāc ceyti. tevvumāṭci eṉpataṉoṭu tem muṉi eṉavum varum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu uriyiṟkōṭal. tevvumuṉi ‘temmuṉi’ eṉavum varum.	(to.e. 382 nac.)
aikāram orumāttiri aḷaviṟṟākak kuṟukum  niliyumuṇṭu eṉpatē nūṟpāc ceyti. aikāram mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉṟa mūṉṟiṭattum kuṟukum eṉpatum, oḷakāram moḻi mutaṟ kaṇ kuṟukum eṉpatum uriyiṟkōṭal.	(to. e. 57 nac.)
to. e. iḷampūraṇar uriyilum ivvuttivaki 131, 141, 155, 211, 269, 471 mutaliya nūṟpākkaḷil koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivvāṟṟāṉ, eṭuttōttāṉ collappaṭāmal, ilēcuka ḷāṉum koḷḷappaṭa iyalāmal uḷḷa ceytikaḷ uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉṟa uttivakiyāṉ koḷḷappaṭutali uri kaḷil kāṇalām.
uriyiṉ irukūṟu, mūvaki, āṟum ēḻum pattum patiṉmūṉṟum ākiya kūṟukaḷ -
tokuttuk kaṇṇaḻittal, virittuk koṇarnturittal eṉpaṉa iru kūṟām.
poḻippu, akalam, nuṭpam eṉpaṉa mūvakiyām.
eṭuttukkōṭal, patam kāṭṭal, patam virittal, patapporu ḷurittal, viṉātal, viṭuttal - eṉpaṉa āṟu kūṟām.
poḻippu, akalam, nuṭpam, nūleccam, patapporuḷurit tal, ēṟpuḻik kāṭṭal, eṇṇal- eṉpaṉa eḻukūṟām.
‘collē, colvaki, coṟporuḷ cōtaṉi,
 maṟinili, ilēcu, eccam, nōkkē, tuṇivē,
 karuttē, celuttal eṉṟu īrintu kiḷaviyum
 neṟippaṭa varuvatu paṉuval uriyē’
eṉpaṉa pattukkūṟām.
cūttiram tōṟṟal, col vakuttal, coṟporuḷurittal, viṉātal, viṭuttal, vicēṭam kāṭṭal, tokuttu muṭittal, virittuk kāṭṭal, tuṇivu kūṟal, payaṉoṭu puṇarttal, utāraṇam kāṭṭal, āciriya vacaṉam kāṭṭal, atikāra varavu kāṭṭal - eṉpaṉa patiṉmūṉṟu kūṟām.
immata vikaṟpam ellām ‘pāṭam karuttē’ eṉṉum iccūt tirattup patiṉāṉkaṉuḷḷē aṭaṅkum.	(naṉ. 20 mayili.)

urivaki cila  -
uri ceytaṟporuṭṭu eṭutteḻutiya mūlamum, “maṉṟu paṟit tuṇṇēl’ eṉpataṉi ‘maṇpaṟit tuṇṇēl’ eṉap pāṭam ōtuvārumuḷar” eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum pāṭavuri.
kuṉṟiyakkāl eṉpatu kuṉṟikkāl eṉa vikārappaṭṭuniṉṟatu
(kuṟaḷ 14) eṉṟāṟ pōlac collukkaḷik kuṟittu eḻutum uri colvakiyuri.
‘matimaruṭṭum ciṟunutaṟ pēramarkkaṭ ceyyavāy aiya nuṇ ṇiṭiyāy’ (yā. kā. 4) eṉpataṟku, ‘aṟiviṉi mayakkum ciṟunutal mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷi uṭiyāy’ eṉat tokuttu eḻutumuri tokutturi.
utāraṇam eṭutteḻutum uri utāraṇavuri. itaṉi mēṟkōḷuri,  kāṭṭuri, eṭuttukkāṭṭuri eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar.
eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ eṉavum, eṉpārum uḷarālō veṉiṉ eṉavum viṉāvi eḻutum uri viṉāvuri.
itu karutiṟṟu eṉavum, itu karuti eṉka eṉavum etirmoḻi eḻutum uri viṭiyuri.
cūttirattu uṭporuḷaṉṟi aṅkē vēṇṭiyiruntāl peyturippatuvicēṭavuri.
vēṟṟumiyurupu mutaliyaṉa tokku niṟpiṉ avaṟṟi viritteḻutuvatu virivuri.
atikarittal, ‘varuvikkappaṭṭa’teṉa atikārattoṭu poruntak kāṭṭi eḻutumuri atikāravuri.
cantēkappaṭa niṉṟaviṭattu, itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉat tuṇintu eḻutum uri tuṇivuri.	(naṉ.21 irāmā.)
uḷaveṉappaṭṭa, paṭāta aḷavu niṟippeyarkaḷ  -
uḷaveṉappaṭṭa aḷavup peyarkaḷ: kalam cāṭitūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku - eṉṟa  kakaram mutalākiya oṉpatu eḻuttukkaḷiyum mutalākak koṇṭu varum coṟkaḷām.
uḷaveṉappaṭṭa niṟippeyarkaḷ: kaḻañcu cīrakam toṭi palam niṟi mā vari anti- eṉpaṉavum, ukaramutal niṟippeyar uṇṭēl atuvum ām.
uḷaveṉappaṭāta aḷavuppeyarkaḷ immi ōraṭi miṭā eṉpaṉavum, tēyavaḻakkāy varum oruñār orutuvali- eṉpaṉavum ām.	(to. e. 170 nac. uri)
uṟaḻcci vāram  -
tiritarum kūṟu eṉṟu ittoṭar poruḷpaṭum; “untiyil tōṉṟum kāṟṟiṉatu tiritarum kūṟukaḷ parā, picanti, matyamā eṉpavi. ivi akkāṟṟu unti mutal miṭaṟuvari eytutaṟkuriya iṭippakutiyil akkāṟṟiṉ tiritaru kūṟukaḷ paṟṟi iṭappaṭṭa peyarkaḷ ākum. pariyil eḻuttukkaḷ ellām orē taṉmiyāy irukkum; atu mūlātārattil nikaḻum. picanti untiyil tōṉṟum; atu yōkikaḷukkē pulappaṭum. matyami neñcil tōṉṟum” eṉa vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.	(to. e. 102 nac.) (e. ku. pak. 106)
nām pēcum eḻuttoli nāṉkāvatu kūṟākiya vikarī. atu ‘turīyam vācam maṉuṣyā vatanti’ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.	(e. ā. pak. 86)
ū
ū -
itu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil āṟāvatu uyir; ukaruyirit taṉakku iṉamākak koṇṭu aṅkāppōṭu itaḻ kuvitalāl piṟakkum eḻuttākum.
itu ceyyū eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyecca vikuti.	(to.co.228 cēṉā.)
ū eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu -
ū eṉpatu iṟicciyi uṇarttum peyar. aḵtu ā eṉṟa col pōla ṉakara oṟṟiṉic cāriyiyākap peṟṟu ūṉ eṉṟākum.
alvaḻikkaṇ ūṉ eṉpatu iyalpākap puṇarum (nakaramutal nīṅkalāka varumoḻi koḷka.)
	e-ṭu : ūṉ kiṭantatu, ūṉ melitu, ūṉ valitu, ūṉaritu	(to. e. 269 nac.)
vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṉakarac cāriyiyoṭu, varumoḻi nāṟkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇartalum, akkuccāriyi peṟṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum ēṉikkaṇam variṉ iyalpāyum puṇartalum nikaḻum.
	e-ṭu : ūṉkuṟi, ūṉmeṉmi, ūṉvalimi, ūṉarumi; ūṉakkuṟi, ūṉañāṟci, ūṉa vaṉmi, ūṉa varumi - eṉa irunili yumuṇṭu.	(to.e. 270 nac. uri)
ū eṉpatu taciyi uṇartti niṉṟa vaḻakkāyt tolkāppiya ṉār kālattatu; aṉṟit tēya vaḻakkēṉum uṇarka.		(to.e. 269 nac. uri)
ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻip potuppuṇarcci -
ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi eḻuvāyttoṭar vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum ēṉikkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum
	e-ṭu : koṇmūk kaṭitu, koṇmū ñāṉṟatu, koṇmū valitu, koṇmū varitu (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)	(to. e. 264 nac.)
ūkāra īṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, ciṟupāṉmi eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭi peṟṟu varutalum, ēṉiya kaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇartalum nikaḻum.
	e-ṭu : āṭūk kuṟiyaṉ, āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ; makaṭūk kuṟiyaḷ, makaṭūuk kuṟiyaḷ;āṭū nallaṉ, āṭū vallaṉ, āṭū veḷiyaṉ (vakaram uṭampaṭumey). 	(to.e. 265 nac. uri)
ūkāra īṟṟu ēvalorumimuṟṟup puṇarumāṟu -
ūkārīṟṟu ēvalorumimuṟṟu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : kitūk koṟṟā, kitū nākā, kitū vaḷavā, kitū varacā (vakaram uṭampaṭumey) (to. e. 265 nac.)
ūkāra īṟṟu viṉiyeccap puṇarcci -
ūkārīṟṟu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
e-ṭu  	uṇṇūk koṇṭāṉ, uṇṇū naṭantāṉ, uṇṇū vāḻntāṉ, uṇṇū vaṭintāṉ (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)	(to. e. 265 nac.)
ūkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumip puṇarcci -
ūkārīṟṟup peyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi mikkum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ vantuḻi iyalpākavum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : koṇmūk kuḻām; koṇmūnīṭci, koṇmū vaḷarcci; koṇmū vaṭivu (vakaram uṭampaṭu mey)
avaṟṟuḷ kuṟṟeḻutti aṭutta ūkārīṟum, ōreḻut toru moḻi ūkārīṟum eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyum peṟum.
	e-ṭu : uṭūuk kuṟi, uṭūu ñāṟci, uṭūu vaṉmi, uṭūu vaṭivu;	tūukkuṟi, tūunīṭci, tūu vaṉmi, tūu vāci
uyartiṇippeyarum āṭūukki makaṭūukki - eṉṟāṟ pōla eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟum.
pū eṉṉum peyar eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi ukaram peṟāmal, vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi vanta valleḻuttum ataṟkotta mel leḻuttum peṟṟum, ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : pūkkoṭi, pūṅkoṭi; pūnīṭci, pūvaṇṇam, pūvaḻaku 
ū eṉṟa peyar ṉakaraccāriyiyum ataṉōṭu akkuccāri yiyum peṟṟu vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : ūṉ kuṟi, ūṉ ceyki; ūṉakkuṟi, ūṉac ceyki
āṭū, makaṭū - eṉpaṉa iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartalu muṇṭu.
	e-ṭu : āṭūviṉ ki, makaṭūviṉ ki, cevi, tali, puṟam- eṉa muṭikka.	(to. e. 266-271 nac.)
ūkāram vakarattoṭu navilāmi -
ūkāram vakaramey varukkattōṭu iṇintu īṟākātu. ākavē vakaramey, yāṇṭum īṟāka vārāta ekaram, vilakkap paṭṭa ūkāram, nakarameyyōṭaṉṟi īṟākāta okaram eṉṟa mūṉṟu uyir nīṅkalāka ēṉiya oṉpatu uyirkaḷoṭum moḻikku īṟāka varum.
	e-ṭu : uva, vā, karuvi, oruvī, katavu, vē, vi, uṟupavō, veḷa	(to. e. 74 nac.)
ūkāramum ōkāramum oḷakāramākat tiriyum vaṭanaṭip pakupatam -
ūvum ōvum oḷavākat tiriyum. cūraṉ eṉṉum cūriyaṉ makaṉām caṉi curi eṉavum, kōcaliyiṭattu (kōcala nāṭṭiṭattu)p piṟantāḷ kucali eṉavum, cōmaṉ eṉṉum cantiraṉuṭiya makaṉām putaṉ cumaṉ eṉavum varum.
aiyākat tirivaṉa ellām ayi eṉṟum, oḷavākat tirivaṉa ellām avu eṉṟum muṭiyum. (ayintiram, kavuravar - eṉak kāṇka) 	(to.vi.86 uri)
ūṉ: puṇarumāṟu -
‘kuyiṉ, ūṉ : puṇarcci’ kāṇka.
e
e -
itu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil ēḻāvatu eḻuttu; oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟāya kuṟil. itaṉ iṉamākiya neṭil ē. ekaram akarakkūṟum ikarakkūṟum cērntu amintatu eṉṟa karuttut tamiḻ ekarattukku avvaḷavu poruntātu. ekaram vāyi aṅkāttalōṭu aṇpal aṭiyi nāviḷimpu uṟap piṟakkum eḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  itu moḻimutaṟkaṇ viṉāvāka varum. evaṉ eṉṟa viṉāviṉikkuṟippukku iḵtu aṭiyāka varuvatu; aṟiyāp poruḷvayiṉ ceṟiyat tōṉṟuvatu. itaṉit tamiḻcciṟappeḻuttu aintaṉuḷ oṉṟu eṉpa. itu pirākirutattilum uḷḷatu. yakara mutal vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil ekara mutalāka, yantra- entiram, yamaṉ- emaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla varum. ekaram aḷapeṭikkaṇṇēyē īṟāki varum. aḵtu emmeyyeḻuttoṭum kūṭi īṟākātu.
ekaram īṟātal ekarīṟṟup puṇarcci -
ekaram peyarkkaṇ moḻi īṟāka vārātu. atu muṉṉili ēval orumikkaṇ aḷapeṭiyāy moḻiyiṟutiyil varum. vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi mikkum, piṟakaṇattu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : ēek koṟṟā, ēe nākā, ēe vaḷavā, ēe varacā (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)
	ēe - eṉakku oru karumam paṇi eṉṉum poruṭṭu.	(to. e. 272. nac. uri)
tēṟṟapporuṭkaṇ ekaram ēkāriṭiccoṟku aḷa peṭi yeḻuttāy iṟutiyil varum.
	e-ṭu : yāṉēe koṇṭēṉ, yāṉēe naṭantēṉ, yāṉēe vantēṉ, yāṉēe yaṭintēṉ (yakaram uṭampaṭu mey)	(to.e. 273 nac.)
 ekara okara vaṭivu  -
‘ekaram okaram mey puḷḷi peṟum’ eṉṟa cūttiratti ‘ēkāra ōkāram mey puḷḷi peṟum’ eṉat tirutta vēṇṭiṟṟu eṉ ṉeṉil, ikkālattār ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷukkē puḷḷi yiṭṭeḻutuvatu peruvaḻakku āyiṉamiyāl eṉka.	(naṉ. 98 irāmā.)
ekaram puḷḷi peṟutal -
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekaram okaram oḻinta pattu eḻuttuk kaḷum puḷḷiyillaṉavāy vaḻaṅka, ivviraṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷ māttiram puḷḷi peṟṟu vaḻaṅkutal nōkka, tamiḻmoḻikku ilakkaṇam vakutta tollāciriyarkaḷ, ekara okaraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illi yātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟku vaṭamoḻiyil varivaṭivu amikka vāyppu iṉmiyāṉ, tamiḻil ekara okaraṅkaḷukku vēṟuvaṭivu amikkāmal, ēkāra ōkāraṅka ḷiṉ varivaṭiviṉkaṇ (e o eṉpaṉa paṇṭi neṭil vari vaṭivu) puḷḷiyiṭṭu (e¶ ō¶ - eṉa) vaḻaṅkiṉar eṉpatu tōṉṟu kiṟatu.
mutaṉ mutalil tamiḻilakkaṇam vakutta āciriyar vaṭamoḻi nōkkit tamiḻneṭuṅkaṇakku vippumuṟiyilē vēṟupāṭu ceytatu pōlavē, varivaṭivilum vēṟupāṭu ceytaṉar eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
mēlē puḷḷiyiṭuvataṉmūlam māttiri pātiyākak kuṟippati aṟivikkum marapiṉit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār koṇṭaṉar.	(e. ā. pak. 20)
e : 2 māttiri, e¶ : oru māttiri; o : 2 māttiri, o¶ : oru māttiri; ka : oru māttiri, k : ari māttiri; m : ari māttiri, m¶ : kāl māttiri; ku : oru māttiri, ku¶ : (kuṟṟiya lukaramām) ari māttiri.
ekaramutal vayiṉ -
ekaramākiya mutalviṉāviyuṭiya vayiṉ eṉṟa col. aḵtu evvayiṉ eṉpatu (evviṭam eṉṉum poruṭṭu).
‘evvayiṉ’ ṉakara īṟṟatāy ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇarttum iṭiccol. itu peyar niliyatāy varu moḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumvaḻi īṟṟu ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tiriya ‘evvayiṟ peyarum’ eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum.
iyalpukaṇam varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum. (evvayiṉ+ naṭantāṉ eṉpuḻi, evvayiṉaṭantāṉ eṉat tiripu nikaḻtaliṉ, meṉkaṇattuḷ nakaram nīṅkalākak koḷka.)	(to. e. 334. nac.)
‘ekaramutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟuti’ -
1.	etōḷi eṉṟa viṉāccol: iḵtu evviṭam eṉṉum poruḷatu. iḵtu etōḷik koṇ ṭāṉ - eṉa varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
2.	evvaḻi eṉṟa viṉāccol: ituvum evviṭam eṉṉum poruṭṭu. iḵtu evvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, evvaḻik koṇṭāṉ- eṉa (iyalpāyum mikkum) varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ uṟaḻntum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum. ivi peyar niliyiṉa.	(to. e. 159 nac. uri)


ekara viṉā muccuṭṭu puṇarcci viti  -
e - a i u - eṉṉum viṉā cuṭṭu iṭiccoṟkaḷ nili-moḻiyāka niṟpa, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ uyirkkaṇamō iṭikkaṇattu yakaramō variṉ, iṭiyē vakara oṟṟu mikum.
	e-ṭu : e+ aṇi = evvaṇi, e + yāṉi = evyāṉi; 
a, i, u + yāṉi = avyāṉi, ivyāṉi, uvyāṉi (a+aṇi - avvaṇi; piṟavum koḷka.)
varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ yakaram oḻinta piṟa meykaḷ variṉ, vanta ammeykaḷē mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : ekkutiri, eccēṉi, ettaṇṭu, eppaṭi; eṅṅaṉam, eññālam, ennāṭu, emmaṉi, evvitam
cuṭṭiṭiccoṟkum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka.
ceyyuḷil cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi iṭiyē yakaram tōṉṟum. (atu varumoḻimutal uyir varumiṭattēyē eṉka.)
	varumāṟu : a + iṭi ḻū ā +  y + iṭi = āyiṭi
cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻiyum potuvitippaṭi meyvariṉ vanta mey mikutalumuṇṭu. yāviṉāvum atu.
	varumāṟu : āṅṅaṉam, īṅṅaṉam, ūṅṅaṉam; yāṅṅaṉam 	(naṉ. 163)
ekiṉ puṇarcci -
ekiṉ eṉpatu oru marattiyum oru paṟaviyiyum kuṟikkum col.
ekiṉ eṉṟa marappeyar, āṇ eṉṟa marappeyar pōla, ammuccāriyi peṟṟu ēṟṟa tiripukaḷuṭaṉ varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarum.
e-ṭu: ekiṉaṅkōṭu, ekiṉanār, ekiṉavaṭṭi, ekiṉaviyalpu (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)	(to. e. 336 nac.)
paṟaviyik kuṟikkum ekiṉ eṉṟa peyar varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu  puṇarumvaḻi, akaramum valleḻuttup pēṟum, ciṟupāṉmi melleḻuttuppēṟum, iyalpu kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi akarappēṟum eyti muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	ekiṉ + kāl = ekiṉakkāl, ekiṉaṅkāl; ekiṉ + nīṭci = ekiṉanīṭci; ekiṉ + yāppu = ekiṉayāppu; ekiṉ  + aṭivu = ekiṉavaṭivu	(to.e. 337 nac.)
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ ekiṉ eṉṟa peyar attum iṉṉum peṟṟu ekiṉatti, ekiṉiṉi- eṉap puṇarum.			(to.e. 194 iḷa. uri)
ekiṉ, puḷiyamaram- aṉṉappaṟavi - eṉa iru poruḷatu.
‘eñciya ellām eñcutal ila’ -
oḷakāram nīṅkalākap patiṉōruyirkaḷum patiṉeṭṭu meyk kaṇṇum vantu moḻikku īṟām eṉṟa potuviti yil, piṉṉi vicēṭittuk kūṟiyavaṟṟi oḻintaṉavum moḻikku īṟātaṟkaṇ oḻivila eṉṟavāṟu.
aviyāvaṉa to.e. 70 mutal 76 muṭiyak kūṟappaṭṭu utāraṇam kāṭṭappeṟṟa ñakaramum nakaramum vakaramum cakaramum pakaramum oru moḻikkum īṟākāta ṅakaramum oḻinta paṉṉiraṇṭu meykkaṇṇum, ekaramum okaramum oḷakāramum oḻinta oṉpatu uyirum ēṟi moḻikku īṟām eḻuttukka ḷām.
moḻikku īṟāy varum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa īṟukaḷil cila ikkālattu vaḻakkil illi. ‘īṟṟil varum uyirmeykaḷ’ kāṇka.
moḻikku īṟām eḻuttukkaḷ 161. ikkālattu utāraṇam illaṉa 20.
moḻikku īṟākāta ēṉiya eḻuttukkaḷum tampeyar kūṟutaṟkaṇ moḻikku īṟām.	(to. e. 77 nac.)
cakara ñakara nakara vakaraṅkaḷoṭu kūṭi ivvivvuyirkaḷ īṟākā eṉṟu vilakkiṉāṟpōla, ēṉiya meykaḷukkum kūṟa vēṇṭuvatu muṟiyātaliṉ, ammeykaḷoṭu, muṉvilakkap paṭṭa e o ōḷa eṉṟamūṉṟum oḻinta oṉpatu uyirkaḷum kūṭi īṟākum eṉṟavāṟu.
moḻikku īṟākātaṉa tam peyar kūṟavē, moḻikku īṟāki varutalukku āṭci illi ātaliṉ, tolkāppiyaṉār moḻikku īṟākātaṉavum tam peyar kūṟumvaḻi moḻikku īṟākum eṉṟu kūṟavilli.	(e. ā. pak. 71)
eṭuttal paṭuttal ōcikaḷ -
coṟkaḷik kūṟuṅkāl poruḷ ciṟakkum eḻuttiṉi eṭuttum, ayal eḻuttiṉi nalintum, ēṉiya eḻuttuk kaḷip paṭuttum kūṟa vēṇṭum. eṭuttal paṭuttal mutaliya ōcikaḷum eḻuttuccāriyiyum taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟa luṭiya uyirkkēyaṉṟi avvāṟṟal illāta meykku illi.
viṉiccollum viṉikkuṟippuc collum pakutiyil poruḷ ciṟantu niṟkum. peyarccol avvāṟaṉṟi vikuti yil poruḷ ciṟantu niṟkum.viṉiccoṟkaḷ pakutiyil poruḷ ciṟattaliṉ vikutipporuḷ vēṟu viḷakkutaṟku ‘uṇṭāṉ cāttaṉ’ eṉṟāṟpōlap peyar orutaliyāṉ vēṇṭappaṭṭatu. iṉi, uṇṭāṉ kariyāṉ eṉṉum peyarccoṟkaḷ vikutiyil poruḷ ciṟattaliṉ, apporuḷi viḷakkutaṟku vēṟōr peyar vēṇṭātu, ‘uṇṭāṉ vantāṉ’ ‘kariyāṉ vantāṉ’ eṉat tāmē eḻuvāyāyp payaṉili koṇṭum, ‘kariyāṉik koṇā’ eṉa urupēṟṟum varum. uṇṭāṉ, kariyāṉ mutaliya viṉic coṟkaḷum vēṟu; uṇṭāṉ, kariyāṉ mutaliya peyar kaḷum vēṟu.
tolkāppiyaṉār peyarkaḷuḷ viṉippeyar eṉṟa pakuppik kūṟiyuḷḷār. cēṉāvariyar ataṟku “varuvār, celvār eṉpaṉa; taccaṉ, kollaṉ- eṉpaṉavum avi” eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantuḷḷār. 
“uṇṭāṉ- tiṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu paṭuttuc collappaṭum toḻiṟpeyar, viṉiccol pōlat tiṇiyum pālum kālamum mutalāyiṉavaṟṟi viḷakki, aṉ āṉ mutaliya īṟṟavāy varutaliṉ toḻilniliyi ottaṉa” eṉṟum avar to.co. 70ām nuṟpāvil kūṟiyuḷḷār. paṇpu aṭiyāka varum peyar paṇpuppeyar eṉṟāṟpōla, viṉi aṭiyāka varum peyar viṉippeyar eṉpatē cēṉā variyar karuttu.
viṉi viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟukkaḷi ōci vēṟupāṭṭāṉ peyarākumeṉil, peyarāyavāṟṟāṉē ōci vēṟupaṭum, ōci vēṟupāṭṭāṉ peyarākum eṉa ‘oṉṟi oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṉum kuṟṟam taṅkumātaliṉ, viṉimuṟṟum viṉippeyarum vevvēṟu coṟkaḷē; aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, colcuruṅkutal poruṭṭu eḻuttoppumi nōkkip ‘pala poruḷ orucol’ eṉpa.	(cū. vi. pak. 54, 55)
eṇ eṉṉum uṇavuppeyarp puṇarcci -
eḷḷiṉi uṇarttum eṇ eṉṉum uṇavuppeyar, eṭkacivu eṉṟāṟpōla ṇakaram ṭakaramākat tirintu puṇarvatu pōla alvaḻikkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi eṭkaṭitu eṉat tiritalum, perumpāṉmi eṇ ciṟitu - eṇ peritu - eṉṟāṟ pōla iyalpātalum uṭiyatu. iyalpukaṇam varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ variṉ iruvaḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum,
	e-ṭu : eṇ māṇṭatu, eṇmāṭci; eṇ yātu, eṇ yāppu; eṇ ṇaḻakitu, eṇṇaḻaku	(to. e. 308, 144 nac.)
varumoḻi nakaram variṉ atu ṇakaramākat tiritalum, uyir variṉ ṇakaram iraṭṭutalum potuvitic ceykikaḷām.
eṇ + naṉṟu = eṇ ṇaṉṟu; eṇ + naṉmi = eṇṇaṉmi; eṇ + aṭintatu = eṇṇaṭintatu, eṇ + aṭivu = eṇṇaṭivu.	(to. e. 150, 160 nac.)
eṇkaḷ ‘iṉ’ peṟutal  -
eṇṇuppeyarellām iṉcāriyi peṟum.
	e-ṭu : oṉṟiṉi, iraṇṭiṉi, mūṉṟiṉi, nāṉkiṉi, piṟavumaṉṉa.	(mu. vī. puṇa. 69)
eṇṇuppeyar urupoṭu puṇartal  -
ēḻ eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar ḻakaramey īṟṟatu. ēṉiya oṉṟu mutal pattu īṟākiya eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉa. ivi perumpāṉmi aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupoṭu puṇarum; ciṟupāṉmi iṉcāriyi peṟum.
	e-ṭu : oṉṟaṉi, ēḻaṉi, pattaṉi; oṉṟiṉi, ēḻiṉi, pattiṉi	(to.e. 194, 198 nac. uri)
eṇkaḷ ‘aṉ’ peṟutal tolkāppiyamarapu.	(to. e. 198 nac.)
eṇṇuppeyarkaḷukkuc ciṟappu viti  -
eṇṇuppeyarum niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum piṟavum varumoḻiyāka amiya, nilimoḻiyāka oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ niṟpiṉ, mutal iraṇṭu eṇkaḷ mutal nīḷum;mūṉṟu āṟu ēḻu - eṉpaṉa mutal kuṟukum; āṟu ēḻu - allātavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟiṉ uyirmeyyum, ēḻu eṉṟa eṇṇiṉ īṟṟuyirum keṭum. oṉṟu eṉpataṉ ṉakaroṟṟu rakara oṟṟu ākum; iraṇṭu eṉpataṉ ṇakara oṟṟum rakaratti ūrntu niṉṟa akaramum keṭa, avviraṇṭu rakarattiṉ mēlum ukaram varutalumuṇṭu. (āṇṭu avviraṇṭu eṇkaḷilum mutaluyir nīṭal illi eṉka.) mūṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaroṟṟuk keṭutalum, varum oṟṟākat tiritalum ām. nāṉkaṉ oṟṟu lakaroṟṟum ṟakaroṟṟum ām. aintu eṉpataṉ oṟṟu varu moḻi mutal oṟṟākiyum, ataṟku iṉamākiyum, keṭṭum muṭivatām. eṭṭu eṉpataṉ ṭakaramey ṇakaramey ākum.
	varumāṟu : oṉṟu+ ili, kōṭu = ōr ili (ōrili), oru kōṭu; iraṇṭu + ili, kōṭu = īr ili (īrili) irukōṭu; mūṉṟu + oṉṟu = mū oṉṟu (mūvoṉṟu); mūṉṟu + kaḻañcu, nāḻi, vaṇṭu = mukkaḻañcu, munnāḻi, muvvaṇṭu; nāṉku + eṭi, kuṇam = nāleṭi (nāleṭi), nāṟkuṇam; aintu + mūṉṟu, kaḻañcu, eṭi = aimmūṉṟu, aiṅkaḻañcu, ai eṭi  (ai yeṭi); āṟu + pattu = aṟupatu; ēḻu + kaḻañcu = eḻu kaḻañcu, ēḻkaḻañcu; eṭṭu + kuṇam = eṇkuṇam 	(naṉ. 188-193)
eṇṇuppeyar peṟum potuccāriyi  -
eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattum, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum perumpāṉmiyum aṉcāriyi peṟum.
	e-ṭu : oṉṟaṉi, iraṇṭaṉi - (to. e. 198 nac.); ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṟku - (to. e. 194 nac.); oṉṟaṉkāyam, iraṇṭaṉ kāyam - (to. e. 419 nac.); ēḻaṉkāyam, ēḻaṉcukku - (to. e. 388 nac.)
eṇ niṟi aḷavuppeyarkaḷ ēkārac cāriyi peṟutal  -
uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum ākiya eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, niṟip peyarkaḷum, aḷavuppeyarkaḷum tamakku iṉamākiya peyar kaḷāyt tammil kuṟinta peyarkaḷ tamakku varumoḻiyāka vantu puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyē ēkārac cāriyi peṟum.
	e-ṭu :	oṉṟu+  kāl	=	oṉṟē kāl	eṇ
		kāl + kāṇi	=	kālē kāṇi    ṉū	
		kaḻañcu + kuṉṟi	= kaḻañcē kuṉṟi
		koḷ +  aiyavi	= koḷḷēyiyavi	ṉū niṟi
		nāḻi + āḻākku	= nāḻiyē yāḻākku
		kalaṉ + tūṇi	= kalaṉē tūṇi	   ṉū aḷavu
eṇ niṟi aḷavup peyarkaḷukku ari eṉpatu varumoḻi yāyp puṇarumiṭattuc cāriyi vārātu.
oṉṟari, kaḻañcari, kalavari (vakaram uṭampaṭumey) eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(to. e. 164, 165, nac.)
‘eṇ muṟi nili’ -
neñcu, miṭaṟu, tali,mūkku, aṇṇam, nā, pal, itaḻ- eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭa vēṇṭiya muṟi amiyum nili. ivi eḻuttiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkuriya piṟappiṭamum karuviyum ākum.	(e. ku. pak. 85)
eṇvaki aḷapeṭi -
‘aḷapeṭiyiṉ eṭṭuvaki’ kāṇka.
etirkāla iṭinili  -
pakaramum vakaramum ākiya iraṇṭu oṟṟum mūviṭattu aimpālkaḷilum etirkālam kāṭṭum terinili viṉi muṟṟup pakupata iṭinilikaḷām.
varumāṟu : uṇpaṉ, uṟaṅkuvaṉ
uriyiṟ kōṭalāṉ, ciṟupāṉmi piṟa iṭinilikaḷum etirkālam kāṭṭutal koḷḷappaṭum.
	varumāṟu : ‘aṇka ṇāḷaṉi nakukam yāmē’ (akanā. 32:21)	iṭinili : k; ‘pāṭukam vāvāḻi tōḻi’ (kali. 41: 1) iṭinili : k; ‘aiya ciṟiteṉṉi ūkki, (kali. 37: 15) iṭinili : k; ‘ītal māṭṭu otti peruma, (kali. 86: 32); iṭinili: t; uṇṭi - iṭinili:  ṭ; uritti - iṭinili: t; tiṉṟi - iṭinili: ṟ
ivvāṟu kakaramum ṭakaramum takaramum ṟakaramum etirkālam kāṭṭiṉa.	(i. vi. eḻut. 49; naṉ. 144 irāmā.)
etirkāla vikuti  -
kuṭutuṟu - eṉṉum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu uṇku - uṇṭu - varutu - cēṟu -mutaliya taṉmi orumi viṉimuṟṟukkaḷum, um eṉṉum iṟuti iṭiccolliṉ ukaram ēṟiya kum ṭum tum ṟum eṉṉum iv vīṟṟu uṇkum- uṇṭum- varutum - cēṟum - mutaliya taṉmip paṉmi viṉimuṟṟukkaḷum, pakara uyirmeyyum, mārum eṉṉum ivvīṟṟu uriñupa-  uṇpa - koṇmār- mutaliya uyartiṇip paṉmi viṉimuṟṟuk kaḷum, miṉ eṉṉum īṟṟu uṇmiṉ- uriñumiṉ- mutaliya muṉṉilip paṉmi viṉimuṟṟukkaḷum, viyaṅkōḷ tiṟattu varum kavvum yavvum ravvum allum ālum mārum ummum miyum eṉṉum ivvīṟṟuc celka- vāḻiya- vāḻiyar- ‘makaṉ eṉal makkaṭ pataṭi eṉal’ (kuṟaḷ - 196) - ‘marīiyatu orāal’ (to.co. 443 cēṉā.) - ‘kāṇaṉmār emarē’ - ‘vāḻtal vēṇṭumivaṇ varinta vikal’ - ‘añcāmi añcuva toṉṟiṉ’ - mutaliya viyaṅkōḷ muṟṟukkaḷum, allum ālum ēlum kāṇum eṉṉum ivvīṟṟu uṇṇal - maṟāl- aḻēl - collikkāṇ- mutaliya muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉimuṟṟukkaḷum, uṇṇalaṉ- uṇṇāṉ- mutaliya etirmaṟi viṉimuṟṟuk kaḷum eṉac collappaṭuvaṉa vākiya pakupataṅkaḷ ellām  etirkālam kāṭṭum.	(i. vi. 50 uri)
etirmaṟi -
viṉinikaḻcci iṉmiyik kuṟippatu etirmaṟiyām. etirmaṟiyiṉi ākārīṟum, ā ē āl il - eṉṟa iṭi nilikaḷum kāṭṭum. ākāra īṟum, al iṭiniliyum, āvum, ēyum mukkālattukkum potuvāvaṉa.
e-ṭu: 
pulikaḷ pul uṇṇā 		-	ākāra īṟu 
	(uṇ+ā)					mukkālattukkum potu.
	avaṉ uṇṇalaṉ 		-	al iṭinili
	(uṇ+al+aṉ)	 		mukkālattukkum potu.
	avaṉ uṇṇāṉ	-	ākāra iṭinili  			(uṇ+(ā)+āṉ)			mukkālattukkum potu
	yāṉ uṇṇēṉ	-	ēkāra iṭinili 
	(uṇ+(ē)+ ēṉ)			mukkālattukkum potu
uṇṭilaṉ - uṇ+  ṭ+  il +  aṉ - iṟantakāla etirmaṟi;
uṇkiṉṟilaṉ - uṇ + kiṉṟu + il + aṉ - nikaḻkāla etirmaṟi;
uṇkilaṉ - uṇ + k + il + aṉ	- etirkāla etirmaṟi.
mēli mūṉṟuviṉimuṟṟukkaḷilum kāla iṭinili yōṭu etirmaṟi yiṭinili ‘il’ puṇarntu vantavāṟu. (viṉi nikaḻcci illaṉavaṟṟi uṭiyaṉa pōlak kālat toṭu puṇarttu urittal ilakkaṇiyām.)	(naṉ. 145 caṅ.)
etirmaṟi iṭinili kālam kāṭṭal -
‘etirmaṟi mummiyum ēṟkum’ eṉpuḻi, mummiyum eṉṉum muṟṟummiyi ‘muṟṟummi orōvaḻi eccamum ākum’ eṉpataṉāṉ eccavummiyākki, naṭantilaṉ naṭavāniṉṟilaṉ - eṉa etirmaṟi (il) oru kālam ēṟṟu varutalum koḷka. (naṭantilaṉ : iṟantakāla etirmaṟi; ēṉiyatu nikaḻkāla etirmaṟi) ceṉṟi, cellāniṉṟi - eṉa varumāyiṉ iṭi nili kālam kāṭṭiya ikara īṟṟavām.	(naṉ. 145 caṅkara.)
etirmaṟi ēval vikuti  -
ēl, al, aṉmō, aṟka- vikutikaḷ etirmaṟi ēvalorumi yām. ēl - ceyyēl, al - ceyyal, aṉmō - ceyyaṉmō, aṟka - ceyyaṟka - eṉa ivi orumiyāka varum.
āmiṉ, aṉmiṉ, aṟpīr - vikutikaḷ etirmaṟi ēval paṉmiyām.
āmiṉ - ceyyāmiṉ, aṉmiṉ - ceyyaṉmiṉ, aṟpīr - ceyyaṟpīr - eṉṟum, muṉiyāmiṉ muṉiyaṉmiṉ muṉiyaṟpīr - eṉṟum varum.
aṟka eṉṉum vikuti mūviṭattu aimpāṟkum ēṟpataṉṟi viyaṅ kōḷiṉum ām.	(to. vi. 114 uri)
etirmaṟi vaṭanaṭip pakupatam -
etirmaṟip pakupatam moḻi mutaṟkaṇ oṟṟu uḷavāyiṉ avvum, uyir uḷavāyiṉ annum, iruvaki moḻikku niruvum puṇarntu poruḷiṉmiyum piṟitum etirmaṟiyum kāṭṭum vaṭanaṭip pataṅkaḷām.
cayamilāṉ - acayaṉ; nītiyiṉmi - anīti; malamiṉmi - amalam; cīraṇamiṉmi - acīraṇam; caramiṉmi - acaram; tarumam iṉmi - atarumam.  piṟavumaṉṉa.
akam eṉṉum pāvamillāṉ anakaṉ; aṅkamillāṉ anaṅkaṉ; ātiyiṉmi- anāti; ācāramiṉmi - anā cāram. piṟavumaṉṉa.
malamiṉmi - nirumalam; nāmam illāṉ- nirunāmaṉ; āyutam illāṉ- nirāyutaṉ; uvamiyillāṉ - niruvamaṉ. piṟavumaṉṉa.
mūvaḻiyum pakupatappeyar vaṭanaṭiyāl vantavāṟu kāṇka. vaṭanulār pakupatattit tattitam eṉpar.	(to. vi. 87)
etirmaṟiviṉi amippu  -
naṭa vā mutaliya ēvalviṉip pakāppatattip pakutiyāka niṟutti iṭiniliyiṉṟi ēṉ ēm ōm āy īr āṉ āḷ ār ā tu a - eṉṟa vikutikaḷi ēṟṟi muṭikkiṉ, mūviṭattu aimpāl etirmaṟi viṉimuṟṟu uṇṭākum.
	e-ṭu : naṭavēṉ (yāṉ), naṭavēm(yām), naṭavōm(yām), naṭavāy(nī), naṭavīr (nīr), naṭavāṉ (avaṉ), naṭavāḷ (avaḷ), naṭavār (avar), naṭavā (kutirikaḷ), naṭavātu (yāṉi), naṭavāviṉa (avi)- eṉṟu varum.	(to. vi. 112 uri)
erupuṇarumāṟu -
eru eṉṟa peyar nilimoḻiyāy varumoḻi vaṉkaṇat toṭu puṇarumiṭattu, valleḻuttum iṉamāṉa mel leḻuttum mikum. uyir nīṅkalāka ēṉiya kaṇattoṭu puṇarumiṭattu ammuc cāriyi iṭiyē varutalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu : erukkuḻi, eruṅkuḻi - valleḻuttum melleḻut tum muṟiyē mikkaṉa. eruvaṅkuḻi, eruvañāṟci, eruvayāppu - ammuc cāriyi peṟṟaṉa. eru + īṭṭam = eru vīṭṭam - eṉa iyalpāka uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟup puṇarntatu.	(to. e. 260 nac. uri)
urupēṟkumiṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu eruviṉi mutalāka 
(to.e. 173) varuvatu pōlap poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum eruviṉ kuṟumi eṉac ciṟupāṉmi iṉcāriyi peṟuta lum koḷka.	(to. e. 260 nac. uri)
ellā eḻuttum meynnili tammiyal mayakkam kiḷattal  -
‘uyir meynnili tammiyal mayakkam kiḷattal’ kāṇka.
ellām eṉpatu urupuēṟṟal  -
ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar aḵṟiṇiyik kuṟikku miṭattu urupēṟkumāyiṉ aṟṟuccāriyiyum aṭuttu urupiṉiyum koṇṭu muṟṟummiyi iṟutiyil peṟṟu ellāvaṟṟiyum eṉa varum. ellāvaṟṟoṭum, ellā vaṟṟukkum mutalākap piṟa urupukaḷoṭum kūṭṭuka.
iṉi, appeyar uyartiṇikkaṇ varumāyiṉ urupēṟku miṭattu, nammuccāriyiyiyum aṭuttu urupiṉiyum peṟṟu iṟutiyil muṟṟummiyiēṟṟu, ellānammiyum - ellānammoṭum - ellā namakkum - eṉṟāṟ pōla varum.		(naṉ. 245 caṅ.)
ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar puṇarumāṟu  -
ellām eṉpatu irutiṇip potuppeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itu vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ aḵṟiṇiyi uṇarttum vaḻi vaṟṟuccāriyiyum, uyartiṇiyi uṇarttumvaḻi nammuccāriyiyum peṟum; iṟutiyil ummic cāriyi peṟum. urupēṟṟaṟkaṇṇum innili uṇṭu; iyalpu kaṇattu muṉṉum iḵtuṇṭu.
e-ṭu	: ellāvaṟṟiyum - (to. e. 189 nac.); ellā nammiyum - (to.e. 190 nac.); ellāvaṟṟuk kōṭum - (to.e. 322nac.); ellānaṅkālkaḷum	- (to. e. 324 nac.)
ellāvaṟṟuñāṇum, yāḻum, uṟuppum.	(to. e. 322 nac.)
ellānaññāṇum, ellānamyāḻum, ellānammuṟuppum		(to. e. 324 nac.)
alvaḻikkaṇ vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟātu iyalpu ātalum, īṟu keṭutalum, valimeli mikutalum, iṟutikkaṇ ummuc cāriyi peṟutalum nikaḻum. varumoḻi iyalpukaṇamāyiṉ ellām eṉpataṉ makaram keṭṭup puṇarum; ciṟupāṉmi iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	ellāk kuṟiyavum eṉa īṟukeṭṭu valiyum ummum mikkaṉa; ellāk kollarum	- īṟu keṭṭu valiyum ummum mikkaṉa; ellāṅ kuṟiyarum - īṟu keṭṭu meliyum ummum mikkaṉa; ellā ñāṇum yāḻum vaṭṭum aḻakum - īṟu keṭṭu um mikkatu; ellām vāṭiṉa, ellām āṭiṉa eṉa iyalpu.  ellā ñāṉṟārum, vaṇikarum, aracarum eṉa	īṟukeṭṭu um mikkatu.  (to. e. 322 -324 nac. uri)
ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum meyyum uyirum ātal  -
moḻimarapil moḻikku mutalāy varuvaṉuyir uyirmey kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉavum, moḻikku iṟutiyil varuvaṉa uyir mey uyirmey kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. moḻi mutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramum, moḻimutal uyirmeyyum mey yākavē koḷḷappaṭum. moḻiyiṟuti uyirmey uyiruḷ aṭaṅkum. eṉavē moḻikku iṟutiyum mutalum mey uyir eṉṟa iraṇṭuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭaṉa.	(to. e. 103 nac.)
ellārum eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu  -
ellārum eṉṟa uyartiṇip paṭarkkip peyar, alvaḻip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi makaram iṉamelloṟ ṟākat tirintum, meṉkaṇam vantavaḻi makaram keṭṭum, iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantavaḻi iyalpākavum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : ellāruṅ kaṭiyar, ellāruñ cāṉṟār - makaram melloṟṟātal; ellāru ñāṉṟār; ellāru nāṇiṉār - makaram keṭutal; ellārum vaḷavar, ellāru maṭintār - iyalpākap puṇartal.
ellārum eṉpatu vēṟṟumiyurupuēṟkumiṭattu, ummiyi nīkki ellār eṉṟākit tammuccāriyi peṟṟup piṉṉar urupum ummiyum peṟṟu ellārtammiyum, ellār tammoṭum, ellārtamakkum - eṉṟāṟ pōla varum. poruṭpuṇarccikkum iḵtu okkum. 
	e-ṭu : ellārtam(ṅ)kiyum, ellārtam(ñ)ñāṉamum, ellārta(m)māṭciyum, ellārtammaḻakum.
ummiyi nīkkic cāriyi peṟātu urupēṟṟu īṟṟil um peṟṟu, ellāriyum ellāroṭum ellārkkum eṉa varutalē perumpāṉmi.	(to. e. 191 nac., naṉ. 246)
‘ellā vaḻiyum niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpu’ āvaṉa  -
niṉṟa col- nilimoḻi. nilimoḻikaḷ uyir12, mey ñ ṇ n m ṉ y r l v ḻ ḷ - eṉa 11, kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟu - eṉṉum 24 īṟṟavāy irukkum. ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ varumoḻi mutalāy ñ nm eṉṟa melliṉa meykaḷum, y v eṉṟa iṭiyiṉa meykaḷum, paṉṉīruyirkaḷum varumāyiṉ, perum pāṉmiyum tiripiṉṟi iyalpākap puṇarum.	(to. e. 144 nac.)
ellīrum eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu  -
ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉili uyartiṇippeyar alvaḻip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi makaram iṉamel leḻuttākat tirintum, meṉkaṇam vantavaḻi makaram kuṉṟi yum, iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantavaḻi iyalpākavum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	ellīruṅ kaṭiyīr, ellīruñ ciṟiyīr, ellīrun tīyīr - makaram tirital; ellīru ñāṉṟīr, ellīru nallīr, ellīru māṇṭīr - makaram keṭutal; ellīrum yāvīr, vārīr, ellīrumaṭintīr - iyalpu.
ellīrum eṉpatu urupēṟkumiṭattu ummiyi nīkki ellīr eṉṟu niṉṟu nummuc cāriyi peṟṟuppiṉṉar urupum ummiyum peṟṟu, ellīrnummiyum ellīr nummoṭum ellīrnumakkum - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum. itu poruṭ puṇarccikkum okkum.
	e-ṭu : ellīrnuṅkiyum, ellīrnuññāṇum, ellīr - numyāppum, ellīrnummaḻakum ;
ummiyi nīkkic cāriyi peṟātu urupēṟṟu iṟutiyil ummi peṟṟu, ellīriyum ellīroṭum ellīrkkum- eṉa varutalum koḷka.	(to. e. 191 nac. uri)  (naṉ. 246)
evaṉ eṉṟa kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar urupēṟṟal  -
evaṉ eṉpatu kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu. atu paṭuttalōciyāṉ peyarāyavaḻi, evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟum urupum koṭuttu, vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟu eṉa ṉakarattik keṭuttu, ‘avi’ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vaṟṟuccāriyiyiṉ vakara oṟṟik keṭuttatu pōla (e.122) itaṟku vakaratti muḻutum keṭuttu urupēṟṟi, eva+ṟṟu+ai= evaṟṟi - evaṟṟoṭu - eṉa muṭikka. mīṇṭum nilimoḻi vakarattik keṭuttu eṟṟi - eṟṟoṭu - eṉavum muṭikka.
eṉavē, evaṉ eṉṟa kuṟippu viṉippeyar urupēṟkum pōtu evaṟṟi eṟṟi- evaṟṟoṭu eṟṟoṭu - eṉa iru tiṟattāṉum varum.
tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil evaṉ eṉpatu kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟākavē aḵṟiṇi irupāṟkum potuviṉiyāka iruntatu.	(to. co. 219 cēṉā.),  (to. e. 122,193 nac. uri)
eḻu, eḻūu: vēṟupāṭu  -
eḻu:taṉviṉi; eḻu +u = eḻū : piṟaviṉi. mutaṉili ukarattoṭu piṟaviṉip poruḷil varum ukaram cēra ‘eḻū’ eṉṟāyiṟṟu. iccol iyalpāṉa ūkāra īṟṟuc col laṉṟu; piṟaviṉip poruḷil varum ukaram cēra eḻū eṉṟā yiṟṟu eṉpataṉi viḷakka, ‘eḻūu’eṉṟu, mutaṉiliyoṭu cērnta ukaratti aṟivittaṟkāka ukaram aṟikuṟiyāy eḻutappaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉam eḻu mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ ukaram cērntu (iru kuṟil oru neṭilāy) eḻū mutaliyaṉavākip piṉ eḻūu - mutaliyaṉavāka iruttal paṇṭi vaḻakku.  ukaram cērntu, eḻu +u = eḻuvu eṉṟāṟpōla varutal piṟkāla vaḻakku. 
maka +ar = makār. vikuti akaram cērntati aṟivittaṟ kākavē, ‘makāar’ eṉṟu iṭiyē akaram iṭṭu eḻutupa.		(e. ā. pak. 16)
eḻuttatikārap puṟaṉaṭiyāl koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa  -
taṭa eṉṟa uriccol, taṭavut tiri eṉa, vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumvaḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟatu. taṭavu nili (puṟa.140) eṉa iyalpukaṇattiṉkaṇ ukaram peṟṟatu.
ata eṉṟa akarīṟṟu marappeyar, atavattaṅkaṉi eṉa, attuccāriyiyum ammuccāriyiyum peṟṟatu.
‘kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ’ (e.203) eṉa, akara īṟākiya eḻuttut taṉṉi uṇaraniṉṟavaḻiyum valleḻuttu mikkatu.
naṟavaṅkaṇṇi, kurava(m) nīṭiya - eṉa, ākāra īṟu akara īṟāki ukaram peṟṟavaḻiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iru niliyi lum ammuccāriyi peṟṟatu.
muḷavumā, piṇavu nāy - eṉa, iyalpukaṇam variṉ ammup peṟātāyiṟṟu.
muḻavoṭu ākuḷi (malipaṭu.3), cuṟa eṟimīṉ, ‘ira vaḻaṅku ciṟuneṟi’ (aka. 318) - ākāra īṟu akara īṟāki ukaram peṟātu vantatu. (muḻa + oṭu)
‘kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkali (aka. 97) - ikara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammuccāriyi peṟṟatu.
tīyiṉ aṉṉa - (malipaṭu. 145) - īkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟatu.
tiruvattavar (nālaṭi. 57) - ukara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attuccāriyi peṟṟatu.
ēp peṟṟa (cīvaka. 2965) - ēkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu.
‘kittu uṇṭām pōtu’ (nālaṭi 19) - ‘kittu illar nallar’ - aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu.
puṉṉiyaṅkāṉal, mulliyan toṭiyal - aikāra īṟu vēṟ ṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu.
kōyil - ōkārīṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.
añcevi - alvaḻikkaṇ, akam eṉṟa nilimoḻi kakaramum akaramum (ka) keṭṭatu.
maravampāvi, maravanākam - vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭaṉkaṇṇum ammup peṟṟu makaram (meṉkaṇam varuvaḻik ) keṭṭu muṭintatu. (marappāvi, nākamaram eṉpaṉa poruḷ)
‘kāṉam pāṭiṉēm’ (puṟa.144) ‘poṉṉantikiri’, ‘poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭu’ - kāṉ poṉ - eṉṟa ṉakara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ iruvaḻi yum ammup peṟṟaṉa.
‘vetirattuk kālporu’ (naṟ.62) - rakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu.
‘nāvalan taṇpoḻil, (cilap. 17: 1),’kāṉalam peruntuṟi’ (aiṅ.158) lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu.
neytalañ ciṟupaṟi - lakarīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu.
‘āyiṭi irupērāṇmi’ (kuṟun. 43) av + iṭi : akaram nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.
‘aṉṟi yaṉittum’- akarac cuṭṭu ‘aṉṟi’ eṉat tirintatu.
teṅkiṉ paḻam - kuṟṟukara īṟu poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟatu.
‘toṇṭu taliyiṭṭa’ - oṉpatu ‘toṇṭu’ eṉat tirintatu.
arumaruntāṉ, cōḻaṉāṭu, pāṇṭiyaṉāṭu, toṇṭi - māṉ nāṭu, maliyamāṉ nāṭu, potuvil - ivi muṟiyē arumantāṉ, cōṇāṭu, pāṇṭināṭu, toṇṭināṭu, malāṭu, potiyil - eṉṟu tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkiṉa.	(to. e. 483 nac. uri)
eḻuttatikāram kūṟum iru ceytikaḷ  -
ippaṭalattil vitikkappaṭuvaṉa ellām karuviyum ceyki yum eṉa iru vakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ karuvi eḻuttiyal pataviyal eṉṉum iraṇṭu ōttāṉum, ceyki uyirīṟṟup puṇariyal mutaliya mūṉṟu ōttāṉum kūṟappaṭum. karuvi potuvum ciṟappum eṉa iruvakittu. mutal iraṇṭu ōttiṉum kūṟappaṭuvaṉa potukkaruvi. uyirīṟṟup puṇariyal mutaṟkaṇ puṇarcci iṉṉateṉak kūṟappaṭuvaṉavum, urupu puṇariyal iṟutikkaṇ cāriyit tōṟṟam kūṟappaṭuvaṉavum ceyki oṉṟaṟkēyuriya karuviyātaliṉ ciṟappuk karuviyām.	(naṉ. 56 civañā.)
eḻuttatikāram: colviḷakkamum poruḷniliyum  -
eḻuttatikāram eṉpatu eḻuttiṉatu atikarittaliyuṭiyatu eṉa aṉmoḻittokiyāy appaṭalam muḻutukkum kāraṇak kuṟiyāyiṟṟu. eḻuttiṉi nutali varum pala ōttiṉatu tokuti yākiya paṭalam eḻuttatikāram āyiṟṟu. eḻuttiṉatu atikāratti yuṭiyatu eṉṟaviṭattu, ‘eḻuttiṉatu’eṉṟa āṟām vēṟṟumi ēṟṟa col eḻuvāyiṉ poruḷatāy, ‘atikārattiyuṭiyatu’ eṉṟa viṉiyik koṇṭu muṭintatu. vēṟṟumi viṉikoṇṭu muṭivatu kārakamām. āṟāvatu nērāka viṉiyik koṇṭu muṭiyātu. ākavē eḻuvāyiṉ poruḷilēyē atu viṉi koṇṭu muṭintatu. eḻuttatikāram eṉpatu āṟāvatu viṉimutaṟ poruṇmi yiṉkaṇ vanta kārakamām.	(cū. vi. pak. 17)
eḻuttatikāram	- 	eḻutti uṇarttiya atikāram							(iḷa. nac.)
	atikāram		-	muṟimi
eḻuttatikārattil eḻuttu iṉiya eṉṟal, iṉṉa peyara eṉṟal, iṉṉa muṟimiya eṉṟal, iṉṉa aḷaviṉa eṉṟal, iṉṉa piṟappiṉa eṉṟal, iṉṉa puṇarcciya eṉṟal, iṉṉa vaṭiviṉa eṉṟal, iṉṉa taṉmiya eṉṟal - eṉṟa eṭṭu vakiyāṉum, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uṇmittaṉmi, kuṟivu, kūṭṭam, pirivu, mayakkam, moḻiyākkam, nili, iṉam, oṉṟu palavātal, tirintataṉ tiripu atu eṉṟal, tirintataṉ tiripu piṟitu eṉṟal, tirintataṉ tiripu atuvum piṟitum eṉṟal, niliyiṟṟu eṉṟal, niliyātu eṉṟal, niliyiṟṟum niliyātum eṉṟal - eṉṟa eṭṭu iṟanta palavakiyāṉum eḻuttu uṇarttappaṭṭatu.	(to. e. 1 iḷa., nac. uri)
eḻuttatikāram: toṭarilakkaṇam  -
atikāram eṉpatu īṇṭu atikarittali yuṭiyatām. ataṉi yuṭiyatu eṉavē, eḻutti nutali varum  pala ōttiṉatu tokuti eḻuttatikāram eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. eḻuttiṉatu atikāratti yuṭiyatu eṉpuḻi, āṟāvatu viṉimutaṟ poruṇmiyiṉkaṇ vanta kārakam.	(naṉ. 56 civañā.)
eḻuttākā olikaḷ  -
muṟku vīḷi ilati - mutaliyaṉa orōvaḻip poru ḷuṇarttiṉavēṉum, eḻuttu ākā olikaḷām. kaṭaloli caṅkoli mutaliyaṉa poruḷ uṇarttāta, eḻuttākā olikaḷām. muṟku - vīrar pōrkku aḻikkum aṟikūval karccaṉi; vīḷi - cīḻkkiyiṭum oli; ilati - aṭinā aṭiyil tōṉṟuvatōr olikkuṟippu; āṭumāṭukaḷi ōṭṭum oli.	(to.pāyiram, nac.)
taṉṉiyē uṇarttum kaṭaloli iṭiyoli mutalāyiṉa vum, taṉṉoṭu maṇaviṉiyum makiḻvum mutalāya piṟa poruḷiyum uṇarttum caṅkoli nakiyoli mutalā yiṉavum, collātal taṉmiyoṭu kūṭiyāyiṉum taṉit tāyiṉum uṟuppātal taṉmi eytāmiyiṉ eḻuttu eṉappaṭāta āyiṉa.	(pā. vi. pak. 170)
eḻuttiyal pataviyal toṭarpu  -
mēl eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭu pakutiyāl uṇarttap paṭum eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, eḻuttiṟkēyuriya pattilakkaṇamum eḻuttiyalāṉ uṇartti, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ niṉṟatu av veḻuttiṉāṉ ākiya patam ātaliṉ ataṉip pataviyalāl uṇarttutaliṉ ivviyal mēliyiyalōṭu iyipuṭit tām.	(naṉ. 127 mayili.)
eḻuttilakkaṇap puṟaṉaṭi  -
eḻuttukkaḷ coṟkaḷāka āyiṉum, accoṟkaḷ tammo ṭum urupoṭum puṇariṉum, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam oru taṉmit tākavē irukkum.	(naṉ. 127)
eḻuttilakkaṇam -
moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamāyum nātattiṉatu kāriyamāyum varum ōci eḻuttu eṉappaṭum. eḻuttōci kāraṇam āmāṟum, moḻi ataṉ kāriyam āmāṟum, nātam kāraṇam āmāṟum eḻuttōci ataṉ kāriyam āmāṟum kāṇka.	(i. vi. eḻut. 3 uri)
eḻuttiṉ tōṟṟamum vakuppum vikāramum eṉṟa immūṉṟa ṉuḷ, eḻuttu vakippāṭu ellām aṭaṅkum. eṉṉi? tōṟṟam eṉpuḻi eḻuttup piṟakkum iṭamum muṟiyum eṇṇum eṉavum, vakuppu eṉpuḻi mutal cārpu uyirmey mutaliya kūṟupāṭu eṉavum, vikāram eṉpuḻip patattilum puṇarppilum varum tiripākkam mutaliya vēṟupāṭu eṉavum tōṉṟum.	(to. vi. 2. uri)
eḻuttilakkaṇa vaki -
eḻuttilakkaṇam eṇ peyar muṟi piṟappu uruvam māttiri mutalnili īṟṟunili iṭinili pōli- eṉavum, patam puṇarppu - eṉavum, pattu akattilakkaṇamum iraṇṭu puṟattilakkaṇamumākap paṉṉiru vakippaṭum.	(naṉ. 57)
 eḻuttiṟku nālvakiyāl peyariṭal  -
iṭukuṟip potuppeyarum, iṭukuṟic ciṟappuppeyarum, kāraṇap potuppeyarum, kāraṇac ciṟappuppeyarum eṉa eḻuttu nālvakiyāṟ peyariṭappaṭum.
	uyir, uyirmey, uṭampu - eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟip potup peyar;
	avaṟṟuḷ, a ā ka ṅa - eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟic ciṟappuppeyar;
	kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam - mutaliyaṉa kāraṇap b pātuppeyar;
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram - mutaliyaṉa kāraṇac ciṟappup peyar  eṉak koḷka. ākiyāl ‘nāku’eṉum moḻi īṟṟeḻuttu (ku), iṭukuṟip potuppeyarāl uyirmey eṉṟum, iṭukuṟic ciṟappup peyarāl ‘ku’eṉṟum, kāraṇap potuppeyarāl kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟum, kāraṇac ciṟappup peyarāl kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟum  peyaroṭu vaḻaṅkum.	(to. vi. 72 uri)
eḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭaṅkaḷ -
eḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭaṅkaḷ tali, miṭaṟu, neñcu eṉṟa mūṉṟu mām. ivaṟṟuḷ uyirum iṭiyiṉameykaḷum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum, valliṉam talivaḷiyāṉum,  melliṉam mūkkuvaḷik kalappālum, āytam neñcuvaḷiyālum piṟakkum.		(to. e. 84, 88, 101 nac. uri)
eḻuttiṉ piṟappiṟku muyaṟcit tāṉaṅkaḷ -
pal, itaḻ, nā, mūkku, mēlvāy ākiya aintum eḻuttiṉ piṟappiṟkuriya muyaṟcit tāṉaṅkaḷ.
i ī ē ē ai - eṉṟa aintum ciṟappākap paṟkaḷiyum, u ū o ō ōḷa- eṉṟa aintum ciṟappāka itaḻkaḷiyum, ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ - eṉṟa āṟum ciṟappāka mūkkiṉiyum, k c ṭ - eṉpaṉa ciṟappāka mēlvāyiṉiyum nāviṉiyum, t - eṉpatu ciṟappāka nāviṉiyum paṟkaḷiyum, ṟ y r ḻ - eṉpaṉa ciṟappāka nāviṉiyum mēlvāyiṉiyum, l ḷ - eṉpaṉa nā paṟkaḷ mēlvāyiṉiyum, p - eṉpatu itaḻkaḷiyum, v - eṉpatu itaḻkaḷiyum paṟkaḷiyum, - muyaṟcit tāṉaṅkaḷākak koṇṭu piṟappaṉavām.	(to. e. 86 - 99 nac.)
eḻuttiṉ māttiri  -
uyiraḷapeṭi nāṉku māttiriya ātalum, aikāra oḷakārak  kuṟukkaṅkaḷ oṉṟari māttiriya ātalum āriṭattuḷḷum avi pōlpavaṟṟuḷḷum aruki vantu ceyyuḷ vaḻu vamitiyāy muṭitaliṉ avaṟṟi oḻittu, ellārkkum oppa muṭintu payiṉṟu varuvaṉa mūṉṟu māttiriyum orumāttiriyumē ātaliṉ ‘mūṉṟu uyiraḷapu’ eṉṟum, ‘oṉṟē kuṟiloṭu ai oḷak kuṟukkam’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻi iṭippaṭiṉ kuṟukik kāl māttiri peṟutal uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāl koḷka. uyirmeykku aḷavu kūṟātoḻintār, mēl ‘uyiraḷavāy’ (89) eṉṟaliṉ.	(naṉ. 99 civañā.)
uyiraḷapeṭimūṉṟum, neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭum, kuṟṟeḻuttu - aikāra oḷakārak kuṟukkaṅkaḷ- oṟṟaḷapeṭi - ivi taṉittaṉi oṉṟum, oṟṟeḻuttu- kuṟṟiyalikaram- kuṟṟiyalukaram - āytam - ivi taṉittaṉi ariyum, makarak kuṟukkam- āytak kuṟukkam- ivi taṉittaṉiyē kālum māttiri peṟum. uyirmeyyiṉ māttiri uyirmāt tiriyē ātaliṉ uyirkkuṟil uyirneṭil māttiriyē ivaṟṟiṟkum ām.	(naṉ. 99)
eḻuttiṉ varivaṭivam  -
meyyiṉ vaṭivum uyirmeyyiṉ vaṭivum palamuṟi vēṟupaṭāmiyāṉum, ekaram ēkāram okaram ōkāram eppōtum oru vaṭivu ākiyāṉum, mayakkam nīppatu vēṇṭi mēṟpuḷḷi koṭuttār pulavar. ākiyāl kuṟṟeḻuttiṉmēl nīṇṭa puḷḷiyum, oṟṟeḻuttiṉ mēl cuḻitta puḷḷiyum varum eṉṟuṇarka.	(to. vi. 12 uri) 
eḻuttiṉi eṭṭiṟanta palavakiyāṉ uṇarttal,
eḻuttiṉi eṭṭuvakiyāṉ uṇarttal  -
‘eḻuttatikāram: col viḷakkamum poruḷ niliyum’ - piṟpakuti kāṇka.
eḻuttiṉik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ  -
irēki eṉiṉum, vari eṉiṉum, poṟi eṉiṉum, eḻuttu eṉiṉum oru poruṭkiḷavi.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 3)
eḻuttu aḷaviṟantu olikkum iṭam  -
uyirum meyyumākiya eḻuttukkaḷ ici,viḷi, paṇṭa māṟṟu,  nāval, kuṟippici, muṟiyīṭu, pulampal- mutaliya vaṟṟil tam māttiri elli kaṭantu  olikkum . (ici nūlār uyir 12 māttiri variyilum, mey 11 māttiri variyilum nīṇṭolikkum eṉpa.
e-ṭu:
‘uppōo eṉavurittu mīḷvāḷ’   - paṇṭamāṟṟu
‘nāvalōo eṉṟicikkum nāḷōti’  - nāval
‘kaḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉum kallatar attam’   -  kuṟippici
‘aṇṇāvōoo’   - viḷi (pulampalum ām)	(naṉ. 101)
eḻuttu iṉṉatu eṉpatu  -
col tōṉṟutalukkuk kāraṇamāṉa oli eḻuttu eṉappaṭum. eḻuttu eṉpatu  kaṭpulaṉākā uruvum kaṭpulaṉākiya vaṭivum uṭiyatāka vēṟuvēṟu vakuttukkoṇṭu taṉṉiyē uṇarttiyum coṟku iyintum niṟkum ōciyām.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻutteṉappaṭuvatu yātō eṉiṉ, kaṇ mutalāya piṟavaṟṟuk kup pulaṉākātu cevippulaṉēyākum olivaṭivum, cevi mutalāya piṟavaṟṟukkup pulaṉākātu kaṇṇukkum meykkum pulaṉākum varivaṭivum uṭittāy, taṉittu niṉṟāyiṉum cārntu niṉṟāyiṉum, taṉṉi uṇarttaluṭaṉ poru ḷuṇarttum collākalum ac collukku uṟuppu ākalum ākiya iru taṉmiyum oruṅku peṟṟāyiṉum, aṉṟi uṟuppāka iyitaltaṉmi oṉṟē peṟṟāyiṉum niṟkum oliyām.	(pā. vi. pak. 170)
kāṟṟuk kaṭpulaṉākā uruviṉatāyiṉum meyppulaṉāyuṟṟu iṉpatuṉpam ākkalāṉum, iyaṅkutalāṉum, maram mutaliya vaṟṟi iyakkalāṉum, ikkāṟṟu valitu - ikkāṟṟu melitu - eṉak kūṟappaṭalāṉum, poruḷ eṉṟu koḷvōm. atupōla, oliyum unti mutalākat tōṉṟi, tali - miṭaṟu - neñcu - pal - itaḻ - nā- mūkku - mēlvāy - eṉṟa eṇvaki nilattum piṟantu, kaṭpulaṉām taṉmiyiṉṟic cevikkaṇ ceṉṟuṟum ūṟu uṭimi yāṉum, iṉpatuṉpam ākka lāṉum, vaṉmi - meṉmi - kuṟumi - neṭumi - kōṭa lāṉum, vicumpiṉkaṇ iyaṅkuvatoru taṉmi yuṭimi yāṉum, pērolikkaṇ maṇ atirtal kāṇappaṭalāṉum, kāṟṟāl kāriyappaṭum taṉmi yuṭiyatākum.	(pā. vi. pak. 171)
moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇam ākiṉṟa aṇuttiraḷiṉ kāriyam oli eḻuttām. aṇuttiraḷ eṉṟatu nātam. oli eṉṟatu ataṉ kāriya oli. aṇu eṉṟatu olinuṭpatti. ‘aṇut tiraḷ oli eḻuttu’ eṉiṉ, muṟku-vīḷi - ilati - mutaliya kuṟippicikaḷum aṇuttiraḷē ātalāl avi eḻut tākāmiyāṉum, ‘moḻi mutaṟ kāraṇamām oli eḻuttu’ eṉiṉ, avvoli iṉṉa oli eṉa viḷaṅkāmiyāl, ‘niṟiuyir muyaṟciyiṉ uḷvaḷi turappa, eḻum aṇuttiraḷ oli’ eṉak kāṭṭa vēṇṭutalāṉum ‘moḻi mutaṟ kāraṇa mām aṇuttiraḷ oli eḻuttu’ eṉṟār.	(naṉ. 58 irāmā.)
eḻuttu, uyir valliṉam  melliṉam  iṭiyiṉam  eṉṟa nāṉku iṉamātal -
nam uṭampiṉuḷ cuvācappiyiṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭum vaḷi kuralvaḷiyuḷ pukuntu, aṭiyaṇṇam iṭiyaṇṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil paṭṭu veḷippaṭutaliṉ oli uṇṭā kiṟatu. avvaḷi aṇṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil paṭukiyil, nāviṉ ceykiyāl oli vēṟupaṭuvatākum. vaḷiyi nāviṉ nuṉi - iṭi - aṭi - viḷimpu - ākiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉāl aṭi yaṇṇam iṭiyaṇṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil taṭuttum taṭukkāmalum veḷiviṭutal kūṭum. taṭukkumpōtu ciṟitaḷavu taṭuttalum muḻutum taṭuttalum iyalum. uyireḻuttukkaḷi olikkumpōtu vaḷiyi nāviṉāl taṭukkāmal veḷiviṭu kiṉṟōm; valleḻuttu melleḻuttukkaḷi olikkum pōtu naṉku taṭuttu veḷiviṭukiṉṟōm; iṭiyeḻuttuk kaḷi olikkumpōtu vaḷiyic ciṟitaḷavu taṭukkiṉṟōm. vaḷiyit taṭukkāmal veḷiviṭutaliṉ uyireḻuttukkaḷ tāmē olikka iyalkiṉṟaṉa. vaḷiyi naṉku naṭuttaliṉ valleḻuttu melleḻuttukkaḷ tāmē olikka varuvaṉavā yilli. ciṟitaḷavu taṭuttaliṉ iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷ tāmē ōrāṟṟāṉ olikka iyalum. olikkum tiṟattil, uyireḻuttuk kaḷukkum valleḻuttu melleḻuttukkaḷukkum iṭip paṭṭiruttalāṉē  y r l vḻ ḷ - eṉpaṉa iṭiyeḻut tukkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. melleḻuttu, valleḻutti ottup piṟantu oli ciṟituciṟitāka mūkkiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷiyiṭap paṭutaliṉ vēṟāyiṉa. ikkāraṇattāl melleḻuttuk kaḷ valleḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭa, eḻuttukkaḷ uyir - vali - meli - iṭi - eṉa nālvēṟu iṉaṅkaḷ āyiṉa.	(e. ā. pak. 11, 12)
eḻuttu eṉpataṉ collamippu  -
eḻutappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruṭkaṇ, eḻutu eṉṉum mutaṉilit toḻiṟcolliṉ muṉṉarc ceyappaṭu poruḷi uṇarttum aikāra vikuti puṇarntu ‘ceyyāy eṉṉum muṉṉili viṉiccol’ (to.co. 450 cēṉā.) eṉṉumcūttirattu ‘moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉiyum muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal’ eṉṉum uttivakiyāṉ avvikāram keṭṭuk keṭṭa vaḻit takaram iraṭṭittu eḻuttu eṉṟu muṭintatu.
eḻutu eṉṉum mutaṉili ‘eḻuttu’ eṉat tāṉē tirintu niṉṟu, aḵtu ākupeyarāṉ eḻutappaṭuvatākiya ceyappaṭu poruḷi uṇarttiṟṟu eṉṟal poruntātu. oru kāraṇa miṉṟit tirital kūṭāmiyāṉum, naṭa-vā-karu-ce- mutaliya mutaṉilikaḷ ellām vikutiyōṭaṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkal āṟṟāmiyāṉum, immutaṉili kaḷ uriccoṟkaḷ ātaliṉpeyarttaṉmippaṭṭuḻiyallatu ākupeyar ātaṟku ēlāmiyāṉum eṉpatu.	(cū. vi. pak. 31, 32)
eḻuttu eṉpatu eḻuti eṉpataṉ tiripāyiṉ, naṭa eṉpatu naṭavāy eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉṟu koḷḷumpōtu, naṭa eṉpatu pōla naṭavāy eṉpatum vaḻaṅkumāṟu pōla, eḻuttu eṉpatu pōla eḻuti eṉpatum vaḻakkāṟṟil iruttal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam iṉmiyiṉ, eḻuttu eṉpatu eḻuti eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉal cālātu. viḷaṅku - peruku- maṭaṅku- mutalāya mutaṉilikaḷ, viḷakku - perukku - maṭakku- mutalākat tirintu, viḷaṅkuvatu- perukuvatu - maṭaṅkuvatu - eṉap poruḷpaṭumiṭattu viṉimutalum,
keṭu eṉpatu, kēṭu eṉat tirintu oruvaṉ keṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya tīviṉiyi uṇarttumvaḻik karuviyum,
nīntu - muṭaṅku - iṭuku - eṉpaṉa, nīttu - muṭakku - iṭukku - eṉat tirintu nīntumiṭattiyum muṭaṅkumiṭattiyum iṭuku miṭattiyum uṇarttumvaḻi nilaṉum,
pāṭu - cūṭu - karutu - eṉpaṉa, pāṭṭu - cūṭṭu - karuttu - eṉat tirintu pāṭappaṭuvatu - cūṭappaṭuvatu - karutappaṭuvatu - eṉap poruḷpaṭumvaḻic ceyappaṭuporuḷum,
cuṭu eṉpatu cūṭu eṉat tirintu cuṭutalāṉākiya vaṭuviṉi uṇarttumiṭattup payaṉum -
eṉa viṉi koḷvavaṟṟuḷḷē toḻil oḻinta ēṉiya poruḷkaḷi ākupeyarāṉ uṇarttumāṟu pōla,
eḻutu - uṇ - mutalākiya mutaṉilikaḷum eḻuttu - ūṇ- mutaliyaṉavākat tirintu toḻiṟpeyarpporuḷiyum, āku - peyarākic ceyappaṭuporuḷiyum uṇarttum eṉpatu. mutaṉili tirintu ākupeyarākik kālam uṇarttal  vantuḻik koḷka.	(pā. vi. pak. 229, 230)
eḻuttu ōraṉṉa  -
1.	 eḻuttōṭu oru taṉmiyaṉa,  2. eḻuttāṉ oru taṉmiyaṉa. 1, ‘cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭiyē, eḻutteṉap paṭupa muppaḵtu’ eṉavē, cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟumē ciṟantaṉa, ēṉiya muppatum avvāṟu ciṟantila eṉavum poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi vilakkic ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu iviyum oppa vaḻaṅkum eṉa, ammuppataṉi yum upamāṉamāṉa mēmpaṭṭa poruḷāyum immūṉṟaṉi yum upamēyamāṉa avaṟṟiṉ tāḻnta poruḷāyum kāṭṭutaṟku, ‘eḻuttōṭu orutaṉmiyaṉa’ eṉṟu poruḷ tarum ‘eḻuttō raṉṉa’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭār.		(to. e. 2 iḷa., nac. uri)  (po. 663 pērā.)
2. eḻuttāṉ oru taṉmiyaṉa eṉpatu, olivaṭivil eṭuttal paṭuttal ōciyāṉ vevvēṟu poruḷtarum coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷ varivaṭivil eḻutumpōtu oṉṟākavē eḻutap paṭutal.
e-ṭu :	cempu oṉpatiṉtoṭiyum, cempoṉ patiṉ toṭiyum varivaṭivil ‘cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi’ eṉa oṉṟāka eḻutappaṭutal pōlvaṉa.	(to. e. 141 nac. uri)
eḻuttu ōraṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci’ -
iraṇṭu vevvēṟu karuttukkaḷit terivikkum coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷ olikkumpōtu eṭuttal paṭuttal ōcikaḷāṉ vevvēṟāka uṇarappaṭiṉum, varivaṭivil eḻutumpōtu oṉṟākavē cērttu eḻutappaṭum nili.
cempu oṉpatiṉtoṭi, cempoṉ patiṉtoṭi- eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum varivaṭivil ‘cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi’ eṉa oṉṟākic cempu eṉpataṉiyum cempoṉ eṉpataṉiyum muṟiyē eṭuttu olittavaḻi vevvēṟu poruḷ uṇarttum coṟṟoṭar ātal.
iḵtu ‘eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavātal’ niliyic cuṭṭuva tām.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
