tamiḻmoḻiyil coṟṟoṭarkaḷiṉ poruḷ aṟiyap paṭātavari puṇarcci vēṟṟumikkaṇṇatā alvaḻik kaṇṇatā eṉa aṟital iyalāmiyiṉ,eḻuttukkaḷatu ‘oṉṟu palavātal nili’yi naṉku uṇartal vēṇṭum.	(e. ku. pak. 145)
eḻuttuk kaṭpulaṉākā uru eṉpatu  -
uru eṉṟatu maṉaṉ uṇarvāy niṟkum karuttupporuḷi. atu ceṟippac cēṟalāṉum, ceṟippa varutalāṉum, iṭi eṟiyappaṭutalāṉum, iṉpatuṉpatti ākkutalāṉum, uruvum uruvum kūṭip piṟattalāṉum, unti mutalākat tōṉṟi neñcu - miṭaṟu - tali- mūkku - aṇṇam - nā - pal - itaḻ - eṉṟa eṇvaki nilattum piṟantu kaṭpulaṉām taṉmiyiṉṟic cevikkaṇ ceṉṟuṟum ūṟu uṭimi yāṉum, vicumpil piṟantu iyaṅkuvatoru taṉmiyuṭimi yāṉum, kāṟṟiṉ kuṇamāvatōr uruvām; vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi kōṭalāṉum uruvē āyiṟṟu,	(to. e. 1 na.c. uri)
eḻuttuk kaṭpulaṉākiya vaṭivu peṟutal  -
cevippulaṉākiya eḻuttoliyi maṉattāṉ uṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvillōrum uṇartaṟku  eḻuttukkaḷukku vevvēṟu vaṭivam kāṭṭi eḻutappaṭṭu naṭattaliṉ, kaṭ pulaṉākiya varivaṭivum eḻuttiṟku uḷatāyiṟṟu .
makarakkuṟukkam veḷiyēyuḷḷa puḷḷiyoṭu taṉ vaṭṭatti ṉuḷḷum oru puḷḷi peṟum(m¶) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa vari vaṭiviṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu.	(to.e. 14, 16 nac.)

eḻuttukkaḷ tampeyar kuṟippiṭappaṭutal  -
moḻikku mutalil vārāta eḻuttukkaḷum tampeyar kuṟippiṭumiṭattu moḻikku mutalāka varum.
varumāṟu 	‘ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum’ - (to.e. 29 nac.); ‘ṭakāra mākum’ - 302; ‘ṇakāra iṟuti’ - 302; ‘yakāra iṟuti’ - 357; ‘rakāra iṟuti’ - 362; ‘laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar’ - 24; ‘ḻakāra iṟuti’ - 383; ‘ḷakāra iṟuti’ - 396; ‘ṟakāra mākum’ - 332; ‘ṉakāra iṟuti’ - 332.
eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta ṅa ṭa ṇa ya ra la ḻa ḷa ṟa ṉa- eṉpaṉavum tampeyar kuṟippiṭumiṭattu moḻikku mutalil vantavāṟu.	(to. e. 66 nac.)
eḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiriyaḷavu aṭṭavaṇi -
tolkāppiyam	1	2	1	1	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	-	ṅu	-	-
vīracōḻiyam	1	2	1	1	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	-	ṅu	-	-
nēminātam	1	2	1ṅū	1ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅu	ṅu	3	-
naṉṉūl	1	2	1	1	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅu	ṅu	3	-	
ilakkaṇa
viḷakkam	1	2	1	1	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	-	ṅu	-	-
toṉṉūl
viḷakkam	1	2	1	1	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅu	ṅu	3	1
cuvāminātam	1	2	1ṅū	1ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅu	ṅu	-	-

eḻuttukkaḷatu oṉṟu palavātal nili -
oṉṟu palavākum coṟṟoṭarkaḷip potumoḻi eṉpa. varivaṭivil oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum coṟṟoṭar olivaṭi vil eṭuttal paṭuttal vēṟupāṭṭāṉ palvēṟu coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷ ākum.
‘eḻuttu ōraṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci’ kāṇka.		(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟivu -
eḻuttukkaḷ tamakku iyalpāyuḷḷa olittal aḷavil kuṟinta kuṟukkaṅkaḷ ātal.
	e-ṭu :	aikārak kuṟukkam (to. e. 57), makarakkuṟukkam (to.e. 13)	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
aṟuvakiyilakkaṇa nūl  ā ī ū ē ō- eṉpaṉa neṭil eṉṟum, ai oḷa-iraṇṭum 1ṅū māttiri peṟuvaṉa eṉṟum, eṉavē ivaṟṟik ‘kuṟilneṭil’ eṉṟum koḷvar eṉṟum kuṟippiṭum.
eḻuttukkaḷatu kūṭṭam -
meyyōṭu uyirkaḷ kūṭi uyirmeyyi uṇṭākkutal mutaliyaṉa.	(to. e. 17nac.) (1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu ‘tirintataṉ tiripu atu’ eṉṟa nili -
al+tiṇi=aṟṟiṇi, aḵṟiṇi.
nilimoḻi īṟṟil lakaram vara, varumoḻi mutalil takaram vantāl, nilimoḻi īṟṟu lakaramum varumoḻi mutal takaramum ṟakaramākat tiriyum eṉpatu viti. lakaram tiriyāmiyu muṇṭu.	(to.e.149,369 nac.)
nilimoḻi īṟṟu lakaram ṟakaramākat tiritalēyaṉṟi āyta mākat tiriyiṉum, ataṉi lakaramākat karuti varumoḻit takaratti ṟakaramākat tirittuk koḷvatu. ‘tirintataṉ tiripu atu’ eṉṟa nayamām.
tirintatu lakaram; ataṉ tiripu āytam. tiripākiya āytatti lakaramākavē karutip puṇarppatu atu eṉpatu.
‘mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum’ eṉṟu kūṟip piṉṉar aṟu eṉpatu akal eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu ‘aṟueṉ kiḷavi mutal nī ṭummē’ (to. e. 458 nac.) eṉṉātu, ‘āṟueṉ kiḷavi mutal nī ṭummē’ eṉṟalum,	(to. e. 440 nac.)
‘mūṉṟaṉ mutalnili  nīṭalum urittē
uḻakkueṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkak tāṉa’	(to. e. 457 nac.)
eṉṟalum pōlvaṉa tirintataṉ tiripu atuvām.
tirintatu āṟu; ataṉ tiripu aṟu; āṟu eṉpatum aṟu eṉpatum oṉṟē;
tirintatu mūṉṟu;ataṉ tiripu muṉṟu;mūṉṟu eṉpatum muṉṟu eṉpatum oṉṟē - eṉṟu karuti amikkum nayam itu.
eṉavē, innayattāl āṟu eṉṟu kūṟiṉum aṟu eṉṟu koṇṭum,  mūṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟiṉum muṉṟu eṉṟu koṇṭum poruḷ  ceyyavēṇṭum eṉpatu, 
“aṟu eṉṉātu āṟu eṉṟār, tirintataṉ tiripu atu eṉṉum nayattāṉ”  	(to. e. 458 nac.) (1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu ‘tirintataṉ tiripu atuvum piṟitum’ eṉṟal -
neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikaḷ yāṉ yām nām nī tāṉ tām- eṉpaṉa. avi āṟaṉurupum nāṉkaṉurupum ēṟkumpōtu. akaraccāriyi peṟṟu,muṟiyē eṉa ema nama niṉa taṉa tama-eṉṟāki, urupukaḷoṭu puṇarum.
	eṉa+ku = valleḻutti mutalāka uṭiya vēṟṟumi yurupu ātaliṉ iṭiyē valloṟṟu mikku eṉakku eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
	eṉa+a = eṉava eṉṟāyiṟṟu (iṭiyē vakaram uṭampaṭu mey).
	eṉa+atu =āṟām vēṟṟumiyurupākiya atu eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa akaram, nilimoḻi(eṉ+a=eṉa-eṉṟu) akaraccāriyi peṟṟu niṉṟamiyiṉ keṭa, eṉa+tu=eṉatu eṉṟāyiṟṟu. neṭumutal kuṟukum coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟil varum akaramē keṭum eṉṉātu, keṭukiṉṟa akaram vēṟē eṉṟal ‘tirintataṉ tiripu atuvum piṟitum’ām. 
tirintatu - yāṉ ‘eṉ’ eṉat  tirintami;
tirintataṉ tiripu - ‘eṉ’ eṉpatu ‘eṉa’ eṉat tirital;
tirintataṉtiripu atuvum piṟitum eṉal - eṉ+a=‘eṉa’ eṉṟāki,  ataṉōṭu atuurupu cērumpōtu, muṉcērnta akaraccāriyi keṭātu atu urupiṉ akaram keṭum eṉal.	(tiripu atu; akarappēṟu; piṟitu; ‘atu’viṉ akarakkēṭu. ivviraṇṭum ippuṇarppil uṇmi kāṇka.	(to. e. 161,114,115 nac.)(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu ‘tirintataṉ tiripu piṟitu’ eṉṟa nili  -
nilimoḻiīṟu piṟitōr īṟākavē tirintu puṇarum eṉṟal.
e-ṭu :maram+kōṭu = marakkōṭu
makaramākiya īṟukeṭṭu mara eṉṟē viti akara īṟāki niṉṟu, akara īṟṟukkuriya ceyki peṟṟu marakkōṭu eṉa varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarkiṟatu. 	(to. e. 310 nac)
e-ṭu :poṉ+kuṭam = poṟkuṭam
ṉakarīṟu ṟakara īṟākat tirintē varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum eṉṟal tirintatākiya ṉakarattiṉ tiripākiya ṟakaram niṉṟē vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇartal (to. e. 332).	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu nili  -
eḻuttukkaḷ moḻimutaṟkaṇ niṟkum niliyum, īṟṟiṉkaṇ niṟkum niliyum.
moḻimutaṟkaṇ niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum, uyiroṭu kūṭiya kctp ñnm yv - eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum, moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramum ākiya irupattiraṇṭām.
moḻiīṟṟil niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ paṉṉīruyirum, ñṇnmṉ - eṉṟa melliṉamey aintum, y r l v ḻ ḷ - eṉṟa iṭi yiṉamey āṟum,īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟum ākiya irupattu nāṉkām.	(to. e. 59-76 nac.) (to.e.1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu‘niliyātu’eṉṟa nili -
nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāka iṇittu eḻutappaṭiṉum, nilimoḻi īṟu varumoḻi mutal ivaṟṟiṟkuriya puṇarcci peṟṟum, poruḷ poruttamuṟa amiyāta taḻāat toṭarkaḷiṉ nili.
‘teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi’	(cilap. 3: 1)
‘teyvam muṉi’ eṉṟē nilimoḻi varumoḻi ākaṟ pālaṉa; iṭiyē ‘mālvarit tiru’ eṉpaṉa ceyyu ḷōci nalam karuti vantaṉa.
teyvam mālvari-eṉpaṉa poruḷiyipu ilavēṉum, nilimoḻi varumoḻi pōlappuṇarntu ‘teyvamāl vari’ eṉṟātal taḻāat toṭarāy, ‘niliyātu’eṉṟa nili peṟṟavāṟu. 
niliyātu eṉṟal - nilimoḻi varumoḻikkaṇ poruḷ toṭarpu niliyātu eṉṟal.	(to. e. 111 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu ‘niliyiṟṟu’eṉṟa nili -
nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum poruḷ poruttamuṟap puṇarum taḻuvutoṭarp puṇarcci vitikaḷ ‘niliyiṟṟu eṉṟal’ eṉṟa  niliyiṉa. 
iṅṅaṉam poruḷ poruttamuṟa nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷ amitalālē, nilimoḻiyi ‘niṟuttacol’eṉṟum, varu moḻiyik ‘kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi’eṉṟum peyariṭṭār tolkāppiyaṉār. 	(to. e. 107 nac.) (to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu ‘niliyiṟṟum niliyātum’ eṉṟa nili -
ōriṭattil peṟṟa puṇarccinili atu pōṉṟa piṟitōriṭat til nilipeṟātu eṉṟal.
kuṟṟeḻutti aṭutta ākāra īṟṟuc col varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarkiyil ‘nilāakkatir, eṉṟāṟpōla akaramākiya eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟum (to. e. 226 nac.) eṉṟu kūṟi, iravuppoḻutiṉi uṇarttum irā eṉṟa col vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ irāviṭattuk kākki eṉṟa poruḷil ‘irāk kākki’ eṉa, akara eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭi peṟātu (to. e. 227 nac.) eṉṟal ‘niliyiṟṟum niliyātum’eṉṟa niliyām.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappamiti -
eḻuttukkaḷ karukkoḷḷa oli ūṉṟum iṭamākiya uṟup pukkaḷ taliyum miṭaṟum neñcum ām. īṇṭu, tali karuttu miyamāka uṇarvu aṭippaṭi yākum;miṭaṟu, uṇarvu miyamākak kuralvaḷi narampukaḷi iyakkum; neñcu vaḷiyiciyip pūrittuc celuttum.
ōciyi oliyeḻuttukkaḷāka veḷippaṭuttum uṟuppukkaḷ miṭaṟum mūkkum. īṇṭu,mūkkāvatu uḷmūkku.
iṉi eḻuttukkaḷi varivaṭivamikkum uṟuppukkaḷ nili yuṟuppum iyaṅkuṟuppumāka iruvakippaṭum. aṇṇamum pallum niliyuṟuppām; nāvum itaḻum iyaṅkuṟuppām.		(to. e. pak-ōṭu ca.pāla.)
eḻuttukkaḷatu mayakkam -
iṉṉa eḻuttukku iṉṉeḻuttu naṭpeḻuttu, iṉṉa eḻuttup pakiyeḻuttu eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭu, oru moḻi toṭarmoḻi eṉṟa īriṭattum , kctp - eṉṟa nāṉkum tammoṭu tāmē  iṇintu varum;rḻ eṉṟa iraṇṭum tammoṭu piṟavē  iṇintu varum; ēṉiya mey paṉṉiraṇṭum tammoṭu tāmum tammoṭu piṟavum iṇintu varum.
iṉṉa iṉṉa meykaḷōṭu iṉṉa iṉṉa meykaḷtām iṇintu varum eṉṟa ceytikaḷi uṭkoṇṭa pakuti eḻuttukkaḷatu mayakkam paṟṟik kūṟuvatām. mayakkam- cērkki. (to. e. 23 - 30 nac.)	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷatu moḻiyākkam  -
moḻiyākkamāvatu eḻuttiṉāṉ colli ākkik koḷḷutalām.  aḵtāvatu uyireḻuttō meyyōṭu uyir kūṭuvataṉālākiya uyirmey eḻuttō, taṉittō iraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷ   iṇintō, iraṇṭaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarntō,  tammi uṇarttuvataṉoṭu nillāmal piṟaporuḷiyum cuṭṭum niliyil amivatām.
e-ṭu 	ā,kā - ōreḻuttorumoḻi; āl - īreḻut torumoḻi; nilavu - mūveḻuttorumoḻi; uttaraṭṭātiyāṉ – oṉpateḻut toru moḻi;
oṉpataṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭa taṉi moḻi tamiḻil illi. (to. e. 45 nac.) (to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṉam  -
eḻuttukkaḷi uyir-mey- cārpu - eṉa mūviṉam ākkalum, avaṟṟuḷḷum uyirik kuṟil - neṭil - eṉa īriṉam ākkalum, meyyi valliṉam - melliṉam - iṭiyiṉam - eṉa mūviṉam ākkalum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṉamām. 	(to.e. 2, 3, 4, 8, 9, 19, 20, 21. nac.)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṉamum muṟiyum -
a ā-kkaḷ piṟappāṉum ceykiyāṉum, aṅku āṅku eṉṉum poruḷāṉum vaṭivāṉum; i īkkaḷ piṟappāṉum ceykiyāṉum, iṅku īṅku eṉṉum poruḷāṉum; u ūkkaḷ piṟappāṉum ceykiyāṉum,uṅku ūṅku eṉṉum poruḷāṉum vaṭivāṉum; e ēkkaḷ piṟappāṉum ceykiyāṉum, evaṉ ēvaṉ eṉṉum poruḷāṉum vaṭivāṉum; o ō-kkaḷ piṟappāṉum ceykiyāṉum vaṭivāṉum ottu ōriṉamāyiṉa.
iṉṉum ivi aḷapeṭuppuḻi neṭṭeḻuttoṭu kuṟ ṟeḻuttiṟku ōci iciyumāṟṟāṉum ōriṉamām eṉ ṟuṇarappaṭum. kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷi muṉṉākak kūṟi avaṟṟiṟku iṉam otta neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉākak kūṟiṉār, orumāttiri kūṟiyē iraṇṭu māttiri kūṟa vēṇṭutaliṉ. aṉṟi iraṇṭi muṉ kūṟiṉ ākātu; oṉṟu niṉṟu ataṉoṭu piṉṉarum oṉṟu kūṭiyē iraṇ ṭāvataṉṟi, iraṇṭu eṉpatu oṉṟu iṉṟu ātaliṉ. itaṉāṉ oṉṟutāṉ palakūṭi eṇ virintatu eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum.
kaṅa-kkaḷum, caña-kkaḷum, ṭaṇakkaḷum, tana-kkaḷum, pama-kkaḷum muyaṟciyāṉum māttiriyāṉum ceykiyāṉum; yara-kkaḷ iṭattāṉum māttiriyāṉum ceykiyāṉum, lava-kkaḷ iṭattāṉum māttiriyāṉum, ‘kalvalitu’ ‘colvalitu’ - eṉṟāṟ pōlat tammil cērntu varum coṟkaḷ palavāta lāṉum; ḻaḷa-kkaḷ iṭattāṉum māttiriyāṉum ‘iṭi yeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa’ eṉṟāl cantaviṉpattiṟku iyipu ātalāṉum; ṟaṉa-kkaḷ muyaṟciyāṉum māttiri yāṉum ceykiyāṉum ottu ōriṉamāyiṉa.
iṉi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi varumāṟu:
‘akara mutala eḻuttellām āti, pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē ulaku’ eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar uvami kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, kaṇṇaṉ ‘eḻuttukkaḷuḷ akaramāka niṟkiṉṟēṉ yāṉ’ eṉṟu uṇmi kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, iṟivaṉ ellāp poruḷiṉ kaṇṇum kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyāyē niṟkumāṟu ellārkkum oppa muṭintāṟpōla akaramum ellā eḻuttiṉkaṇṇum kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyāyē niṟkum eṉpatu cāṉṟōrkkellām oppa muṭintatu ākaliṉ, acciṟappu nōkki ellā eḻuttiṟkum muṉṉar akaram vikkappaṭṭatu. akarattiṟku iṉamākaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ākāram vikkappaṭṭatu. piṟappum ceykiyum cuṭṭup poruṭṭātalum akarattōṭu aḷavum ottaliṉ ataṉ piṉṉar ikaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉ piṉṉar īkāramum vikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṭamum ceykiyum cuṭṭup poruṭṭātalum ikarattōṭu aḷavum ottaliṉ avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar ukaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉ piṉṉar ūkāramum vikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṭamum ceykiyum ukarat tōṭu aḷavum ottaliṉ avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar ekaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ēkāramum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.  piṟappum ceykiyum ēkārattōṭu aḷavum ottaliṉ, taṉakku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttu iṉṟēṉum, ataṉ piṉṉar aikāram vikkappaṭṭatu. iṭamum ceykiyum ottaliṉ ataṉ piṉṉar okaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ōkāramum vikkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟappum ceykiyum vaṭivum ōkārattōṭu aḷavum ottaliṉ, taṉakku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttu iṉṟēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar ōḷakāram vikkappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu uyireḻuttukkaḷ tammuḷ iyiya muṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṉi meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi vippu varumāṟu.
mutalnāvum mutalaṇṇamum uṟap piṟattalāṉ meykaḷil muṉṉark kakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ṅakaramum, iṭināvum iṭiyaṇṇamum uṟap piṟattalāṉum aḷavāṉum kakarattōṭu iṭam ottalāṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarc cakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ñakaramum, nuṉināvum nuṉiyaṇṇamum uṟap piṟattalāṉum aḷavāṉum cakarattōṭu iṭam ottalāṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar ṭakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ṇakaramum, nuṉināvum aṇpalmutalum uṟap piṟattalāṉum aḷavāṉum ṭakarattōṭu iṉam ottalāṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉart takaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar nakaramum, itaḻiyiyap piṟattalāṉum aḷavāṉum takarat tōṭu iṉam ottalāṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarp pakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar makaramum tammuḷ iyiya vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
valleḻuttukkaḷi muṉṉākak kūṟi, avaṟṟiṟkuiṉam otta melleḻuttukkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉākak kūṟi, iṭi yeḻuttu āṟaṉiyum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉākak kūṟiṉār. valleḻuttuḷ nāṉkum, melleḻuttuḷ mūṉṟum, iṭiyeṭuttuḷ iraṇṭum moḻikku mutalātal nōkki immuṟivippu amintatu. uyirkaḷ pōla miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷi kaṇṇuṟṟu aṭiyap piṟattalāṉ, iṭi yeḻuttukkaḷuḷ muṉṉar yakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar rakaramum, iṭamum aḷavum ottalāṉum cantaviṉpattiṟku iyipuṭimiyāṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar lakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉ piṉṉar vakaramum, iṭamum aḷavum vakarattoṭu muyaṟciyum ottalāṉum cantaviṉpattiṟku iyipuṭimiyāṉum ataṉpiṉṉar ḻakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉpiṉṉar ḷakaramum, aḷavāṉum ḻakaram pōlat tamiḻeḻuttu ākalāṉum avaṟṟiṉpiṉṉar ṟakaramum, ataṟku iṉamātaliṉ ataṉ piṉṉar ṉakaramum tammuḷ iyiya vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ṟakaraṉakaraṅkaḷi valliṉa melliṉaṅkaḷic cāraviyātu iṟutikkaṇ vittami, avi tamiḻeḻuttu eṉpatu aṟivittaṟkum, ṉakaram vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriya āṇmakaṉi uṇarttum ciṟappuk karutiyum eṉpatu.	(i.vi.eḻut. 8 uri)
(cū.vi. kūṟum ‘eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟivippu (2)’ - kāṇka.)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ pirivu -
uyirmey puṇarccikkaṇ mey eṉavum uyir eṉavum pirintu vēṟu niṟṟal. uyirmeyyi moḻimutaṟkaṇ mey mutal eṉavum, moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ uyirīṟu eṉavum, iṭikkaṇ variṉum uyir eṉavum pirittukkoṇṭu puṇarcci viti kūṟappaṭutalik kāṇkiṟōm.
‘nilavu’ eṉpataṉi n+i+l+ a+v + u - eṉṟu pirittuk kōṭaṟkaṇ, uyirmeymutal meymutalākavum, uyirmey yīṟu uyirīṟākavum amitalik kāṇkiṟōm.
varaku eṉpataṉkaṇ, va+r+a+ku- eṉa iṭiyēyuḷḷa uyir meyyip pirippiṉ, ku eṉṟa īṟṟeḻuttukku ayalatāka varuvatu ra eṉṟa uyirmeyyiluḷḷa akara uyirātaliṉ, varaku eṉpataṉi uyirttoṭar moḻiyākiya kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc col eṉkiṟōm. 	(to. e. 103, 106 nac.) (to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamum karaṇamum -
eḻutti olikkiyil enta iṭattil toṭutal nikaḻ kiṟatō, anta iṭam ataṉ piṟappiṭamākum. etaṟku etu toṭutalic ceykiṟatō, ataṟku atu karuviyākum.
toṭutal-upasamhāram-sama°parcnāti samclēṣam; toṭum karuvi - karaṇam.
akarākāraṅkaḷukkup piṟappiṭam kaṇṭṭam(miṭaṟu). akara vuyirkku itaḻiyum kavuḷiyum mikak kuvittal ceyyāmi yum, ākāravuyirkku itaḻiyum kavuḷiyum mika virittal ceyyāmiyum vēṇṭum;iviyē akara ākāraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattukkuk karuvi. 	(e. ā. pak. 77) 
neñcu miṭaṟu tali-ivaṟṟi āciriyar kūṟiyataṟkuk kāraṇam avvaḷi koppūḻiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu vāyi aṭitalēyām. vāykkuḷ kāṟṟu van taṭinta piṉṉarē, aḵtu uyirākavō valliṉa melliṉa mākavō iṭiyiṉamākavō ākum. ciṟitu mūṭiyum ciṟitu tiṟantum irukka eḻuttākumāyiṉ aḵtu iṭiyeḻut tākavēṉum ṟakaramākavēṉum ākum. vāy muḻutum mūṭappaṭ ṭirukka avvaḷi eḻuttākumāyiṉ ṟakaram oḻinta valliṉa melliṉa eḻuttām. valliṉam olikkum pōtu toṇṭiyi luḷḷa iraṇṭu narampukaḷ iṭam viṭṭu niṟkum; mel liṉaṅkaḷ olikkumpōtu avi neruṅki niṟkum.(e. ku. pak. 87)
‘valliṉam uram, āytam ciram, uyir iṭi kaṇṭam, meli mūkku’ eṉṟa vīracōḻiya uriyum naṉṉūlum poruntā. unti mutalākat tōṉṟiya vaḷi evveḻuttāka māṟiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉiṉṟu vāykkuḷ varum vari taṉniliyil tiriyātu, vāykkaṇ avviṭattil avvaḷi meyyāka māṟumpōtu taṭipaṭum;uyirāka māṟumpōtu avvaḷi taṭipaṭāmal vāyviṭṭu veḷivariṉum aḵtu avvuyirāka māṟum iṭam vāytāṉ.
paṉṉīruyirum tattam iṭaṅkaḷil tiriyā(-tirintu) olikkum. tiritalukkuk kāraṇam karuvi. eḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappiṭam neñcu - miṭaṟu - tali - eṉavum, karuvi,mūkku - aṇṇam - nā - pal - itaḻ - eṉavum muṟiyē koḷka. 	(e. ku. pak. 87-89)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu  -
untiyil tōṉṟum kāṟṟu, mārpu - kaḻuttu- mūkku - tali- eṉṉum tāṉaṅkaḷil uṟṟu, pal - itaḻ - nā- aṇṇam- eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ muyaṟci vēṟupāṭṭāl vevvēṟu eḻuttoliyāy veḷippaṭutal eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappām.	(naṉ. 74)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappup paṟṟiya vīracōḻiyak kuṟippu -
untiyiṉiṉṟu eḻukiṉṟa vāyu, uram(-mārpu)-ciram - kaṇṭam- mūkku- ivaṟṟiṉ iṭamākap poruntip puṟappaṭumpōtu aṇṇam-pal-itaḻ- nā-eṉṉum uṟuppukkaḷiṉ muyaṟci yāl vevvēṟu eḻuttoliyāyp piṟakkum. 	(vī. cō. cantip. 6)
ivaṟṟuḷ uratti valliṉamum, ciratti āytamum, kaṇṭatti uyirum iṭiyiṉamum, mūkki melliṉamum iṭamākap poruntum eṉpa.	(6. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar  -
mutal, cārpu; uyir, mey (mutal);kuṟil, neṭil (uyir); valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam (mey); uyirmey, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiya lukaram, aikārakkuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarakkuṟukkam, āytakkuṟukkam (cārpu) - eṉa ivi eḻuttiṉ peyar kaḷām.	(naṉ. 59, 60, 63, 68-70)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar kāraṇakkuṟiyātal  -
uyirkaḷ pōla meykaḷi iyakkutalāṉ uyir eṉṟum, tamakku iṉamāki īraḷapu icikkum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi nōkkat tām kuṟiyavāy ōraḷapu icittalāṉ kuṟil eṉṟum, tamakku iṉamāki ōraḷapu icikkum kuṟṟeḻut tukkaḷi nōkkat tām neṭiyavāy īraḷapu icittalāṉ neṭil eṉṟum, cuṭṭupporuḷi uṇarttutalāṉ cuṭṭu eṉṟum, viṉāp poruḷi uṇarttutalāṉ viṉā eṉṟum,
mey pōla uyirāṉ iyaṅkutalāṉ mey eṉṟum, melleḻuttiyum iṭiyeḻuttiyum nōkkattām valleṉṟu icittalāṉum valleṉṟa talivaḷiyāṉ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttu eṉṟum, valleḻuttiyum iṭiyeḻuttiyum nōkkat tām mel leṉṟu icitta lāṉum, melleṉṟa mūkkuvaḷiyāṉ piṟattalāṉum mel leḻuttu eṉṟum, melleḻuttiyum valleḻuttiyum nōkkat tām iṭinikaravāy olittalāṉum, iṭi nikart tāya miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṉ piṟattalāṉum iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṟum, tammāl iyalum cārpeḻuttiṟkuk kāraṇamāki mutal niṟṟalāṉ mutaleḻuttu eṉṟum, aviyē tammoṭu tām cārntum iṭam cārntum paṟṟukkōṭu cārntum tōṉṟalāṉ cārpeḻuttu eṉṟum, ōraḷapu icikkum ikarukaram kuṟuki ariyaḷapu icittalāṉ kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟum, aṭuppukkūṭṭup pōla āytavaṭi vāka eḻutappaṭutaliṉ āytam eṉṟum, uyirum meyyum kūṭi olittalāṉ uyirmey eṉṟum, uyir aḷapeṭutta lāṉ uyiraḷapeṭi eṉṟum, oṟṟu aḷapeṭuttalāṉ oṟṟaḷapeṭi eṉṟum, īraḷapu icikkum aikāram oḷakāram kuṟuki ōraḷapu icittalāṉ aikārak kuṟukkam oḷakārak kuṟukkam eṉṟum, ariyaḷapu icikkum makaram kuṟukik kālaḷapu icittalāṉ makarakkuṟukkam eṉṟum, a ā eṉpaṉa a ā eṉṟu icittalāṉ aā eṉṟum kāraṇak kuṟi āyiṉa.	(i. vi. eḻut. 7 uri)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ potuppiṟappiṭam -
paṉṉīruyirum iṭiyeḻuttu āṟum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, valleḻuttu āṟum ucciyiṉkaṇṇum, melleḻuttu āṟum mūkkiṉkaṇṇum nilipeṟṟicikkum ōciyāṉ piṟakkum.	(i. vi. 10)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutalum īṟum -
taṉieḻuttu ovvoṉṟaṟkum atuvē mutalum īṟum ākum. uyirmey eḻuttiṟku olitta muṟiyē mey mutalum, uyir īṟum ākum. puṇarccikkaṇ, uyirmey mutali meymutal eṉavum, uyirmey īṟṟi uyirīṟu eṉavum kūṟutal kāṇka. appoṭu peyta uppē pōla, uyiroṭu puṇarttiya mey taṉ māttiri tōṉṟātu oṉṟāy niṟṟaliṉ uyirmeyyi ōreḻuttu eṉṟum, olittu niṉṟa neṟiyāṉ īreḻuttu eṉṟum koḷpa. ‘uyirmey’ eṉpatu oli vakiyāṉ ummittoki, māttiri vakiyāṉ ummittoki aṉmoḻi.	(naṉ. 109 caṅ.)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟivippu  (1) -
“eḻuttukkaṭku ellām akaram mutalātaṟkuk kāraṇam ‘meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum”  (to. e. 46) eṉpataṉāṉ kūṟupa. vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriya āṇmakaṉi uṇart tum ciṟappāṉ ṉakaram piṉ vittār. iṉi eḻuttukkaṭkuk kiṭakki muṟi āyiṉavāṟu kūṟutum:
“kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷi muṉṉākak kūṟi avaṟṟiṟku iṉamotta neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉākak kūṟiṉār, oru māttiri kūṟiyē iraṇṭu māttiri kūṟa vēṇṭutaliṉ. aṉṟi, iraṇṭi muṟkūṟiṉālō eṉiṉ, ākātu; oṉṟu niṉṟu ataṉoṭu piṉṉarum oṉṟu kūṭiyē iraṇṭāvataṉṟi iraṇṭu eṉpatoṉṟu iṉṟātaliṉ. itaṉāṉ oṉṟutāṉ palakūṭiyē eṇ virintateṉṟu uṇarka.
“iṉi, akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaram eṇṇum piṟappum poruḷum ottaliṉ vittār. ikarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaram vittār, piṟappu ovvātēṉum ‘a i u am mūṉṟum cuṭṭu’ (to. e. 31) eṉac  cuṭṭupporuṭṭāy niṟkiṉṟa iṉam karuti. avi aimpāṟkaṇṇum perumpāṉmi varumāṟu uṇarka. ekaram ataṉpiṉ vittār, akara ikaraṅkaḷoṭu piṟappu oppumi paṟṟi. aikāra oḷakāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya kuṟ ṟeḻuttu ilavēṉum piṟappu oppumi paṟṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅ kaḷiṉ piṉṉar aikāra oḷakāram vittār. okaram no eṉa meyyoṭu kūṭi niṉṟallatu tāṉāka ōreḻuttorumoḻi ākāta ciṟappiṉmi nōkki aikārattiṉpiṉ vittār. a i u e- eṉṉum nāṉkum akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ ekkoṟṟaṉ - eṉa meyyoṭu kūṭāmal tām iṭiccollāy niṉṟāyiṉum mēl varum peyarkaḷoṭu kūṭic cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttum. okaram ‘no’ eṉa meyyoṭu kūṭiyē taṉ poruḷuṇarttu vatallatu tāṉākap poruḷ uṇarttātu eṉṟuṇarka.  iṉṉum a ā, u ū,  e ē, o ō, - eṉpaṉa tammuḷ vaṭivu okkum. i ī ai - tammuḷ vaṭivu ovvā. iṉṉum ivi aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl, neṭṭeḻuttoṭu kuṟṟeḻuttiṟku ōci iyiyumāṟṟāṉum uṇarka. iṉic cuṭṭu nīṇṭu ākāra īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷ ātalāṉum poruḷ okkum. puṇarcci oppumi uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷ peṟutum. immuṟi vaḻuvāmal mēl āḷumāṟu uṇarka.
“iṉi, kakāra ṅakāramum, cakāra ñakāramum, ṭakāra ṇakāramum, takāra nakāramum, pakāra makāramum tamakkup piṟappum ceykiyum ottaliṉ, valloṟṟiṭiyē melloṟṟuk kalantuvittār. mutal nāvum mutalaṇṇamum, iṭināvum iṭiyaṇṇamum, nuṉināvum nuṉiyaṇṇamum nuṉināvum aṇpal mutalum uṟutalum, itaḻiyitalum ākip piṟakkiṉṟa iṭattiṉ muṟimi nōkki avveḻuttuk kaḷik  ka ca ṭa ta pa ṅa ña ṇa na ma - eṉa immuṟiyē vittār. piṟappu oppumiyāṉum ṉakāram ṟakāramāyt tiritalāṉum ṟakāramum ṉakāramum cēra vittār. ivi tamiḻeḻuttu eṉpatu aṟivittaṟkup piṉṉar vittār. iṉi iṭi yeḻuttukkaḷil yakāram muṉ vittār, atuvum uyirkaḷ pōla miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷi aṇṇam kaṇṇuṟṟaṭiyap piṟattaliṉ. rakāram ataṉoṭu piṟappu ovvātēṉum, ceyki ottaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. lakāramum vakāramum tammil piṟappum ceykiyum ovvāvēṉum, kalvalitu colvalitu - eṉṟāṟ pōlat tammil cērntu varum coṟkaḷ perumpāṉmi eṉpatu paṟṟi lakāramum vakāramum cēra vittār. ḻakāramum ḷakāramum oṉṟāṉum iyipila vēṉum ‘iṭiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa’ (to. e. 21) eṉṟāl cantaviṉpattiṟku iyipuṭimi karutic cēra vittārpōlum.”	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṉamum muṟiyum eṉṟa talippil, ilakkaṇaviḷakka nūlār cuṭṭum muṟivippiṉik kaṇṭu koḷka. 	(i. vi. eḻut. 8 uri)
(naṉṉūl viruttiyuri cū.vi. uriyē.)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟivippu (2) -
ciṟappu, iṉam - eṉṟa iraṇṭu kāraṇattāṉum oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka eḻuttukkaḷ akara mutalāka ṉakaram īṟāka vaḻaṅki varutalē eḻuttukkaḷ niṟkum muṟiyām. taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭiya uyireḻuttukkaḷ avvāṟu iyaṅkum āṟṟal illāta meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku muṉ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷḷum, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ avaṟṟatu vikāramākiya neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷukku muṉniṟkiṉṟaṉa. mey yeḻuttukkaḷuḷ, valiyār meliyavarkaḷukku muṉ niṟpatu pōla, valleḻuttukkaḷ melleḻuttukkaḷukku muṉ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivi niṟkumuṟi ciṟappu eṉum kāraṇam paṟṟiyatu. kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷi aṭuttu avaṟṟiṉ iṉam otta neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ muṟiyē niṟpatum, valleḻuttukkaḷi aṭuttu avaṟṟiṉ iṉam otta melleḻuttukkaḷ muṟiyē niṟpatum iṉam eṉṉum kāraṇam paṟṟiyaṉa.	(naṉ. 73)
“uyirkaḷuḷ a i u - eṉpaṉa muṟiyē aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉum, avvaṅkāppōṭu aṇpallaṭi nāviḷim puṟak kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉum, avvaṅkāppōṭu itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉum piṟattalāṉ, appiṟappiṭattu muṟiyē muṟiyāka vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ākāra īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷ akaram mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṉam ātaliṉ, avaṟṟic cāra vikkappaṭṭaṉa.  neṭṭeḻuttāvatu nīrum nīrum cērntāṟ pōlak kuṟṟeḻuttu iraṇṭu ottu niṉṟu nīṇṭicippa toṉṟu ātaliṉ, aḵtu uṇarntu kōṭaṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ piṉṉar neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṉi ekaramāvatu akarak kūṟum ikarakkūṟum tammuḷ otticittu naramaṭaṅkal pōl niṟpatoṉṟu ākalāṉum, okaramāvatu akarakkūṟum ukarak kūṟum tammuḷ otticittu avvāṟu niṟpatoṉṟu ākalāṉum, avi avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar muṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ iṉam ākaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ piṉ muṟiyē vikkap paṭṭaṉa. akaramum yakaramum ikaramum tammuḷ otticittu niṟpatoṉṟu ātaliṉ ekara ēkāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar aikāramum, akaramum vakaramum ukaramum tammuḷ otticittu niṟpatoṉṟu ākaliṉ okara ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar oḷakāramum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.  ivvāṟātal paṟṟi ē ō ai oḷa - eṉṉum nāṉkiṉiyum vaṭanūlār cantiyakkaram eṉpa. kiyaṭaṉār naramaṭaṅkal pōl eṉṟu uvamiyum kūṟiṉār. ikkaruttē paṟṟi āciriyar ‘akara ikaram aikāram ākum’ eṉak kūṟi, ai eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivu pulappaṭutaṟku, akara ikaraṅkaḷē aṉṟi avaṟṟiṭiyē yakaramum ottu icikkum eṉpār, ‘akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum, aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi mey peṟat tōṉṟum’ eṉṟum, ‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟa ilēcāṉē, oḷa eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivu pulappaṭutaṟku akara ukaraṅkaḷē aṉṟi avaṟṟiṭiyē vakaramum otticikkum eṉṟum......‘ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
“iṉi, kaṅa - kkaḷum caña-kkaḷum ṭaṇa-kkaḷum tana-kkaḷum pama-kkaḷum aṭinā aṇṇam - iṭinā aṇṇam - nuṉinā aṇṇam - aṇpal aṭi - itaḻ - eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ muyaṟci yāl piṟattalāṉ, appiṟappiṭattiṉ muṟiyē muṟiyāka vikkappaṭṭaṉa. valiyāri muṉ vittu meliyārip piṉ vittal marapākaliṉ, valleḻuttukkaḷ muṉṉum avvavaṟ ṟiṟku iṉam otta melleḻuttukkaḷ avvavaṟṟiṉ piṉṉu māka vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avviraṇṭum nōkkiyallatu iṭi nikaraṉavāy olittal aṟiyappaṭāmiyiṉ atu paṟṟi iṭi yeḻuttukkaḷ avviru kūṟṟiṟkum piṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
“ḻakara ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ mūṉṟum tamiḻeḻuttu eṉpatu aṟivittataṟku iṟutikkaṇ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum ḻakaram iṭiyeḻuttākaliṉ, atupaṟṟi iṭiyeḻuttoṭu cārtti avaṟṟiṟutikkaṇ vikkappaṭṭatu. vaṭamoḻiyil lakāram ḷakāramākavum uccarikkap paṭuvataṉṟi taṉiyē ōreḻuttu aṉmiyiṉ, acciṟappiṉmi paṟṟi iṭi yeḻuttākiya ḷakāram ḻakārattiṟkum piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. yaralava-kkaḷ nāṉkum muṟiyē aṭiyaṇṇamum iṭi yaṇṇamum aṇpalmutalum itaḻum eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ muyaṟciyāl piṟattalāṉ, appiṟappiṭattu muṟiyē muṟi yāka vikkappaṭṭaṉa.”	(cū. vi. pak. 22-25)
uyireḻuttukkaḷ taṉittu iyaṅkum iyalpiṉa ātaliṉ, avi avviyalpu illāta meyyeḻuttukkaṭku muṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ akaram, vēṟu muyaṟciyiṉṟi vāy aṅkāntu kūṟap piṟattaliṉ acciṟappu nōkki mutaṟkaṇ vikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉiyē piṉṉum oru māttiriyaḷavu nīṭṭi olikkap piṟattaliṉ ākāram ataṉ piṉ vikkap paṭṭatu. mēl, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷukkup piṉṉar neṭṭeḻut tukkaḷi niṟuttiyataṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. ikaram, mēṟpalliṉ aṇiya iṭattil nāviḷimpu uṟap piṟattaliṉ piṟappiṭam nōkki ataṉpiṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. ukaram, itaḻ kuvittu olikkap piṟattaliṉ piṟappiṭam nōkki ikarattiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u eṉṉummūṉṟumē ciṟappuṭiyaṉa; ataṉāṉē avi poruḷkaḷic cuṭṭi uṇartta varalāyiṉa.ekaram ikaram piṟakkumiṭattē piṟappatu ākalāṉum, ikara oliyiṉatu tiripu ākalāṉum acciṟappiṉmi nōkki ukarattiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. aikāram akaramum ikaramum kūṭip piṟappatu ātaliṉ, aḵtu ēkārattiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. okaram ukaram piṟakkumiṭattē piṟappatu ākalāṉum, ukara oliyiṉatu tiripu ākalāṉum, akara ikaram kūṭippiṟakkum aikārattiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. oḷakāram akaramum ukaramum kūṭip piṟappatu ātaliṉ, ōkārattiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. ākavē piṟappiṭattu muṟiyum, ciṟappum ciṟappiṉmiyum nōkkiyē uyireḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉi meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi varumāṟu:-
kakāra ṅakāraṅkaḷ aṭinā aṭiyaṇṇatti uṟap piṟattaliṉ mutalil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. cakāra ñakāraṅkaḷ iṭinā iṭi yaṇṇatti uṟap piṟattaliṉ  avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vikkap paṭṭaṉa. ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷ nuṉinā nuṉiyaṇṇatti  uṟap piṟattaliṉ avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ṟakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷ nuṉinākku mēlvaḷintu ceṉṟu aṇṇatti oṟṟap piṟattaliṉ, ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷi aṭuttu vikkap paṭal vēṇṭum; ivviraṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil illāmiyiṉ īṟṟil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. takāra nakāraṅkaḷ mēṟpalliṉ aṭiyil nānuṉi parantu oṟṟappiṟat taliṉ (ṟaṉa-kkaḷiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭavēṇṭuvaṉavākavum, 
ṟaṉa-kkaḷ iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭataṉāl), ṭaṇa-kkaḷiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭṭaṉa. pakāra makāraṅkaḷ itaḻkaḷ cērap piṟattaliṉ, tana-kkaḷiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟāl aṭiyaṇṇam mutal itaḻvariyilu muḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil piṟattal kāraṇamāka, kaṅa - caña - ṭaṇa - ṟaṉa - tana - pama - kkaḷ muṟiyāy amivaṉavātal kāṇka. kaṅa-kkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭiraṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷ ōrōr iṭattil piṟappaṉa vāyiṉum, valleḻuttukkaḷ mārpiṉ vaḷiyāl piṟantu valiyavāy olittalāṉum, melleḻuttukkaḷ mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāl piṟantu meliyavāy olittalāṉum, ac ciṟappu nōkki valleḻuttukkaḷ muṉṉum, ciṟappiṉmi nōkki melleḻuttukkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉum vikkap paṭṭaṉa. iṉi iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi varumāṟu:
miṭaṟṟu eḻunta vaḷi aṇṇam cērntu piṟattaliṉ, yakaram mutalil vikkappaṭṭatu. nuṉinākku mēṟceṉṟu aṇṇatti varuṭutalāṉ, rakaramum ḻakaramum piṟappaṉa eṉiṉum, avaṟ ṟuḷḷum iṭam nōkki ḻakaram muṉṉum rakaram ataṉpiṉ ṉum vikkappaṭal vēṇṭum. āyiṉum ḻakaram vaṭamoḻi yil illāmiyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa iṭiyeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ piṉ - aḵtāvatu yaralava-kkaḷukkup piṉ - vikkap paṭṭatu. rakāram piṟappiṭam nōkki (ḻakārattiṉ piṉṉar) vikkappaṭa vēṇṭuvatāyiṉum ḻakāram vakārattiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭṭataṉāṉ) yakārattiṉpiṉ vikkap paṭṭatu. ḷakāram nāviḷimpu taṭittu aṇṇatti varuṭap piṟattaliṉ, atu rakārattiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭa vēṇṭuva tāyiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyil taṉiyeḻuttāka illātataṉāṉ, ḻakarattiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭṭatu. ḻakāram ḷakāram ākiya iraṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil illiyāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷḷum piṟappiṭam nōkki ḻakāram muṉṉum ḷakāram piṉṉumāka vikkappaṭṭaṉa. nāviḷimpu taṭittu aṇpal laṭiyi oṟṟutalāṉ lakāram piṟakkiṉṟatu ākaliṉ, atu ḻakāra ḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭa vēṇṭuvatāyi ṉum avviraṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷum īṟṟil vikkappaṭṭami yiṉ, atu rakārattiṉ piṉvikkappaṭṭatu. vakaram mēṟpal lum itaḻum iyiyap piṟattaliṉ, atu lakārattiṉ piṉṉar vikkappaṭṭatu. ākavē, iṭiyiṉaṅkaḷ āṟum piṟappiṭam kāraṇamāka, ya ḻa ra ḷa la va - eṉa vikkappaṭa vēṇṭuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ḻakāra ḷakāraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illāmi karuti īṟṟil vikkappaṭṭaṉa ākaliṉ,  ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - eṉṟu amintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl,
a, ā; ka ṅa - ivi aṭiyaṇṇattilum,
i, ī; ca  ña ya	- ivi iṭiyaṇṇattilum (tālattilum),
e, ē, ai; ṭa, ṇa, ḻa, ra - ivi nuṉiyaṇṇattilum,
ṟa, ṉa, (ḷa) - ivi nuṉiyaṇṇatti aṭutta iṭattilum,
ta, na, la - ivi palliṉ aṭiyilum,
u, ū, o, ō, oḷa, pa, ma, va - ivi itaḻilum piṟappaṉavātal teḷivākum.	(e. ā. pak. 7 -10)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaṭivam -
eḻuttukkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkum paḻiya varivaṭi viṉiyuṭiyaṉa. aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkumiṭattut taṉittum meyyūrntum varum ekaramum okaramum ākiya iraṇṭu uyireḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttukkaḷum tammēl puḷḷi peṟum eṉpar naṉṉulār. (piṟkāla ōliccuvaṭikaḷil in nili kāṇalām. puḷḷiyiṭutal māttiriyic  cempāti yākak kuṟippataṟkum payaṉpaṭum.)	(naṉ. 98)
eḻuttukkaḷukku vevvēṟu oli uṇṭāvataṉ kāraṇam -
cuvācappiyiṉiṉṟu veḷippaṭum vaḷi aṭiyaṇṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil paṭukiyil  nāviṉ ceykiyāl vevvēṟu eḻuttoli ākiṉṟatu. vevvēṟuvaki oli uṇṭāvataṉ kāraṇam aintu. avi 1. uḷmuyaṟci, 2, veḷi muyaṟci, 3. kūṭṭam, 4. vaḻi vēṟupāṭu, 5. iṭa vēṟupāṭu - eṉpaṉa.
uḷmuyaṟci: uḷniṉṟu eḻum vaḷiyi veḷiyē viṭumpōtu, nāviṉ nuṉi iṭi aṭiviḷimpu - ākiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉāl vaḷiyi aṭiyaṇṇam iṭiyaṇṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil taṭuttum taṭukkāmalum veḷiviṭutal kūṭum. taṭukkumpōtu ciṟitaḷavu taṭuttalāl iṭiyiṉameykaḷum, muḻutum taṭuttalāl valliṉa melliṉa meykaḷum, taṭukkāmal viṭuvatāl uyireḻuttukkaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
veḷimuyaṟci- vaḷiyi veḷiviṭumpōtu nam kuralvaḷi yiṉ irupakkattum uḷḷa aivvukaḷ pirintu virintum, cērntu curuṅkiyum, nātoliyiyum cuvāca oliyiyum uṇṭākkum. valleḻuttukkaḷ cuvāca kāriyam, maṟṟa ellā eḻuttuk kaḷum nātakāriyam.
kūṭṭam: ōroliyil maṟṟōr oliyiyum oruṅku cērttal. cempum īyamum cērntu veṇkalamāvatu pōlap pirikkamuṭiyātapaṭi oṉṟēyāy, iraṇṭoliyiṉ cērkkiyāl ōroliyēyāy amiyum tamiḻ aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ ippakuppic cārntaṉa eṉpa.
vaḻi vēṟupāṭu: uḷniṉṟu veḷippaṭum vaḷi veḷiyē varum vaḻikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭu. aṇṇam vari varum vaḷiyi ataṟkumēl vāyvaḻiyākavō mūkkuvaḻiyākavō veḷi viṭutal kūṭum. valliṉam nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya eḻuttukkaḷi olikkumpōtu kuralvaḷiyiṉ tuvāram curuṅkac ceytu ciṟitu ciṟitāka veḷivarum vaḷiyi mūkkiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷiviṭṭāl avvaḷi melliṉam ākum. vaṭamoḻiyil uyir, ya va la-kkaḷum mūkkiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷivarutalumuṇṭu. avi anunāsikam eṉap peyar peṟum.
iṭa vēṟupāṭu: eḻuttup piṟappataṟkuriya vāyiṉ uḷ ḷiṭaṅkaḷ ākiya aṭiyaṇṇam, iṭiyaṇṇam, nuṉi yaṇṇam, nuṉiyaṇṇatti aṭutta iṭam (vartsam), pal, palliṉ mēliṭam, itaḻ- eṉṟa iṭavēṟupāṭṭāṉē valleḻuttum melleḻuttum āṟu āṟu āyiṉa. 	(e. ā. pak. 11-13)
eḻuttu kāraṇap peyarātal -
eḻuttu eṉpatu ‘eḻupputaliyuṭiyatu’ eṉṉum poruḷit tarum karuvippeyar. aḵtu īṇṭuc cevippulaṉām oli yeḻuttic cuṭṭik kaṭpulaṉām varivaṭivattiṟkum uriyatāka niṟṟaliṉ, kāraṇap peyarām. cāttaṉ eṉpā ṉoruvaṉatu uṭampu, uyirum uṇarvumākat tikaḻum ac cāttaṉic cuṭṭi uṇarttumāṟu pōla, eḻuttu varivaṭi viṉiyuṟṟu niṉṟu oliyuruvi uṇarttum taṉmittāy ilakkaṇakkuṟiyīṭu āyiṟṟu.	(to.e.pak. 71 ca.pāla.)
eḻuttuc cāriyikaḷ  -
kāram, karam, kāṉ -eṉpaṉa eḻuttuc cāriyikaḷ. kārac cāriyi oṉṟē tamiḻukkum āriya moḻikkum potuvāṉa cāriyi. kāraccāriyi oṉṟē uyir mey ellāvaṟṟukkum varum.
	varumāṟu : ākāram, īkāram, ūkāram........... ōḷakāram, akāram, kakāram.
kāram, karam, kāṉ- eṉṟa mūṉṟum uyirkkuṟilukkum uyirmeyk kuṟilukkum varum.
e-ṭu:	akāram, akaram, aḵkāṉ; makāram, makaram, maḵkāṉ. (kuṟil kāṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl iṭiyē āytam varutal koḷḷappaṭum.)
ai, oḷa- eṉpaṉa kāṉcāriyiyum peṟṟu aikāṉ, oḷakāṉ - eṉa varum.  
āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam - eṉṟa eḻuttuccāriyikaḷum uḷa. uyirmey neṭilukkuc cāriyi illi.		(to. e. 134- 137 nac. uri)
mey patiṉeṭṭum akarattiyum, neṭṭuyir ēḻum kārattiyum, aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ kārattuṭaṉē kāṉiyum, uyirum uyirmeyyumāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttuaintum kāram, kāṉ- ivaṟṟuṭaṉē karam eṉpataṉiyum cāriyiyākac cārntu naṭakkum.	(naṉ. 125 mayili.)
eḻuttuccāriyi piṟa  -
‘piṟa’ eṉṟataṉālē, kuṟiloṭu kāṉcāriyi puṇarumpōtu iṭiyē āytam tōṉṟutalum (aḵkāṉ), a āṉā - e ēṉā - o ōṉā- aiyaṉā - oḷavaṉā - eṉa, āṉā ēṉā ōṉā aṉā - mutaliya cāriyikaḷ peṟṟu varutalum, ‘a i u’ ‘ā ī ū’ eṉac cāriyi peṟātu varutalum koḷka.	(naṉ. 126 irāmā.)
eḻuttup piṟappu  -
paṉṉīruyirkkum āṟuiṭiyiṉattiṟkum miṭaṟē mutaliṭa mākavum, āṟu valliṉattiṟkum neñcē mutaliṭamākavum. āṟu melliṉattiṟkum ucciyē mutaliṭamākavum, aṉṟi utaṭum mūkkum aṇṇamum pallum nāvum eṉa iv vintē tuṇiyiṭamākavum, eḻuttellām piṟakkum eṉṟuṇarka.	(to. vi. 3 uri)
eḻuttuppēṟu aḷapeṭi -
iḵtu uyiraḷapeṭi vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iḵtu iṉ ṉōcikkākavō, ceyyuḷil ici niṟippataṟkākavō amintataṉṟu.  kuṟili aṭutta ākāra īṟṟup peyarkkum taṉi ākāra īṟṟup peyarkkum ummittokikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum akaramum, kuṟili aṭuttum taṉittum varum ūkāra īṟṟup peyarkku vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ ukaramum, ēkāra īṟṟukku ekaramum, ōkāra īṟṟukku okaramum eḻuttup pēṟaḷapeṭi yāka varum.	
u vāap patiṉāṉku - ummit toki	(to. e. 223 nac.)
palāakkōṭu uvāap paṭṭiṉi
arāak kuṭṭi - vēṟṟumittoki	226 nac.
uṭūuk kuṟi - vēṟṟumit toki	267 nac.
ēek koṭṭil - vēṟṟumit toki	277 nac.
kōok kaṭumi - vēṟṟumit toki	292 nac.
ivveḻuttup pēṟaḷapeṭi piṟkālattē vaḻakku iṟantatu. nī ir- eṉpatu (326 nac.) paṇṭu orumoḻikkaṇ vanta eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭi eṉalām. itu kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu ici niṟikkum neṭṭeḻuttu allāvaḻi vanta pulutaccanti (pi.vi. 5)
eḻuttupporuḷpaṭumāṟu -
akaram mutal ṉakaram īṟākiya muppatukkum eḻuttu eṉpatu taṉittaṉiyākavum oru cēravum potuppeyar. “eḻuttu eṉpatu oru poruḷ; ataṟku a ā - mutaliyaṉa peyar” eṉpatu poruntātu. eḻuttu eṉpatu muppataṉukkum potuppeyar eṉavē, kuṟil - neṭil - uyir - vaṉmi - meṉmi - iṭimi - eṉpaṉa ciṟappuppeyarām.	(cū. vi. pak. 19, 20)
eḻuttu eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar, apporuḷi viṭṭup pālpakā aḵṟiṇip poruṭ potuppeyarāy, ap poruḷi viṭṭu ōviyam mutaliyaṉa pōla aṉṟi akaram ṉakaram mutaliya vaṭivi uṇarttum ciṟappuppeyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu oliyi uṇarttum ākupeyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu avvoliyiṉatu ilakkaṇatti uṇarttum irumaṭiyākupeyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu avvilakkaṇatti uṇarttum nūliṉi uṇarttum mummaṭiyākupeyarāy, apporuḷi viṭṭu ‘iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṟṟu eḻuttu, iṅṅaṉam aṟivittatu eḻuttu’ eṉak karumakaruttāviyum karuvikkaruttāviyum uṇarttum nāṉmaṭiyāku peyarāy niṉṟu pala poruḷ paṭṭatu.	(i. ko. pak. 52)


eḻuttup pōli -
akaramum vakaroṟṟum kūṭi oḷakārattiṉ payatta ākalum, akaramum yakaroṟṟum kūṭi aikārattiṉ payatta ākalum eḻuttup pōliyām. 
	e-ṭu : avvi - oḷavi eṉavum, ayyar - aiyar eṉavum varum.	(nēmi. eḻut. 9)
moḻiyiṟutiyil makarattiṟku ṉakaram pōliyāka varum. e-ṭu : mukam- mukaṉ. 
moḻi mutalilum iṭiyilum cakara ñakara yakaraṅkaḷukku muṉ akarattukku aikāram pōliyāka varum.
e-ṭu : pacal - pical, mañcu- miñcu, mayal - miyal;		aracu - aricu. ilañci - iliñci, arayar - ariyar
moḻi iṭikkaṇ cilaviṭattu aikārattiṉ piṉṉarum yakara oṟṟiṉ piṉṉarum nakarattukku ñakaram pōliyāka varum.
e-ṭu:minniṉṟa - miññiṉṟa - ceyyuḷ
	ainnūṟu - aiññūṟu - ulaka vaḻakku
	ceynniṉṟa - ceyññiṉṟa - ceyyuḷ
	cēynalūr - cēyñalūr - ulaka vaḻakku	(naṉ. 122- 124)
eḻuttum patamum  -
eḻuttu, tūḷilē mañcaḷ pukiyili mutaliyaṉa vaṭivu vēṟu paṭuvatu pōla vēṟupaṭāmal, māliyiṉiṭattē malarpōla niṟpataṉāl ‘muṉṉaṉittu’ eṉṟār.	(naṉ. 127 irāmā.)
eḻuttumuppattumūṉṟu eṉal -
tolkāppiyaṉār vaṭamoḻiyil vallunarāy aintiram niṟintavarāyiṉum, tamiḻmarapi yuṭkoṇṭē, “tamiḻ moḻikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ uyir 12, mey 18 ākiya muppatumē” eṉṟār. oru moḻiyic cārntu varum iyalpu aṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkum iyalpu tamakku illi eṉṟaliṉ avitammi eṭuttōtik kāṭṭalākāk kuṟṟiyalikaram - kuṟṟiyalukaram - āytam - eṉpaṉa, taṉittu eḻutappaṭā āyiṉum moḻiyoṭu cārtti eḻutappaṭutaliṉ, eḻuttu eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭukku uriyaṉa ātaliṉ avaṟṟi ‘eḻuttu ōraṉṉa’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭu, ammūṉṟaṉiyum cērkkat tamiḻeḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟu eṉṟu avar terivittatu tamiḻmarapu paṟṟiyē eṉpatu. 	(to. e. 1)
eḻuttu muṟi kāṭṭal  -
eḻuttum collum ceykiṉṟuḻi  muṉṉi nūl pōla eḻuttilakkaṇam colluḷ ceṉṟu mayaṅkāta muṟimi- yāṉē eḻuttilakkaṇam terivittu...(to. pāyi. iḷa. uri)
mūvaki ilakkaṇamum mayaṅkāta muṟimiyāl ceykiṉṟa miyiṉ, eḻuttilakkaṇatti muṉṉark kāṭṭi...	(nac. uri)
iyaṟṟamiḻum icittamiḻum  nāṭakattamiḻum muṉṉūluḷ pōla viravāta taṉmiyāṉē, iyaṟṟamiḻi vēṟupirittu muṟiyāṉē ulakiṟku aṟivittu....	(cū.vi. pak. 3)
oṉṟaṉuḷ piṟitoṉṟu kalavāta marapiṉiyuṭiya nūl muṟiyik kāṭṭi....	
‘muṟikāṭṭi’ eṉpataṉi iraṇṭaṉ tokiyākkāmal ‘muṟiyāṉē aṟivittu’ eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉtokiyākak koḷḷiṉ, ‘aikāra iṟutip  peyarnili muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē’ (to.e.280. nac.) eṉṟa nūṟpāvitippaṭi valleḻuttu mikku, ‘muṟikkāṭṭi’ eṉa amital vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ ‘muṟi kāṭṭi’ eṉpatu mūṉṟaṉ toki ākātu.	(pā. vi. pak. 234)
mēlum pāyirattuḷ aviyaraṅkēṟalik kūṟalē muṟi yaṉṟi, ulakiṟku aṟivittalik kūṟalilakkaṇamaṉṟu ātaliṉ, ’muṟi kāṭṭi’ eṉṟa toṭarkku ‘muṟiyāṉē ulakiṟku aṟivittu’ eṉṟu poruḷ ceytal poruntātu.	(pā. vi. pak. 141)
eḻuttuvakiyāṉ nālvakip puṇarcci -
nilimoḻi uyirīṟu, nilimoḻi meyyīṟu, varumoḻi uyirmutal, varumoḻi meymutal - eṉṟa nilikaḷ uṇmi yāṉ, uyirīṟu uyirmutal- uyirīṟu meymutal - meyyīṟu meymutal - meyyīṟu uyir mutal - eṉṟa nālvakiyāṉ puṇarcci nikaḻum.	(to. e. 107 nac.)
eḻutteṇṇikki  -
eḻuttu eṉat tokiyāṉ oṉṟum, mutaleḻuttum cārpeḻuttum eṉa vakiyāṉ iraṇṭum, ivviraṇṭaṉ pakutiyum kūṭṭa viriyāṉ tamiḻeḻuttu irunūṟṟeḻupatum ām eṉa uyttuṇarka.	(i. vi. 5 uri)
ilakkaṇa viḷakka āciriyar taṉiyeḻutti yuṭkoṇṭē, uyirmey 216, uyiraḷapeṭi 7, oṟṟaḷapeṭi 11, ēṉiya vākiya kuṟṟiyalikaram - kuṟṟiyalukaram - aikārakkuṟukkam- oḷakārak kuṟukkam - āytam - makarak kuṟukkam- ākiya āṟum ovvoṉṟu, ākac cārpeḻuttu viri 240; uyirum meyyumākiya mutaleḻuttu 30;ivvirutiṟamum kūṭṭat tamiḻ eḻuttu 270 ām eṉṟār.
‘eḻutteṉappaṭupa’ eṉṟa mutal cūttirac ceyti -
ellārāṉum aṟiyappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷuḷ, tamiḻ moḻikkut tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭa mutaleḻut tukkaḷ muppatē, cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟē eṉeḻuttiṉ tokiyi aṟivittalē ‘eḻutteṉap paṭupa’ eṉṟa mutal cūttirac ceytiyākum. piṟkālattuk kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar tam ciṉṉūlil mutaleḻuttukkaḷ 31 eṉavum, naṉṉūlār mutaleḻuttukkaḷ 30 cārpeḻuttukkaḷ 10 eṉavum, ilakkaṇaviḷakka nūlār cārpeḻuttu 9 eṉavum kūṟutal pōlvaṉa tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭalla. tolkāp piyaṉār kālattukku muṉṉum tamiḻeḻuttiṉ toki paṟṟi muraṇpaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ iruntirukkalām āyiṉum, avaṟṟi uṭaṉpaṭātu, ‘mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatē, cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟē’ eṉṟu toki kōṭaṟkē iccūttiram eḻuntatu.	(e. ā. pak. 5)
eḻuttu eṉpatu oliyeḻuttiṉiyē kuṟikkum. vari-vaṭivatti urupu, iyaṟki eṉṟa coṟkaḷ kuṟikkum.	(e.ku. pak. 4 (14-17cū.)
akaratti mutalākavum ṉakaratti īṟākavum uṭiyavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttu eṉṉum potuppeyar vitittaṟku eḻuntatu iccūttiram.	(cū. vi. pak. 19)
akaratti mutalākavum ṉakaratti īṟākavum uṭiyavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttu eṉṟu kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutalē iccūttirak karuttu.		(e. ku. pak. 6)
eḻutti eḻuvakiyāṉ uṇarttutal  -
eḻutti eḻuvakiyāṉ uṇarttiṉāṉ. eḻuvakiyāvaṉa 1. eḻuttu iṉiya eṉṟalum, 2. iṉṉa peyariṉa eṉṟalum, 3. iṉṉa muṟiyiṉa eṉṟalum, 4. iṉṉa piṟappiṉa eṉṟalum, 5. iṉṉa māttiriyiṉa eṉṟalum, 6. iṉṉa vaṭiviṉa eṉṟalum, 7. iṉṉa puṇarcciyiṉa eṉṟalum ām.	(nēmi. eḻut. pāyi. uri)
eḻuttōci veḷippaṭal -
pari picanti mattimi vikari- eṉṉum nālvaki vākki ṉuḷ, akattu eḻuvaṉavākiya pari mutal mūṉṟaṉiyum oḻittu, ‘eḻuntu puṟattu ici’p patākiya vikarivāk kiṉiyē (to.e. 102 nac.) ivar eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappām eṉṟār.
parivākku - untiōci; picantivākku- neñcu ōci allatu niṉivu ōci; mattimivākku - miṭaṟṟu ōci; vikarivākku - ceviōci. 	
ivaṟṟiṉ vikaṟpamellām civākamattuḷ kāṇka.		(i. vi. 9 uri)
‘eḻuntu puṟatticikkum meyteri vaḷiici’ -
untiyil tōṉṟum kāṟṟiṉ uḷḷē tiritarum kūṟṟiṉavākiya pari - picanti - mattimi - eṉṟa pakutikaḷ nīṅka, tali -miṭaṟu- neñcu- eṉṟa nilikkaḷaṅkaḷil, pal itaḻ nā mūkku mēlvāy - eṉṟa aintaṉ muyaṟciyāṉē eḻuttoliyāka veḷiyē cevippulaṉākap pulappaṭum vikarioliyē ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil eṭuttu viḷakkic collappaṭuvatām.	(to. e. 102 nac.)
eḻuvāyum viḷiyum alvaḻi āyiṉami  -
eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷil urupukaḷ tokkum virintum niṉṟu puṇarum āṟṟaluṭiya iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi mutal ēḻām vēṟṟumi īṟākiya āṟum vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarnili yaṉa. eḻuvāyurupum viḷiyurupum tokku niṟkum āṟṟaliṉṟi virintē niṟṟaliṉ, avaṟṟi vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil cērkkātu ‘alvaḻippuṇarcci’ eṉṟaṉar.
eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi āṟu payaṉiliyoṭum (to. co.66 cēṉā.) puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viḷivēṟṟumi taṉ poru ḷoṭu (-muṭikkum colloṭu) puṇarnta puṇarcciyum alvaḻiyāyiṉa.	(to. e. 112. nac. uri)
eḷḷāṭṭiyavaḻi yallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭātavāṟu pōla  -
“neṭilum kuṟilum kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṟantu piṉṉarp piḷavupaṭā ōciyi aḷapeṭi eṉṟu āciriyar vēṇṭi ṉār. ivi kūṭṭic colliya kālattallatu pulappaṭā, eḷḷāṭṭiyavaḻiyallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭāvāṟu pōla eṉṟu uṇarka” eṉṟu, ‘neṭil kuṟil’ eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉ kūṭṭamākiya piḷavupaṭāta ōciyē aḷapeṭi eṉṟa  karuttil nac. kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. 	(to. e. 6. nac.)
neṭiliyum kuṟiliyum cērttuc coṉṉālaṉṟit taṉittuc collumiṭattu aḷapeṭi oli pulappaṭātu. ‘eḷḷāṭṭutal’ cērttuc collutaṟkum, ‘eṇṇey pulappaṭutal’ aḷapeṭi oli tōṉṟutaṟkum uvamamām.
eṉpa: collilakkaṇam -
eṉpa eṉpatu pakara īṟṟup palaraṟicollākavum, akara īṟṟup palavaṟi collākavum varum.
palaraṟicol eṉ + pa- eṉap pakukkappaṭṭu iṟutinili yākiya pakaramē etirkālam kāṭṭa amintiruppatām. palavaṟicol eṉ + p + a - eṉap pakukkappaṭṭu, akaram palaviṉpāli māttiram uṇartta, pakara iṭinili etirkālam kāṭṭa amintiruppatām.
ākavē, eṉpa eṉṟa palaraṟicol pakara īṟṟatu, palavaṟicol akara īṟṟatu eṉpatu uṇarattakkatu.	(cū. vi. pak. 52)
palavaṟicol īṟu akaram aṉṟu; vakaramē eṉpatu pālacuntaraṉār karuttu. (e. 1)
eṉmaṉār: collamippu  -
‘ceymmaṉa eṉṉum toḻiliṟu collum’ 	(to. e. 210. nac.)
‘ceymmaṉa ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum’	(to. co. 222 cēṉā.)
‘icikkumaṉa collē’	(to. co. 1)
eṉṟu ceymmaṉa eṉṉum vāypāṭu oṉṟu tolkāppiyat til kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉi viṉimuṟṟuc col eṉpar cēṉāvariyar; peyareccac col eṉpar nac.
ceyyum eṉṉum viṉimuṟṟup pōlavē paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiya cey(m) maṉa eṉṉum muṟṟuccollōṭu ārvikuti cērac ‘ceymaṉār’ eṉṟu ākum. atē vāypāṭṭiṉatākum eṉmaṉa eṉṉum muṟṟuccollōṭu ārvikuti cēra eṉmaṉār eṉṟu ākum.
eṉpa+maṉ+ār; eṉpa eṉṟa muṟṟuccolliṉ pakaram keṭuttu, maṉ ār- ivaṟṟi iṇikka eṉmaṉār eṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar, kallāṭar, yāpparuṅkala uriyāciriyar, iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uriyāciriyar eṉṉumivarkaḷ koḷvar. cēṉāvariyar eṉ +maṉ+ār eṉak koṇṭu, maṉ etirkāla iṭinili eṉṉum karuttuṭiyār.	(e. ā. pak. 17)
eṉappaṭupa: collilakkaṇam  -
eṉ eṉṉum mutaṉilimītu ceyappaṭuporuṇmi uṇarttum paṭu eṉṉum vikutiyum akaraccāriyiyum vantu puṇarntu ‘eṉappaṭu’ eṉṟu niṉṟavaḻi, atuvum mutaṉilit taṉmippaṭṭu mēlvarum akaravikutiyum pakara iṭinili yum peṟṟu ‘eṉappaṭupa’ eṉa muṭinta palavaṟi collām.	(cū. vi. pak. 40,41)
ē
ē -
iḵtu ampu eṉṟa poruḷil varum peyarccol; ‘eṉakku oru karumam paṇi’ eṉṟa poruḷil varum muṉṉili ēval viṉiccol; tēṟṟam, viṉā, pirinili, eṇ, īṟṟaci, iciniṟi, viḷikkuṟippu, etirmaṟi, ikaḻccikkuṟippu, irakkakkuṟippu - mutaliya poruḷkaḷil varum iṭic col; perukkam eṉṟa poruḷil varum uriccol.
ēviṉā iṭiccol ēvaṉ, avaṉē - eṉamoḻi mutalilum iṟutiyilum varum.
ē emperumāṉ - viḷikkuṟippu; viḷiyiṟutiyil varutalē perumpāṉmi.
‘ē e iḵtottaṉ nāṇilaṉ’               - ikaḻccik kuṟippu.
‘ēē ivaḷorutti pēṭiyō eṉṟār’    - iciniṟi.
‘ēe pāvam’ - ikaḻccikkuṟippu mutaṟkaṇṇēyē varum.
ē eṉum col puṇarumāṟu -
ē eṉpatu ampu eṉṟa poruḷil varum peyarāka, valleḻuttu mutalākiya varumoḻiyoṭu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ‘eṉakku oru karumam paṇi’ eṉṟa poruḷil orumi ēvalviṉimuṟṟāka, valleḻuttu mutalākiya varumoḻiyoṭu viḷipporuḷil varum al vaḻippuṇarccikkaṇṇum aḷapeṭi ekaramum valleḻut tum peṟum.
e-ṭu : ē+ koṭṭil = ēek koṭṭil - vēṟṟumi
ē+koṟṟā = ēek koṟṟā - alvaḻi
ciṟupāṉmi ēppuḻi, ēñāyil eṉṟāṟ pōla aḷapeṭi eḻuttup peṟātu varutaluṇṭu.	 (to. e. 277,  272 nac. uri)
ēkāra iṭiccol moḻi īṟākiyavaḻit tēṟṟap poru ḷil varum ēkāram māttiram ekaram peṟṟum, ēṉiya atu peṟāmalum iyalpāka varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum.
e-ṭu :: ‘yāṉēe kaḷvaṉ’ - tēṟṟa ēkāram (273 nac.)
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ ikaḻccik kuṟippu, irakkak kuṟippu - ivaṟṟiṉkaṇ varum ēkāram ekaram peṟutalum, iciniṟi ēkāram aṭukki varutalum, ēṉiya poruḷil varum iṟuti ēkāraṅkaḷ iyalpāka varutalum koḷḷappaṭum.
ē ō  muṉ vaṉkaṇam varutal  -
ēkāra ōkāra iṭiccoṟkaḷ nilimoḻi īṟṟil niṟpa, varu moḻi mutaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākappuṇarum.
	e-ṭu : ‘yāṉē kaḷvaṉ’; ‘yāṉō tēṟēṉ’ (kuṟun.21) 	(naṉ. 201)
ēkāra īṟṟu  iyalpupuṇarcci -
māṟukōṭaliyuṭiya eccapporuḷ, viṉāpporuḷ, pirinilip poruḷ, īṟṟacip poruḷ, eṇṇup poruḷ - mutaliyavaṟṟil varum ēkāra iṭiccol varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : yāṉē koṇṭēṉ? - eṉpatu yāṉ koṇ ṭilēṉ eṉa māṟukōṭal ākiya etirmaṟip poruḷic collu vāṉatu olittal kuṟippāṉ uṇarttuvatu. 
nīyē koṇṭāy? - ēkāram colvāṉatu olittal kuṟippāṉ viṉāpporuḷi uṇarttuvatu.
avaruḷ ivaṉē takkavaṉ- ēkāram palariṉiṉṟum oruvaṉip pirittamiyāṉ, pirinilip poruḷi uṇarttuvatu.
kāṭu kaḻintōrē kātalar  - īṟṟacip poruḷil vantatu.
nilaṉē nīrē tīyē - eṇṇup poruḷil vantatu.
iviyāvum iyalpākap puṇarntavāṟu.
tēṟṟapporuḷil varum ēkāram aḷapeṭuttalum, īṟṟaci yāka varum ēkāram ciṟupāṉmi oru māttiri nīṇ ṭolittalum paṇṭi marapu.
cāttaṉē celka - ēkāram viḷipporuḷil vantatu. (ici niṟi, ikaḻccikkuṟippu, irakkak kuṟippu- mutaliya poruḷ kaḷil varum ēkāraṅkaḷ aṭukkiyum aḷapeṭuttum variṉum aviyum vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattum iyalpākavē puṇarum.)		(to.e. 275 nac. uri)

ēkāra īṟṟup potuppuṇarcci -
ēkārīṟṟuc col ūkārīṟṟuc col pōla varumoḻi yil vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṇum valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : ēk kaṭitu, cēk kaṭitu -	alvaḻi
		ēkkaṭumi, vēkkuṭam	-	vēṟṟumi (vēkkuṭam - 			vētaliyuṭiya kuṭam)
ciṟupāṉmi urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭpuṇarccik- kaṇṇum porunta, ēviṉ kaṭumi- eṉa iṉcāriyi peṟutalu muṇṭu.
vēṟṟumikkaṇ ēkāra īṟu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ekarappēṟum valliṉamum peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpu kaṇattuḷ ekaram peṟṟu muṭitalum koḷka.
	e-ṭu : ēekkoṭṭil; ēenekiḻcci, ēevaṉmi, ēe arumi (ēevarumi)
cē eṉṟa peyar marattik kuṟippiṉ varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpāṉa melleḻuttuppēṟum, peṟṟattik kuṟippiṉ iṉcāriyip pēṟum koḷḷum;
cēṅkōṭu (maram) ; cēviṉ kōṭu, cēviṉ maṇi, cēviṉimil (peṟṟam)
ciṟupāṉmi iṉ peṟātu ‘cēmaṇi’ eṉa iyalpāka muṭitalum koḷka.	(to. e. 274, 276-279 nac.)
‘ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ’ puṇarumāṟu -
ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷāvatu ēval taṉmi karutik kūṟappaṭṭa ēvalporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭittali uṇarttum akarīṟṟu viṉiccol. atu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : (avaṉ) celka kāṭṭiṉkaṇ, ceṟuviṉkaṇ, tāṉikkaṇ, pōriṉkaṇ
	‘ēval kaṇṇiya’ eṉavē, ēval kaṇṇāta viyaṅ kōḷum uṇṭu. aḵṟiṇi ēvaṟporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭikkātu.
e-ṭu : (atu) celka kāṭṭiṉkaṇ,....
tolkāppiyaṉār akarīṟu oṉṟaṉiyē viyaṅkōḷ īṟāka eṭuttōtiyuḷḷār; viyaṅkōḷ paṭarkkiyiṭat tiṟkē uṇṭu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.	(to. e. 210. nac.)
ēval kaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷukku ‘maṉṉiya peruma nīyē’ eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantuḷḷār (211 iḷa.). kūṟukiṉṟāṉ, avaṉ nilipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟē karutikkūṟaliṉ, atuvum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟēyām eṉpar nac.
vāḻiya eṉpatu ēval kaṇṇātatu eṉpatum, vāḻi eṉpatu ēval kaṇṇiyatueṉpatum iḷampūraṇar karuttu. (212 iḷa.)
‘ēval kuṟitta uṇarayaci miyā’ puṇarumāṟu -
muṉṉili ēvalviṉiyik karuti varum etirmukamākkum colliṉic cērnta miyā eṉṉum ākāra īṟṟuc col. iḵtu iṭiccollām. itu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : kēṇmiyā +koṟṟā = kēṇmiyā koṟṟā	(to. e. 224 nac.)
muṉṉiliyil ēvalviṉiccollik kuṟittu varum uri yaciyākiya miyā eṉṉum ākāra īṟṟu iṭiccol. 	(225 iḷa.)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ‘uriyaci’ eṉpataṟkuk kēḷ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa etirmukamākkum col eṉṟu poruḷ koṇ ṭār. iḷampūraṇar viṉiyic cērntē muṉṉilip poruḷ tarum miyā eṉṟa iṭiccol eṉṟē poruḷ koṇṭār.
ēval kuṟitta uriyaci eṉpatum, muṉṉili aciccol eṉpatum oruporuṭ kiḷaviyākum. ākavē, muṉṉili aciccollākiya miyā eṉpatē ēval kuṟitta uri yacic collām.	(e.ku.pak. 213)
ēval vikuti orumi -
āy ti mō - vikuti orumi ēvalām.
āy - uriyāy, ti- uritti, mō- urimō- eṉṟum,
naṭavāy- kēḷāy, pōti- aruḷti, koṇmō- ceṉmō - eṉṟum varum.	(to. vi. 113 uri)
ēvalvikutip paṉmi -
īr tīr miṉ miṉīr - vikuti paṉmi ēvalām.
īr - uriyīr kēḷīr, tīr - pōtīr aruḷtīr, miṉ- urimiṉ
kēṇmiṉ, miṉīr- urimiṉīr kēṇmiṉīr - eṉṟu varum.
aṉṟi, kuvvikuti orumikkum paṉmikkum ākum.
‘aṉṉiyē aṉiyārk kivvā ṟaṭuttavāṟu aruḷuku eṉṟāṉ’			(kamparā. ai : 9 : 16)
‘nī iṅku irukku eṉ ṟēki’	(cilap. 24)
‘ēṟṟiyal kāṇṭum nām ivaṇ taruku eṉṉavē’ 	(cīvaka. 1837)
eṉpavaṟṟil aruḷuku, irukku, taruku - eṉpaṉa orumikku ēvalām. mīḷavum, ‘entimārkaḷ eḻuku eṉṟāṉ’ (cīvaka.) eṉpataṉuḷ eḻuku paṉmikku ēvalām. (pāṭṭaṭi nikaḻiṭam terintilatu.)
orōvaḻi ivvikuti viyaṅkōḷ viṉikkum ām.
‘āyira mātarkkuḷḷa aṟikuṟi uṉakkuṇ ṭāku eṉṟu ēyiṉaṉ’ 			(kamparā. ai : 9 : 21)
‘paciyum piṇiyum pakiyum nīṅki
vaciyum vaḷaṉum curakkueṉa vāḻtti’ 	(cilap. 5:73, 74)
eṉpavaṟṟil uṇṭāku, curakku - eṉpaṉa uṇṭāka, curakka - eṉa viyaṅkōḷāy vantavāṟu kāṇka.
aṉṟiyum, varal taral - eṉṉum col, vārāy tārāy - varuti taruti - eṉṟum, vārīr tārīr varutīr tarutīr - eṉṟum, vammiṉ tammiṉ vammiṉīr tammiṉīr - eṉṟum varutalum aṟika.	(to. vi. 113 uri)
ēvalviṉi ukaraccāriyi peṟutal  -
uriñ, maṇ, tuṉ - mutaliyavaṟṟi eṭuttu uccarittu  peyari varuvittu ēval viṉiyākki ukaram peṟumāṟu kāṇka. (uriñ- urāy, maṇ - kaḻuvu, tuṉ - neruṅku)
e.ṭu: uriñu koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā, ñeḷḷā, nākā, māṭā, vaḷavā, eṉa mūviṉa mey varuvaḻiyum ukaram peṟṟavāṟu.
‘naṉi’ eṉṟataṉāl, maṇali vāru, caruki vāru- eṉa rakaram ēvaṟkaṇ ukaram aritiṉ peṟum.	(naṉ. 206 mayili. uri)
ēvalviṉikkuc ciṟappu viti  -
cey eṉpatu ēval. ataṉoṭu piṟaviṉi vikutiyākiya vi, pi - ivaṟṟil ēṟṟatoṉṟu iṇiyiṉ īrēvalām. mīṇṭum pivvikuti iṇiyiṉ mūvēvalām. aḵtāvatu ceyyeṉṟa ēvalmēl ēvalum, īrēvalum ām.
	e-ṭu : cey - ceyvi - ceyvippi; uṇ - uṇpi - uṇpippi; vā - varuvi - varuvippi	(naṉ. 138)
ēḻ urupēṟkumiṭattuc cāriyi peṟumāṟu -
āyirattukkuk kīḻuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷuḷ ēḻ eṉa ḻakara īṟāka varum eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ tavira, ēṉiya yāvum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉavām.ēḻ eṉpatoṉṟē meyyīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarām. āyiṉum ēḻ eṉpatu, oṉṟu mutaliya ēṉiya eṇṇuppeyar kaḷip pōla, urupēṟkumiṭattut tolkāppiyaṉāratu kālattē aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟṟatu; piṟkālattē iṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum.
	varumāṟu : ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṉoṭu, ēḻaṟku;ēḻiṉi, ēḻi ṉoṭu, ēḻiṟku	(to. e. 194 nac.)
ēḻ eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarp puṇarcci -
ēḻ eṉṟa ḻakara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar urupēṟkuṅkāl aṉcāriyi peṟum.	(to.e. 194 nac.)
varumāṟu : ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṉoṭu
poruṭpuṇarccik kaṇṇum ēḻ aṉcāriyi peṟum.
e-ṭu: ēḻaṉkāyam, ēḻaṉcukku, ēḻaṉtōri, ēḻaṉpayaṟu, (ivaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉāṟ koṇṭa kāyam - eṉṟāṟpōla, vēṟṟumi vaḻiyāṉ poruḷ ceyka.)	(388 nac. uri)
ēḻ eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka, varumoḻikkaṇ eṇṇup peyarum niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum variṉ, ēḻ eṉpatu ‘eḻu’ eṉṟu mutal kuṟuki ḻakaram ukaram ēṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu : eḻunāṉku, eḻukaḻañcu, eḻunāḻi
ciṟupāṉmi poruṭpeyar varumoḻiyāya vaḻiyum, ēḻ ‘eḻu’ eṉat tirintu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : eḻukaṭal, eḻucili, eḻutici, eḻupiṟappu	(389 nac. uri)
ēḻ nilimoḻiyāka varumoḻiyākap pattu eṉpatu variṉ eḻupaḵtu eṉap puṇarum.	(390 nac.)
ēḻ nilimoḻiyāka āyiram varumoḻiyākavariṉ, ēḻ eṉpatu ‘eḻ’ eṉak kuṟuki, eḻāyiram eṉappuṇarum. (391 nac.)
ēḻ nilimoḻiyāka varumoḻi nūṟāyiram variṉ, iyalpāka ēḻ nūṟāyiram eṉṟē puṇarum.	(392 nac.)
ciṟupāṉmi ēḻāyiram eṉavum, eḻu nuhṟāyiram eṉavum amitalum uṇṭu. varumoḻip poruṭpeyarkaḷ iyalpu kaṇattil toṭaṅkiṉum, ēḻ neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟṟu, eḻuñāyiṟu, eḻunāḷ, eḻuvaki - eṉap puṇarum.	(392 nac. uri)
tāmari veḷḷam āmpal eṉṟa pēreṇkaḷ vara, ēḻ tāmari - ēḻveḷḷam - ēḻāmpal - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarum. uyirmutal moḻi variṉum, ēḻakal - ēḻuḻakku- ēḻoṉṟu - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarum.	(393, 394 nac.)
ēḻaṉ tokiyil nilimoḻip peyar varumoḻi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyumāṟu -
ēḻām vēṟṟumiyurupu tokka tokiyil nilimoḻip peyar varumoḻi viṉiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattup potuviti yāl aikāra iṟuti vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikā malum, makara īṟu tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikāmalum iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : ‘varipāy (varuṭi)’, ‘pulampukkaṉaṉē pullaṇal kāḷi’  (puṟa. 258)	(to. e. 157 nac.)
ēḻām vēṟṟumip poruṇmi uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ puṇarumāṟu -
atōḷi, itōḷi, utōḷi, etōḷi, ītōḷi, āṇṭi, īṇṭi, ūṇṭi, yāṇṭi- eṉṉumivi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi mikkup puṇarum. avvaḻi, ivvaḻi, uvvaḻi, āṅkavi, īṅkivi, ūṅkavi, yāṅkavi - eṉṉumivi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi iyalpāyum mikkum uṟaḻntum puṇarum. ivi peyar niliyiṉa. (to. e. 159. nac. uri 160 iḷa.uri)
āyiṭi mutaliyaṉa tiripuṭiyaṉavām; iviyum vaṉ kaṇam vantuḻi uṟaḻntu puṇarum.	(159 nac. uri)
iṉi, aṇi - eṉpaṉa mikkup puṇarum.	(236 nac.)
aṅkaṇ, iṅkaṇ, uṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, yāṅkaṇ, avaṇ, ivaṇ, uvaṇ, evaṇ- eṉṉumivi, vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi, ṇakara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟākat tirintē muṭivaṉa. 	(307 uri nac.)
āṉ, īṉ, piṉ, muṉ - eṉṉumivi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṉakaroṟṟu ṟakaroṟṟākat tirintēpuṇarum.	(333 nac.)
avvayiṉ, ivvayiṉ, uvvayiṉ, evvayiṉ - eṉṉumiviyum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟākat tirintē puṇarum.		(334 nac.)
ūṉ (uvviṭam) eṉpatu ūṉ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla iyalpākavē puṇarum.	(333 nac. uri)
avvāy, ivvāy, uvvāy, evvāy- eṉṉumivi vaṉkaṇam variṉ vanta valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.		(361 nac. uri.)
 akkāl eṉpatu vaṉkaṇam variṉ lakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu puṇarum.	(368 nac. uri)
atōḷ, itōḷ, utōḷ, etōḷ - eṉṉumivi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ḷakaram ṭakaramākat tirintu puṇarum.	(398 nac. uri)
āṅku, īṅku, ūṅku, yāṅku, aṅku, iṅku, uṅku, eṅku- eṉṉumivi vaṉkaṇam variṉ valloṟṟu mikkum ‘yāṅku’oṉṟum orōvaḻi iyalpākavum puṇarum.	(427, 428 nac. uri)
muntu, paṇṭu, iṉṟu, aṉṟu - eṉpaṉa vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum.	(429 nac. uri)
munti, paṇṭi, iṉṟi, aṉṟi- eṉpaṉa vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikkē puṇarum. ivi yāvum peyar niliyiṉa.	(159 nac. uri)
ēṉi ekiṉ puṇarumāṟu -
ekiṉ eṉpatu puḷiya marattiyum aṉṉap paṟaviyiyum kuṟikkum. ‘ēṉi ēkiṉ’ eṉpatu aṉṉappaṟaviyik kuṟikkum col. ‘ekiṉ’ kāṇka.
ēṉi ‘otta kāṭciyiṉ’ iyaṟal -
eḻuttolikku variyaṟukkappaṭṭanilikkaḷamākiya tali - miṭaṟu - neñcu - eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ, valliṉam, talivaḷi yāṉum, uyirum iṭikkaṇamum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟat taliṉ, eñciya neñcuvaḷiyāṉāytam piṟakkum eṉpatu ittoṭariṉ karuttāka nac. kūṟuvār. i.vi. āciriyarum ikkaruttip piṉpaṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār.	(to. e. 101. nac.)
ēṉi mūṉṟu  -
arimāttiri peṟuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, 18 meykaḷiyum tavira, avi pōla arimāttiri aḷaviṉavāy olikkum kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟu eñciya mūṉṟeḻut tukkaḷ ‘ēṉi mūṉṟu’ām.	(to. e. 12 nac.)
ēṉi vakaram puṇarumāṟu -
vakaratti īṟāka uṭiya  coṟkaḷ av iv uv tev- eṉa nāṉkām. mutalaṉa mūṉṟum cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷām. ēṉi vakara īṟṟuc collākiya tev  eṉpatu uriccollām. atu paṭuttal ōciyāṉ peyarām. atu peyarākiyavaḻi iṉ cāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟṟut tevviṉi - tevviṉoṭu- tevviṟku- eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(to.e. 184. nac.)
aḵtu alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttuppēṟum, meṉkaṇattum iyalpukaṇattu vakarattum ukarap pēṟum, yakaram varuvaḻi iyalpum, uyir varuvaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum eytip puṇarum. makara mutalmoḻi varuvaḻi, tev eṉpataṉ īṟṟu vakaroṟṟu makaroṟṟākat tirintu puṇartalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu	:	alvaḻi	vēṟṟumi
			tevvuk kaṭitu	tevvukkaṭumi
			tevvu naṉṟu	tevvu naṉmi
			tevvu valitu	tevvu valimi
			tev yātu	tevyāppu
			tevvaritu 	tevvarumi
			(vakaram uṭampaṭumey)
			temmāṇṭatu,
			tevvu māṇṭatu	alvaḻi
			temmāṭci, tevvu māṭci   	vēṟṟumi				(to. e. 382 nac. uri)


ai
ai  -
iḵtu oṉpatām uyireḻuttu. akaramum ikaramum, akaramum yakaramum itaṟkup pōliyāka varuvaṉa. iḵtu aṇpal mutali nāviḷimpu uṟutalāl piṟakkiṟatu. itu vilaṅkal vaḷiyāṉ tōṉṟuvatu eṉpa.
itu, paṟavi - paṟattalic ceyvatu eṉa viṉimutaṟ poruḷ vikutiyākavum,
toṭi-toṭukkappaṭuvatu eṉac ceyappaṭuporuḷ vikutiyākavum,
pārvi - pārttaṟkuk karuvi yāvatu eṉak karuvipporuḷ vikutiyākavum,
koli - kollutalākiya toḻil eṉat toḻiṟpeyar vikutiyākavum,
tolli - paḻamit taṉmi eṉap paṇpuppeyar vikutiyākavum,
avaṉi (ai) eṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupākavum, 
ceṉṟaṉi - cel(ṉ) + ṟ + aṉ + ai eṉa muṉṉili orumi vikutiyākavum,
paṇṭu - paṇṭi eṉa oru cāriyiyākavum
viyappupporuḷil varum  uriccollākavum (‘ai viyappā kum’ to. co. 385 cēṉā.) - eṉap pala tiṟamāka varuvatu.	
a i y - ivaṟṟiṉ cērkkiyāl aikāram uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar.  (cū.vi. pak. 25)
ai am paleṉa varūum alpeyar  -
ai, am, pal - eṉṟa īṟukaḷiyuṭiya aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum allāta eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ. ai: tāmari;am: veḷḷam; pal: āmpal. tāmari - veḷḷam- āmpal - eṉpaṉa tamiḻil pēreṇkaḷ.
ivi varumoḻiyāka, nilimoḻi ēḻ eṉpatuniṟpiṉ, iyalpāka ēḻtāmari, ēḻveḷḷam, ēḻāmpal - eṉap puṇarum.	(to. e. 393 nac.)
ai oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟātal   -
aikāram oru colliṉ mutal iṭikaṭi eṉṟa mūviṭat tum kuṟukum. atu ceyyuṭkaṇ ōci iṭarppaṭṭu olikku miṭattuk kuṟukutalē perumpāṉmi.
‘aiyam tīrap poruḷi uṇarttalum
meynaṭu niliyum mikumniṟi kōṟkē’
‘aṭippiyiyāy kōltā eṉalum’
(‘yāri’ eṉpuḻippōla aikāram aci;  ‘aṭippiyāy’ eṉpatē viḷi.)
tavaḷi, kuvaḷi
eṉa aikāram mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉṟa mūviṭattum moḻik kaṇ kuṟukiṟṟu.	(to. e. 57 nac. uri)
moḻimutaṟkaṇ aikāram kuṟukiṉ oṉṟari māttiri aḷaviṟṟu eṉavum, iṭikkaṇṇum iṟutikkaṇṇum kuṟukiṉ oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟu eṉavum koḷpa. 	(naṉ. 95. civa.) (avinayam.)
moḻimutaṟkaṇ aikāram kuṟukātu eṉṟār iḷampūraṇar.		(to. e. 57)
civañāṉamuṉivarum ikkaruttiṉar.	(cū. vi. pak. 31)
nac. ōreḻuttorumoḻiyum kuṟukum eṉṟār. ki, pi- eṉpaṉa kuṟukiṉ kay, pay, - eṉa oṉṟari māttiri aḷaviṉavām.	(to. e. 57 uri)
aikāram moḻimutaṟkaṇ kuṟukātu. iṭiyiṉum iṟuti yiṉum ciṟupāṉmi kuṟukum. avvāṟu kuṟukumiṭattu oru māttiriyaḷavu olittal perumpāṉmi; oṉṟari māttiri olittal ciṟupāṉmi  eṉak koḷka. 	(e. ā. pak. 65)
aikāram oṉṟari māttiri aḷaviṟṟāyk kuṟukum eṉpatu yāpparuṅkala uri. nēminātam mūṉṟiṭattum aikāram kuṟukum eṉṟatu. vīracōḻiyam aikāram oṉṟari māttiri yāyk kuṟukum eṉṟatu. naṉṉūl aikāram mūviṭattum kuṟukum eṉṟatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkamum atuvē.
ai oḷak kuṟukkaṅkaḷiṉ māttiri  -
aikārak kuṟukkattiṟkum oḷakārak kuṟukkattiṟkum orō voṉṟu oṉṟari māttiri. oṉṟari aṟivatu eṟṟālō eṉiṉ, ‘kaṭa’ eṉpuḻi ṭakaraakaram orumāttiri āyavāṟum, ‘kaṭā’ eṉpuḻi ṭakarākāram iraṇṭu māttiri āyavāṟum, ‘kaṭi’ eṉpuḻi ṭakaraikāram oru māttiriyil ēṟi iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉ kuṟintavāṟum kaṇṭukoḷka. 	(nēmi. eḻut. 5 uri)
ai oḷak kuṟukkam -
aikārak kuṟukkam moḻi mutaṟkaṇ oṉṟari māttiriyā yum, ēṉiya iṭaṅkaḷil oru māttiriyāyum, oḷakārak kuṟukkam moḻi mutaṟkaṇ oṉṟari māttiriyāyum kuṟukum eṉpatu uyttuṇarntu koḷka. avvāṟu uyttuṇarntu koḷḷākkāl,
‘vikalum vikal varakkaṇṭum aḵtuṇarār
vikalum vikali vikumeṉ ṟiṉpuṟuvār’	(nālaṭi. 39)
eṉavum,
‘kivēl kaḷiṟṟoṭu pōkki varupavaṉ’	(kuṟaḷ 774)
eṉavum,
‘oḷaviya neñcattāṉ ākkamum’ 	(kuṟaḷ. 169)
eṉavum varum ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku ilakkaṇam iṉṟāy muṭiyum.	(naṉ. 95 civañā.) 
ai oḷak kuṟukkam cārpeḻuttātal  -
ki, pi, mi, ku, veḷa - eṉpaṉavum poruḷic cuṭṭiya vaḻik kuṟukum eṉak koḷka. iṅṅaṉam aḷavu kuṟukalāṉum, cīrum taḷiyum citiyumiṭattu alaku peṟāmiyāṉum, aikāra oḷakārak kuṟukkaṅkaḷ cārpeḻuttu eṉuyiriṉ vēṟāyiṉa.	(i. vi. 21 uri)
ai oḷat tōṟṟam paṟṟi vīracōḻiyam kuṟippatu  -
ai oḷa - eṉpaṉa īreḻuttolic cērkkiyāl ākiya cantiyakkaraṅkaḷ. ivi uyirneṭilēyāyiṉum oṉṟari māttiriyē olikkum eṉkiṟatu vīracōḻiyam.	(cantip. 3)
akaram vakarattō ṭiyintu oḷa ākum pōliyit tol kāppiyaṉār kuṟippiṭavilli. avarkaruttuppaṭi ai eṉpatu ‘ay’ eṉap pōlieḻuttāka varum.	(to.e. 56 nac.)
ai oḷa māttiri paṟṟi vīracōḻiyam kūṟuvatu -
neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ taṉittaṉi oṉṟari māttiri aḷaviṉa eṉpatu vīracōḻiyam kuṟip piṭum putucceyti. (avi cantiyakkaraṅkaḷ ātaliṉ am māttiriyaḷavē peṟum eṉpatu āciriyar putta mittiraṉār karuttu. avaṟṟik kuṟukkaṅkaḷākak koṇṭu eḻutum peruntēvaṉār urippakuti piḻipaṭṭuḷḷatu.)	(cantip. 5)

aikāra īṟṟu alvaḻip puṇarcci -
aikārīṟṟup peyar eḻuvāyttoṭarkkaṇ iyalpāyum uṟaḻntum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	kāri kuṟitu - iyalpu; tiṉi kuṟitu, tiṉikkuṟitu - uṟaḻcci
aikārīṟṟup peyar irupeyaroṭṭu ātaṟkaṇ mikkup puṇarum.
e-ṭu : cittirittiṅkaḷ, pulikkoṟṟaṉ.
uvamattokikkaṇṇum kuvaḷikkaṇ- eṉṟāṟpōla mikkup puṇarum. viṉi iṭi uric coṟkaḷ perum pāṉmi mikkup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	ollik koṇṭāṉ - aikāra īṟṟu viṉiccol 	valleḻuttu mikkatu; tillic col - aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccol valleḻuttu mikkatu; paṇit tōḷ - aikāra īṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkatu.
eṉavē, aikāra ī.ṟṟuc coṟkaḷ eḻuvāyttoṭarkkaṇ perumpāṉmi iyalpākavum, ciṟupāṉmi uṟaḻntum, irupeyaroṭṭum uvamattokiyum eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mikkum, viṉi iṭi uriccoṟkaḷāyiṉ perumpāṉmi mikkum puṇarum.	(to. e. 158. nac. uri)
aikāra īṟṟuk kēṭṭāmūlam  pāṟāṅkal eṉṉumivi puṇarntavāṟu -
aikāra īṟṟuk kēṭṭi - pāṟi- eṉṟa coṟkaḷ, īṟṟu aikāram keṭṭu ām cāriyiyum ammuccāriyiyum peṟṟu, varumoḻikaḷoṭu kēṭṭi + mūlam = kēṭṭāmūlam; pāṟi+ kal = pāṟaṅkal eṉap puṇarntavāṟu. pāṟāṅkal eṉpatum atu. (āṇṭuc cāriyi ‘ām’ eṉka.)	(to. e. 284, 288 nac. uri)

aikāra īṟṟuc col vēṟṟumippuṇarcci  -
vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyiṉkaṇ, aikāra īṟṟuccol iṟuti aikāram keṭṭu ammuccāriyi peṟṟum, aikāram keṭātu ammuccāriyi peṟṟum, aikāram keṭātu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkum muṭivaṉa uḷa.
	e-ṭu : vaḻutuṇi + kāy ḻū vaḻutuṇ  + am  + kāy = vaḻu tuṇaṅkāy; puṉṉi + kāṉal ḻū puṉṉi + am + kāṉal = puṉṉiyaṅkāṉal; mulli + puṟavam = mullippuṟavam	(naṉ. 202)
aikāra īṟṟut tiṅkaṭ peyarp puṇarcci -
tamiḻil tiṅkaṭ peyarkaḷ ikara aikāra īṟṟaṉa. aikāra īṟṟut tiṅkaṭ peyar varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi iṭiyē ikkuc cāriyi peṟum.
cittiri+ikku+koṇṭāṉ = cittirikkuk koṇ ṭāṉ - eṉa varum. cittirittiṅkaḷiṉkaṇ koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. 	(to. e. 286, 127 nac.)
aikāra īṟṟu nāṭpeyarp puṇarcci -
tamiḻil nāṭpeyarkaḷ ikara aikāra makara īṟṟaṉa. aikāra īṟṟu nāṭpeyar varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi āṉcāriyi iṭiyē peṟum. vaṉkaṇam variṉ āṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaram ākum.
cittiri + āṉ + koṇṭāṉ = cittiriyāṟ koṇṭāṉ - eṉa varum. cittirināḷiṉkaṇ koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu poruḷ.	(to. e. 286, 127 nac.)
aikāra īṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ puṇarumāṟu -
āviri, paṉi, tūtuṇi, vaḻutuṇi, tilli, ōli, tāḻi - mutaliyaṉa īṟṟu aikāram keṭṭu ammuccāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum; ciṟupāṉmi urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa iṉcāriyi poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum peṟuva tuṇṭu.
	varumāṟu :	āviri + kāy ḻū āvir+ am+ kāy= āviraṅ kāy; paṉi + kāy ḻū paṉ + am+ kāy = paṉaṅ kāy; tūtuṇi + kāy ḻū tūtuṇ + am + kāy = tūtuṇaṅkāy; vaḻutuṇi + kāy ḻū vaḻutuṇ+ am + kāy = vaḻutuṇaṅkāy; tilli + kāy ḻū till + am + kāy = tillaṅ kāy; ōli + pōḻ ḻūōl + am + pōḻ = ōlampōḻ; tāḻi + kāy ḻū tāḻ + am + kāy = tāḻaṅkāy 	(to. e. 283 nac.)
īṟṟu aikāram keṭāmal iṉcāriyi peṟṟu, āviriyiṉ kāy -  paṉiyiṉ kāy - tutuṇiyiṉ kāy - vaḻutuṇiyiṉ kāy - tilliyiṉ kāy - ōliyiṉpōḻ - tāḻiyiṉ kāy - (viciyiṉ kōṭu - ñemiyiṉ kōṭu - namiyiṉ kōṭu) - eṉavum varum.	(to. e. 283, 285 nac. uri)
aikāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumip puṇarcci -
aikārīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcciyāyiṉ iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	yāṉikkōṭu, yāṉiccevi, yāṉittali, yāṉippuṟam. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, ‘muṟikāṭṭi’ - tiṉi koṇarn tāṉ - paṉi piḷantāṉ - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarum
urupēṟṟavaḻiyum yāṉiyik koṇarntāṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōla valleḻuttu mikum.	(to. e. 280 nac.)
aikāra oḷakārak kuṟukkaṅkaḷ -
aikāram taṉṉic cuṭṭumaḷavil kuṟukātu maṟṟu moḻiyiṉ mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉṟa mūviṭaṅkaḷilum taṉ iyalpāṉa iraṇṭu māttiriyiṟ kuṟukivarum. oḷakāramum moḻi mutaṟkaṇ  māttiram varutaliṉ āṇṭu avvāṟu kuṟuki varum. moḻi mutaṟkaṇ varum aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ māttiri oṉṟari eṉavum, moḻi iṭiyiṟutikaḷil varum  aikārattiṉ māttiri oṉṟu eṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu  	aippaci - mippuṟam, valiyaṉ, kuvaḷi, muval	(naṉ. 95)
ōreḻuttorumoḻiyām aikāram taṉittu varumiṭattum, nilimoḻi  varumoḻiyāyt toṭarntu  varumiṭattum kuṟukātu.
aikāra oḷakāraṅkaṭku māṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ -
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ akaramum ikaramum iṇintu aikārat tiṟku māṟṟeḻuttukkaḷām. moḻi mutaṟkaṇ akaramum ukaramum iṇintu oḷakārattiṟku māṟṟeḻuttukkaḷām.
	e-ṭu : a) aivaṉam eṉpatu ayivaṉam eṉa varum. (yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu varutalē cāṉṟōr vaḻakkil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ayivaṉam - ayivaṉam)
viram, kili eṉpaṉa vayiram kayili eṉavarum. (ivi vaṭacoṟkaḷ)
 ā)	kuriyar eṉpatu kavuriyar eṉavum, muriyar eṉpatu mavuriyar eṉavum,	muli eṉpatu mavuli eṉavum,
oḷaṭatam eṉpatu avuṭatam eṉavum varum. (ivi vaṭa coṟkaḷ).
iṉi, moḻiyiṭiyilum īṟṟilum varum aikārattiṟkup piṟitoruvaki māṟṟeḻuttu varumāṟu:
akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaramēyaṉṟi, akaramum ataṉpiṉ yakara oṟṟum ai eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu poruḷpaṭa varum.
	e-ṭu : niliyam - nilayam; kuvaḷi - kuvaḷay; viṉiyam - viṉayam
ay eṉṉum ivveḻuttukkaḷ moḻi mutal aikārat tiṟku māṟṟeḻuttukkaḷāy variṉ māttiriyaḷavu oṉṟumē vēṟupaṭukiṟatu. ōciyālum acikoḷḷum niliyālum evvēṟupāṭum aṅku iṉṟu. ātaliṉ, ivveḻuttukkaḷ māṟṟeḻuttukkaḷāy moḻiyiṭiyilum īṟṟilumē varum eṉṟu uyttuṇarappaṭum.
immāṟṟeḻuttiṉ payaṉāvatu, ceyyuṭkaṇ cīrum taḷiyum citiya varumiṭattu avi citiyāmal ceytukōṭalām.
e-ṭu 	‘aṉṉiyiyāṉ nōva tavamāl’ eṉpuḻi, veṇpāvil nālaciccīr vantu cīrum taḷiyum citiyum; aṉṉayiyāṉ’ eṉa māṟṟeḻuttāṟ kūṟiṉ avi citiyāvām eṉka. (to.e.56. ca.pāla.)
aikārak kuṟukkam  -
‘ai oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟātal’ kāṇka.
‘aikārak kuṟukkam moḻi mutaṟkaṇ vārāmi  -
aikāram moḻimutaṟkaṇ kuṟukum eṉavum, ki - pi - mutaliyaṉavum poruḷic cuṭṭiyavaḻik kuṟukum eṉavum kūṟutal poruntātu. iṭiyaṉ maṭiyaṉ tiṉi  paṉi - eṉpuḻik kuṟukutal pōla, ‘vikalum vikal varakkaṇṭum’ (nālaṭi. 39) eṉpuḻi aikāram mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukāmi cevi karuviyāka uṇarappaṭutalāṉum, ‘vikalum vikal’ eṉpuḻik kuṟukumāyiṉ, ‘vikal’ eṉpatu kuṟiliṇi oṟṟāy veṇṭaḷi cititalāṉum, aikāram moḻiyiṭippaṭuttu icippiṉ yāṇṭu variṉum kuṟukum eṉṟal poruntāmi yāṉum, aikāram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukātu eṉavē koḷḷu tal civañāṉa muṉivar karuttām. eḻuttatikāra ārāycci yum itaṉiyē koḷkiṟatu.	(cū. vi. pak. 31, e. ā. pak. 65)
aikāra vikuti ceyappaṭuporuṇmi uṇarttal  -
aikāravikuti ceyappaṭuporuṇmi uṇarttal naṭavi, cēkki, uṭukki, toṭi, viṭi - pōṉṟavaṟṟil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
naṭavi	-	naṭattappaṭuvatu	-	pakuti: naṭa
cēkki	-	taṅkappaṭuvatu	-	pakuti: cē
uṭukki	-	uṭukkappaṭuvatu	-	 pakuti: uṭu
toṭi	-	toṭukkappaṭuvatu	-	pakuti: toṭu
viṭi	-	viṭukkappaṭuvatu	-	pakuti:viṭu
cū.vi. pak. 33
aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripukaḷ -
1. ‘melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu varutal’: aḵtāvatu melleḻuttu mika vēṇṭiya iṭattu valleḻuttu mikutal.
akara īṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyil vaṉ kaṇam variṉ, vanta valleḻuttiṉ iṉamelleḻuttu mikum. (to. e. 217 nac.) e-ṭu : viḷaṅkaṉi, viḷampū
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyāyiṉ viḷak kuṟittāṉ - eṉa vanta valleḻuttē mikum.
2. ‘valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟal’:
makara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ makaram keṭa, vanta valleḻuttu iṭiyē mikkup puṇartal marapu (310 nac.) e-ṭu :	marakkiḷi, maracciṉi, marattōl, marap paṭṭi.
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumittokiyāyiṉ, makaram keṭa, vanta valleḻuttiṉ iṉamelleḻuttē mikum. e-ṭu :	maraṅ kuṟittāṉ.
3. ‘iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟal’:
yakara  īṟṟut tāy eṉṉum muṟippeyar vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. 	 (358 nac.) e-ṭu : tāyki, tāycevi, tāytali, tāypuṟam.
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcciyil, tāyik koṉṟa koli - eṉṟa poruḷil, tāyk koli - eṉa valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.
4. ‘uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutal’:
kuṟṟeḻutti aṭuttō taṉittō varum ākāra ūkāra ēkāra īṟṟup peyarkaḷ, vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyil vaṉkaṇam vara, eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyum valleḻuttum mikkup puṇarum. (226, 267, 277 nac.) e-ṭu : palāakkōṭu, uṭūukkuṟi, ēekkoṭṭil
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ, vanta valleḻuttē mikkup puṇarum. e-ṭu :	 palāk kuṟittāṉ, kaḻūk koṇarntāṉ, ēk kaṭṭiṉāṉ
5. ‘cāriyi uḷvaḻic cāriyi keṭutal’:
vaṇṭu, peṇṭu - eṉpaṉa vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccik kaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum eṉpatu viti.	(420 nac.) e-ṭu : vaṇṭiṉkāl, peṇṭiṉ tali
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ cāriyi iṉṟi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. e-ṭu : vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ, peṇṭu koṇarntāṉ
6. ‘cāriyi uḷvaḻit taṉurupu niliyal’:
cāriyi peṟumiṭattu, vaṇṭiṉ kāl - peṇṭiṉ tali - eṉa āṟām vēṟṟumiyurupu maṟintu varuvatip pōlal lātu, perumpālum taṉṉurupu nilipeṟṟē vaṇṭiṉik koṇarntāṉ - peṇṭiṉik koṇarntāṉ - eṉṟu taṉṉurupu virintē varutal.
7. ‘cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻattōṉṟal’:
puḷi, paṉi, vēl - mutaliya marappeyarkaḷ ammuccāriyi peṟṟē vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻip puṇarum. (244, 283, 375 nac.) e-ṭu : puḷiyaṅkāy, puḷiyañ cetiḷ;paṉaṅkāy, paṉantōl; vēlaṅkāy, vēlampaṭṭi
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ cāriyi peṟātu vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi, valleḻuttu mikāmalum mikkum, īṟu tiriyāmalum tirintum puṇarum. e-ṭu : puḷi kuṟittāṉ, puḷik kuṟittāṉ; paṉi taṭintāṉ, paṉit taṭintāṉ; vēl taṭintāṉ, vēṟṟaṭintāṉ
8. ‘uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutal’:
uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākap puṇarumeṉpatu viti. (153 nac.) e-ṭu : nampi ki, nampi tali; naṅki ki, naṅki cevi.
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumikku vaṉkaṇam vantuḻiurupu virintu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku varalvēṇṭum. 
e-ṭu : nampiyik koṇarntāṉ, naṅkiyik kaṇṭāṉ 
ciṟupāṉmi urupu maṟintu, ‘oṉṉārt teṟalum’ (kuṟaḷ 264), eṉṟu mikkum, ‘āṭūu aṟicol’ eṉṟu ukarappēṟu eytiyum, avaṟkaṇṭu (aka. 48)  makaṟ peṟṟāṉ, makaṭ peṟṟāṉ eṉa īṟu tirintum, ‘maḻavar ōṭṭiya’ (akanā.1) eṉṟu iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iyalpāyum varutalumuṇṭu.
9.  aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku avviyal niliyal’:
uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravum potuppeyar vēṟṟumippuṇarccikkaṇ uyartiṇippeyar pōla vaṉkaṇam vantuḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum. (155 nac.) e-ṭu : cāttaṉ ki, koṟṟaṉ cevi, cāttitali
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumikkaṇ urupu virintē varutal vēṇṭum.	(155 nac.)
e-ṭu: koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ, cāttaṉit takittāṉ. ciṟupāṉmi cāttaṟ kaṇṭu, koṟṟaṟ cārntu - eṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyurupu tokku nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu varutalumuṇṭu. (urupu tokkami yāṟṟāṉ ittiripu eṉka)
10. ‘meypiṟitu ākuiṭattu iyaṟki ātal’:
ṇakara ṉakara īṟukaḷ vēṟṟumipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ muṟiyē ṭakara ṟakaraṅkaḷākat tiriyum eṉpatu potuviti. (302, 332 nac.) e-ṭu : maṭkuṭam, poṟkuṭam
āyiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyurupu tokka puṇarcciyil vaṉkaṇam varumoḻiyāka varuvuḻit tiripiṉṟip puṇarum. 
e-ṭu : maṇ koṇarntāṉ, poṉ koṇarntāṉ
11. ‘aṉṉa piṟavum aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripu’:
yāṉ eṉpatu eṉ eṉṟu tirintu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyil vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. ēṉi neṭumutal kuṟukum peyarkaḷum avvāṟē muṭiyum.
e-ṭu : eṉki, eṉcevi, eṉtali, eṉpuṟam
āyiṉ, yāṉ yām nām nī tāṉ tām - eṉpaṉa neṭumutal kuṟuki eṉ em tam niṉ taṉ nam- eṉṟākiya niliyil, iraṇṭaṉ urupu tokka puṇarccikkaṇ, eṉ+ kaṇṭu = eṟkaṇṭu; taṉ+ koṇṭāṉ = taṟkoṇṭāṉ; nam + puṇarvu = nappuṇarvu; eṉṟāṟpōla, nilimoḻi īṟṟu mey tirintu puṇarum.  (urupu tokkamiyāṟṟāṉ ittiripu eṉka.)
aikārīṟṟup peyarum rakarīṟṟup peyarum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, vaṉkaṇam variṉ, valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum eṉpatu potu viti. 	(280, 362 nac.)
e-ṭu : yāṉikkōṭu, tērkkāl
āyiṉ, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyāyiṉ vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpām.
e-ṭu : tiṉi piḷantāṉ, mayir kuṟittāṉ
cilaviṭattē iṉcāriyi peṟum nilimoḻikaḷ iraṇṭa ṉurupu viriyātu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum niliyum arukiyuṇṭu. e-ṭu :	‘maṟaṅkaṭanta aruṅkaṟpiṉ... tuṇiviyar’ (puṟa. 166) (kaṟpiṉiyuṭiya tuṇiviyar) ‘cilcolliṉ.... tuṇiviyar’ (coṟkaḷiyuṭiya tuṇiviyar) ‘āyiru tiṇiyiṉ icikkumaṉa collē’ (co.1) (irutiṇi yiṉiyum icikkum)
iviyē yaṉṟip potuvitiyip piṉpaṟṟik kaṭukkuṟit tāṉ (254 nac.) ceppukkoṇarntāṉ (414 nac.) eṉṟu muṭivaṉavum, mikoṇarntāṉ- mikkoṇarntāṉ, vilkōḷ - viṟkōḷ eṉa uṟaḻvaṉavum koḷka.	(to. e. 157 nac. uri)
kaḻi kuṟittāṉ, tiṉi piḷantāṉ - eṉṟa iyalpum koḷka.		(158 iḷa. uri)
aintaṉ oṟṟu yakaramātal -
varumoḻiyil āyiram variṉ, nilimoḻiyākiya aintu eṉpataṉ īṟākiya tukaram keṭa, niṉṟa ‘ain’ eṉpataṉ nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟāka, aiy+āyiram = aiyāyiram eṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar tol.	(to. e. 468 nac.)
‘aivaki aṭiyum’ eṉpuḻi, aikāram nīṅkalāka ēṉiya eḻuttukkaḷ keṭṭup puṇarvatu pōl, ai+ āyiram= aiyāyiram eṉa, yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarntatu eṉpatē ēṟṟatu.
aiyīrāyiram, aiyuṇarvu - eṉpaṉavaṟṟi nōkka, yakaram uṭampaṭumey eṉpatē poruntum.	(e. ā. pak. 175)
ai  neṭṭuyir eṉpatu -
aikāratti neṭil eṉṟu colvataṟku ataṟku iṉamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttu iṉṟēṉum, neṭil pōla iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṟṟāy olittaliṉ ataṉi neṭilākavē kūṟi, ataṉ oliyamippi nōkki aikārattukku ikaratti iṉamākak koḷḷa vittu aḷapeṭi eḻuttamippukku vāyppu aḷikkappaṭṭatu.	(to. e. 4 iḷa., nac. uri)
aiyīṟṟuṭik kuṟṟukaram  -
paṇṭik kālam - iṟṟi nāḷ - eṉavum, aṟṟik kūli - iṟṟi nalam- eṉavum varum. ‘aiyīṟṟuṭik kuṟṟukaram’ eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyamiyāl, nēṟṟip poḻutu eṉa (meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram allā)p piṟa toṭarkaḷ aikāram peṟutalum, ‘aiyīṟṟuṭik kuṟṟukaram’ eṉa uṭimi yākkik kūṟiyamiyāl, oṟṟi - iraṭṭi - vēṭṭi- eṉa orumoḻiyāy niṉṟu aikāram peṟutalum, īrāṭṭi mūvāṭṭi - eṉat toṭarmoḻiyāy niṉṟu aikāram peṟutalum koḷka.	(naṉ. 185 caṅkara.)
aiyīṟṟup pakāppatam īṟukeṭa ‘ēyaṉ’ vikuti peṟutal -
aiyīṟṟup pakāppataṅkaḷil ai oḻittu vikutiyāka ‘ēyaṉ’ eṉṟu muṭintāl, ‘īṉṟa makaṉ’ eṉṟu kāṭṭum vaṭanaṭip pakupataṅkaḷām. 
kārttikiyiṉ makaṉ kārttikēyaṉ, kaṅkiyiṉ makaṉ kāṅkēyaṉ, vinatiyiṉ makaṉ vinatēyaṉ- eṉṟu varum		(to.vi. 86 uri)
‘aiyum meyyum keṭṭa iṟuti’yum ataṉ iyalum -
iṟuti aiyum meyyum keṭṭuc cāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum col ‘yāvi’ eṉpatu. yāvi eṉṟa col vaṟṟuc cāriyiyum urupum peṟṟup puṇarumiṭattu īṟṟiluḷḷa aikāramum aikārattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa vakara oṟṟum keṭṭu yā eṉa niṉṟu, yāvaṟṟi - yāvaṟṟoṭu - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(to. e. 183 nac.)
itu pōlavē, av iv uv - eṉpaṉavum, vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟāka nilimoḻiyīṟṟu vakaram keṭa, a i u -eṉa niṉṟu, vaṟṟum urupum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	varumāṟu : av+ vaṟṟu + ai ḻū a +vaṟṟu + ai = avaṟṟi 		av +vaṟṟu + oṭu ḻū a +vaṟṟu + oṭu = avaṟṟoṭu	(to. e. 183. nac.)
o
o -
itu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil uyireḻuttukkaḷil pattāvatu; aṅkāttalōṭu itaḻ kuvitalāṉpiṟakkum uyireḻuttuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟu; tamiḻc ciṟappeḻuttu aintaṉuḷ oṉṟu; ōkāra neṭilukku iṉamāṉa kuṟil; ōkāram aḷapeṭukku miṭattu ataṉi aṭuttu varuvatu; nakaroṟṟu oṉṟuṭaṉēyē kūṭi (no - eṉa) moḻiyiṟutiyil varuvatu; muṉṉili ēva lorumi viṉiyākiya ō eṉpatu aḷapeṭukkumiṭattum, ciṟappup poruḷil varum ōkāram aḷapeṭukkumiṭattum, moḻiyiṟutiyil okaram aḷapeṭi yeḻuttāka nikaḻum.
okara īṟṟup puṇarcci -
muṉṉili moḻikkaṇ varum okarīṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : ōok koṟṟā; ō - ‘iṅṅaṉam ceytali oḻi’ eṉṉum poruḷatu.	(to. e. 272 nac.)
ciṟappup poruḷil varum okarīṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	 nīyōo koṭiyi	(273 nac.)
okaram puḷḷi peṟutal -
‘ekaram puḷḷi peṟutal’ kāṇka.
okaram moḻiyīṟātal -
okaram nakarameyyuṭaṉ kūṭiyē ‘no’ eṉa moḻiyīṟām. atu vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇartalē perum pāṉmi; meṉkaṇam variṉ meli mikutalum ām.
	e-ṭu :	no koṟṟā - itu viṭṭicittaliṉ valleḻuttu mikātāyiṟṟu.
non nākā, nom māṭā - eṉa melleḻuttu mikkum
no nākā, no māṭā - eṉa viṭṭicittu iyalpākavum varum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nok koṟṟā  eṉa valleḻuttu mikum eṉṟār. ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṉṉili viṉiccol mikkē muṭital koḷka eṉṟār avar.	(to. e. 151 nac.)
ō eṉṟa muṉṉili ēvalviṉi, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam vantaviṭattu aḷapeṭuttu valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyumvaḻi, okaram nilimoḻi yīṟṟil varum.	(272 nac.)
e-ṭu : ōok koṟṟā
ciṟappup poruḷil varum ōkāra iṭiccol aḷapeṭuttu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(273 nac.)
e-ṭu : yāṉōo koṭiyaṉ
oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭi mikutal -
oṭṭiya oṟṟāvatu nilimoḻi īṟṟeḻutti oṭṭi varumoḻi mutalil varum uyirmeyeḻuttiṉkaṇ uḷḷa meyyeḻuttu.
nilimoḻiyākum a i u - eṉṟa mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṭic coṟkaḷum varumoḻi meṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumvaḻit tamakkup poruntiya oṟṟukkaḷāka varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varum oṟṟukkaḷ mikkup puṇartal.
e-ṭu :	aññāṇ,  annūl, ammaṇi - to.e. 205 nac.
	iññāṇ, innūl, immaṇi - to.e.238 nac.
	uññāṇ, unnūl, ummaṇi - 256 nac.
oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakku -
oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkāvatu coṟkaḷ cāriyi peṟutaṟku ēṟṟa moḻiyamippām. ākavē ellāc coṟkaḷum cāriyi peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa variyaṟi iṉṟu. mēlum iṉṉa iṉṉa coṟkaḷ iṉṉa iṉṉa cāriyi peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa variyaṟiyum uṇṭu. ivvari yaṟi cāṉṟōr vaḻakkum, cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷum nōkkik koḷḷappaṭṭatām.
nilā eṉpatu attuccāriyi peṟum eṉṟa vitiyi (e. 228 nac.), (varumoḻi peyarāy vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci ākiyaviṭattu) oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakku aṉmiyiṉ, nilākkatir - nilāmuṟṟam - eṉpaṉa peṟāvāyiṉa.	(to.e. 132 nac.)
oṭṭuppeyar -
pala coṟkaḷ oṭṭi niṉṟu oru peyarik kuṟikka varuvatu oṭṭuppeyarām. pakāppatam iraṇṭu mutal ēḻeḻuttu īṟākavum, pakupatam iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu eḻuttu īṟākavum toṭarum. āṉāl kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻapuram, irata nūpuraccakkaravāḷam (cūḷā. irata.12.), pāṇṭiyaṉ pal yākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti - eṉṟal toṭakkattu oṭṭuppeyarkku variyaṟi illi eṉka.(naṉ. 129 mayili.)

oṭu eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
oṭu eṉṟa marappeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, uti eṉṟa marappeyar pōla, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ otta melleḻuttu iṭiyē mikku muṭiyum.
e-ṭu : oṭuṅkōṭu, oṭuñcetiḷ, oṭuntōl, oṭumpū
ciṟupāṉmi oṭuvaṅkōṭu - eṉa, ammuccāriyi iṭiyē peṟutalum uṇṭu.	(to. e. 262 nac. uri)
otta oṟṟu mikal -
varumoḻi mutalil ka ca ta pa - eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ variṉ, avvoṟṟukkaḷē nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷukku iṭiyē mikutal.
e-ṭu : viḷakkuṟitu, viḷacciṟitu, viḷattītu, viḷapperitu
eṉavē, nilimoḻiyīṟum varumoḻi valliṉamutalum puṇarumvaḻi, iṭiyē varum valloṟṟu varumoḻi mutalil varum valloṟṟēyām.  avi iyipu valleḻuttu eṉappaṭum.	(to. e. 203 nac.)
otta kuṟṟeḻuttu -
uyirneṭilkaḷukku iṉamāka varum avvak kuṟṟeḻut tukkaḷ. ākārattukku akaramum, īkārattukku ikaramum, ūkārattukku ukaramum, ēkārattukku ekaramum, ōkārat tukku okaramum otta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷām. aikārattukku ikaramum, oḷakārattukku ukaramum aḷapeṭiyāy ici niṟikkum otta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ.	(to. e. 41, 42 nac.)
orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ ēṉiya eṇkaḷ -
orupaḵtu mutalāya eṭṭu eṇkaḷ nilimoḻiyāka niṟpa, varu moḻiyākoṉṟu mutal oṉpatu eṇkaḷum avi yūrnta piṟapeyarum varumāyiṉ, nilimoḻiyiṉ āytam keṭat takara oṟṟu aṅku varum.
	varumāṟu : orupaḵtu + oṉṟu ḻū orupattu + oṉṟu = orupat toṉṟu; eṇpaḵtu + oṉpatu ḻūeṇpattu + oṉpatu = eṇpattoṉpatu; irupaḵtu + mūṉṟu kalam ḻū irupattu + mūṉṟukalam = irupattu mūṉṟukalam.
orupatu mutaliyavaṟṟukkum ippuṇarcci okkum.
orupatu + oṉṟu = orupattoṉṟu - eṉavarum.	(naṉ. 196)
orupaḵtu mutaliyavi urupēṟkumpōtu aṭiyum tiripukaḷ -
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa urupēṟkumiṭattu iṭiyē āṉ cāriyi vara, paḵtu eṉpataṉ pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalāka ‘aḵtu’ eṉpatu keṭa, āṉcāriyi peṟṟu, orupāṉi, irupāṉi, muppāṉi, nāṟpāṉi, aimpāṉi, aṟupāṉi, eḻu pāṉi, eṇpāṉi - eṉa muṭiyum.	(to.e.199 nac.)
potuvitiyāṉ eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟum marapi oṭṭi, orupaḵtu + aṉ + ai = orupaḵtaṉi...... eṇpaḵtaṉi eṉa muṭitalumuṇṭu.	( 199 nac. uri)
oṉpaḵtu eṉpatum paḵtu eṉa muṭiyum collātaliṉ, atuvum oṉpāṉi, oṉpaḵtaṉi - eṉṟu urupēṟkum  muṭivu koḷḷum.	( 199 nac. uri)
orupaḵtu mutaliyavi niṟi aḷavup peyarkaḷoṭu puṇarumāṟu -
orupaḵtu muṉṉar niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuvaḻi, orupatiṉkaḻañcu, orupatiṉkalam, orupatiṟṟukkaḻañcu, orupatiṟṟukkalam- eṉa ‘iṉ’ peṟṟum iṉ ‘iṟṟu’ ākiyum puṇarum.
immuṭipi orupaḵtu mutal eṇpaḵtu muṭiyavum koḷka.
eṇpatiṉkalam, eṇpatiṟṟukkalam - eṉap puṇarkka.
varumoḻi poruṭpeyarāyavaḻiyum patiṟṟuvēli, patiṟṟut toṭi- eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭivaṉavum koḷka.	(to. e. 436 nac.)
uyir varuvaḻip patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku - eṉa iṉ ‘iṟṟu’ ākum eṉka.	(121 nac. uri)
orupatu mutaliyavi urupoṭu puṇartal  -
orupatu,  irupatu..... eṇpatu - eṉṉum eṭṭu eṇkaḷ nili moḻiyāka niṟka, urupu vantu puṇarumiṭattu, iṭiyē āṉcāriyi varutalumuṇṭu; vārāmiyumuṇṭu. atu varumiṭattu nilimoḻiyil ‘patu’ eṉpataṉkaṇ pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalākap piṟa keṭum. oṉpatu eṉṟa nilimoḻikkum itu poruntum. orupaḵtu irupaḵtu.... oṉpaḵtu - eṉa āytam peṟiṉum ivviti poruntum.
e-ṭu: 	orupatu, orupaḵtu+ aiḻū orup + āṉ + ai = oru pāṉi; oṉpatu, oṉpaḵtu +ai ḻū oṉp +  āṉ+ ai = oṉpāṉi.	orupatu, orupaḵtu + ai = orupati, oru paḵti; oṉpatu, oṉpaḵtu + ai =  oṉpati, oṉ paḵti eṉac cāriyi peṟātu; muṭintaṉa.	(naṉ.249)
oru puṇarccikkaṇ muttiripum varutal -
makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ - maka(m)+ attu+ āṉ+ koṇṭāṉ; ippuṇarccikkaṇ, attum āṉum mikutal; attiṉ akaram keṭutal; āṉ cāriyiyiṉ ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāka mey piṟitu ātal.
ivvāṟu oru puṇarccikkaṇṇēyē, meypiṟitātal - mikutal - kuṉṟal - eṉṟa muttiripukaḷum vantaṉa.	(to. e. 109 nac.)
e-ṭu :	yāṉi + kōṭu  = yāṉikkōṭu - vali mikal; nilam + paṉi =  nilappaṉi - makaram keṭutal, pakarammikal; paṉi + kāy = paṉaṅkāy -  ai keṭutal, ‘am’mikal, ammiṉ  makaram ṅakaramākat tirital. ivvāṟu oru puṇarcciyil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu vikāramum varutal kāṇka.	(naṉ. 157)
orumoḻi ilakkaṇam -
‘āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār orumoḻi ilakkaṇam kūṟāmi’ kāṇka.
olvaḻi oṟṟu mikutal -
nilimoḻikaḷi aṭuttu valleḻutti mutalāvatākak koṇṭa ku - kaṇ - eṉṟa vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ puṇariṉ, poruntumiṭattu valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum mikum. ciṟupāṉmi iyalpātalum, ciṟupāṉmi nili moḻi īṟutiritalum uḷa.
e-ṭu: maṇikku, maṇikkaṇ; īkku, īkkaṇ; tiṉikku, tiṉikkaṇ; vēykku, vēykkaṇ; vērkku, vērk kaṇ; vīḻkku, vīḻkkaṇ - ivi valloṟṟumikkaṉa. valloṟṟu mikum nilimoḻi īṟukaḷ i, ī, ai, y, r, ḻ - eṉṟa āṟākum.
tām, nām - eṉpaṉa kaṇṇurupu varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟuki makaram keṭṭu ṅakara melloṟṟu mikuvaṉavām.
taṅkaṇ, naṅkaṇ - eṉa varum.
uyartiṇippeyar viravuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉ kaṇurupu iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	nampikaṇ, naṅkikaṇ, aracarkaṇ;
			tampikaṇ, tantikaṇ, tāykaṇ - eṉa varum.
uyartiṇippeyar viravuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉ kuvvurupu mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	nampikku, naṅkikku, aracarkku;
			tampikku, tantikku, tāykku - eṉa varum.
iṅṅaṉam kaṇurupu varuvaḻi iyalpākum nilimoḻi īṟukaḷ i, ai, y, r - eṉpaṉa.
vēṟku, vēṟkaṇ; vāṭku, vāṭkaṇ - eṉa lakara ḷakara īṟukaḷtirintaṉa.	(to. e. 144 nac. uri)
‘ovvoṉṟu koṭu’: taḻāt toṭar -
‘ovvoṉṟu koṭu’ eṉṟāl, immuṟiyē palavaṟṟiyum koṭu eṉap poruḷtantu niṟṟaliṉ, ‘ovvoṉṟu’ eṉpatu aṭukkaṉṟu; vēṟṟumi allaṉa ellām alvaḻi ātaliṉ, taḻāttoṭarāy alvaḻiyuḷ oṉṟām eṉpatu. 	(naṉ. 199 caṅkara.)
oḻintataṉ nili -
oḻiyici ōkārattiṉ nili. ōkāra iṭiccol cuṭṭum pala poruḷkaḷil oḻiyicip poruḷum oṉṟu. ēṉiya ōkāraṅkaḷi aṭutta nilimoḻiyīṟukaḷ varumoḻi vaṉ kaṇattōṭu iyalpākap puṇarvatu pōlavē, oḻiyici ōkāramum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	koḷalō koṇṭāṉ . (koṇṭu uyyakkoṇ ṭāṉ allaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ.); celalō ceṉṟāṉ (ceṉṟu uyyac ceṉṟāṉ allaṉ.); taralō tantāṉ (tantu uyyat tantāṉ allaṉ); pōtalō pōntāṉ (pōntu  uyyap pōntāṉ allaṉ) - ivi iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.	(to. e. 291 nac. uri)
oṟṟaḷapeṭi -
