ṅñ ṇ n m ṉ v y l ḷ ḵ - eṉṉum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum kuṟiṟkīḻum kuṟiliṇikkīḻum aḷapeṭukkum, eṉṉi?
‘vaṉmiyoṭu raḵkāṉ ḻaḵkāṉ oḻittāṅku
almey āytamōṭu aḷapeḻum orōvaḻi’
eṉpa vākaliṉ.
	varalāṟu : maṅṅkalam, maññcu, maṇṇṇu, panntu, ammpu, miṉṉṉu, tevvvar, meyyyar, vellka, koḷḷka, eḵḵku - ivi kuṟiṟkīḻ aḷa peḻuntaṉa.
araṅṅkam, muraññcu, muraṇṇṭu, marunntu, arummpu, muraṉṉṟu, kuravvvi, arayyyar, kurallkaḷ, tiraḷḷkaḷ, varaḵḵku - ivi kuṟiliṇikkīḻ aḷapeḻuntaṉa.	(nēmi. eḻut. 3 uri)
ceyyuṭkaṇ ōciyi niṟippataṟku ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa va ya la ḷa - eṉṟa pattu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum āytamum, kuṟilkīḻum kuṟiliṇikkīḻum, moḻi yiṭiyiṉum iṟutiyilum tam māttiriyiṉ mikku orumāttiriyāka olippatu oṟṟaḷa peṭiyām. av voṟṟu aḷapeṭuttami tōṉṟa atuvē aṭuttu varivaṭivil aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭum.
	e-ṭu : 
‘naṅṅ kaḷaṅkaṟuppām nām’
‘ilaṅṅku veṇpiṟicū ṭīcaṉ aṭiyārkku’
‘veḵḵku vārkkilli vīṭu’
‘ilaḵḵku muttiṉ iṉam’
patiṉoru puḷḷiyeḻuttukkaḷum kuṟilkīḻum kuṟiliṇak kīḻum colliṉ iṭiyum iṟutiyumākiya nāṉkiṭattum aḷapeṭuppavē, varum oṟṟaḷapeṭi 44 ākum. āytam moḻiyiṭik kaṇaṉṟi vārāmiyiṉ, iṟutikkaṇ iraṇṭu nilikaḷiyum ataṟku nīkka oṟṟaḷapeṭi 42ām.	(naṉ. 92)
oṟṟaḷapeṭi cārpeḻuttātal  -
oru māttiriyāy alaku peṟutalāṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum oṟṟaḷapeṭi cārpeḻuttu eṉa, oṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.			(i.vi. 20 uri)
oṟṟaḷapeṭi tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟappaṭāmi -
oṟṟaḷapeṭi ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ, eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟappaṭāmal, poruḷatikārattuc ceyyuḷiyalil kūṟap paṭṭatu. (āyiṉ ‘oṟṟici nīṭal’ (e.33 nac.) eṉa eḻuttati kārattil atu cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu)	(to. e. 6 nac. uri)
oṟṟu ici nīṭal -
meyyeḻuttukkaḷ tamakkuriya arimāttiriyiṉ nīṇ ṭolittal. oṟṟukkaḷ icinūliṉpaṭi patiṉoru māttiriyaḷavu nīṇṭicikkum eṉpa. oṟṟukkaḷ nīṇ ṭolikkumiṭattu oṟṟaḷapeṭi eṉṟu peyar peṟum. ōr oṟṟu oli nīḷumpōtu aṭuttu eḻutappaṭum atē oṟṟu oṟṟaḷapeṭi eḻuttākum.	(to. e. 33 nac.)
oṟṟutalum varuṭutalum -
oṟṟutal taṭṭutal eṉavum aḻuttit taṭavutal eṉavum poruḷ peṟum. varuṭutal mellat taṭavutal eṉṟu poruḷ peṟum.
nānuṉi mēlnōkkic ceṉṟu mēlvāyi oṟṟutalāl ṟa ṉa -vum, nānuṉi mēlnōkkic ceṉṟu mēlvāyi varuṭuta lāl ra ḻa - vum piṟakkum. eṉavē, varuṭutalāl iṭinikarāṉa oliyum, oṟṟutalāl aḻuttamāṉa oliyum piṟakkum eṉpatu. (ṟa ṉa ra ḻa: meyyeḻuttik kuṟippaṉa.)	(to. e. 94, 95 nac.)
oṉ cāriyi -
ōkāra īṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ orōvaḻi oṉcāriyi peṟum. oṉcāriyi piṟkālattu ‘ṉ’ cāriyi āyiṟṟu.
	e-ṭu :	kō+ oṉ + ki = kōoṉki -	(to.e.294 nac.)
		kō+ oṉ + ai = kōoṉi	(180 nac.)
oṉpatu ‘nūṟu’ eṉpataṉoṭu puṇartal  -
oṉpatu eṉpataṉ okarattoṭu takaram cēra, (‘patu’ keṭa) ṉakaram ḷakaramāki iraṭṭa, varumoḻi nūṟu ‘āyiram’ ākat tiriyat toḷḷāyiram- eṉa muṭiyum.
oṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṉ+ nūṟu ḻū
toḷḷ + nūṟu ḻū toḷḷ +āyiram =  toḷḷāyiram
(to. e. 463 nac.)
oṉpatu ‘paḵtu’ eṉpataṉoṭu puṇartal  -
nilimoḻi oṉpatu;varumoḻi paḵtu. nilimoḻi okarattoṭu takaroṟṟuc cēra (nilimoḻi ‘patu’ keṭa), ṉakaram ṇakaramāki iraṭṭa, varumoḻiyiṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramum keṭa, ūkāram varat tukaram ṟukaramākat tiriya, nilimoḻi ṇakarattiṉmēl ūkāram ēṟi muṭiyat toṇṇūṟu ākum.
oṉpatu+ paḵtu ḻū toṉpatu+ paḵtu ḻū toṉ+ paḵtu ḻū toṇṇ + paḵtu ḻū toṇṇ+ ūṟu= toṇṇūṟu. 	(to. e. 445. nac.)
‘oṉpatum iṉittē’ -
oṉpatu eṉpataṉ īṟṟuyirmeyyik keṭātu kuṟṟukara īṟṟup potuvitiyāṉ uyir oṉṟumē keṭa, iṉṉum iṟṟum ēṟpatu ēṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ ‘oṉpatum iṟṟē’ eṉat taṉmiyaṇi ākkātu, uvamiyaṇi ākki ‘iṉittē’ eṉṟār. oṉpatu eṉpataṉ iṟuti pattu eṉpatu pōla muraṇi niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉōṭu itu māṭṭeṟiyap paṭṭatu.
	varumāṟu : oṉpatu+ āyiram ḻū oṉpat +iṉ + āyiram = oṉpatiṉāyiram
		oṉpatu + oṉṟu ḻū oṉpat + iṟṟu + oṉṟu = oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu	(naṉ. 197 caṅkara.)
oṉpatuvaki mayakkam -
uyirōṭu uyir, uyiroṭu mey, uyirōṭu uyirmey, meyyoṭu mey, meyyōṭu uyir, meyyōṭu uyirmey, uyirmeyyōṭu uyirmey, uyirmeyyōṭu uyir, uyir meyyoṭu mey - eṉpaṉa oṉpatu vaki mayakkaṅkaḷām. ivaṟṟuḷ mey, aṭuttuvarum taṉimeyyuṭaṉō, aṭuttu varum uyirmeyyiluḷḷa meyyuṭaṉō mayaṅkum mayakkamē ciṟantamiyiṉ, ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷāl atuvē viḷakkap paṭṭatu.
 e-ṭu:	pārttār - taṉimeyyoṭu taṉimey; yāttār - taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey	(to.e.22 nac. uri)
oṉpatu, varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumāṟu -
oṉpatu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻikaḷoṭu puṇarum. iṉcāriyi ‘iṟṟu’ eṉat tirintu puṇartalumuṇṭu.
oṉpatiṉ kalam, oṉpatiṟṟuk kalam, oṉpatiṉ cāṭi, oṉpatiṟṟuc cāṭi mutalāka aḷavuppeyarum, oṉpatiṉ kaḻañcu, oṉpatiṟṟuk kaḻañcu, oṉpatiṉ toṭi, oṉpatiṟṟut toṭi - mutalāka niṟippeyarum, oṉpatiṉāyiram, oṉ patiṟṟukkōṭi - mutalāka eṇṇuppeyarum eṉa ivaṟ ṟoṭu nilimoḻi ‘oṉpatu’ puṇarntavāṟu.	(to. e. 459, 470 nac.)
oṉpatiṉāḻi eṉpuḻi, varumoḻi nakaram varuvaḻi, iṉcāriyiyiṉ ṉakaram keṭa, varumoḻi nakaram ṉakaramākat tirintu puṇarum. 	(459 nac.)
oṉpatu + pattu = toṇṇūṟu, oṉpatu + nūṟu = toḷ ḷāyiram; oṉpatu + āyiram = oṉpatiṉāyiram, oṉpatu + kaḻañcu, kalam = oṉpatiṉkaḻañcu, oṉpatiṉ kalam; oṉpatu + oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, kuruṇi = oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu, oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭu, oṉpatiṟṟuk kuruṇi - eṉa varum.
oṉpatu+ pattu : varumoḻi pattu ‘nūṟu’ām;okarattoṭu takaramey poruntum; nilimoḻiyil ‘patu’ keṭum; ṉakaram ṇakaramākat tiriyum.
oṉpatu + pattu ḻū oṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṇ + nūṟu = toṇṇūṟu.
oṉpatu +  nūṟu : varumoḻi nūṟu ‘āyiram’ ām. okarattoṭu takara meyporuntum; nilimoḻiyil ‘patu’ keṭum : ṉakaram ḷakaramākat tiriyum. oṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū oṉpatu + āyiram ḻū toṉpatu + āyiram ḻū toṉ + āyiram ḻū toḷ + āyiram = toḷḷāyiram.
oṉpatu, eṇ niṟi aḷavuppeyarkaḷum piṟapeyarum varu moḻiyāka nikaḻumiṭattu, iṉ iṟṟu - eṉṟa cāriyiyuḷ ēṟpatoṉṟu iṭiyē varap puṇarum. eṭuttukkāṭṭu mēlē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇka.	(naṉ. 194, 197)
oṉpatu vikāramum tiripumūṉṟil aṭaṅkutal -
virittal tōṉṟalākavum, valittalum melittalum nīṭṭa lum kuṟukkalum tiripākavum, tokuttalum mūviṭattuk kuṟitalum keṭutalākavum aṭakki oṉpatu ceyyuḷ vikāramum tiripu mūṉṟeṉa amiyum eṉpatu.	(i.vi. 58)
ceyyuḷiṭattu alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyāl varum mūṉṟu vikāramum valittal mutaliya oṉpatu vikāramum varutalāl vēṟupāṭu aṟivataṟkum, virittal tōṉṟal vikāramākavum, valittalum melittalum nīṭṭalum kuṟukkalum tiritalāka vum, tokuttalum mūvaḻikkuṟitalum keṭutalākavum, inta mūvakiyuḷ avvoṉpatu vaki vikāraṅkaḷum aṭaṅkum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟkum, ceyyuṭkēyuriya oṉpatu vikāraṅ kaḷiyum puṇarcci vikāraṅkaḷoṭu kūṟiṉār.	(naṉ. 156 irāmā.)
oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu paṟṟutal  -
itu vaṭamoḻiyil itarētarācrayam eṉṉum kuṟṟamām; anyōnyācrayam eṉavum peyar peṟum. itaṉit taṭumāṟṟam eṉavum kūṟupa.
iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum cārntu  ukaram kuṟukum eṉavum, iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kuṟṟiyalukarattukkuc cārpāka varum eṉavum kūṟutal oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu paṟṟutalām.	(cū. vi. pak. 50)
viṉimuṟṟuc col peyarāyavāṟṟāl ōci vēṟupaṭum eṉavum, ōci vēṟupaṭṭa kāraṇattāṉ viṉimuṟṟuc col peyarām eṉavum kūṟutalum ‘oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṟum kuṟṟamām.	(cū. vi. pak. 54)
kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭāl nāku+ yātu = nākiyātu - eṉṟu puṇarcciviti kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam ceyki ceyta piṉṉar avveḻuttik kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ‘oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṉum kuṟṟamām eṉiṉ, ākātu. nāku+yātu=nākiyātu - eṉṟu puṇarntavaḻi, appuṇarcci vaḻu eṉṟu kāṇaluṟṟuḻi, ‘yakaram varuvaḻi ikaram kuṟukum’ eṉṉumcūttiram vaḻānili uṇartta vantatallātu, muṉ illāta kuṟṟiyalikaratti vitikka vantataṉṟu ātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam kōṭaṟkaṇ ‘oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṟa kuṟṟamilli.	(cū. vi. pak. 20, 21)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ nūṟu eṉṟa nilimoḻiyoṭu 
puṇartal  -
nūṟu eṉpatu nūṟṟoṉṟu nūṟṟiraṇṭu, nūṟṟumūṉṟu, nūṟ ṟeṭṭu, nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟut toṇṇūṟu, nūṟṟukkuṟi, nūṟṟitaḻt tāmari, nūṟṟukkāṇam, nūṟṟuk kāl maṇṭapam- eṉa iṉoṟṟu mikku, vaṉkaṇamāyiṉ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.	(to. e. 472. nac.)
nūṟṟorupaḵtu, nūṟṟirupaḵtu, nūṟṟeṇpatu - eṉa āṇṭum īṟṟiṉ iṉoṟṟu iṭiyē mikkup puṇarum. nūṟṟukkalam, nūṟṟukkaḻañcu - mutalāyiṉavum aṉṉa.	(473, 474 nac.)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ nūṟu eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartal  -
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷiṉ (ēḻ nīṅkalāka uḷḷavi) īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum keṭṭu, mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, oṉṟu iraṇṭu - eṉpaṉa oru iru - eṉṟāki, mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu nakaramāka, nāṉkum aintum oṟṟunili tiriyāvākap puṇarum.
	varumāṟu : oru nūṟu, iru nūṟu, munnūṟu, nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu.	(to. e. 460- 462 nac.)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ paḵtoṭu puṇarntu urupēṟṟal -
oṉṟu +paḵtu+ āṉ + ai
oṉṟu eṉpataṉoṭu pattu eṉpatu puṇarntu orupaḵtu eṉṟākum. ivvāṟē irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu (eḻupaḵtu) eṇpaḵtu - eṉpaṉavum ām.
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa urupukaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, paḵtu eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya keṭa, āṉcāriyi peṟṟu orup+āṉ= orupāṉ- eṉṟāki, orupāṉi - orupāṉāl - orupāṟku- orupāṉiṉ - orupāṉatu - orupāṉkaṇ- eṉa urupēṟkum. iṅṅaṉamē irupāṉi, irupāṉāl.... mutalāyiṉavum koḷka.	(to. e. 199 nac.)

oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ muṉ ‘pattu’ puṇartal  -
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ nilimoḻiyāka niṟka, varumoḻiyākap pattu eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar varumiṭattu, varumoḻi iṭiyoṟṟu nīṅkip ‘patu’ eṉavum aḵtu āytamākat tiriyap ‘paḵtu’ eṉavum amintu puṇarum.
	varumāṟu :	oṉṟu + pattu = orupatu,  orupaḵtu
		eṭṭu  + pattu =  eṇpatu, eṇpaḵtu	(naṉ.195)
nilimoḻi tirital ‘eṇṇuppeyarkaḷukkuc ciṟappuviti’  eṉpataṉkaṇ kāṇka.
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷōṭu āyiram puṇarumāṟu -
orāyiram, ōrāyiram, irāyiram, īrāyiram : oru, iru - eṉpaṉa ukaram keṭṭum mutaleḻuttu uḷḷapaṭiyum nīṇṭum puṇarum.		(to. e. 464, 465 nac.)
mūvāyiram, muvvāyiram - mūṉṟaṉ ṉakaram vakaramāka, mutaleḻuttu uḷḷapaṭiyum kuṟukiyum puṇarum.	(466 nac.)
nālāyiram - nāṉkaṉ ṉakaram lakara oṟṟu ākum.	(467 nac.)
aiyāyiram - aintaṉ nakaram yakaramākum.	(468 nac.)
āṟāyiram, aṟāyiram - ukaram, varumoḻi ā ēṟi muṭintum, mutal ākāram kuṟukiyavaḻik keṭṭum puṇarum.	(469 nac.)
ēḻāyiram, eḻāyiram - ‘ēḻ’ iyalpākiyum neṭil kuṟuki yum puṇarum.	(392, 391 nac.)
eṇṇāyiram - eṭṭu ‘eṇ’ āki ṇakaroṟṟu iraṭṭip puṇarum.	(444, 160 nac.)

oṉṟu mutalāṉa eṇkaḷ iraṭṭumāṟu -
oṉṟu mutal pattu īṟāṉa eṇkaḷuḷ oṉpatu oṉṟum nīṅkalāṉa oṉpatu eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum iraṭṭumāyiṉ (nilimoḻi eṇṇuppeyarē varumoḻiyākavum nikaḻumāyiṉ), nilimoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu nīṅkalākap piṟavellām keṭum. varumoḻi mutalil uyir varumāyiṉ vakarameyyum, meyvarumāyiṉ avvanta meyyum iṭiyē mikappeṟum. (nilimoḻi neṭil mutal kuṟukum eṉka.)
	varumāṟu : oṉṟu+ oṉṟu ḻū o + oṉṟu  ḻū o + v + oṉṟu = ovvoṉṟu; mūṉṟu + mūṉṟu  ḻū mu + mūṉṟu ḻū mu+m+mūṉṟu = mummūṉṟu. piṟavum ivvāṟē ivviraṇṭu, nannāṉku, aivintu, avvāṟu, ev vēḻu, evveṭṭu, pappattu - eṉa muṭiyu māṟu koḷka.	(naṉ. 199)
oṉṟu muṉṉar uyirum yāvum vantu puṇarumāṟu -
varumoḻi mutalil uyirō, yā eṉṟa uyirmeyyō varu miṭattu, nilimoḻiyākiya ‘oṉṟu’eṉpatu ‘oru’ eṉṟu tirinta niliyi nīkki ‘ōr’ eṉṟāki,
ōrakal, ōrāṭu, ōrili, ōrī, ōrural, ōrūcal, ōrerutu, ōrēṇi, ōriyam, ōroḻuṅku, ōrōṭam, ōruviyam, ōryāṉi - eṉavarum. īraṭi, īryāṉi, mūvaci, mūyāṉi - eṉac ceyyuṭkaṇ varutalum koḷḷappaṭum.
(varumoḻi aḵṟiṇiyāyavaḻiyē immuṭipu koḷka.)
oṉṟu mutaliya eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇiyām. eṇṇiyaṟ peyarē uyartiṇiyām. (to. e. 479 nac. uri)


ō
ō -
itu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil patiṉoṉṟām uyir; aṅkāttalōṭu itaḻ kuvittu eḻuppum uyirolikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; peyarākavum viṉiyākavum varum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyām neṭil. ōkāra iṭiccol pirinili, viṉā, etirmaṟi, oḻiyici, terinili, ciṟappu - mutaliya pala poruḷil varum. 

ōo periyaṉ - eṉa uyarvuciṟappup paṟṟiyum, ō o koṭiyaṉ- eṉa iḻivuciṟappup paṟṟiyum, ōo tamakkōr uṟuti uṇarārē eṉak kaḻivirakkap poruḷ paṟṟiyum, ō perituvappak kēṭṭēṉ- eṉa makiḻccikkuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ōo koṭitu koṭitu - eṉa viyappuk kuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ō terintatu - eṉa ñāpakakkuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ō makaṉē - eṉa viḷikkuṟippup paṟṟiyum - ōkāram aḷapeṭuttum aḷapeṭātum mutaṟkaṇ varum.
ōkāra iṭiccol puṇarumāṟu -
ōkāra iṭiccol epporuḷil variṉum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpākavē puṇarum.
‘ivaruḷ, yāṉō tēṟēṉ’(kuṟun.21) - pirinili; naṉṟō tītō	 - terinili; ōo koṇṭāṉ - ciṟappu; ‘kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷiya mā eṉkō’ - eṇ (puṟa.387); yāṉō koṇṭēṉ - māṟukōḷ eccam; nīyō koṇṭāy? - viṉā; puṟṟō putalō - aiyam; koḷalō koṇṭāṉ - oḻiyici.		(to. e. 290, 291 nac. uri)
ōkārīṟṟu iyalpu puṇarcci  -
ōkāra iṭiccol moḻiyīṟṟil māṟukōḷ eccam, viṉā, aiyam, oḻiyici - eṉṟa poruḷkaḷmēlum, teri nili, eṇ, ciṟappu, īṟṟaci - eṉṟa poruḷkaḷmēlum varum. atu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu : yāṉō koṇṭēṉ.
kō eṉṟa peyar il eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, okaramākiya eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟātu (vakara) uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu iyalpākap puṇarum.
varumāṟu : kō+il ḻū kō+v+il = kōvil
	(to. e. 290, 291,293, nac.) (mu.vī. puṇar. 24)
ōkārīṟṟuc cāriyi -
ōkārīṟṟup peyar urupēṟkumiṭattum poruṭpuṇarccik - kaṇṇum oṉcāriyi peṟum. cāriyi peṟuṅkāl varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikātu.
e-ṭu : kō+ ai ḻū kō + oṉ + ai = kōoṉi 	(to.e.180 nac.)
		kō+ki ḻū kō+ oṉ + ki = kōoṉki (294 nac.)
piṟkālattil oṉcāriyi ṉakarac cāriyiyākavē, kōṉi, kōṉki eṉṟu puṇarva āyiṉa.
ōkārīṟṟuc col illoṭu kiḷattal  -
nilimoḻi ōkāra īṟṟuc collāka, varumoḻi il eṉṟa col variṉ, ōkāra iṟutikkuriya oṉcāriyi peṟātu uṭampaṭumey aṭuttu iyalpākap puṇarum.
varumāṟu: kō+ il= kōvil (vakaram uṭampaṭumey) (to.e. 293 nac.)
‘kōmā muṉvariṉ yakaramum kutikkum’ (mu.vī. puṇariyal 24) eṉpataṉāl yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu, kō + il = kōyil - eṉṟātalē ikkālattup perumpāṉmi āyiṟṟu.
ōkārīṟṟup potuppuṇarcci -
ōkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.	
e-ṭu  ōk kaṭitu, cōk kaṭitu (to. e.289 nac.)
ōkāra iṭiccol epporuḷil variṉum, vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākavē puṇarum.
e-ṭu : yāṉō koṇṭēṉ (290, 291 nac.)
ōkārīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyākiya okaramum valleḻuttum peṟutalum, ṭu :	ōok kaṭumi, kōok kaṭumi, cōok kaṭumi;
	kōoṉki, kōoṉcevi	urupupuṇarcci pōl oṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
	e-  (292, 294 nac.)
ōkārīṟu oṉcāriyi peṟutal -
‘ōkāra īṟṟup potuppuṇarcci’ kāṇka.
ōci iraṇṭāvaṉa -
eṭuttuk kūṟalum, paṭuttuk kūṟalum eṉa ōci iruvakip paṭum.	(mu.vī. eḻut. 39)
ōr aḷapu ākum aikāramum oḷakāramum -
moḻi iṭiyilum kaṭiyilum ai, ay - eṉpaṉa pōla olikkum aikāram orē māttiri aḷaviṟṟātalum aruki nikaḻvatu.
cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil (933) ‘iviyiṉ, kaviñar, cuviyiṉ, aviyiṉ’ eṉa aṭiyetukiyil aikāram ikaram pōla oru māttiri aḷavu olippatikkuṟippatām. āyiṉ, moḻi mutaṟkaṇ aikāram curuṅkātu.
piṟkālattil, moḻi mutal aikāram ‘ay’ pōlavum, oḷakāram ‘av’ pōlavum olittaliṉ, vīracōḻiyamum nēminātamum ai - ay eṉavum, oḷa - av eṉavum olikkum eṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl anta ai, oḷa - oṉṟari māttiri olikkum. avi avvāṟu olittal moḻimutaṟkaṇṇām.
‘kuvi, vevvēl, avvēlē’; ‘koytaki, ceyyacan,  kitaru, peytoḷi’ - eṉṟa aṭi mutaṟcīrkaḷi nōkki, oḷa- av eṉavum, ai- ay eṉavum olippatik kāṇalām.		(pu. ve. mā. 4 : 23; cīvaka. 1267)
tolkāppiyaṉārkku oḷakārakkuṟukkam uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. 		(e. ā. pak. 63, 64)
ōr aḷapu icittal  -
oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟāka olittal. a i u e o - eṉṟa kuṟṟuyir aintum, ivi meymītu porunta uṇṭākum uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ toṇṇūṟum - ovvoṉ ṟum oru māttiri aḷaviṟṟāka olippatām.	(to.e. 3, 10 nac.)
aikārakkuṟukkam moḻi iṭikaṭikaḷil orumāttiri aḷaviṟku olippatu muṇṭu. kuṟṟukaram puṇarmoḻiyiṭi muṟṟiyalukaramāy orumāttiri aḷaviṟṟākum.	(57, 409 iḷa.)
oḷakārak kuṟukkamum oṟṟaḷapeṭiyum orumāttiri aḷaviṉavāka olikkum eṉpatu naṉṉūl. (98)
ōr mayakka vaki -
makarīṟāṉa aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷuḷ cila ṉakarattōṭu uṟaḻntu varum eṉpatu ōr mayakkavaki uṇarttalām. 
e-ṭu: nilam - nilaṉ.
moḻi mutalilum iṭiyilum niṉṟa akara aikāraṅkaḷ tammil vēṟupāṭiṉṟic cakara ñakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ variṉ okkum eṉpatum atu. e-ṭu : pacal - pical; mañcu - miñcu; mayal - miyal; aracu - aricu; ilañci - iliñci; arayar - ariyar.
aikāram yakaram eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉvaḻiyē varum nakarattuṭaṉ ñakaram uṟaḻum eṉpārumuḷar.
	e-ṭu : ainnūṟu - aiññūṟu;  meyn naṉṟu - meyñ ñaṉṟu 	(naṉ. 121 - 123 mayili.)
ōreḻuttoru moḻi -
ā ī ū ē ai ō ōḷa - eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ ēḻum eḻuttām taṉmiyaṉṟic collām taṉmiyum peṟum āṟṟala ātaliṉ,  ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷām.
ā - pacu; ī - koṭu;  ū- iṟicci; ē- ampu; ō - matakunīr tāṅkum palaki; ai - viyappu; oḷa- avi (oḷakāram uyir meykkaṇallatu vārātu eṉpatē nac. karuttu)
iṉi, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u - cuṭṭiṭic coṟ kaḷākavum, ekaram viṉāiṭiccollākavum, okaram oppiṭutalik kuṟikkum pakutiyākavum variṉum ivi iṭiccoṟkaḷātaliṉ, peyar viṉikaḷāka varum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi otta ciṟappila.	(to.e. 43, 44 nac.)
uyir neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷilum oḷakāratti vilakki ēṉiya āṟaṉiyum koḷvatē tolkāppiyaṉār karuttām. avar oḷakāratti ‘īraḷapu icikkum iṟutiyil uyir’ eṉpar. 	(to.co. 281 cēṉā.)
uyir varukkattil āṟu, makara varukkattil āṟu, ta pa na - varukkaṅkaḷil aivintu, ka va ca varukkaṅkaḷil nannāṉku, yakara varukkattil oṉṟu - ākiya neṭilkaḷ 40, no tu - eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ iraṇṭu - āka nāṟpattiraṇṭum ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷām. ivi ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. ciṟappillātaṉa piṟavumuḷa.
	varumāṟu : ā ī ū ē ai ō; mā mī mū mē mi mō; tā tī tū tē ti; pā pū pē pi pō; nā nī nē ni nō; kā kū ki kō; vā vī vi veḷa; cā cī cē cō;yā; no tu - eṉpaṉa.
uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aintum, kuvvum, ku veḷa pī ci  eṉṉum uyirmeyneṭṭeḻuttukkaḷum ciṟappila. 	(naṉ. 129 irāmā.)
ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷ:
ā - pacu; ū- iṟicci; ē-ampu; ai- viyappu;  ō- matakunīr tāṅkum palaki; mī, mē - mēl; mū- mūttal eṉpataṉ pakuti; mō - mōttal eṉpataṉ pakuti; tū - paṟṟukkōṭu; tē- teyvam, pā- pāṭṭu; pē- nuri; nē - aṉpu; ni- varuntu; nō- nōy; kū-pūmi; kō- aracu; vī- malar; vi- kūrmi; veḷa- kikkoḷ; cī- nīkku; cē-erutu; cō- araṇ; yā- yāvi; no-tuṉpappaṭu; tu-uṇ. iviyāvum tamakkut tāmē mutalum iṟutiyum āvaṉa.	(naṉ. caṅ. 129)
tolkāppiyaṉār uyirmeyyi viṭuttu neṭil ēḻumē ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷ eṉṟum, kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyāka niṟintu nillā eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṉavē, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u - cuṭṭākavum e viṉāvākavum varum eṉṟa karuttuppaṭavum uyir varukkattiṟkē ōreḻut torumoḻi kūṟiyuḷḷār.	( to. e.  43, 44 nac.)
iṉi, ciṟappillaṉavum ōreḻuttorumoḻiyuḷ cila uḷavāyiṉa. avi āṟām uyirum, pakara īkāramum, ci cū ci ku veḷa- eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavum koḷka.	(naṉ. 128 mayili.)
vakarīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarp poruḷi oppumiyāṉ uṇartti niṟṟalāṉ oḷa eṉṉum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum, cuṭṭu - viṉā- uvamip poruḷit tarum iṭiccol - ātalāṉ kuṟṟuyir aintāṉ ākiya ōreḻuttorumoḻi kaḷum,  ku- eṉṉum uyirmeyyāṉāya ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum, ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum ciṟappillaṉa eṉak koḷka.	(naṉ. 129 caṅkara.)
oḷa
oḷa -
itu neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉuyirvariciyil paṉṉiraṇṭāva tākiya iṟuti yeḻuttām. iḵtu aṅkāttalōṭu itaḻ kuvitalāl  piṟakkum ainteḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iḵtu aḷapeṭuppuḻi ukaram iciniṟikka varum. iḵtu aḷa peṭukkuṅ kālattum aḷapeṭi iṉṟi varuṅkālattum poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaluṇṭu eṉat tolkāppiyam kūṟum. ‘au’ eṉpatu oḷakārattiṉ pōlivaṭivām; ‘av’ eṉpatum atu. oḷakāram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukum eṉpar cilar. ‘auv’ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭam oḷakāram eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar (cū. vi. pak. 25). uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ taṉittu iṟutiyil vārā eḻuttu iḵtu.
oḷakārīṟṟup puṇarcci  -
oḷakārīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukarappēṟum val leḻuttum, meṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam variṉum ukarap pēṟumeytip puṇarum. ciṟupāṉmi  urupu puṇarccikku vakutta iṉcāriyi peṟṟum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcci nikaḻum.
e-ṭu : kuvuk kaṭitu, kuvuc ciṟitu, kuvut tītu, kuvup peritu; kuvu ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu; kuvu yātu, valitu, - eṉa al vaḻikkaṇṇum; kuvukkaṭumi, kuvucciṟumi, kuvuttīmi, kuvupperumi; kuvuñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci; kuvuyāppu, valimi; - eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ukaram peṟṟum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttum uṭaṉpeṟṟum puṇarnta vāṟu. (uyirkkaṇam variṉ ukaram peṟātu uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟē muṭital kuvaḻakitu, kuvaḻaku - eṉa iruvaḻiyum koḷka.)
iṉi, vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci iṉcāriyi peṟṟu, kuviṉ kaṭumi, kuviṉ ñāṟci, kuviṉ valimi, kuviṉarumi- eṉa muṭintavāṟum kāṇka.	(to. e. 295 nac.)
oḷakārīṟu urupoṭu puṇartal  -
oḷakārīṟṟuc col iṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟkum. 
e-ṭu  	kuviṉi, kuviṉoṭu, kuviṟku.... kuviṉ kaṇ	(to. e. 173 nac.)
oḷakārīṟu muṉṉiliviṉikkaṇ vārāmi -
oḷakārīṟu muṉṉili orumi ēvaṟkaṇ vārātu. aḵtu avvāṟu varuvatāyiṉ ukaraccāriyi peṟṟē varum; vaṉkaṇam variṉ uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : kuvu koṟṟā, veḷavu cāttā, veḷavu tēvā, veḷavu pūtā; kuvuk koṟṟā, veḷavuc cāttā....	(to. e. 152 nac.)
oḷakārak kuṟukkam -
oḷakārak kuṟukkam varumāṟu: oḷavi, puvam - eṉa mutaṟkaṇ oḷakāram kuṟukiṟṟu. iṭiyum īṟum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. 
‘mummi iṭattum aioḷavum kuṟukum’ eṉa eṅkum oṭṭik koḷka. (aikāram moḻi mūviṭattum kuṟukumāṟu pōla, oḷakāramum moḻi mūviṭattum vantu kuṟukum eṉpatu nēmināta āciriyar karuttu. oḷakāram moḻi iṭikaṭi kaḷil vārāmiyiṉ avviṭaṅkaḷil oḷakārak kuṟukkam iṉṟu eṉpatu teḷivu.)	(nēmi. eḻut. 4 uri)
‘aikāra oḷakārak kuṟukkaṅkaḷ’ kāṇka.


oḷakāram aḷapeṭuttum aḷapeṭātum varutal  -
oḷoḷa iṉic cālum - māṟupāṭu; oḷu iṉi vekuḷal - pōtumāṉatu eṉṟa kuṟippu; oḷu oruvaṉ iravalarkku īttavāṟu - viyappu (co. 281 cēṉā.)
ku - ‘niṉakkuk karuttāyiṉ kikkoḷ’ eṉṟa aiyap poruḷ; kuu - ‘kikkoṇṭēviṭu’ eṉṟa tuṇivup poruḷ; veḷa - ‘niṉakkuk karuttāyiṉ kippaṟṟu’  eṉṟa  aiyapporuḷ; veḷu - ‘kippaṟṟiyēviṭu’ eṉṟa tuṇivup poruḷ.	(co. 283 nac.)
ka
kaṭappāṭu aṟital  -
kaṭappāṭu - toṉṟutoṭṭu varum marapu. kaṭappāṭu aṟital - toṉṟutoṭṭu varum neṟimuṟiyi aṟital.	(to. e. 37 nac.)
kaṭikkuṟi -
oru pakāppatam īṟṟil kuṟintuniṉṟu kuṟiyāta colliṉ poruḷ tantu ceyyuḷil payaṉpaṭuvatu kaṭikkuṟi eṉṉum vikāramām.
	e-ṭu : ‘nīluṇ tukiliki kaṭuppa’ (nīlam eṉpataṉ kaṭik kuṟi ‘nīl’ eṉpatu)	(naṉ. 156)
kaṇkāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa  -
kaṇ, kāl, puṟam - mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum vēṟṟumiyurupāyum niṟkum. urupāyavaḻi urupiṉ ceykiyum peyarāyavaḻip peyariṉ ceykiyum koṭukka.	(to. e. 202 nac. uri)
kaṇṇimi aḷavu -
iyalpāka orumuṟi iru kaṇkaḷum imittaṟku nikaḻum kāla aḷavu. iḵtu eḻuttoli aḷavu kāṇpataṟku oru māttiri eṉṉum aḷaviṉik kuṟippatām.	(to. e. 7 nac.)
kam: puṇarumāṟu -
kam eṉpatu kammiyaratu toḻil.  ittoḻili uṇarttum kam eṉṟa col iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar pōla, vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukaramum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : kammuk kaṭitu, kammu māṇṭatu, kammu valitu - al vaḻi; kammukkaṭumi, kammumāṭci, kammuvalimi - vēṟṟumi; yakaram varuvaḻi kam yātu, kam yāppu - eṉa iruvaḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum; uyir varuvaḻi makaram iraṭṭittu kam maṭintatu, kammaṭivu - eṉap puṇarum.	(to.e.328 nac. uri) vēṟṟumikkaṇ peyar varumoḻiyāka nikaḻiṉ, akkuc cāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟuk kammakkuṭam, kammaneruppu, kammaviṟaku - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(329 nac. uri)
kamukan tōṭṭam
tōṭṭattuḷ piṟa maram ceṭikoṭikaḷ uḷavāyiṉum, mikuti paṟṟiya vaḻakkāl kamukantōṭṭam eṉappaṭumāṟu pōla, uyirīṟṟup puṇariyaluḷ puṇariyal paṟṟiya piṟa potuc ceytikaḷ iṭam peṟiṉum, perumpāṉmiyum uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ varumoḻip puṇarcciyē kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, appeyar, mikuti paṟṟiya vaḻakkāl vantatu.	(naṉ. 151 caṅkara.)
kariyavi mutaliya coṟkaḷ urupēṟṟal  -
yāvi eṉṟa viṉāppeyar iṟuti aikāram vakarattoṭum keṭa vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟṟu yāvaṟṟi - yāvaṟṟoṭu - eṉa muṭivatu pōla, aikāra īṟṟup paṇpukoḷ peyarkaḷākiya kariyavi - neṭiyavi - kuṟiyavi - mutaliyaṉavum īṟu mey yoṭum keṭa, vaṟṟuccāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟuk kariya vaṟṟi - neṭiyavaṟṟi - kuṟiyavaṟṟi - eṉṟāṟ pōla urupoṭu puṇarum. ivi karumi - neṭumi - kuṟumi - mutaliya paṇpup peyaraṉṟik kariyavi - neṭiyavi - kuṟiyavi - mutalāṉa paṇpu koḷ peyarāka niṉṟu ‘vi’ keṭṭu ‘vaṟṟup’ peṟṟup puṇarntaṉa. (to.e. 178 nac. uri)
karuviyiṉ nāl vakikaḷ -
akakkaruvi, akappuṟakkaruvi, puṟakkaruvi, puṟappuṟakkaruvi - eṉak karuvi nālvakippaṭum. ivi muṟiyē puṇarccikkuriya nilimoḻi paṟṟi varumvitikaḷik kūṟuvaṉa, puṇarcci yilakkaṇam- tiripu- puṇarccivaki- cāriyi- mutaliyaṉa paṟṟik kūṟuvaṉa, nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāy niṟkum moḻikaḷik kūṟuvaṉa, moḻikaḷ ātaṟkuriya eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum piṟappum kūṟuvaṉa ām.	(to.e. 1 nac. uri)
kaṉ:puṇarumāṟu -
kaṉ eṉpatu kaṉṉāruṭiya toḻiliyum, kaṉṉāṉiyum, cempu eṉṟa ulōkattiṉiyum  kuṟippatākum. aḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukaramum, yakaram variṉ iyalpum, uyirvariṉ ṉakaram iraṭṭutalum peṟṟu muṭiyum. 
	e-ṭu :	kaṉṉuk kaṭitu; kaṉṉu naṉṟu, kaṉṉu valitu; kaṉ yātu; kaṉ ṉaritu.
vēṟṟumipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ alvaḻikkōtiya vitiyum peṟum; ukaraccāriyiyi viṭuttu akkuccāriyi peṟutalu muṇṭu. 
	e-ṭu : kaṉṉukkaṭumi, kaṉṉakkaṭumi, kaṉṉakkuṭam, eṉavum; kaṉṉuñāṟci, kaṉṉañāṟci - eṉavum; kaṉṉuvaṉmi, kaṉṉavaṉmi - eṉavum varum. 	(to. e. 345, 346, nac. uri)
yakaram varuvaḻi iyalpākavum, uyir varuvaḻi ṉakaram iraṭṭittum puṇarum.  e-ṭu : kaṉyāppu, kaṉṉarumi
ciṟupāṉmi alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu akaramum mel leḻuttum, meṉkaṇattu akaramum koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu : kaṉṉaṅ kaṭitu, kaṉṉa ñāṉṟatu, kaṉṉa māṇṭatu
		kaṉṉaṅkaṭumi eṉac ciṟupāṉmi kuṇa vēṟ ṟumikkaṇṇum varum.	(345, 346 nac. uri)
miṉ - piṉ - paṉ- eṉum ṉakarīṟṟuppeyar pōlavē, kaṉ eṉṉum nilimoḻiyum, iruvaḻikkaṇṇum toḻiṟpeyar pōlavē ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum. kaṉ eṉpatu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ, poruṭ peyaroṭu puṇaruṅ kāl akaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : kaṉṉuk kaṭitu, kaṉṉuc ciṟitu kaṉṉut tītu, kaṉṉup peritu; kaṉṉu ñāṉṟatu, kaṉṉu nīṇṭatu, kaṉṉu māṇṭatu; kaṉṉu valitu - alvaḻi
	kaṉṉukkaṭumi, kaṉṉucciṟumi, kaṉṉuttīmi,  kaṉṉupperumi; kaṉṉuñāṟci, kaṉṉunīṭci, kaṉṉu māṭci; kaṉṉuvalimi - vēṟṟumi
	kaṉṉakkuṭam, kaṉṉaccāṭi, kaṉṉattūti, kaṉṉap pāṉi - vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci
iṉi, kuṇappeyaroṭu puṇaruṅkālattum, ciṟupāṉmi akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭitalum, akaramum mel leḻuttum peṟṟu muṭitalum  koḷka. kaṉṉak kaṭumi, kaṉṉaṅkaṭumi - eṉavarum.	(i.vi.eḻut. 127)
kā eṉṉum niṟippeyar puṇarumāṟu -
kā eṉṉum niṟippeyar kuṟi eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, iṭiyē iṉcāriyi peṟum. kāviṉ kuṟi - eṉavarum. iḵtu ummittoki. ciṟupāṉmi kākkuṟi eṉaccāriyi peṟātu valleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu.		(to. e. 169 nac. uri)

kāriyakāla pakkam -
iḵtu atikārattiṉ iruvakiyuḷ oṉṟu. aracaṉuṭiya paṭiyil tāmē ceṉṟu naṭattum taṇṭattalivarip pōla, ōriṭattu  niṉṟa col pala cūttiraṅkaḷoṭum ceṉṟu iyintu taṉ poruḷip payappippatu kāriyakāla pakkamām.
e-ṭu :	‘kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ (to.e.34 nac.) eṉṟa nuṟpāviluḷḷa ‘niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar ‘kuṟṟiya lukaram vallāṟu ūrntē’ (36 nac.) eṉṟa nuṟpāt toṭaroṭu ceṉṟiyintu poruḷ payappippatu pōlvaṉa. ēṉiya vaki yatōttēca pakkamām.	(cū.vi.pak. 17)
kālappeyarp pakupatam -
kārāṉ, kārāḷ, kārār, kāratu, kāraṉa, kārēṉ, kārēm, kārāy, kārīr - eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa, ikkālatti yuṭiyār eṉṉum poruṇmik kālappeyarp pakupata mām. (kār eṉṉum kālappeyar aṭiyāka ivi piṟantavi)	(naṉ. 133. mayili.)
kālam kāṭṭum vikutikaḷ -
ṟu, ṟum - iraṇṭum iṟappum etirvum kāṭṭum.
	e.ṭu : ceṉṟu, ceṉṟum (ceṉṟēṉ, ceṉṟōm); cēṟu, cēṟum (celvēṉ, celvōm).
tu,tum - iraṇṭum avviru kālamum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu : vantu, vantum (vantēṉ, vantōm); varutu, varutum (varuvēṉ, varuvōm)
ṭu, ṭum- iraṇṭumiṟappiṉik kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇṭu, uṇṭum (uṇṭēṉ, uṇṭōm)
ku, kum- iraṇṭum etirviṉik kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇku, uṇkum (uṇpēṉ, uṇpōm)
miṉ, ēvalviṉikaḷiṉ īṟukaḷ, viyaṅkōḷ, i, mār - ivi  etirviṉik kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇmiṉ, uṇ- tiṉ- cel- vā, uṇka, uṇṇuti, uṇmār (vantār)
pavvikuti iṟappum etirvum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇpa
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu nikaḻvum etirvum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	(kutiri) uṇṇum
etirmaṟi ākāravikuti mukkālamum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇṇā	(naṉ. 145)
kiruttu
viṉippakutimēl varum peyarvikuti kiruttu eṉappaṭum. 
	e-ṭu : naṭappatu, celpavaṉ - eṉpavaṟṟiluḷḷa tuvvikuti, aṉvikuti pōlvaṉa. 	(cū. vi. pak. 55)
‘kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirina’ -
eṭuttu ōtappaṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu muṭivaṉa eṉpatu iccoṟṟoṭariṉ poruḷ. avi varumāṟu:
akarīṟṟu uriccol taṭavuttiri, taṭavunili - eṉa varutal.
atavattaṅkaṉi - eṉa akarīṟṟup peyar attup peṟutal.
naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi, kuravam nīṭiya - eṉa ākārīṟṟup peyar akaramākak kuṟuki ammuccāriyi peṟutal.
muḻa, ira, cuṟa- eṉa ākārīṟu kuṟuki ukaram peṟātu varutal.
kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu - eṉa ikarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟutal.
‘tīyiṉ aṉṉa’ - eṉa īkārīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉ peṟutal.
‘tiruvattavar’ - eṉa ukarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟutal.
‘ēp peṟṟa māṉ’- eṉa ēkāram vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekarappēṟiṉṟi varutal.
‘kittu uṇṭām pōḻtu’, ‘kittu illavar’- eṉa aikāra īṟu attup peṟṟu varutal.
puṉṉiyaṅkāṉal, mulliyantoṭiyal - eṉa aikāra īṟu ammup peṟutal.
kōyil - eṉa ōkārīṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟutal.
akam+ cevi = añcevi eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ kakaroṟṟum makaramum keṭutal.
maravam pāvi, marava nākam - eṉa iruvaḻiyum maram eṉpataṉ makaram keṭa ammup peṟutal. (marattāl āṉapāvi, nākamaram)
kāṉ- poṉ- eṉpaṉa, kāṉam - poṉṉantikiri - poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭu - eṉa ṉakarīṟu iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟutal.
‘vetirattu naral ici’ - eṉa rakarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟutal.
‘nāvalan taṇpoḻil’- ‘kāṉalam peruntuṟi’- eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumiyil ammup peṟutal.
neytalañ ciṟupaṟi - eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ lakarīṟu ammup peṟutal.
av + iṭi = āyiṭi - eṉa vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭital.
tev +muṉi = temmuṉi - eṉa vakaram keṭṭu makaram peṟṟu muṭital.
a eṉṉum cuṭṭu ‘aṉṟi’ eṉattirital.
kōṅkiṉ muki, teṅkiṉ paḻam - eṉak kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu ‘iṉ’ peṟutal.	(to. e. 483. nac uri)
āraṅkaṇṇi - eṉa ār eṉṟa rakarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟu muṭintatu.
muḷavu mā, piṇavu nāy - eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ meṉkaṇam vantuḻi, ‘kuṟiyataṉ iṟuticciṉi’ keṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
‘arumaruntaṉṉāṉ’ eṉaṟpālatu, arumaruntāṉ aru mantāṉ - eṉa marūuvāy muṭintatu.
cōṇāṭu - malāṭu - eṉpaṉavum cōḻaṉāṭu - maliya-māṉāṭu -eṉpavaṟṟiṉ  marūumuṭipām. potuvil eṉaṟ pālatu potiyil eṉa muṭintatum atu.	(483 iḷa. uri)
kiḷippeyarkaḷ puṇarumāṟu. -
kiḷippeyarkaḷ, ṇakaram - ṉakaram- kuṟṟiyalukaram- eṉṟa mūṉṟu īṟṟaṉa. kiḷippeyarāvaṉa ōriṉatti uṇarttum peyarkaḷ. uyartiṇiyil ivi tiripiṉṟi varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	umaṇkuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi
		eyiṉkuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi
		purōcukuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi
pārppu + kuḻavi = pārppaṉak kuḻavi - eṉa aṉcāriyiyum akkuc cāriyiyum peṟṟatu. itu pārppiṉuḷ kuḻavi - eṉa vēṟṟumi muṭipiṟṟu.
aracu + akku+ kaṉṉi = aracakkaṉṉi, eṉa akkuccāriyi peṟṟatu.	(to. e. 307, 338, 418 nac.)
eyiṉakkaṉṉi eṉa ṉakarīṟu akkup peṟutalum, veḷḷā ḷaṉ+ kumari = veḷḷāṇkumari - eṉavum vēḷāṇkumari eṉavum muṭital pōlvaṉavum koḷḷappaṭum.(338 nac. uri)
kīḻ:puṇarumāṟu -
kīḻ eṉṟa col varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum mikkum puṇarum; iyalpē valiyuṭittu.
e-ṭu :	kīḻ + kulam = kīḻkulam, kīḻkkulam (kīḻākiya kulam eṉap paṇputtokiyātaliṉ alvaḻippuṇarcci eṉka.) (kiḻakku eṉṉum ticippeyariṉ tiripākiya kīḻ vēṟu: kīḻmippaṇpiṉik kuṟikkum innili- moḻi vēṟu.)	(naṉ. 226)
kīḻmi eṉṉum paṇpoṭu varumoḻi puṇartal pataviyaluḷ muṭittup pōntamiyiṉ, ‘kīḻiruntum kīḻallār kīḻ allavar’ (kuṟaḷ 973) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kīḻ eṉpatu īṇṭup paṇpākupeyarām. ākavē, kīḻkulam kīḻkkulam, kīḻcāti kīḻccāti - eṉṉum ittoṭarkaḷ irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyām eṉka.	(226 caṅkara.)
kīḻ eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka varumoḻi mutalil vaṉkaṇam varumāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum mikāmiyum eṉṟiru niliyum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :kīḻkkuḷam, kīḻkuḷam; kīḻccēri, kīḻcēri; kīḻt tōṭṭam, kīḻtōṭṭam; kīḻppāṭi, kīḻpāṭi	(to. e. 395 nac.)
neṭumutal kuṟukātu ukaram peṟṟuk kīḻu kuḷam, kīḻucēri, kīḻu - tōṭṭam, kīḻupāṭi - eṉavum varum.	(396 iḷa. uri)

kuṇacanti -
akarākāraṅkaḷ iṟutiyākiya moḻikku muṉṉar ikara īkāraṅkaḷ mutalākiya col variṉ, nilimoḻiyiṟutiyum varumoḻimutalum keṭa, ōr ēkāram varum.
	e-ṭu : nara+intiraṉ = narēntiraṉ; umā+īcaṉ= umēcaṉ
akarākāraṅkaḷ iṟutiyākiya moḻikku muṉṉar ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ mutalākiya col variṉ, nilimoḻiyiṟuti yum varumoḻimutalum keṭa, ōr ōkāram varum.
e-ṭu  	tāma + utaraṉ = tāmōtaraṉ; kaṅkā+ uṟpatti = kaṅkōṟ patti. 	(mu. vī. moḻi. 39, 40)
akarākāraṅkaḷil oṉṟaṉ muṉṉar ikarīkāraṅkaḷil oṉṟu vantāl ēkāramum, avviraṇṭil oṉṟaṉ muṉ ukara ūkāraṅkaḷil oṉṟu vantāl ōkāramummuṟiyē nilippata īṟum varumpata mutalum keṭat tōṉṟutal kuṇacantiyākum.
	e-ṭu : cura+ intiraṉ = .curēntiraṉ, nara + intiraṉ = narēntiraṉ; tarā + intiraṉ = tarēntiraṉ, carva +  īcuraṉ = carvēcuraṉ; umā + īcaṉ = umēcaṉ, cita + intu= citēntu;		amala + uṟpavi = amalōṟpavi, makā + utaram = makōtaram; cutta + utakam = cuttōtakam, ñāṉa + ūrccitaṉ = ñāṉōrccitaṉ; mantira + ūki = mantirōki, tāma + utaraṉ = tāmōtaraṉ; tayā + uṟpatti = tayōṟpatti, tayā + ūrccitaṉ = tayōrccitaṉ.	(to.vi. 38 uri)
kuṇappeyarp pakupatam -
ceyyāṉ, ceyyāḷ, ceyyār, ceyyatu, ceyyaṉa, ceyyēṉ, ceyyēm, ceyyāy, ceyyīr, - eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa ikkuṇattiyuṭiyār eṉṉum poruṇmik kuṇappeyarp pakupatamām. (īṇṭuc cemmi eṉṟa kuṇappaṇpiṉ aṭiyāka ippeyarp pakupataṅkaḷ piṟantaṉa.)	(naṉ. 133 mayili.)
kumiḻ eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
kumiḻ eṉṟa marappeyar,pīr eṉṟa koṭiyiṉ peyar pōla, oruvaḻi melleḻuttum, oruvaḻi ammuccāriyiyum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : kumiḻṅkōṭu, kumiḻaṅkōṭu	(to. e. 386 nac.)
kuyiṉ, ūṉ puṇarcci  -
ivviruvakip peyarum iruvakip puṇarccikkaṇṇum varumoḻi mutal vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. kuyiṉ - mēkam: ūṉ - iṟicci.
	e-ṭu :	kuyiṉ kaṭitu,ciṟitu, tītu, peritu - alvaḻi
		kuyiṉkaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi - vēṟṟumi
		ūṉ kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu peritu - alvaḻi
		ūṉkaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi - vēṟṟumi			(naṉ. 216)
kuyiṉ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
kuyiṉ eṉpatu mēkatti uṇarttum col. atu vēṟṟumik kaṇ vaṉkaṇam varumvaḻiyum tiriyātu iyalpāyp puṇarum
	e-ṭu : kuyiṉkuḻām, celavu, tōṟṟam, paṟivu 											(to. e. 335 nac.)
kuṟṟiyalikaram (1) -
nāku yātu - eṉṟa toṭaril, ku oru māttiri peṟṟa muṟṟiyalukaram. atu keṭa, ataṉiṭattu ikaram vantu oli kuṉṟi arimāttiri aḷaviṟṟāya kuṟṟiyalikaram ākum. ākavē, kuṟṟiyalukaramē kuṟṟiyalikaramākat tiriyum eṉṟu naṉṉūl mutaliyaṉa kūṟum ceyti tolkāppiyaṉār karuttaṉṟu.
kuṟṟiyalikaram orumoḻikkaṇṇum toṭarmoḻik kaṇṇum varum. orumoḻikkaṇ muṉṉili aciccol lākiya miyā eṉpataṉ makaratti ūrntu varum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramām.
kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻikkaṇ iṭiyē varumiṭattu muṟṟiyalukaramām. nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ukaram keṭa, av viṭattu nāku + yātu = nākiyātu eṉak kuṟṟiyalikaram varum.		(to. e. 409, 411 iḷa.)
kuṟṟiyalikaram ceyyuḷil mey pōla alaku peṟātu; mey aḷapeṭuttal pōl aḷapeṭukkātu. kuṟṟiyalikarattukkum muṟṟiyalikarattukkum iṭiyē poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. avvēṟupāṭṭik kaṟpōr eḷitāka uṇaravēṇṭik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ mēl puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭupa.
nāṭi)yātu, kokki)yātu, kuṟumpi)yātu - kuṟṟiyalikaram
nāṭi yātu, kokki yātu, kuṟumpi yātu - muṟṟiyalikaram		(e. ā. pak. 89) (cey. 4 nac. uri)
kuṟṟiyalikaram (2)  -
orumāttiri peṟṟa ikaram taṉ māttiri kuṟintu ariyāka olippatu kuṟṟiyalikaramām. varumoḻi mutalil yakaram vara, nilimoḻi īṟṟil niṟkum kuṟṟiyalukaram ikaramākat tiriyum. av vikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramām. miyā eṉṉum muṉṉili aciccolliṉ makaratti ūrntu niṉṟa ikaramum taṉ māttiriyil kuṟintolikkum kuṟṟiyalikaramām. īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 ātaliṉ av vakiyāl varuvaṉavum, aciccol miyāviṉkaṇ varuvatum eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟṟiyalikaram 37 āmāṟu kāṇka.	(naṉ. 93)
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram cārpeḻuttātal  -
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅkōl ākātu cantaṉak kōlē āmāṟu pōla, uyiriṉ kuṟukkamum uyirēyām eṉiṉum, puṇarcci vēṟṟumiyāṉum, poruḷ vēṟṟumi yāṉum, cīrum taḷiyum citiyumiṭattu alaku peṟāmi yāṉum, kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ cārpeḻuttu eṉa uyiriṉ vēṟāyiṉa.	(i.vi. 16)
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram : peyarkkāraṇam  -
kēṇmiyā, varaku eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ ikaramum ukaramum, ‘pōlum’ eṉpuḻi arimāttiriyāy niṉṟa makaram ‘pōṉm’ eṉa lakaram ṉakaramāyt tirintu īroṟṟuṭaṉ niṉṟa kāraṇattāl piṉṉark kālmāttiriyāy niṉṟāṟpōla, muṉṉar orumāttiriyāy niṉṟu piṉṉar oru kāraṇam paṟṟi arimāttiri ākāmal, makaramum valleḻuttumākiya meyyiṉic cārntu eṉṟum arimāttiriyāyē niṟkum oru taṉmiya āyiṉum, appeṟṟi uṇarātu, ivi ari māttiri peṟutal makarakkuṟukkam pōlac ceyaṟkiyāl pōlum eṉṟu ulakam maliyāmip poruṭṭu, ivi iṅṅaṉam ātal iyalpu eṉpār, kuṟṟikaram kuṟṟukaram eṉṟoḻiyātu, kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu kuṟiyiṭṭār.
‘ikaram kuṟukum’, (to. e. 410) ‘ukaram  kuṟukiṭaṉ’ (e. 406) eṉṟa toṭarkaḷukku, ikaram aṇukum (vantu poruntum), ukaram vantu poruntum iṭaṉ - eṉpaṉavē poruḷ.	(cū. vi. pak. 27)
kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇariyal niliyiṭik kuṟukal  -
kuṟṟiyalikaram coṟkaḷ puṇarntiyalum nilimikkaṇ taṉakkuriya ari māttiriyiṉum kuṟuki olittaṟkum uriyatu; kuṟuki oliyāmiyē perumpāṉmi eṉka.
e-ṭu :	āṭu+yātu= āṭiyātu; kavaṭu + yātu = kavaṭi yātu; toṇṭu+  yātu = toṇṭiyātu - īṇṭuk  kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟuki olittaṉa.
	teḷku + yātu = teḷkiyātu; nāku+ yātu = nākiyātu - īṇṭuk kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟukātu ari māttiriyē olittaṉa.
muṉṉavi kuṟukum eṉṟaṟkum piṉṉavi kuṟukā eṉṟaṟkum kāraṇam varumāṟu: āṭi (tiṅkaḷ), kavaṭi (veḷvaraku), toṇṭi (ūr) eṉṉum (muṟṟiyal) ikara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ yātu eṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, āṭiyātu - kavaṭiyātu - toṇṭiyātu - eṉa varum. ātaliṉ, āṭu+yātu = āṭiyātu. kavaṭu + yātu= kavaṭiyātu, toṇṭu + yātu = toṇṭiyātu - eṉa varum ivvirutiṟattiṟkum vēṟṟumi pulappaṭa vēṇṭi, kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭa āṇṭut tōṉṟum āṭi - kavaṭi - toṇṭi - eṉṉum kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ māttiri ariyiṉum kuṟukik kālāka olikka vēṇṭuva tāyiṟṟu. tiṅkaṭ peyarākiya āṭi mutaliyavaṟṟatu iyalpu ikara īṟṟiṉi oru māttiriyaḷaviṟku icittal vēṇṭum.
iṉit teḷkiyātu - nākiyātu - eṉpavi kuṟukāmikkuk kāraṇam, teḷki - nāki - eṉpaṉa iyaṟpeyarkaḷ ākāmi yāl nilimoḻi teḷku - nāku - eṉa aiyamiṉṟi uṇarap paṭum ātaliṉ, avi ari māttiriyākavē kuṟiyātu olikkum eṉṟavāṟu.	(to.e.35. ca. pāla.)
kuṟṟiyalikaram ‘puṇariyal niliyiṭik kuṟukalum urit’tātal -
kuṟṟiyalikaram taṉimoḻikkaṇṇē yaṉṟi, nilimoḻi varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartal iyaṉṟa nilimiyākiya toṭar moḻikkaṇṇum vantu poruntutal uṭiyatām. iṅṅaṉam varuvatu irumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram ām.
kuṟukutal - aṇukutal, vantu poruntutal.
kuṟṟiyalikaram āytam pōla vēṟeḻuttāvatām; aṉṟi, oru māttiri peṟṟu niṉṟa ikaram, makarakkuṟukkam  pōla, oru kāraṇam paṟṟi arimāttiriyāyk kuṟintu niṉṟataṉṟu.		(cū. vi. pak. 27, 28)

kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliyaṉa tamiḻcciṟappeḻuttu ātal -
iṅku (peruṅkāyam), ētu tātu eṉpaṉa kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu pōla iruppiṉum, avi vaṭacollātaliṉ muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉa alla eṉka. eṉavē, kuṟṟiya lukaram tirintu kuṟṟiyalikaram ātalum vaṭamoḻikkaṇ illi. āytam tamiḻcciṟappeḻuttu eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. ākavē, kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum vaṭamoḻikkaṇ kāṇappaṭāta, tamiḻiṟkē uriya ciṟappeḻut tukkaḷām.	(cū. vi. pak. 28)
kuṟṟiyalikara muṟṟiyalikaram poruḷ vēṟupāṭu -
nilimoḻi kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatāka varumoḻi mutalil yakaram variṉ, ukaram keṭak kuṟṟiyalikaram peṟṟup puṇarum.
iyalpāṉa muṟṟiyalikarac col muṉṉarum yakaram vantu puṇarum.
kokku nāṭu kuṟumpu eṉpaṉa mutalāṉa kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuccoṟkaḷum, kokki nāṭi kuṟumpi eṉpaṉa mutalāṉa muṟṟiyalikara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷum, kokkiyātu - nāṭiyātu -kuṟumpiyātu eṉṟē puṇarum. nilimoḻiyīṟu ki ṭi pi eṉak kuṟṟiyalikaram ākiyavaḻi, nilimoḻikaḷ  kokku nāṭu  kuṟumpu  eṉpaṉa; ki ṭi pi muṟṟiyalikaram ākiyavaḻi nili moḻikaḷ kokki nāṭi kuṟumpi eṉpaṉa. (kuṟumpu pālinilattūr; kuṟumpi - puṟṟam paḻañcōṟu)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcci  -
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷellām perumpālum avvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : nāṭu kaṭitu, varaku kaṭitu, teḷku kaṭitu, eḵku kaṭitu, kuraṅku kaṭitu - eṉa valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi nīṅkalāka ēṉiyavi iyalpākap puṇarum.
valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi kokkuk kaṭitu eṉa varumoḻi mutalil varum valleḻuttu iṭiyil mikkup puṇarum.
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiccollum viṉikkuṟippuc collum iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	kiṭantatu kutiri, karitu kutiri
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu nilimoḻi vaṉkaṇam vara irupeya roṭṭup paṇputtokipaṭap puṇarumiṭattu, iṉoṟṟu mikku valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇam vara avvāṟu puṇarumiṭattu iṉoṟṟu mikutalum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	karaṭu + kāṉam = karaṭṭukkāṉam; kuruṭu + kōḻi = kuruṭṭuk kōḻi; karaṭu + vaḻi = karaṭṭu vaḻi; kuruṭu + maṉitaṉ = kuruṭṭu maṉitaṉ.
ciṟupāṉmi aṉcāriyiyum akkuccāriyiyum peṟutalu muṇṭu. e-ṭu : pārppu + kuḻavi ḻū pārppu + aṉ + akku + kuḻavi = pārppaṉakkuḻavi
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ, melloṟṟu valloṟ ṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum uḷa; melloṟṟut tiriyātu aikāramum valloṟṟum peṟuvaṉavum uḷa.
	e-ṭu : ōryāṇṭu + kuḻavi = ōryāṭṭik kuḻavi	paṇṭu + cāṉṟōr = paṇṭic cāṉṟōr
iyalpukaṇam vantavaḻi, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram māttiram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
	e-ṭu : ōryāṇṭu + yāṉi = ōryāṭṭi yāṉi	aiyāṇṭu + erutu = aiyāṭṭi yerutu
viṉiyeccat toṭarkkaṇ vaṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	cettuk kiṭantāṉ, naṭṭup pōṉāṉ
meṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam iyalpāka varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	iruntu koṇṭāṉ, vantu pōyiṉāṉ
ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭic coṟkaḷākiya āṅku īṅku ūṅku yāṅku yāṇṭu āṇṭu īṇṭu - mutaliyaṉavum, aṅku iṅku uṅku eṅku - mutaliyaṉa vum, meṉtoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷā yiṉum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi mikkup puṇarum.
āṅkucceṉṟāṉ.... yāṅkucceṉṟāṉ.... īṇṭuc ceṉṟāṉ.... aṅkuc ceṉṟāṉ..... mutalākap puṇartal kāṇka.
āṇṭu eṉpatu iṭiccollāyiṉ, āṇṭuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉa valleḻuttu mikum. āḷ eṉṟa pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟanta iṟantakālac ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyecca māyiṉ, āṇṭu koṇṭāṉ eṉa iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(to. e. 425 - 429 nac).
uṇṭu eṉpatu uṇmittaṉmip paṇpiṉi uṇarttiya-vaḻi, nāṟkaṇamum variṉum uṇṭu poruḷ, ñāṉam, yāḻ, āṭi eṉa iyalpāyum, pakaram varumoḻimutalil varuvaḻi,
uṇṭu poruḷ, uḷporuḷ eṉa irumuṭivum peṟṟum puṇarum.
uḷporuḷ eṉa ōci piḷavupaṭātu kūṟiṉ paṇput toki yām; ōci iṭiyaṟavupaṭac colliṉ (‘uṇṭu poruḷ’ eṉpatu poruḷātaliṉ) kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟut toṭarām.	(nac. uri).


kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟup puṇarcciyiṉ ciṟappuviti -
avvaḻippuṇarccikkaṇ, vaṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟiṉ muṉ varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikum; ēṉit toṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟukaḷ iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : pākkuk kaṭitu; nāṭu ciṟitu, eḵku tītu, varaku peritu, vantu pōṉāṉ, eytu poruḷ.
vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ, iṭittoṭar - āytat toṭar - iṭiyil oṟṟu mikap peṟāta neṭil toṭar -iṭiyil oṟṟu mikāta uyirttoṭar - eṉṉum ikkuṟṟiya lukara īṟukaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikā.
	e-ṭu : mārpu kaṭumi, eḵku ciṟumi, nāku tīmi, aracu perumi 
ṭu ṟu - iṟutiyākiya neṭiltoṭarum uyirttoṭarum nili moḻiyāka niṟpa, varumoḻimutal vaṉkaṇam variṉ, nilimoḻi ṭ ṟ eṉpaṉa iṭiyil mika, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; piṟakaṇam variṉum iraṭṭutal koḷka.
	e-ṭu : yāṭṭuk kāl, pāṟṟuc ciṟi; muraṭṭu maṉitaṉ, vayiṟṟiṭi
ippuṇarcci perumpāṉmiyum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇatu. ‘muraṭṭu maṉitaṉ’: alvaḻi muṭipu.
cila meṉtoṭark kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcci yil vaṉtoṭark kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ ākum.
	e-ṭu : maruntu + pi = maruttuppi; kaṉṟu + ā = kaṟṟā
cila meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ puṇarccikkaṇ aikāra īṟṟaṉavām. vaṉtoṭarāyt tirintu īṟṟu aikāram peṟuvaṉavumuḷa.
	e-ṭu : paṇṭu + kālam = paṇṭikkālam; iṉṟu + nāḷ = iṟṟināḷ.
ticippeyarkaḷ īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram, piṟaticippeyarum ēṉiya peyarum varumoḻiyāka nikaḻumiṭattu, tāṉ ēṟiya meyyoṭu keṭa, īṟṟayal kakara oṟṟuk keṭṭum, ṟakaram ṉakaramākavum lakaramākavum tirintum, piṟavāṟum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : vaṭakku + kiḻakku, mēṟku, cēri = vaṭakiḻakku, vaṭa mēṟku, vaṭacēri; teṟku + kiḻakku, mēṟku, cēri = teṉkiḻakku, teṉmēṟku, teṉcēri; mēṟku + kāṟṟu, ūr = mēlkāṟṟu, mēlūr; kiḻakku + kāṟṟu, cēri = kīḻkāṟṟu, kīḻiccēri; mēṟku + cēri = mēliccēri.
teṅku eṉṟa nilimoḻimuṉ ‘kāy’ variṉ, nilimoḻi mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu īṟṟuyirmey keṭṭut tēṅkāy eṉa muṭiyum. (iṭiyē amcāriyi peṟṟut teṅkaṅkāy eṉa muṭitalumuṇṭu).	(naṉ. 181 - 187)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu marappeyarp puṇarcci -
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉavākiya (akakkāḻaṉavākiya) marappeyar kaḷum (puṟakkāḻaṉavākiya) puṟpeyarkaḷum ammuccāriyi peṟṟu vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarum.	e-ṭu	:	tēkku + am + kōṭu, cetiḷ tōl, pū = tēkkaṅ kōṭu, tēkkañ cetiḷ, tēkkantōl, tēkkam pū; kamuku + am + kāy = kamukaṅkāy; cīḻku + am + pul = cīḻkampul; kampu + am + pulam = kampam pulam; payaṟu + am + kāy = payaṟṟaṅkāy.(to. e. 415 nac).
marappeyarkaḷuḷ īṟṟayal eḻuttākiya melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriyāta marappeyarkaḷum, tiriyum marap peyarkaḷum uḷa.
	e-ṭu :	puṉku + am + kōṭu = puṉkaṅkōṭu;kuruntu + am + kōṭu = kuruntaṅkōṭu - melloṟṟu val loṟṟu ākātaṉa.
		vēmpu + am + kōṭu = vēppaṅkōṭu; kaṭampu + am + kāy = kaṭappaṅkāy; īñcu + am + kuli = īccaṅkuli - ivi melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tirintaṉa.	(416 nac.)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ vārāmi  -
muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu vārātu. vārāteṉavē, muṉṉili ēvalorumikkaṇ varum ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu īṟukaḷ muṟṟiyalukaramākavē olikkap paṭum. e-ṭu : muṟukku, paracu, kaṭṭu, kattu, eḻuppu, tīṟṟu 	(to. e. 152 nac. uri)
kuṟṟiyalukara eṇṇikki -
taṉineṭil ēḻuṭaṉē, āytam oṉṟum, moḻi iṭiyīṟu kaḷil varappeṟāta oḷakāram oḻittu oḻinta uyir patiṉoṉṟum, valleḻuttu āṟum, melleḻuttu āṟum, valleḻuttoṭu toṭarāta vakaram oḻittu oḻinta iṭiyeḻuttu aintum ākiya muppattāṟu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ, yātāṉum oṉṟu īṟṟukku ayaleḻuttākat toṭarappaṭṭu, moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ valleḻuttukkaḷuḷ yātāṉum oṉṟu paṟṟukkōṭāka ataṉi ūrntu varum ukaram taṉ ari māttiriyiṉ kuṟukum. (īṟṟayal eḻuttākiya iṭavaki yāṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 āyiṟṟu)	(naṉ. 94 civañā.)
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal -
itu tolkāppiya eḻuttuppaṭalattiṉ oṉpatāvatākiya iṟuti iyal. ukaram kuṟuki varum iṭaṅkaḷ, īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ, toṭarmoḻiyil varum ukaram, palavakik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi - alvaḻi - eṉṟa iru niliyilum nikaḻum puṇarccikaḷ, cuṭṭuppeyar viṉāppeyar niṟippeyar aḷavuppeyar ivaṟṟiṉ puṇarccikaḷ, virivāṉa muṟiyil eṇṇuppeyarp puṇarcci, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalil oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuḷmuṭipukaḷ, puṇariyalniliyiṭip pakuttuk kāṇātu uḷḷapaṭiyē koḷḷattakka toṭarkaḷ, eḻuttatikārap puṟaṉaṭi - eṉpaṉa kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariya luḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.	(to. e. 406 - 483 nac.)
kuṟṟiyalukarap potuppuṇarcci -
nilimoḻiyīṟṟiṉkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟka varumoḻi mutalil uyir varumāyiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ēṟiniṉṟa meyyi viṭuttut tāṉ keṭum; ciṟupāṉmi keṭātu niṉṟu uṭampaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	nāku + aritu ḻū nāk + aritu = nākaritu; aḻippatu + ē ḻū aḻippatu + v + ē = aḻippatuvē (naṉ. 303); ātu + um ḻū ātu + v + um = ātuvum (naṉ. 300)	(naṉ. 164)
kuṟṟiyalukaram (1) -
itu tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. itu taṉakkeṉat taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil piṟappiṭa miṉṟit tāṉ ēṟiniṟkum meyyiṉ piṟappiṭamē taṉakkup piṟappiṭamākak kōṭaliṉ, orē eḻuttākiya kuṟṟiyalukaram piṟappiṭam nōkki āṟākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. itu tamiḻilum maliyāḷattilumē uḷḷatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram ēḻu toṭarkkaṇ varum eṉavum, ataṉāl nāṟpattiraṇṭu ākum eṉavum, āṟu toṭarkkaṇ varum eṉavum, ataṉāl muppattāṟu ākum eṉavum kūṟuvaṉa ēṟpuṭiyaṉa alla. toṭarnōkkik kuṟṟiyalukaratti āṟākak kōṭalē amiyum. aḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻi, uyirttoṭar moḻi, iṭiyoṟṟut toṭarmoḻi, āytattoṭar moḻi, valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi, melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi eṉa eṭuttukkūṟi viti kūṟutaṟkup payaṉpaṭu kiṟatu. moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyuṭiya col kiḷippeyarākiya nunti eṉpatu. im moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟē. itu nunti eṉṟa peyarkkaṇaṉṟi, viṉikkaṇ vārātu. ikkuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟuta liṉṟu.
taṉimoḻi īṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟutaliṉ atu puḷḷiyīṟu ākum. ataṟku māttiri ari.
alvaḻi vēṟṟumip poruṇmikkaṇ varum toṭar- moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻikku iṭiyē varuvaḻi, atu muṟṟiyalukaramām. āyiṉ valloṟṟu iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram varumoḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu varuvaḻit taṉ paḻiya ari māttiri aḷaviṟṟākavē, cekkukkaṇi - cukkukkoṭu mutaliya coṟṟoṭarkaḷil niṟpatumuṇṭu.
valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram val leḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyākiyavaḻik kāl māttiri peṟum eṉavum, ēṉiya alvaḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ arimāttiri peṟum eṉavum kūṟuvatu tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēlātu. paṉmoḻip puṇarcci yākiya ceyyuḷil muṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalukaram iraṇṭaṉik koṇṭum nērpu niripu acikaḷi amittuk koṇ ṭami, kuṟṟiyalukaram toṭarmoḻikkaṇ orumāttiri peṟum iyipi nōkkiyē ām.
peyarccoṟkaḷuḷ arimāttiri peṟum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuccoṟkaḷ accollē ēvalviṉiyāyiṉ avi orumāttiri peṟum muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc collām. perukku - kaṭṭu - kātu - eṉpaṉa peyarāyiṉ, kuṟṟukara īṟāy arimāttiri peṟum īṟṟeḻuttuṭiyaṉavām. kuṟṟiya lukara īṟu ēval viṉikkaṇ vārātu eṉpatu tolkāppi yaṉār karuttām.
kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōlat taṉmītu varumoḻimutal uyir ēṟa iṭam koṭukkum. muṟṟiyalukarīṟu keṭum eṉṟu kūṟum tolkāppiyaṉār ōriṭattum kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭavilli. eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōla arimāttiri koḷvataṉoṭum puḷḷi peṟuvata ṉoṭum amiyātu, meyyīṟṟiṉ ceyki peṟṟup puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatu.
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟil avvukaram keṭa niṉṟa meyyīṟṟiṉ mītu uyir ēṟi muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvatum cālātu. moḻiyiṟuti yil vārāta k c ṭ t p ṟ eṉṟa valliṉameykaḷ āṟaṉiyum īṟṟil koḷḷa uṭaṉ iṇittuk koḷḷappaṭṭa eḻuttē kuṟṟiyalukaram. meyyiṉ tāṉattil vantuḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ niliyum meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ niliyē. kuṟṟiyalukaram meykkuriya arimāttiri aḷavē olitta liṉ, āṇṭu ukaroli ammeyyeḻuttiṉit terivikkum tuṇiyē niṉṟatu. olittal eḷimi karutiyē, ēḻ uṇ tiṉ vav maṇ pal eṇ mutaliya coṟkaḷ ēḻu uṇṇu tiṉṉu vavvu maṇṇu pallu eṇṇu mutaliya coṟkaḷākat tiritali iṉṟum kāṇkiṟōm.
kuṟṟiyalukaram toṭarmoḻikkaṇ orumāttiri peṟṟu muṟṟiyalukaram pōla niṟṟaliṉāltāṉ, ceyyuḷil acik kuriya eḻuttukkaḷ uyir - uyirmey - kuṟṟiyalukaram - eṉa mūṉṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ceyyuḷāyiṉum cīriṭi aṭutta colloṭu cērātu ōci iṭiyaṟavupaṭṭavaḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram arimāttiri aḷaviṟṟē yāki alaku peṟāta niliyik kāṇkiṟōm. itu piṟkāla nili.
‘kuṉṟu kōṭu nīṭu’ - mūvaciccīr
cīr īṟṟil varum kuṟṟukaram muṟṟukaramāki alaku peṟutali yum kāṇkiṟōm.
‘pāṟṟiṉam ārppap paruntu vaḻippaṭara’ - paruntu: muṟṟukara īṟu. kuṟṟiyalukarattit tolkāppiyaṉār uyirīṟṟil aṭakkavilli. īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟutaliṉ atu meyyīṟu pōlak koḷḷappaṭṭu uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum eṉpatē tol. karuttu.
naṉṉūlār kuṟṟiyalukaratti uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭakki akkuṟṟiya lukaram uyirvariṉ keṭum eṉṟār. 	(vē. reṭṭiyār āyvukkaruttu)
kuṟṟiyalukaram (2) -
taṉineṭil, āytam, (meyyiṉi ūrnta) uyir, valliṉa mey, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉamey eṉṉum ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu īṟṟayal eḻuttāka vara, colliṉ iṟutiyil valliṉap puḷḷiyi ūrntu varum ukaram, taṉ oru māttiriyiṉ kuṟintu arimāttiriyāka olittaliṉ kuṟṟiyalukaramām. īṟṟayaleḻutti nōkkik kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram - āytat toṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram - uyirt toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram - vaṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram, meṉ toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram, iṭittoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉa āṟu vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiya lukaram oṉṟē īreḻuttoru moḻiyāka varum; ēṉiya ellām pala eḻuttukkaḷālākiya moḻiyāka varum.
kuṟṟiyalukaram āṟu valliṉa meykaḷiyum paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu varutaliṉ, āṟu vakiyum āṟu īṟṟeḻuttoṭum uṟaḻak kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 eṉpar.
	e-ṭu : nāku, kaḵcu, kavaṭu, pattu, ceṉṟu, mārpu eṉa āṟu toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷiyum muṟiyē kāṇka.	(naṉ. 94)
kuṟṟiyalukaram iṭavēṟṟumi paṟṟi aṟuvakippaṭum eṉpatu -
neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu, āytam oṉṟu, iṭiyiṟutikaḷil vārāta oḷakāram nīṅkalāka uyir patiṉoṉṟu, valleḻuttu āṟu, melleḻuttu āṟu, valleḻuttukkaḷoṭu toṭa rāta vakaram nīṅkalāka iṭiyeḻuttu aintu - ākiya ivaṟṟi aṭuttu varum kuṟṟiyalukaram, īṟṟayaleḻutti nōkkik kaṇakkiṭa (7+1+11+6+6+5) muppattāṟām; iṭatti nōkka, neṭil - āytam - uyir - vali - meli - iṭi - eṉṉum ivaṟṟi aṭuttu varutaliṉ aṟuvakippaṭum.	(naṉ. 94 civañā).
kuṟṟiyalukaram iṭavēṟṟumi paṟṟi 42 ātal  -
taṉineṭil, kuṟiliṇi, kuṟilneṭil, neṭiloṟṟu, kuṟiliṇi oṟṟu, kuṟilneṭiloṟṟu, kuṟṟoṟṟu eṉṟa ēḻu acikaḷiyum aṭuttu k c ṭ t p ṟ eṉṟa āṟu mey kaḷiyum ūrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram varavē, ataṉ eṇṇikki (7 ō 6 =) 42 ākum eṉpa. iṅṅaṉam vakutta variyaṟiyil, piṇṇākku - cuṇṇāmpu - paṭṭāṅku - viḷiyāṭṭu - iṟumpūtu mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkā. avaṟṟuḷḷē, ṇākku - ṇāmpu - ṭāṅku - yāṭṭu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi neṭiloṟṟiṟuti eṉavum, pūtu eṉpataṉi neṭiliṟuti eṉavum koṇṭu, ivaṟṟiṉkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, pōvatu - varuvatu - oṉpatu - eṉpatu - mutaliyaṉa ippakutiyil aṭaṅkā. ātaliṉ kuṟṟiyalukaratti ataṉmuṉ niṟkum acikaḷ paṟṟi 42 eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭal cālātu.(naṉ. 94 caṅkara.)
kuṟṟiyalukaram iṭavēṟṟumi paṟṟi 36 eṉal -
īṟṟayal eḻuttic ciṟappākak koṇṭu, neṭil 7-āytam - oḷa nīṅkalāṉa uyir 11 - valliṉam 6 - melliṉam 6 - vakaram nīṅkalāṉa iṭiyiṉam 5 āka 36 eṉṟu toki koḷvar. immuṟiyil ekara okaraṅkaḷoṭu vallukaram cērntuvarum colliṉmiyāl, aviyiraṇṭiyum nīkkak kuṟṟiya lukaram 34 ākum. ātaliṉ immuṟi eṇṇikkiyum cālātu.
taṉineṭil, āytam, uyir, vali, meli, iṭi eṉṟa aṟuvakippaṭṭa ivaṟṟit toṭarntu ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu eṉṟa āṟu kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum varutaliṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 eṉpa. iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷ āṟaṉuḷ enta eḻuttiyum ṭu ṟu eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarāmiyiṉ, ivaṟṟi nīkkak kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattu nāṉkē ātaliṉ, immuṟiyil kuṟṟiyalukarattik kaṇakkiṭṭu 36 eṉalum cālātu.
kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ūrntu varum valliṉamey paṟṟiyō, taṉmuṉ uḷḷa eḻuttiṉ iṉam paṟṟiyō āṟu eṉṟa kaṇakkiṭutalē poruttamāṉatām. kuṟṟiyalukaram eḻuttu vēṟṟumiyāl āṟu; iṭavēṟṟumiyāl āṟu eṉa iv viru tiṟaṉiyum uṟaḻntu kūṟāmal taṉittaṉiyākak kūṟutalē takkatu.	(e. ā. pak. 163).


kuṟṟiyalukaram orōvaḻi uṭampaṭumey peṟutal  -
‘taṉmuka mākat tāṉaḻip patuvē’ (naṉ. 303) - itil ‘aḻippatu’ kuṟṟukaramoḻi; ataṉōṭu ēkāra iṭic col vantu ‘aḻippatē’ eṉa muṭiya vēṇṭiyatu vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu ‘aḻippatuvē’ eṉa niṉṟatu. ‘āṟaṉ orumikku atuvum ātuvum’ (naṉ. 300) - itaṉkaṇ ‘ātu’ kuṟṟukaramoḻi; ataṉōṭu um eṉṉum iṭiccol vantu ‘ātum’ eṉ muṭiya vēṇṭiyatu, vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu ‘ātuvum’ eṉa niṉṟatu.	(naṉ. 164 irāmā.)
kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭutal  -
‘uyirvariṉ ukkuṟaḷ meyviṭ ṭōṭum’ eṉa naṉṉūlār nūṟpā amittami pōlat tolkāppiyaṉār potuviti amittilar. avar nūṟu eṉpataṉ muṉṉum āṟu eṉpataṉ muṉṉum muṟiyē oṉṟu mutaliya eṇkaḷum āyiramum vantu puṇariṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭa, niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyir vantu oṉṟi muṭiyum eṉṟār. (nūṟu+ oṉṟu = nūṟṟoṉṟu; āṟu + āyiram = āṟāyiram) oṉṟiṉam muṭittal eṉpataṉāl ēṉiya kuṟṟiyalukaramum uyirmutal moḻi variṉ keṭa, niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl varumoḻi mutal uyir vantu oṉṟi muṭiyum eṉpatu peṟṟām.
kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉmītu uyirēṟi muṭiyum eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē muṟṟiyalukarattiṉ mītum uyirēṟi muṭiyalām. avvāṟē oruvarum kūṟilar. kuṟṟiyalukarattukkum uyir eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu koṇṭamiyiṉ, oṟṟiṉmēlaṉṟi uyiriṉmēl uyirēṟi muṭital poruntātu. nākaritu - eṉpuḻi, itaḻ ciṟitu kuvitalākiya muyaṟci peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukara ōci ittoṭarkkaṇ illi. yakaram varuvaḻi ikaram ukarattiṉ mītu ēṟi muṭiyum eṉṉātu, ukaram keṭa, ikaram kuṟukik kuṟṟiyalikaramāki varum eṉpatē āciriyarkaruttu. eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukaram uyir varuvaḻik keṭumē aṉṟi uyirēṟa iṭam tārātu.	(cū. vi. pak. 40)
nūṟu muṉ mūṉṟu, nāṉku eṉpaṉa puṇarumvaḻik kuṟṟiya lukaram keṭṭatāyiṉ, nūṟṟ mūṉṟu - nūṟṟ nāṉku - eṉṟē attoṭar amiyum. āṟu mutal kuṟuki ‘aṟu’ eṉṟu muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatu āyiṉamiyiṉ, avvukaram keṭṭatu. kuṟṟiyalukarattukku uyir eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭit tol. vaḻaṅkavilli. toṭarmoḻiyil kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiya lukaram eṉṟa tol. karutti yuṭkoṇṭāl, kuṟṟiya lukaram keṭum eṉṟu karutaṟku iṭaṉ ēṟpaṭātu - eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa karuttukkaḷāl civañāṉamuṉivar karuttu maṟukkap paṭukiṟatu.	(to. e. 472, 469 nac.) (e. ā. pak. 176, 177)
kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarum muṟi -
īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷi peṟum. atuvum puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum. kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭamkoṭukkum eṉpatē iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, i.vi. āciriyar, e. ā. āciriyar ākiyōr karuttām. naṉṉūlār uyir variṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiya lukaram keṭum eṉṟār. kuṟṟiyalukaramum muṟṟiyalukaram pōlak keṭum eṉpatē civañāṉamuṉivar karuttām.
nāku + aritu = nākaritu. nākaritu eṉpuḻi, muṉṉark kuṟṟukara ōciyum piṉṉar uyirōciyum peṟṟu, avviraṇṭum kūṭi niṉṟallatu apporuḷ uṇarttal ākāmiyiṉ, uyirēṟuṅkāl kuṟṟukaram keṭṭup pōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyir ēṟiṟṟu eṉṟal poruntātu.	(i.vi. eḻut. 65 uri)
kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟutal -
īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukarattiṉ māttiriyil tāṉ pātiyē peṟuvatu eṉpatik kuṟippiṭa, meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷiyiṭṭu eḻutappeṟum eṉpar pērāciriyar, mayili nātar, caṅkaranamaccivāyar, civañāṉamuṉivar, e.ku. āciriyar, e.ā. āciriyar mutaliyōr. puḷḷi peṟātu eṉṉum karuttiṉar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum. naṉṉūlār itupaṟṟi oṉṟum kūṟavilli.
moḻi mutalil kuṟṟukaram varum nunti eṉṟa colli luḷḷa nu - puḷḷi peṟātu.	(e.ku. pak. 110)
‘kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa’ (to. e. 105 nac.) eṉṟu nūṟpāvil, kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyata ṉāl, moḻimutal kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭākalām.
kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu -
kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu eṉṟatu eṉṉeṉiṉ, orumatam kuṟinta āṉimukakkaṭavuḷi ‘mummatattaṉ’ eṉṟāṟpōla, ilakkaṇi eṉṉum viti paṟṟik kūṟiṉār eṉka. neṭil ēḻum, āytam oṉṟum, moḻi iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum vārāta oḷakāram nīṅkiya uyir patiṉoṉṟum, valloṟṟu āṟum, melloṟṟu āṟum, valleḻuttoṭu toṭarāta vakaroṟṟu nīṅkiya iṭiyoṟṟu aintum āka muppattāṟum īṟṟayal eḻuttāka vara, avaṟṟi nōkkak kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu eṉpa.	(naṉ. 94 irāmā.)
‘āṉimukattāṉi mummatattaṉ eṉal’ kāṇka.
kuṟṟiyalukaram meḻimutaṟkaṇ varal -
nunti eṉṟa muṟippeyariṭattu varum ‘nu’, itaḻic ciṟitu kuvitta aḷavil tōṉṟum oliyāy, moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukara māyiṟṟu. itaṉi muṟṟiyalukaramāka olippiṉum poruḷvēṟupā ṭiṉṟu.	(to. e. 67, 68 nac.)
‘kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟu’ -
kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkum.		(to. e. 106 iḷa., 105 nac. uri)
kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōla īṟṟil varuvaḻip puḷḷi peṟum.	(pērā., cū. vi., e. ku., e.ā.)
kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷi peṟum. puḷḷi peṟutal māttiri cempātiyātalik kuṟippatākalā ṉum, kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟutal ‘muppāṟ puḷḷi’ eṉṟa toṭarālēyē pulaṉāvatākalāṉum, moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum kuṟṟukaram puḷḷi peṟātu eṉpatu āciriyar karuttākātu.
kuṟṟiyalukara muṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
kuṟṟiyalukaram arimāttiri aḷaviṟṟu; varivaṭivil puḷḷi peṟuvatu. eṉavē īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷiyīṟākum. puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉmītu varumoḻimutalil varum uyirēṟi muṭiyum. ākavē, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭuppatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭātu; muṟṟiya lukaramāyiṉ ukaram keṭa niṉṟa meyyiṉmītu uyirēṟi muṭiyum.
“aṟu eṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭu kēṭu kūṟiṉār; eṉṉi? kuṟṟukaramāyiṉ ēṟimuṭitaliṉ”	(to. e. 469.nac. uri)
“aḵtu + aṉ + ai - āytam keṭāmuṉpē aṉṉiṉ akarattik kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl ēṟṟuka; āytam keṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy muṭitaliṉ.”	(422 nac. uri)
ittolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra nac. urippakutikaḷ uḷaṅkoḷat takkaṉa.
kuṟṟiyalukara muṟṟiyalukarap poruḷ vēṟupāṭu -
perukku, kaṭṭu mutalāyiṉa peyarākiyavaḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷām. avi muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉiyākiyavaḻi muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷām. kātu, kattu, murukku, teruṭṭu mutalāyiṉavum aṉṉa.	(to. e. 68 nac. uri)
kuṟṟiyalukara muṟippeyar -
nunti eṉṟa muṟippeyaril mutaleḻuttākiya nakaratti ūrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram vantuḷḷatu. itaṉi itaḻic ciṟitu kuvittuk kuṟṟiyalukaramāka olippiṉum, mikutiyum kuvittu muṟṟiyalukaramāka olippiṉum poruḷ vēṟupāṭu illi.		(to. e. 68 nac. uri)
kuṟṟukarīṟṟut terinili viṉimuṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷ -
naṭa vā - mutalāka aḵku īṟākak kūṟappaṭṭa ivvirupattu mūṉṟum uyirum oṟṟum kuṟṟukaramum ākiya īṟṟavākip paṭuttalōciyāṉ acceykimēl peyarttaṉmip paṭṭu viṉimāttiriyē uṇartti niṟpaṉa. (viṉippakuti kaḷākiya mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyariṉ iyalpuṭiya ivaṟṟi vaṭanūlār ‘tātu’ eṉpa). kuṟṟukaratti vēṟu pirittataṉāl, pōkku - pāyccu - ūṭṭu - naṭattu - eḻuppu - tīṟṟu - it toṭakkattu vāypāṭṭāṉ varuvaṉavum koḷka.	(i.vi. 43 uri)
kuṟṟukara īṟṟu nāṟperunticikaḷ -
avi vaṭakku,teṟku, kuṇakku, kuṭakku eṉpaṉa.		(naṉ.185 mayili.)
kuṟṟukaram, kuṟṟikaram iraṇṭiṉmēlum puḷḷiyiṭutaliṉ payaṉ -
tātu - ētu - eṉṟal toṭakkattu āriya moḻikaḷum, eṭṭu - koṭṭu - eṉṟal toṭakkattup potumoḻikaḷum, kuṉṟi yātu - nāṭiyātu - eṭṭiyāṇṭuḷatu - eṉṟal toṭakkattup puṇarmoḻip poruḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷum aṟitaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟṟukaram kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēl puḷḷi koṭuppārumuḷar.		(naṉ. 97 mayili.)
āriyamoḻikaḷil īṟṟukkuṟṟukaram nikaḻātu. eḷ + tu, koḷ+tu - ivi eṭṭu koṭṭu eṉa vanta coṟkaḷ ēval viṉiyātaliṉ āṇṭuk kuṟṟukaram vārātu. eṭṭu - koṭṭu - eṉpaṉa puṇarmoḻi yākātu taṉiccollāy niṉṟavaḻi viṉic collākāmal peyarāyiṉaviṭattē kuṟṟukaramām. kuṉṟi - nāṭi - eṭṭi - eṉpaṉa nilimoḻiyākātu, kuṉṟu- nāṭu - eṭṭu 
eṉpaṉavē nilimoḻiyāy. varumoḻi mutaṟ kaṇ yakaram varutaliṉ īṟṟu ukaram tirinta ikaramē kuṟṟiya likaramām.
kuṟṟukara vāypāṭṭu viṉippakutikaḷ -
kuṟṟukaratti vēṟu pirittu ōtiya ataṉāṉ, pōkku pāyccu uruṭṭu kaṭattu eḻuppu tīṟṟu - eṉṟal toṭakkattu vāypāṭṭāṉ varuvaṉavum, allā īṟukaḷāṉ varum vāypāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉavum ‘cey’ ēvalil aṭaṅkum eṉṟu koḷka.	(naṉ. 136 mayili.)
kuṟṟeḻut taḷapeṭi -
kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷāy nīṇṭu mīṇṭum oli mika aḷapeṭi eḻuttup peṟum nili kuṟṟeḻuttaḷa peṭiyām.
varum eṉpatu, ‘varūum iṟuti’ (to. co. 9) ‘yāteṉa varūum’ (co. 32) eṉṟāṟ pōla, varūum eṉak kuṟṟeḻut taḷapeṭi āyiṟṟu.
	e-ṭu	:	eḻu	-	‘eḻūut tāṅkiya katavu’	(puṟanā. 97)
			kuḻu	-	‘kuḻūuk kaḷiṟṟuk kuṟumpu’	(puṟanā. 97)
			paḻu	-	‘paḻūuppal aṉṉa’	(kuṟun. 180)
			paru	-	‘parūuppiṇiya toṭi’	(puṟanā. 97)
ivvāṟu kuṟṟeḻuttuḷḷaviṭattu aḷapeṭi vantatu.		(to. e. 261 nac. uri)
‘kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻi niṟi’ yāmi -
kuṟṟeḻuttu (uyir) aintāvaṉa a i u e o eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mutal mūṉṟum cuṭṭu iṭiccol; ekaram viṉāiṭiccol; okaram ‘oppu’ eṉṟa paṇpiṉ pakuti yāyt taṉittu vārātu eṉpa.
ivi peyarō viṉiyō ākāmal iṭiccollākap peyarccollic cārntu niṉṟē cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttutalāṉum, okaram oppuppaṇpiṉ pakuti eṉpatu ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭu aṉmiyāṉum, ivi moḻiniramputal illi eṉpatu. ‘aintum moḻi niṟipila’ eṉavē, nāṉku iṭiccollāy moḻi niṟikkalām eṉpatum karuttām.	(to. e. 44 nac. uri)
kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ iṭiniṉṟa oṟṟi māttiri mikuttal -
kuṟṟeḻuttup palavāka varutalāṉ tōṉṟum vaṇṇam kuṟuñcīrvaṇṇam. akkuṟṟeḻuttup payiṉṟu varutalāṉ, iṭiniṉṟa oṟṟiṉ oli mikum.
 e-ṭu : ‘kuraṅṅkuḷip polinta koycuval puravi’  (akanā. 4)
eṉa muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉ ōciyāṉ ṅakara oṟṟoli nīṇṭavāṟu. (kuraṅṅkuḷi)	(to. e. 50 nac. uri)
kuṟṟeḻuttu  vēṟupeyarkaḷ -
kuṟumi eṉiṉum, iraccuvam eṉiṉum, kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉiṉum oruporuṭkiḷavi.	(mu.vī. eḻut. 9)
kuṟṟoṟṟāka vārā meykaḷ -
rakaroṟṟum ḻakaroṟṟum taṉikkuṟiliṉpiṉ oṟṟāka vārā. avi neṭil, kuṟṟiliṇi ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarē oṟṟāka varum; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyākavē varum.
	e-ṭu :	tār, avar, vara, karu; tāḻ, itaḻ, uḻa, maḻu					(to. e. 49 nac. uri)
kuṟaḷ maḵkāṉ -
makarakkuṟukkam. lakara ḷakara meykaḷ tirinta ṉakara ṇakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ varum makaram, taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukik kāl māttiriyāy oru moḻi iṟutikkaṇ varum. nilimoḻi makarīṟṟu viṉiyāka niṟka, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vakaram nikaḻumāyiṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram kuṟukik kāl māttiriyāy irumoḻikkaṇ varum. ivvāṟu iṭa vakiyāl makarak kuṟukkam mūṉṟām.
	varumāṟu : pōlum ḻū pōlm ḻū pōṉm, maruḷum ḻū maruḷm ḻū maruṇm - orumoḻi makarakkuṟukkam	
tarum vaḷavaṉ - irumoḻi makarakkuṟukkam.	(naṉ. 96)
kuṟiccūttiram -
palavaṟṟiyum kuṟittu aṟiya varum cūttiram kuṟiccūttiram. ‘ammutal īrāṟu āvi’ eṉavum, ‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’ eṉavum, aṟital aḷavāy varum cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟic cūttiraṅkaḷ.	(naṉ. 20 irāmā.)
kuṟi, ceyki viḷakkam -
cūttiravaki āṟaṉuḷ ivi cila. kuṟiyāvaṉa ivi uyir, ivi oṟṟu, ivi peyar, ivi viṉi eṉṟal toṭakkattu aṟitalmāttiriyāy varuvaṉa. kuṟi eṉpatu aṟitali uṇarttiya mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar. ceyki yāvaṉa patam muṉ vikutiyum patamum urupum puṇarum puṇarcci viti aṟintu, aṅṅaṉam aṟital māttiriyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻip puṇarttalic ceytalum, peyarum viṉiyum koḷḷum muṭipu viti aṟintu aṅṅaṉam aṟital māttiriyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻi muṭittalic ceytalum mutaliyaṉa.	(naṉ. 20 caṅkara.)
‘kuṟittu varu kiḷavi’ -
nilimoḻip poruḷ niramputaṟku ataṉik kuṟittu varum collākiya varumoḻi.
	e-ṭu : cāttaṉ vantāṉ - eṉṟa toṭaril, cāttaṉ eṉpatu nili moḻiyākiya niṟutta col; vantāṉ eṉpatu varumoḻi yākiya kuṟittu varu kiḷavi. 	(to. e. 107 nac.)

kuṟippici -
‘āviyum oṟṟum aḷaviṟantu olikkum iṭaṅkaḷ’ kāṇka.
kuṟiyataṉ kīḻ ā -
taṉikkuṟili aṭutta ākārīṟṟup peyar kuṟukutalum, ataṉōṭu ukaram ēṟṟalum, iyalpākavē ākārīṟāy varutalum eṉṟa mūṉṟu niliyum ceyyuṭku uriya.
e-ṭu : nilā, nila, nilavu; palā, pala, palavu; 	cuṟā, cuṟa, cuṟavu; kaṉā, kaṉa, kaṉavu
‘nilā vaṇaṅku’ eṉavum, ‘nila viri kāṉal’ eṉavum, ‘eṉ ceyyumō nilavu’ eṉavum ceyyuṭkaṇ varumāṟu kāṇka.		(naṉ. 172)
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉ uru iraṭṭal -
taṉikkuṟṟeḻutti aṭuttuc colliṉ iṟutiyil varum oṟṟukkaḷ varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ uyirvariṉ iraṭṭum. iṅṅaṉam oṟṟu iraṭṭuvaṉa ñakāra ṅakāra rakāra ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. īṟṟil varum meykaḷ ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṟa patiṉoṉṟām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñakāram uriñ eṉṟa orē moḻikkaṇṇum, nakāram porun - verin - eṉṟa iru moḻikkaṇṇum aṉṟi vārā. ivi kuṟṟoṟṟu alla. ra ḻa eṉpaṉa kuṟṟoṟṟāka vārā. eṉavē, ṇa ma ṉa ya la va ḷa eṉṟa ēḻu meykaḷumē taṉikkuṟili aṭuttu moḻi yīṟāy variṉ iraṭṭum.
	e-ṭu : maṇ + aritu = maṇṇaritu; kam + aritu = kammaritu; poṉ + akal = poṉṉakal; mey + arumi = meyyarumi; kal + aritu = kallaritu; tev + aritu = tevvaritu; kaḷ + arumi = kaḷḷarumi.
iraṭṭutal iruvaḻikkaṇṇum ām.	(to. e. 160 nac. uri)
kuṟiyatu -
kuṟṟeḻuttu; kuṟil eṉavumpaṭum. ippeyarāṉ, uyirkkuṟ ṟeḻuttum, uyirmeykkuṟṟeḻuttum amiyum. itaṉ māttiri oṉṟu.	(to. e. 160 nac).
kuṟil -
a i u e o eṉṟa aintum oru māttiriyē peṟum uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttām. ivi 18 meykaḷmītu ēṟavē amiyum (18 ō 5 =) 90 eḻuttukkaḷum uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttām. kuṟilukku māttiri oṉṟu.	(naṉ. 64, 87)
kuṟiliṇi, kuṟilneṭil muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutal -
kuṟil māttiri nīṇṭu neṭilākum. ākavē, kuṟil neṭil eṉpaṉa māttirikoṇṭu vevvēṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa. irukuṟil iṇintu iraṇṭu māttiri koṇṭaṉavum, kuṟil neṭil iṇintu mūṉṟu māttiri koṇṭaṉavum, iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṟṟāya neṭilpōla, varu moḻik kaṇ t n variṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa l ḷ - eṉṟa meykaḷ keṭum.  (varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa takaram ṟakaramāka vum ṭakaramākavum tiriyum; nakaram ṉakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākat tiriyum.)
e-ṭu :	kōḷ + tītu = kōṭītu; viral + tītu = viraṟītu; varāl + tītu = varāṟītu; kōḷ + nimirntatu = kōṇi mirntatu; viral + nimirntatu = viraṉimirntatu; varāl + nimirntatu = varāṉimirntatu; 
tol. neṭilmuṉ oṟṟuk keṭaṟkē viti kūṟi, kuṟiliṇi muṉṉarum kuṟilneṭil muṉṉarum oṟṟuk keṭaṟku viti kūṟātu, pōnta poruḷāl  koḷvittār.	(to. e. 50 iḷa. uri)
kuṟukkal vikāram -
ceyyuḷ vikāram āṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ceyyuṭkaṇ taḷi citiyāmi vēṇṭi neṭṭeḻuttuk kuṟukuvatu kuṟukkal vikāramām. e-ṭu : ‘tiruttārnaṉ ṟeṉṟēṉ tiyēṉ’
‘tīyēṉ’ eṉaṟpālatu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷi piḻiyāmip poruṭṭākat ‘tiyēṉ’ eṉa neṭil kuṟuki niṉṟatu. (naṉ.155)
kūṭṭi eḻūutal -
eḻu - taṉviṉi; eḻūu - piṟaviṉi. kūṭṭi eḻūutalāvatu cērttu eḻupputal. itaṉāl taṉviṉiyip piṟaviṉi yākkutaṟ poruṭṭāka aḷapeṭi payaṉpaṭuttavāṟu pulaṉām. itu collici yaḷapeṭiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(to. e. 6 nac.)
keṭutal -
puṇarcci vikāram mūṉṟaṉuḷ keṭutalum oṉṟu.
	e-ṭu :	maram + vēr = maravēr
nilimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram keṭṭami ivvikāramām.			(naṉ. 154)
kēṇmiyā : collamippu -
kēḷ eṉṟa muṉṉili viṉippakutiyoṭu ‘miyā’ eṉṟa uriyaciccol cērak kēṇmiyā eṉṟākum eṉpar tolkāppiṉār.
‘kēṇm’ eṉṟa colloṭu, yā eṉṉum (muṉṉili) aciccol, iṭiyē ikaram tōṉṟappeṟṟuc cēra, kēṇmiyā eṉṟākum eṉpar ikkāla āyvāḷar.	(e. ā. pak. 36)
kinnoṭi aḷavu -
kaṭṭivirali naṭuviralil ūṉṟi iraṇṭaṉiyum muṟukki oliyeḻupputaṟkaṇ nikaḻum kāla iṭiveḷiyum, eḻuttiṉ aḷavākiya orumāttiri kāla iṭiveḷiyum oṉṟākum. ivvāṟu olieḻupputaṟkaṇ, niṉittamāttiriyāṉē kālmāttiriyum, kaṭṭivirali naṭuviralil ūṉṟiyavaḻi ari māttiriyum, muṟukkiyavaḻi mukkāl māttiriyum, ōci eḻuppiyavaḻi oru māttiriyum ākiya kālakkaḻivu nikaḻum eṉpa.
noṭittaltoḻilil piṟanta ōciyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum, a eṉap piṟanta ōciyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum okkum.	(to. e. 7 nac. uri)
kolyāṉi mutaliya viṉittokiyi oru collākak kōṭal -
mukkālattukkum potuvāṉa viṉittokiyi oru kālattiṟkuriya peyareccat toṭarāka virittal kuṉṟak kūṟalām eṉṟu karuti, viṉittokiyi nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pakuttavaḻit tokipporuḷ citiyum eṉṟa karuttāṉ āciriyar viṉittokiyip ‘puṇariyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟātu’ eṉa, orucol nīrmiya tākavē koṇṭamiyiṉ, nac. viṉittokiyi oru collākak kāṭṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷoṭu kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.	(to. e. 24 nac.)
koḷ eṉṉum vikuti -
koḷ eṉpatu viṉippayaṉ viṉimutalic ceṉṟaṭita lākiya taṟporuṭṭup poruṭkaṇ vanta vikuti. itaṉi vaṭanūlār ‘āṟpaṉēpatam’ eṉpa.
	e-ṭu :	ceytukoṇṭāṉ.
itaṉkaṇ, ceytalākiya viṉiyiṉ payaṉ eḻuvāyākiya āṇpāṟporuḷiyē ceṉṟaṭital uṇarttappaṭṭatu.		(cū.vi. pak. 41)
kō eṉum col puṇarumāṟu -
kō eṉṟa ōkāra īṟṟuccol urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum oṉcāriyipeṟṟu varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarum. oṉcāriyi piṟkālattu ‘ṉ’ cāriyi āyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu 	kō + oṉ + ai = kōoṉi; kō + oṉ + ki = kōoṉki
ōkārīṟṟup peyar okaramākiya eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi peṟutalum, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkup puṇartalum vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ uṇṭu.
e-ṭu : kō + kaṭumi = kōokkaṭumi
kō eṉpatu aracaṉik kuṟikkum uyartiṇipporuḷatu āyiṉum, kō vantatu eṉṟāṟ pōla aḵṟiṇi viṉi yoṭu muṭitaliṉ, collāṉ aḵṟiṇiyām. aracu, amiccu, tūtu, purōcu, oṟṟu mutalāyiṉavum aṉṉa.	(to. e. 292, 293 nac. uri)
kōṭṭunūṟum mañcaḷum kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cevvaṇṇam -
cuṇṇāmpum mañcaḷum iraṇṭaṟak kūṭiyavaḻiyē cenniṟam tōṉṟuvatu pōla, neṭilum kuṟilum iṇintu oṉṟākak kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṟantu, piṉṉar appiḷavupaṭā ōciyi aḷapeṭi eṉṟu tol. vēṇṭiṉār eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.  (e. 6 nac. uri) aḷapeṭi iraṇṭu māttiriyum oru māttiriyumākap pirittu aci koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, aḷapeṭik kuṟil alaku peṟāta niliyumuṇṭu ātalāṉum, aḷapeṭikkaṇ neṭilum kuṟilum viralum viralum cēraniṉṟāṟpōla aḷapeṭukkum eṉpatē tol. karuttātal peṟappaṭukiṟatu.	(e. ā. pak. 45,46).
kō, mā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ yakaram peṟutal -
kō, mā eṉpavaṟṟu muṉ uyir variṉ, vakaramē aṉṟi yakaramum uṭampaṭumeyyām.
	varumāṟu :	kō + il = (kōvil) kōyil
		mā + iru (ñālam) = māyiru (ñālam)
(mu.vī. puṇa. 25)

kōṉ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
kōṉ eṉṟa iyaṟpeyar ‘tanti’ eṉṟa muṟippeyaroṭum, ‘makaṉ’ eṉṟa muṟiyil kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyāka varum ēṉiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷoṭum poruntumvaḻi, ṉakarīṟṟa iyaṟ peyarkaḷiṉ ciṟappuviti peṟāmal, aḵṟiṇi iyaṟpeyar kaḷukkuriya potuvitiyāṉ iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	varumāṟu :	kōṉ + tanti = kōṉṟanti
		kōṉ + koṟṟaṉ = kōṉ koṟṟaṉ				(to. e. 351 nac.)
ṅa
ṅakaram mutal ātal -
ṅaṉam eṉpatu iṭattiṉiyum taṉmiyiṉiyum uṇarttum palaporuḷ orucollāy variṉum, taṉittu varum taṉmiyataṉṟi, muṭavaṉ kōl ūṉṟi vantāṟpōlac cuṭṭum viṉāvum ākiya iṭiccoṟkaḷi muṉṉiṭṭu varutalāṉ ‘vaḻi’ eṉṟum, ēṉiya meykaḷ pōla mutalākā miyiṉ ‘avvoṭu’ eṉṉātu  ‘avvi oṭṭi,  eṉṟum, oruvāṟṟāṉ mutalātaliṉ iḻivu ciṟappāka ‘ṅavvum’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.	(naṉ. 106 caṅkara.)
ṅakaram moḻimutal ākāmi -
naṉṉūlār ‘cuṭṭiyā...... mutalākummē’ eṉṟu ṅakaram, a i u eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭum - yāviṉāvum - ekaraviṉāvum- eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉvaḻi, akarattoṭu moḻimutalākum eṉṟu kūṟiyami poruntātu. moḻikku mutalām eḻuttu ivi, īṟāvaṉa ivi eṉa īṇṭuk karuvi ceytatu, mēl puṇariyalil, nilimoḻi īṟu varumoḻi mutalōṭu iyiyap puṇarkkum poruṭṭaṉṟē? avvāṟu puṇarttaṟku iyipillāta ṅakaramum, aṅṅaṉam - iṅṅaṉam - uṅṅaṉam - yāṅṅaṉam - eṅṅaṉam - eṉa ivvāṟu moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal payaṉil kūṟṟām. aṉṟiyum, aṅku - āṅku - yāṇṭu- yāṇṭiyatu - aṉṉa - eṉṉa - eṉṟāṟpōlum ivvoṟ ṟukkaḷum (ṅ, ṇ, ṉ eṉpaṉa) moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal vēṇṭutaliṉ, avarkkum atu karuttaṉṟu eṉpatu.		(i.vi. eḻut. 27 uri)
ca
cakara akaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ vārāmi -
cattāṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkaṟpāla col cettāṉ eṉṟu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. iccol teluṅkil cacceṉu eṉavum, kaṉṉaṭattil cattaṉu eṉavum, maliyāḷattil cattu eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ivaṟṟāl cakaraakaram paṇṭu cakarekarac cāyiyil tamiḻil olittirukkum eṉṟu uyttuṇaralām.
cari camaḻppu caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi caḷi cakaṭu caṭṭi cavaḷi cavi caraṭu cantu cataṅki caḻakku mutaliya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiya ṉār kālattu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭila eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.	(e. ā. pak. 66, 67)
cakaram moḻimutal ātal -
cari camaḻppu caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi caḷi cakaṭu caṭṭi cavaḷi cavi caraṭu cantu cataṅki caḻakku mutaliyaṉa vaḻakku ceyyuḷ eṉum īriṭattum varum cakaramutaṉ moḻikaḷ.	(naṉ. 105 mayili.)
cakara mutaṉmoḻi -
‘cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē, a ai oḷeṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē’ eṉpatu mutalāka iṉṉōraṉṉa cila eḻuttuk kaḷi moḻikku mutalākā eṉa āciriyar tol. vilakkiṉā reṉiṉ, ivvaṭamoḻikaḷum ticiccoṟkaḷum akkālattu ivvāṟu tamiḻiṉkaṇ payiṉṟu vārāmi paṟṟi eṉka.	(naṉ. 106 civañā.)
caṅkatam mutalākiya nāṉku -
col, caṅkatam eṉavum pākatam eṉavum canukkirakam eṉavum avappirañcaṉam eṉavum nāṉku vakippaṭum.	(mu.vī. moḻi. 28)
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉaviṭattup pirappaṅkōl ākāmi -
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉaviṭattup pirappaṅkōl ākātu; atu pōla, ikarukaraṅkaḷ kuṟukiṉaviṭattum avi uyirākaṟ pālaṉa. avaṟṟip puṇarccivēṟṟumiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumiyum nōkki vēṟu eḻuttu eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉār.	(to. e. 2 iḷa. uri)
ikara ukaram kuṟukiniṉṟaṉa, vikāravakiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟu paṭutaliṉ. ivaṟṟi iṅṅaṉam kuṟiyiṭṭu āḷutal ellāruk kum oppa muṭintatu. cantaṉak kōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅkōl ākātu; atu pōla uyiratu kuṟukkamum uyirēyām.	(2 nac. uri)
aikārakkuṟukkam mutaliyaṉa oru kāraṇam paṟṟik kuṟukiṉa vākaliṉ, ciṟumaram peruttuḻiyum perumaram ciṟuttuḻiyum vēṟoru maram ākātavāṟu pōla, vēṟeḻuttu eṉappaṭā.		(cū.vi. pak. 30).
canti muṭivu mūṉṟē -
tōṉṟiya cantiyum, tirinta cantiyum keṭṭa cantiyum eṉac canti mūṉṟām.
yāṉikkōṭu eṉpatu tōṉṟiya canti; maṭkuṭam eṉpatu tirinta canti; maravēr eṉpatu keṭṭa canti. (ivaṟṟuḷ muṟiyē kakaramey tōṉṟiyavāṟum, ṇakaram ṭakaramāyt tirintavāṟum, makaram keṭṭavāṟum kāṇka.)
cantiyiṉi nāl eṉa iyalpucantiyum kūṭṭic cilar colla, innūluṭiyār iyalpucantiyi nīkkiyatu eṉṉiyō eṉiṉ, iyalpucantikaḷil mikkum tirintum keṭṭum varuvaṉa illi yātaliṉ, muṭikkavēṇṭum cantikaḷ illāmalē (illāmiyālē eṉpatu poruḷ) nīkkiṉār.	(nēmi. eḻut. 12 uri)
cantiyakkaram -
akarattiṉ muṉṉar ikaramum yakaramum tammuḷ ottu eytiṉ ai eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttu olikkum. akarattuṭaṉ ukaramum vakaramum tammuḷ ottu orutaṉmiyavāka eytiṉ oḷa eṉṉum neṭ ṭeḻuttu olikkum.
‘moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyāta taṉiyum muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal’ eṉṉum uttiyāṉ, akarak kūṟum ikarakkūṟum tammuḷ ottu ekaram olikkum; akarak kūṟum ukarak kūṟum tammuḷ ottu okaram olikkum eṉak koḷka.
iṉi iccūttirattiṟku ivvāṟu poruḷ koḷḷātu, tamiḻ nūṟpayiṟci oṉṟumēyuṭiyār eḻuttuppōli uṇarttiṟṟu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu, 
	a i = ai; ay = ai; ki = ki; kay = ki
	a u = oḷa; av = oḷa; ku = ku; kav = ku
eṉa utāraṇam kāṭṭuvar. avvāṟu poruḷkoṇṭu utāraṇam kāṭṭumāṟṟāl perumpayaṉ iṉmiyāṉum, vaṭanūloṭu māṟupaṭum ātalāṉum atu poruntātu eṉka.	(naṉ. 125 civañā.)
‘cār’: puṇarumāṟu -
cār eṉṉum marappeyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi iṉamel leḻuttu mikum.
e-ṭu : cārṅkōṭu, cārñcetiḷ, cārntōl, cārmpū
varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ kāḻ eṉṟa col variṉ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam mikkup puṇarum.
	cār + kāḻ = cārkkāḻ eṉa varum (to. e. 363, 364 nac.)
cārpu  vēṟupeyarkaḷ -
pullal eṉiṉum, cārtal eṉiṉum, puṇartal eṉiṉum, cārpeṉṉum oruporuṭ kiḷavi.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 23)

cārpeḻuttiṉ iṭamum muyaṟciyum -
cārpeḻuttukkaḷ  tattam mutaleḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum iṭamē tamakkup piṟappiṭamāy avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟattukkuriya muyaṟciyē tam tōṟṟattukkum muyaṟciyāyp piṟakkum. ākavē, avi tamakkeṉat taṉippiṟappiṭamō muyaṟciyō uṭiyaṉa alla.	(naṉ. 87)
cārpeḻuttu -
taṉittāṉum kakaroṟṟu mutaliyaṉa pōla akaramoṭu civaṇi yāṉum iyaṅkum iyalpiṉṟi, oru moḻiyic cārntu varutalē tamakku ilakkaṇamākavuṭiya kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttām. cārntuvaral marapiṉiyuṭiya mūṉṟum akaram pōlat taṉittu niṟṟal āṟṟāmiyiṉ, neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉuḷ peṟap paṭā ātaliṉ, ivi ‘eḻuttu ōraṉṉa’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.	(cū. vi. pak. 18, 19)
tamakkeṉat taṉitta piṟappiṭamiṉṟit tām cārnta eḻuttiṉ piṟappē piṟappiṭamākat tōṉṟum kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiya lukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟē cārpeḻuttām.	(to. e. 101 nac.)
uyirum meyyumākiya mutaleḻuttic cārntu avaṟṟiṉ iṭamākap piṟappaṉa cārpeḻuttukkaḷām. aviyāvaṉa uyirmey, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi, kuṟṟiya likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, aikārak kuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉṟa pattām.	(naṉ. 60)
uyirmey uyirum meyyum kūṭip piṟattalāṉum, āytam uyirpōla orōvaḻi alaku peṟṟum meypōla orōvaḻi alaku peṟātum orupuṭi ottu avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē cārntu varutalāṉum, ēṉiyavi tattam mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripu vikārattāl piṟattalāṉum cārpeḻuttāyiṉa. (naṉ. 60 caṅ.)
tammoṭu tām cārntum, iṭaṉ cārntum, iṭaṉum paṟṟuk kōṭum cārntum vikārattāl varutaliṉ cārpeḻut tāyiṉa.	(mayili.)
cārpeḻuttu oṉpatu eṉṟal tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭē -
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttu eṉṟa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum, ēṉiya uyirmey uyiraḷapeṭi oṟṟaḷapeṭi aikārakkuṟukkam oḷakārakkuṟukkam makarak kuṟukkam eṉṟa āṟaṉiyum piṉṉar oruvāṟṟāṉ taḻuvutalāṉum, (mutalum cārpum aṉṟi) mūṉṟāvatoru pakuti iṉṟātalāṉum, mutaleḻuttām taṉmi ivvoṉpataṟkum iṉmiyāṉum, avi cārpil tōṉṟutaluṭimiyāṉum, ivvāṟaṉiyum avaṟṟuṭaṉ talippeytu ‘oṉpatum cārpiṉ pāla’ eṉṟār. ,	(i. vi. eḻut. 5 uri).
cārpeḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu -
cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum, tām cārntu tōṉṟum talimi yeḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaḷiyici - viṉikkaḷa muyaṟci – piṟappiyalpu kaḷōṭu oruṅkottu, tattam iyalpoṭu kūṭi avviraṇṭu taṉmiyum otta tōṟṟattōṭu uruvākip piṟakkum.
	varumāṟu : miyā eṉpataṉkaṇ niṟkum kuṟṟiyalikaram, makarattiṉatu piṟappiṭamākiya iyinta itaḻi yum yakarattiṉ piṟappiṭamākiya aṭinā aṇṇat tiyum cārntu, taṉatu iyalpu tōṉṟap piṟakkum.
nākaritu eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ niṟkum kuṟṟiyalukaram, taṉatu paṟṟukkōṭākiya kakaramey piṟappiṭattiyum cārpākiya akarattiṉ piṟappiṭattiyum cārntu, taṉatu iyalpu tōṉṟap piṟakkum.
aḵtu eṉṉum coṟkaṇniṟkum āytam, akarattiṟkum takara meykkum uriya aṇṇam - pal- nā- ākiya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ toḻilāṉ avaṟṟic cārntu taṉatu iyalpu tōṉṟap piṟakkum.	(to.e.101. ca.pāla.)
cārpeḻuttukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki paṟṟiya ilakkaṇa nūlār koḷki -
